# LexGo mobile — complete product, screen and data brief for the redesign

> Audience: Claude Design (or any designer). Scope: the whole mobile app — every role, every screen, what each screen does, what data feeds it and what the client/professional must be able to do on a phone. Everything is read from the shipped Expo/React Native code on 2026-10-28; nothing is invented. Uzbek UI labels are quoted verbatim in backticks. The short, length-limited prompts for Home and the documents tab are in `PROMPT-SHORT.md`.

## 0. What LexGo is

LexGo is an Uzbekistan legal-services ecosystem: an AI legal assistant, online legal services (templates, documents written or checked by a duty advocate), a marketplace of advocates and jurists with paid orders, urgent "reach an advocate now" requests, a personal-advocate subscription, and a support/call-centre layer. The mobile app is one Expo client (iOS + Android) of the same backend as the Next.js web portal. Primary language Uzbek (Latin); Russian and English are full locales. Light and dark themes ship; iOS 26 Liquid Glass is available for the floating layer (tab bar, sheets, header buttons).

Base API: `https://lexgo.api.cognilabs.org` (JSON, bearer token; responses are loosely typed, many fields optional, money in UZS integers, ids are UUIDs or human numbers like `ADV-1042`, `CASE-1039`). Web socket for live events on the same host (`wss://…`). Support phone `+998 78 777 00 00`.

## 1. Roles the app supports

| Role (UI)            | Backend role | Who                                                                              | Lands on      | Tab bar                                                        |
| -------------------- | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Guest**            | —            | not signed in                                                                    | Home          | client bar; `Ishlarim` and `Xabarlar` open sign-in             |
| **Client** (`mijoz`) | `client`     | a person or company with a legal need                                            | Home          | `Bosh sahifa` · `Hujjatlar` · **AI** · `Ishlar` · `Xabarlar`   |
| **Advokat**          | `advocate`   | licensed advocate selling on the marketplace, taking urgent cases, running cases | `Ish maydoni` | `Ish maydoni` · `So'rovlar` · **AI** · `Mijozlar` · `Xabarlar` |
| **Yurist**           | `lawyer`     | jurist (no advocate licence): documents, marketplace jobs, cases                 | `Ish maydoni` | same bar as the advokat                                        |

Other backend roles (staff, admin, call-centre, sales, organisation) are refused at sign-in since 2026-10-26: the app is for these four only. A professional whose account is still under review (`limited_access`) sees a reduced drawer and only the workspace + AI tabs until approved (onboarding gate).

Design principle: **one design system, three tuned experiences.** The client is a worried person who needs one clear next step; the advokat/yurist is a daily power user who needs the next deadline, order or client one glance away. Section order and navigation per role must stay predictable; only how things look is open.

## 2. How to read the screen sections

Every screen below is written as: who sees it, its purpose, the layout top→bottom with real labels, every action, every state, the backend data it reads/writes (method, path, key fields), and a UX note. Routes are expo-router paths (`/pro/cases/[id]` = one case). "Sheet" = a bottom sheet. "Tab" = a slot in the bottom bar. "Drawer" = the side menu behind the ☰ button on every tab page.

---

# PART A — CLIENT (and guest): every screen

### `/` — `Bugun qanday yuridik muammoni hal qilamiz?` (Home tab; no header title, the hero headline is the title)

- **Who:** signed-in client and guest see the same page (`app/(tabs)/index.tsx` → `src/pages/home/HomePage.tsx` → `ClientHome.tsx`). A guest gets a sign-in card (`DashGuest`) where a client has `Faol so'rovlaringiz` and the referral progress. A professional (advokat/yurist) never sees this tab: `HomePage` renders nothing for the "seller" role and the tabs layout redirects them to their first tab (`Ish maydoni`). No plan gating on the page itself; the upsell banner depends on the client's plan (below).
- **Purpose:** the client types their problem in one field or picks one of six quick ways in (describe / urgent advocate / find a specialist / car-accident consultation / ask AI / document analysis), and sees their open cases and referral progress.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (everything scrolls together, `HeroScroll`, bottom padding 130 for the tab bar)
  1. **Dark gradient hero** (`DashHero`, rounded bottom 28, cyan glow top-right).
     - Top row (`HeroTopRow`): menu button (opens the drawer; hidden on docked tablets), caption `Assalomu alaykum` + name (or `Mehmon` for a guest); right: the same round glass buttons as every tab header (`HeaderActions`): `Demo` pill (sample data toggle, green dot when on, label `Demo yoqilgan`), support headset (client/seller only, opens the support sheet), bell (unread dot; guest → `/auth/login`), avatar → `/(tabs)/profile` (guest: person icon → `/auth/login`).
     - `Salom, {name}` (cyan, small; empty name for guest), headline `Bugun qanday yuridik muammoni hal qilamiz?`, lead `Vaziyatingizni oddiy so'zlar bilan yozing — LexGo tushunadi, to'g'ri mutaxassisni topadi va yechimga yo'naltiradi.`
     - Ask field (`DashAsk`): pill input, placeholder `Yuridik muammoyingizni yozing…`, round gradient send button (a11y `LexGo'dan so'rash`). Enter or the button opens `/(tabs)/ai` with `?q=<text>` (empty text just opens the AI tab).
     - Trust chips (`DashTrust`): `Maxfiy` · `Bir necha daqiqada javob` · `Tasdiqlangan mutaxassislar`.
     - Rail of six quick actions (`DashRail`/`RailCard`, horizontal, 164×188 cards, snap, 3D cut-out picture bottom-right, icon chip + bold title + subtitle). Order and copy:
       1. `Muammoni tavsiflash` / `AI yordamida yechim toping` — the only bright gradient card → opens the **WhoSheet** (below).
       2. `Advokatga tezkor bog‘lanish` / `Sun’iy intellekt emas — advokatning o‘zi javob beradi.` — green "live" dot on the icon → `/urgent`.
       3. `Mutaxassis topish` / `Tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar` → `/(tabs)/lawyers`.
       4. `Avtoavariya bo‘yicha huquqiy konsultatsiya olish` (no subtitle) → `/urgent/new?service=traffic_accident_consultation`.
       5. `LexGo Sun'iy intellekt'dan so'rash` / `AI bilan suhbat` → `/(tabs)/ai`.
       6. `Hujjat tahlili` / `Hujjatni yuklang — AI va advokat tekshiradi` → `/doc-analysis`.
  2. **Upsell banner** (`HeaderExtra` → `GiftNudge`, signed-in **client only**, never for guests). Shown once `GET /subscription-plans` and `GET /clients/me/entitlements` have loaded; one variant is picked at random per app session and can be dismissed (×, `Yopish`) for the session. Variants (title / text / CTA → route): `gift`: `Yaqinlaringizga g'amxo'rlik qiling` / `Yuridik xizmatni emas — yuridik xotirjamlikni sovg'a qiling.` / `Sovg'a qilish` → `/profile/gifts`; `pro`: `LexGo Pro bilan cheksiz imkoniyat` / `AI yordamchidan cheklovsiz foydalaning, shablonlarni erkin yuklab oling va ustuvor yordam oling.` / `Pro'ga o'tish` → `/subscription` (hidden when the client's AI plan slug is `lexgo-ai-pro`); `lite`: `LexGo Lite — arzonroq, qulayroq` / `Ko'proq AI so'rovlari va hujjat shablonlarini yuklab olish imkoniyati — Lite tarifda.` / `Lite'ni ko'rish` → `/subscription` (hidden for `lexgo-ai-lite` and `lexgo-ai-pro`); `lawyers`: `Eng yaxshi advokatlarni toping` / `Reytingi yuqori, tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar bilan bevosita bog'laning.` / `Advokatlarni ko'rish` → `/lawyers`.
  3. **Lawyer promo banner** (`DashPromo`, guest and client): appears 2.2 s after the page opens unless it was closed/tapped in the last 3 days (AsyncStorage `lexgo_lawyer_promo_hidden`). Gradient card, lawyers picture bleeding off bottom-right: eyebrow `Yangi`, title `Muammoingizga mos advokat`, text `Tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar — narxi oldindan ma'lum, aloqa maxfiy.`, white pill CTA `Advokatlarni ko'rish` → `/(tabs)/lawyers`; × `Yopish`.
  4. **Signed-in client only — `Faol so'rovlaringiz`** card (`DashRequests`): header with link `Barchasini ko'rish` → `/(tabs)/works`. Rows (`RequestRow`): status dot, bold title (`title`/`case_type`, falls back to the case number), chip with the case number (only when both title and number exist), stage label, `→ next_action` line (when sent), status badge (tone: green for done/closed/complete/resolved, amber for progress/review/wait/pending, blue otherwise), chevron. Tap → `/works/{case_number || id}`. Completed and archived cases are filtered out. Stage/status labels come from `cdash.st` (see the enum list below).
  5. **Signed-in client only — referral progress** (`DashReferral`, rendered only once `GET /referrals/me` has data): gift icon, `Do'stlarni taklif qiling`, line `{joined} ta qo'shildi ({invited} taklif) — yana {remaining} tadan keyin {reward}` where reward is `{pct}% chegirma` (or `{pct}% chegirma faol` when `discount_unlocked`), or `Maqsadga erishdingiz: {reward}` when the goal is reached; progress bar `{joined}/{goal}` (goal = `eligible_after`, default 5). Tap → `/profile/referral`.
  6. **Guest only — sign-in card** (`DashGuest`, replaces 4 and 5): person icon, `Shaxsiy kabinetingizni oching`, `Kiring yoki ro'yxatdan o'ting: so'rovlaringiz, to'lovlaringiz va xabarlaringiz shu yerda bo'ladi.`, gradient button `Kirish` → `/auth/login`, soft button `Ro'yxatdan o'tish` → `/auth/register`, then the specialist invite row `Mutaxassis sifatida qo'shiling` / `Yurist yoki advokat bo'lib mijozlar toping` → `/auth/register?role=advokat`.
  7. **AI card** (`DashAiCard`, dark gradient, both roles): `Yordam kerakmi?`, `Savolingiz bo'lsa, AI yordamchisi bilan suhbatlashing — tez va maxfiy.`, glass button `AI bilan suhbatlashish` → `/(tabs)/ai`.
  - **WhoSheet** (bottom sheet, snaps 80/95 %, opened by rail card 1): title `Kimdan maslahat olasiz?`, lead `Muammoingiz kimga borishini tanlang — advokatgami yoki LexGo sun’iy intellektigami.`; two cards with a picture: **Advocate** — `Professional va tajribali advokat maslahati` / `Advokat video yoki yozishma orqali javob beradi.` / perks `Faqat tasdiqlangan advokatlar`, `Aynan sizning holatingizga javob` / blue CTA `Advokat bilan bog‘lanish` → `/urgent`; **AI** — `LexGo sun’iy intellekti javobi` / `Savolingizga bepul limit doirasida javob beradi.` / perks `Bir zumda javob`, `Murakkab holatda advokatga yo‘naltiradi` / CTA `AI’dan so‘rash` → `/(tabs)/ai`.
- **Actions:** ask field / send → `/(tabs)/ai?q=`; six rail cards (routes above); WhoSheet CTAs → `/urgent` or `/(tabs)/ai`; promo CTA → `/(tabs)/lawyers`, promo × (hides 3 days); upsell CTA/body → variant route, upsell × (session); `Barchasini ko'rish` → `/(tabs)/works`; case row → `/works/{id}`; referral row → `/profile/referral`; guest `Kirish` / `Ro'yxatdan o'tish` / specialist invite; AI card → `/(tabs)/ai`; header: menu (drawer), `Demo` toggle (invalidates every query), support sheet, bell → `/notifications` (guest → `/auth/login`), avatar → `/(tabs)/profile`.
- **States:** requests loading → one 64 pt skeleton; requests empty → `Faol so'rov yo'q` / `Boshlash uchun yuqorida muammoni yozing.`; requests error → nothing special (the card shows the empty state since `open` is empty); referral missing/error → the block is simply absent; promo hidden for 3 days after close/tap; upsell absent until plans+entitlements load, or after dismiss, or when the client already has the plan the variant sells; offline → no dedicated copy on this page (blocks that failed just stay empty). Status/stage enum → Uzbek (`cdash.st`): `new` Yangi, `request` So'rov, `matching` Ijrochi tanlash, `payment` To'lov, `work` Ish jarayoni, `done` Yakun, `pending` Kutilmoqda, `open` Ochiq, `active` Faol, `in_progress` Jarayonda, `started` Ish boshlandi, `accepted` Qabul qilindi, `seller_selection` Buyurtma qabul qilindi, `sent_to_seller` Ijrochiga yuborildi, `waiting_info` Ma'lumot kutilmoqda, `waiting_docs` Hujjatlar kutilmoqda, `waiting_payment`/`pending_payment`/`payment_pending`/`payment_required`/`awaiting_payment` To'lov kutilmoqda, `unpaid` To'lanmagan, `paid` To'langan, `payment_rejected`/`payment_cancelled` To'lov bekor qilingan, `review` Ko'rib chiqilmoqda, `in_review` Tayyorlanmoqda, `pending_review` Tekshiruv kutilmoqda, `lawyer_review` Advokat ko'rib chiqmoqda, `lawyer_review_requested` Advokat tekshiruviga yuborildi, `quality_check` Sifat tekshiruvida, `result_ready` Natija tayyor, `delivered` Topshirildi, `client_confirmation` Tasdiqlashingiz kutilmoqda, `completed` Yakunlangan, `rated` Baholandi, `closed` Yopilgan, `archived` Arxivlangan, `rejected` Rad etilgan, `declined` Rad etildi, `lost` Bekor bo'ldi, `canceled` Bekor qilingan, `cancelled` Bekor qilindi, `expired` Muddati o'tdi, `suspended` To'xtatilgan, `failed` Muvaffaqiyatsiz, `refunded` Qaytarilgan, `processing` Amalga oshirilmoqda, `trial` Sinov muddati, `past_due` To'lov muddati o'tib ketgan, `grace` Imtiyozli muddat, `open_pool` Bo'sh navbatda, `claimed` Olingan, `questionnaire` Ma'lumotlar to'ldirilmoqda, `ready_to_generate` Hujjat yaratishga tayyor, `file_ready` Hujjat tayyor, `scheduled` Rejalashtirilgan, `meeting_active` Uchrashuv ketyapti, `investigation` Tergov, `court` Sud, `appeal` Apellyatsiya. An unknown value is shown as the raw key with underscores replaced by spaces and the first letter capitalised.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                              | Used for                      | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /cases` (signed-in only, stale 30 s)                                             | `Faol so'rovlaringiz` rows    | list under `cases`/`items`/`data`: `id`, `case_number` (or `work_id`), `title`/`case_type`, `stage`, `status`, `next_action`; rows with status `completed`/`archived` are dropped |
| `GET /referrals/me` (signed-in only, stale 60 s)                                      | referral progress block       | `invited_count`, `joined_count`, `discount_unlocked`, `discount_percent`, `eligible_after` (default 5), `remaining_to_unlock`                                                     |
| `GET /subscription-plans` (unauthenticated) + `GET /clients/me/entitlements` (client) | which upsell variant may show | plans: `id`, `slug`; entitlements: `active_subscriptions[].plan_id`                                                                                                               |
| (no call by this page)                                                                | bell badge                    | unread count comes from the app's notification badge store, not fetched here                                                                                                      |

- **UX note:** On a phone the hero (greeting, headline, ask field, rail) fills the first screen; the first rail card is the one the user should see, the other five can scroll off. The promo and the upsell banner both land between the hero and the case list, so a signed-in client can see two banners before their own cases — one of them could go. For a guest the page has four sign-in entry points (bell, avatar, guest card ×2) which is more than needed.

### `/ai` — `LexGo.AI` (AI tab, always dark, tab bar hidden)

- **Who:** everyone. A guest chats under a persistent anonymous client id and is subject to the guest quota (daily/total) echoed by the server; a signed-in client chats under their own id and is subject to the plan's AI limit (402). A professional can open it too (the ✕ sends them to their own first tab instead of Home). No sign-in required.
- **Purpose:** ask the legal AI a question and get a streamed, sourced answer with follow-up questions, under the free/plan limit.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. **Header** (`ChatHeader`, round glass buttons): ✕ (`AI dan chiqish`) → the role's first tab; gradient zap badge + `LexGo.AI`; status line: `● Onlayn` (green) or, once a guest reply carried `limit_status`, `Bugun yana {n} ta bepul so'rov`; clock button `Suhbatlar tarixi` → `/chats`; pencil button `Yangi suhbat` (resets the open chat, haptic, toast `Yangi suhbat boshlandi`).
  2. **Message list** (`MessageList`, FlatList) — **empty state** (`AiWelcome`): animated orb (190 pt), `Salom, {name}` (signed-in with a name only), `Qanday yordam bera olaman?`, `Muammoingizni yozing yoki quyidagi mavzulardan birini tanlang.`, **suggestions grid** (`PromptGrid`, 2 columns, 6 cards, a tap sends the text): `Tergovga chaqirishdi`, `Shartnoma buzildi, tovar kelmadi`, `Ish haqim to'lanmayapti`, `Meros bo'linmayapti`, `MChJ ochmoqchiman`, `Obuna qancha turadi?`; footnote `AI xato qilishi mumkin · yakuniy huquqiy qaror advokatda qoladi`.
     - **User bubble** (right, brand gradient): text, time; 70 % opacity while pending; red outline + `Qayta yuborish` link when the send failed (tap = resend the same bubble).
     - **Assistant bubble** (left, glass): markdown (headings, numbered/bulleted lists, quotes, hr). Legal-agent shapes rendered specially: a `Qisqa javob`/`Xulosa` lead block with an accent left border; italic lines as a grey note; a line starting `Manba…` as a shield-icon footer; a bold `Aniqroq javob uchun…` title followed by list items becomes **follow-up chips** (arrow chips; tappable only on the latest answer and only when nothing is sending, otherwise dimmed). Under the markdown (`AgentFoot`): `Qayta urinish` (glass, latest answer only) when the answer text says lex.uz is not responding / a technical error; `Hujjat so'rovi yaratish` → `/services` when the answer is a "hujjat so'rovi". Then **sources** (`Manbalar`: up to 3 chips, link chips open the URL (https only), others and the `+{n} ta` chip open the **sources sheet** `Manbalar` / `{n} ta manba` with title, snippet and `Manbani ochish`), then **contract cards** (file icon, `contract_type`, `status · Hujjatni ochish`; tap opens the in-app file preview of `GET /contracts/{id}/file`), then the time.
     - **Actions row** under a finished (non-streaming, server-id) assistant answer (`MessageActions`): `Nusxalash` (toast `Nusxalandi`), `Ulashish` (system share of plain text), `Foydali javob` (sends at once), `Foydali emas` (opens the **feedback sheet**: `Javob foydali bo'lmadimi?` / `Fikringiz javoblarni yaxshilashga yordam beradi`, field `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Nima noto'g'ri yoki yetishmadi?` max 500, `Yuborish`). After a vote both thumbs lock; success toast `Fikringiz uchun rahmat`.
     - **Footer slot:** while sending → typing dots `Javob tayyorlanmoqda…`, after 15 s `Murakkab savol: javob bir daqiqagacha cho'zilishi mumkin`. When the server refused the last question → **LimitCard** (below). Otherwise, for a guest with a known quota, after the last answer → **LimitHint**: ℹ `Qoldi: bugun {today} ta, jami {total} ta bepul savol` and, when `register_offer`, the link `Ro'yxatdan o'ting: +{n} ta bepul savol` (or `Ro'yxatdan o'ting`) → `/auth/register`.
  3. **PII note** above the composer: `Maxfiylik: telefon raqamlari, email manzillar, JShShIR (PINFL) va pasport raqamlari Sun'iy Intellektga yuborilishidan oldin yashiriladi.`
  4. **Composer** (`Composer`, docked edge to edge, keyboard-aware): multiline field placeholder `Savolingizni yozing…` (max 2000), round send button; disabled until a client id exists and whenever a non-rate limit error is shown.
  - **`?q=` param:** a question typed on the Home hero arrives as `?q=`; once the client id is ready it is sent automatically (once) and the param is cleared.
  - **Send flow:** optimistic user bubble → the first message creates the chat (`POST /clients/{id}/chats` with the first 60 chars as title) → `POST …/messages`. Tokens may also stream over the socket (batched every 80 ms into one assistant bubble); the final `message.created` replaces it. On a network cut with an open chat the app first polls the chat (3, 5, 8, 12, 18, 25, 35, 45 s) and only then marks the bubble failed. The conversation is kept in memory across tab switches and wiped on logout / user change.
  - **Socket:** one `ReconnectingSocket` per open chat (`ws …/ws/clients/{clientId}/chats/{chatId}?token=`), ping 25 s, closed while the app is in background; every reconnect/foreground re-reads the chat (`GET …/chats/{chatId}`) and the server order wins. It is hosted globally (`ChatSocketHost`), so when an answer lands while the user is on another tab a toast says `AI javob berdi — AI bo'limida o'qing`.
- **Actions:** ✕ → first tab; history → `/chats`; new chat; suggestion card / follow-up chip → sends; `Qayta yuborish` on a failed bubble; `Qayta urinish` (re-asks the previous question); `Hujjat so'rovi yaratish` → `/services`; source chip → URL or sources sheet; contract card → file preview; copy / share / 👍 / 👎 (+ sheet); LimitCard buttons: `Tarifni ko'rish` → `/subscription` (signed-in), `Hisob yaratish` → `/auth/register` and `Kirish` → `/auth/login` (guest); LimitHint link → `/auth/register`; send.
- **States:** empty → welcome (above); sending → typing dots (+ long-wait line after 15 s); failed send → red bubble + `Qayta yuborish` and an error toast: network `Internet aloqasi yo'q yoki uzildi. Qayta urining.`, 503 `AI vaqtincha ishlamayapti. Birozdan so'ng urinib ko'ring.`, other 5xx `Serverda xatolik. Birozdan so'ng urinib ko'ring.`, otherwise the server `detail` or `Xabarni yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; plain 429 (rate) → toast with the server detail or `Juda ko'p urinish. Bir ozdan so'ng qayta urining.` (composer stays enabled). **LimitCard** (dark gradient, composer disabled): guest daily (`429 guest_daily_limit_exceeded`) → `Bugungi bepul savollar tugadi` + `Ro'yxatdan o'ting va yana {n} ta bepul savol oling yoki limit yangilanishini kuting.` (or `Ro'yxatdan o'ting yoki limit yangilanishini kuting.` when `registered_free_limit` is 0) + `Ishlatildi: {used} / {cap}` + `Kunlik limit {when} da yangilanadi`; guest total (`guest_total_limit_exceeded`) → `Mehmon uchun bepul savollar tugadi` + `Davom etish uchun ro'yxatdan o'ting — sizni yana {n} ta bepul savol kutmoqda.` (or `Davom etish uchun ro'yxatdan o'ting.`); **402 plan gate** → title by code: `feature_limit_exhausted` `Limit tugadi`, `active_subscription_required` `Obuna kerak`, `feature_not_in_plan` `Tarifingizda yo'q`, `ai_subscription_required` `AI limiti tugadi`, unknown `Tarif kerak`; text e.g. `«{feature}» limiti shu davr uchun tugadi. Yangilanishini kuting yoki kattaroq tarif tanlang.` / `Umumiy AI limitingiz tugadi. Davom etish uchun tarifni yangilang.` / `Faol obunangiz yo'q. Davom etish uchun tarif tanlang.` / `Bu imkoniyat joriy tarifingizga kirmaydi.` / `Bu imkoniyat uchun mos tarif kerak.` (the server's own sentence for an unknown 402); `Ishlatildi: {used} / {cap}` when counted; `Yangilanadi: {date}`; button `Tarifni ko'rish` → `/subscription`. Offline: the socket reconnects by itself; sends fail with the network toast.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                           | Used for                                                                            | Key fields                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | -------- | ----- | ---- | ------------------------ |
| `POST /clients/{clientId}/chats`                   | create the chat on the first message                                                | req `{title}` (first 60 chars); resp `id`/`chat_id`, `title`, `updated_at`                                  |
| `POST /clients/{clientId}/chats/{chatId}/messages` | send a question                                                                     | req `{content}`; resp `user_message`, `assistant_message` (`id`, `role`, `content`/`text`, `sources[]{title | name                   | document  | source,url,snippet}`, `contracts[]{id,contract_type,status,download_url | inline_url                                                                                                                                          | url,file_name,mime_type}`, `created_at`), top-level `sources`, `contracts`, `limit_status` (`guest_daily_limit`, `guest_total_limit`, `used_today`, `used_total`, `remaining_today`, `remaining_total`, `register_offer`, `registered_free_limit`, `daily_window_resets_at`); errors: 429 `detail.code` `guest_daily_limit_exceeded`/`guest_total_limit_exceeded`with the same limit fields +`message`; 402 `detail.code` (`feature_limit_exhausted`, `active_subscription_required`, `feature_not_in_plan`, `ai_subscription_required`, …), `feature_key`, `limit`/`monthly_limit`, `used`, `remaining`, `unit`, `resets_at` |
| `GET /clients/{clientId}/chats/{chatId}`           | open a past chat, recover after a network cut, reconcile after reconnect/foreground | `messages[]`/`items`/`data` (same message shape), top-level `contracts` attached to the last answer         |
| `POST /ai/feedback`                                | 👍 / 👎 on an answer                                                                | `{useful, comment, request_id: <message id>}`                                                               |
| `GET /contracts/{id}/file` (authed, in-app viewer) | open a generated contract                                                           | binary                                                                                                      |
| `GET /subscription-plans`                          | feature title inside the 402 card (`useGateText`)                                   | `functions[].key/title`                                                                                     |
| WS `/ws/clients/{clientId}/chats/{chatId}?token=`  | streamed answers                                                                    | final frames `event                                                                                         | type`=`message.created | completed | final                                                                   | updated`with`message{…}`, `contracts`, `limit_status`; fragment frames (name containing delta/chunk/fragment/stream/partial/token) with `message_id | id`+`delta                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | chunk | fragment | token | text | content`, optional `seq` |

- **UX note:** The welcome screen must show the six suggestions and the composer on one phone screen; the orb can shrink. Three different limit treatments (status line, hint under the answer, limit card) plus the PII line and the footnote compete for the same small space — the PII note and footnote could be merged or moved to a help sheet.

### `/chats` — `Suhbatlar tarixi`

- **Who:** everyone (no gate; a guest sees the chats of their anonymous id). Reached from the AI header's clock button.
- **Purpose:** find and reopen a previous AI conversation, or start a new one.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Standard `Header` with back and title `Suhbatlar tarixi`; right icon (pencil) `Yangi suhbat` → resets the chat (toast `Yangi suhbat boshlandi`) and goes to `/ai`.
  2. Search box placeholder `Suhbatlardan qidirish` (on-device filter of title + last message, debounced 150 ms; the API has no query param).
  3. Section list grouped by day, newest activity first: kicker headers `Bugun`, `Kecha`, `Shu hafta`, `Avvalroq`. Row (`ChatRow`): message icon, title (or `Yangi suhbat` when untitled), 2-line plain-text preview of the last message, time (today) or date; the currently open chat has an accent outline. Pull to refresh.
- **Actions:** row tap → `GET /clients/{id}/chats/{chatId}` then `/ai` with that chat open (a failed load still navigates); new chat; search; pull to refresh; `Qayta urinish` on error.
- **States:** loading → 6 skeleton rows; empty → `Hali suhbatlar yo'q` / `Birinchi savolingizni bering — suhbat shu yerda saqlanadi.`; search without a hit → `Hech narsa topilmadi`; error → `ErrorView` (`Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.` offline, `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` otherwise) with `Qayta urinish`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                 | Used for                                | Key fields                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /clients/{clientId}/chats`          | the list (sorted by activity on device) | list under `chats`/`items`/`data`: `id`/`chat_id`, `title`, `last_message` (string or `{content, created_at}`), `updated_at`/`created_at` |
| `GET /clients/{clientId}/chats/{chatId}` | reopen a chat                           | `messages[]` (see `/ai`)                                                                                                                  |

- **UX note:** Search first, then today's chats — fine on a phone. The page is light; the only confusion is that "new chat" lives in two places (here and the AI header).

### `/intake` — `Sun'iy intellekt muammo tahlili`

- **Who:** signed-in client only (`RoleGate roles=["client"]`): a guest is redirected to `/auth/login`; a professional sees `Ruxsat yo'q` / `Bu bo'lim sizning rolingiz yoki ruxsatlaringiz uchun ochiq emas.` with `Orqaga`. Reached from in-app links (not from the Home rail, which sends "describe" to `/urgent` or `/ai`).
- **Purpose:** describe the problem once; the AI classifies it (category, executor, urgency), creates a lead for the call centre, proposes up to three priced service levels and the best-matched advocates.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `Header` with back, title `Sun'iy intellekt muammo tahlili`; subtitle `Muammoingizni yozing — Sun'iy intellekt turini aniqlaydi va mos yo'nalishni ko'rsatadi.`
  2. **Composer sheet** (`IntakeForm`): small orb + label `Muammoingiz`; borderless multiline field, placeholder `Masalan: ish beruvchi ikki oydan beri maoshni to'lamayapti…`, max 4000, disabled while analysing; outline turns accent on focus, red on error; footer: live counter `{n}/4000` with a green tick once ≥ 8 chars, gradient pill `Tahlil qilish` (→ `Tahlil qilinmoqda…` with spinner). Under it the hint `Sun'iy intellekt tahlili keyin navbatchi mutaxassisga uzatiladi.` and the **business-hours chip** (`BusinessHoursBadge`): dot + `Hozir ish vaqti` / `Hozir ish vaqti emas` / `Bugun dam olish kuni`, schedule `{days}, {start}–{end} (Toshkent vaqti)`, and while closed the note `Navbatchi advokat ish vaqtida siz bilan bog'lanadi.`
  3. **Before the first analysis:** `Oldingi mos advokatlar` (`MatchPicks` from the earlier `GET /matching/me`, skeleton while loading) — best pick as the gradient hero card, the rest as a monogram stack with `Yana {n} ta mos mutaxassis` → `/matches`; then a card `Rasmiy huquqiy manbalar` / `Sun'iy intellekt javoblari shu rasmiy manbalarga tayanadi.` listing `GET /ai/legal-corpus` rows (title, `Rasmiy` pill when `trust_level = official`, external-link arrow when `url`); skeleton while loading.
  4. **After analysis (result card):**
     - **Urgent block first** (only when `urgency` ∈ urgent/high/critical): red-bordered alert `Bu masala shoshilinch` / `Kutmang — hozir navbatchi advokat bilan bog'laning.`, red button `Hozir advokat bilan bog'lanish` → `/sos`, outline button `+998 78 777 00 00` → phone call.
     - `Tahlil natijasi` + urgency pill (`Juda shoshilinch` red when urgent, otherwise the map: `low` Past, `normal` O'rtacha, `high` Yuqori, `critical` Juda shoshilinch, `urgent` Shoshilinch); row `Yo'nalish` → category (`criminal` Jinoyat ishlari, `administrative` Ma'muriy ishlar, `family` Oila huquqi, `contract` Shartnomalar, `court` Sud ishlari, `general` Umumiy yuridik masala; unknown keys shown raw); row `Ijrochi` when `executor_type` (`advokat` Advokat, `yurist` Yurist, `yurist_advokat` Yurist yoki advokat); the AI `summary` unless it merely echoes the input.
     - **Offers** `Mos xizmatlar` `3 darajada` when `offer_levels` exist — one `OfferCard` per level: pill `Asosiy`/`Standart`/`Premium`, title, price `{n} so'm` or `Kelishilgan narx`, facts `{n} kun`, `{n} ta hujjat`, `Faqat advokat` (when `advokat_required`), buttons `Xizmat pasporti`/`Pasportni yopish` (loads `GET /services/{id}/passport` inline) and `Buyurtma berish` → `/services/{service_id}`. Otherwise, when `recommended_service` is set, one gradient button `Bu xizmatni buyurtma qilish` → `/services?q=<category label>`.
     - **Matches** `Sizga mos advokatlar` (`MatchPicks`, skeleton while `GET /matching/me` reloads after the classify).
     - **Sources** `Manbalar` (same row style, `Rasmiy` pill).
     - **AnswerFooter** (eases in): amber warning `Bu savol bo'yicha modda va sanasi aniq ko'rsatilgan manba topilmadi. Javob faqat dastlabki yo'nalish — qaror qabul qilishdan oldin yurist yoki advokat bilan maslahatlashing.` when `answer_status = reliable_source_required`; grey disclaimer = server `disclaimer` or `Bu javob yuridik axborot va dastlabki yo'nalishdir. Yakuniy qaror uchun yurist yoki advokat bilan maslahatlashing.`; **feedback panel** `Javob foydali bo'ldimi?` with tiles `Foydali` / `Foydasiz` (spinner while sending) → `Rahmat! Fikringiz qabul qilindi.` or `Rahmat! Javob yurist-moderatorga tekshiruvga yuborildi.`; red row `Shoshilinch bo'lsa — SOS` → `/sos` (hidden when urgent, since the urgent block is already there).
- **Actions:** `Tahlil qilish` → `POST /ai/classify` then `GET /matching/me`; hero match card → `/specialist/{user_id}`; `Yana {n} ta…` → `/matches`; source rows → external URL; `Xizmat pasporti` toggle; `Buyurtma berish` → `/services/{id}`; `Bu xizmatni buyurtma qilish` → `/services?q=`; urgent CTA / SOS row → `/sos`; hotline → tel; `Foydali`/`Foydasiz` → `POST /ai/feedback`.
- **States:** validation: `Bu maydonni to'ldiring`, `Kamida 8 ta belgi kiriting`, `Eng ko'pi bilan 4000 ta belgi`; analysing → button `Tahlil qilinmoqda…`, field locked; classify failed → card `Tahlil qilinmadi` / `Iltimos, keyinroq urining yoki to'g'ridan-to'g'ri mutaxassisga murojaat qiling.` + button `Shoshilinch bo'lsa — SOS` → `/sos`; matches loading → skeleton, matches failed → simply no matches block; feedback failed → toast with server detail or `Amalni bajarib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` (can retry); corpus/previous-matches loading → skeletons, failures → blocks absent; business hours fall back to default hours.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                  | Used for                                                               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `POST /ai/classify`                       | the analysis (creates a lead)                                          | req `{text}`; resp `category`, `executor_type`, `urgency` (default normal), `summary`, `recommended_service`/`service`, `lead_id`, `offer_levels[]{level, service_id, title, base_price, passport{standard_price, standard_duration, required_documents[], advokat_required}}`, `sources[]{key,title | name,url,trust_level}`, `disclaimer`, `answer_status` |
| `GET /matching/me`                        | previous picks before analysis; matched advocates after it (refetched) | list under `items`/`data`/`matches`: `id`/`lawyer_user_id`, `lawyer_user_id`/`user_id`, `name`/`lawyer_name`, `area`/`specialization`, `region`, `rating`, `rating_status`, `reviews_count`, `seller_type`, `match_pct`/`score`, `reason`                                                            |
| `GET /ai/legal-corpus` (stale 1 h)        | `Rasmiy huquqiy manbalar` before the first analysis                    | list under `items`/`data`/`sources`: `key`, `title`/`name`, `url`, `trust_level`                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /services/{id}/passport` (on demand) | passport facts inside an offer                                         | `service{…}`, `passport{…}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /calendar/business-hours`            | the hours chip                                                         | `schedule{days[], start, end}`, `current`, `holidays[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `POST /ai/feedback`                       | `Foydali` / `Foydasiz`                                                 | `{useful, comment: "", request_id: lead_id}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

- **UX note:** The composer must be the first thing on the phone; the corpus list below it is reference material and can collapse. After analysis the result is a very long single card (urgent block, head, up to 3 offers, matches, sources, two notices, feedback, SOS) — group it into tabs or collapsible sections so the first screen shows urgency + the first offer. The "describe" entry points on Home do not lead here, which makes this screen hard to discover.

### `/matches` — `Siz uchun mos advokat va yuristlar`

- **Who:** signed-in client only (`RoleGate`: guest → `/auth/login`; professional → `Ruxsat yo'q` state).
- **Purpose:** see the full ranking of specialists the platform matched to the client's last problem and open a profile.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `Header` with back, title `Siz uchun mos advokat va yuristlar`.
  2. Lead line: `{n} ta mutaxassis topildi` when there are matches, else `Sun'iy intellekt so'rovingizga mos mutaxassislarni tanladi.`
  3. **Best pick hero** (`BestMatchCard`, blue gradient): tag `Eng mos` + big `{pct}%`, white match bar, monogram with rating ring (ring only when rated), name, `area · region`, `★ {rating}` or `Hali baholanmagan`, the AI `reason` (3 lines), white button `Profilni ochish` → `/specialist/{user_id || id}`.
  4. `Boshqa mos variantlar` + ranked rows (`RankedMatchRow`, divider between): rank number from 2, monogram, name, `area · region`, `★ rating` or `Hali baholanmagan`; right: `{pct}%`, thin bar, `mos`. Tap → `/specialist/{id}`.
  - Pull to refresh.
- **Actions:** hero button / row → specialist profile; pull to refresh; empty-state button `Muammoni tasvirlash` → `/ai` (note: the AI chat, not `/intake`); `Qayta urinish` on error.
- **States:** loading → one 260 pt skeleton + three 64 pt rows; empty → `Hali tavsiya yo'q` / `Muammoingizni tasvirlang — biz mos advokat va yuristlarni topamiz.` + `Muammoni tasvirlash`; error → `ErrorView` (offline `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`, 403 `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.`, 404 `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, else `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`) with `Qayta urinish`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                         | Used for    | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /matching/me` (stale 5 min) | the ranking | see `/intake`: `lawyer_user_id`/`user_id`, `name`, `area`, `region`, `rating`, `rating_status`, `reviews_count`, `seller_type`, `match_pct`/`score`, `reason`; rows without a name or id are dropped; pct capped at 100 |

- **UX note:** The hero card plus the first two rows fit one phone screen, which is right. The empty state sends the client to the AI chat rather than to the intake that actually produces matches — this is the confusing part.

### `/sos` — `Shoshilinch yuridik yordam`

- **Who:** signed-in client only (`RoleGate`: guest → `/auth/login`; professional → `Ruxsat yo'q`; advocates have `/pro/urgent` instead).
- **Purpose:** in an emergency, reach the duty advocate with one tap (or call the hotline), with a category and an optional one-line description.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `Header` with back, title `Shoshilinch yuridik yordam`.
  2. **Hero** (`SosHero`, dark-red vertical gradient): animated red radar beacon, `Shoshilinch yuridik yordam`, `Bir tugma — va navbatchi advokat siz bilan bog'lanadi.`
  3. **Hotline strip** (`SosHotline`, glass row, pulsing red phone icon): `Hoziroq qo'ng'iroq qiling` / `+998 78 777 00 00`, chevron; tap → phone dialler.
  4. **Idle stage — form** (`SosForm`): `Vaziyatni tanlang`; `Vaziyat turi`; **category grid** (2 columns, 6 tiles, selected tile accent-filled): `arrest` `Hibsga olindi`, `police` `Politsiya chaqirdi`, `court` `Sud jarayoni`, `search` `Tintuv`, `contract` `Shoshilinch shartnoma`, `other` `Boshqa`; field `Qisqacha nima bo'ldi?` placeholder `Vaziyatni bir-ikki jumlada yozing…` (optional, max 1000); big pulsing red button `Navbatchi advokatga ulanish`; caption `Tugmani bosganingizda navbatchi advokat darhol xabardor qilinadi.`
  5. **Idle stage — info** (`SosInfo`): `Qanday ishlaydi` with a vertical timeline of three red-numbered steps: `Vaziyat turini tanlang va qisqacha yozing.`, `Navbatchi advokatga so'rov darhol yuboriladi.`, `Advokat maxfiy chat yoki qo'ng'iroq orqali bog'lanadi.`; then `Nega LexGo SOS?` with three tiles `24/7 navbatchi`, `Maxfiy`, `Tezkor javob`.
  6. **Status panel** (`SosStatus`, replaces 4–5 after the button): see States.
- **Actions:** hotline strip → tel `+998 78 777 00 00`; category tile → select (no category picked = `other`); `Navbatchi advokatga ulanish` → `POST /sos`; connected: `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/{chat_room_id}`; sent: `Hoziroq qo'ng'iroq qiling  +998 78 777 00 00` → tel, `Qaytadan yuborish` → back to the form.
- **States:** validation only `Eng ko'pi bilan 1000 ta belgi` (an emergency is never blocked by the form); **connecting** → card with large spinner, `Ulanmoqda…` / `Navbatchi advokat qidirilmoqda`; **connected** (response has `chat_room_id`/`room_id`) → green shield, `Advokat topildi`, avatar + duty name (or `Navbatchi advokat`) / `Navbatchi yuridik yordam`, button `Chatni ochish`; **sent** (no room, or the POST failed — fail-soft, no error is ever shown) → green shield, `So'rovingiz qabul qilindi` / `Navbatchi advokat tez orada siz bilan bog'lanadi.`, outline hotline button, soft `Qaytadan yuborish`. Offline: same as failure → "sent" with the hotline button.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint    | Used for              | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------- | --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `POST /sos` | the emergency request | req `{category, description}` (category one of arrest/police/court/search/contract/other); resp `id`, `status` (default pending), `category`, `duty_lawyer{name}`/`assigned_to{name}`/`duty_name`, `chat_room_id`/`room_id`, `created_at` |

- **UX note:** Hero + hotline + the red button must all be on the first screen; today the six category tiles and the description push the button below the fold on small phones — the info section is rightly last and could be dropped. Showing "accepted" even when the request failed is deliberate but means the client cannot tell the difference; the hotline button covers that.

### `/services` — `Hujjatlar`

- **Who:** guest and client (the `services` tab is in both tab configs — `src/widgets/tab-bar/tabConfig.ts`). Browsing is free for guests; the page itself never asks to sign in. Sign-in is demanded only by actions reached from here: on `/templates/[id]` every action row sends a guest to `/auth/login`; the "new document" sheet pushes its destination pages without checking sign-in itself. No plan or entitlement gate on this page (the `template_download` plan gate sheet is mounted but nothing on this tab can open it — see Sheets).
- **Purpose:** find a ready legal document by name or branch and open its page; or (when an advocate was picked in the directory) pick one of that advocate's services and order it from them.
- **Two modes of the same route** (`src/pages/services/ServicesPage.tsx`): (A) the default — the ready-documents catalogue (`DocCatalog`); (B) `?lawyer=<userId>&name=<name>` — that advocate's service list (the old services hub `AdvocateServices`), until the client taps `Boshqa advokat`.

**Layout, top → bottom — mode A (catalogue, `src/widgets/doc-catalog/DocCatalog.tsx`):**

1. Tab header (`TabPage`): the tab actions row, then the big title `Hujjatlar` (no subtitle). The header scrolls away with the list (it is the FlatList header).
2. **Search row** (`CatalogSearch`, pinned — `stickyHeaderIndices=[1]`): pill field with search icon, placeholder `Hujjat nomi bo'yicha qidirish…`, an `×` clear button (a11y `Qidiruvni tozalash`) while text is typed; to its right a 52 px icon button `Filtrlar` (sliders icon) with a dot badge while any filter is on. Under it, only when a language filter is set: one removable chip with the language name (`O'zbekcha (kirill)` etc.; the category is not chipped — it shows as the selected chip in the row below). Under that a live caption: `{n} ta hujjat` (no search, no language) or `{n} ta natija ({total} tadan)` (search text or language on); nothing while loading/error. Search is debounced 300 ms, max 200 chars, matched on the phone over name + description + category, every word must match, Cyrillic/Latin folded (`shartnoma` finds `Шартнома`).
3. **Category chips row** (`CatalogChips`, horizontal, scrolls away): `Hammasi · {total}` first, then the four known branches in fixed order, each `{name} · {count}` with its colour: `Fuqarolik` (blue/accent), `Iqtisodiy` (teal/ok), `Ma'muriy` (amber/warn), `Jinoiy` (red/err); any other server category after them (server `title`, else humanised key), grey. Tap selects; tapping the active chip clears it. Counts are the server's `templates_count`.
4. **Two quiet pills** (`CatalogPills`, same scrolling row): `AI ga yozish` (zap icon, accent tint) → `/intake`; `Tezkor yordam` (alert-triangle, red tint) → `/sos`.
5. **Document rows** (`CatalogRow`, 10 at a time, +10 on end-reached or button): glass card, min 76 px: a drawn paper tile (`PaperTile`) in the category colour — folded corner, 4 text lines, a small paperclip badge when `has_source_file` (DOCX exists); title (2 lines, semibold); meta line: colour dot + category name, clock + `≈ {n} daqiqa` (only when the template has fields: `max(2, round(fields × 0.3))`, rounded to 5 above 10), price `{price} so'm` (accent, bold) or `Bepul` (grey); chevron. Tap → `/templates/{id}`.
6. **Footer** (`foot`): the state block (see States), then caption `{n} / {total}` (shown rows / matched rows), button `Yana {n} ta` (soft, small) while more rows exist, a spinner line `Boshqa hujjatlar yuklanmoqda…` while other branches are still downloading, then the dashed **new-document row** `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` (plus badge, arrow; `NewDocOrder`) that opens the new-document sheet.
7. **Filter sheet** (`CatalogFilterSheet`, title `Filtrlar`, snaps 0.7 / 0.92): caption `Yo'nalish` + radio list `Barcha yo'nalishlar`, then each category (label + count, radio check); caption `Til` + radio list only when more than one language exists: `Barcha tillar`, `O'zbekcha (kirill)` (`uz-cyrl`), `O'zbekcha (lotin)` (`uz-latn`), `Ruscha` (`ru`), `Inglizcha` (`en`), unknown codes shown upper-case. Pinned bar: icon button `Tozalash` (resets category + language) and gradient button `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · {n}` (closes). Picking in the sheet applies immediately (same state as the chips).

**Layout — mode B (`?lawyer=`, `AdvocateServices`):**

1. Tab header: title `Xizmat buyurtma qilish`, subtitle `LexGo katalogini ko'ring, mutaxassis tanlang va boshlang.`; pull-to-refresh on the scroll view.
2. **Pre-seller banner** (`PreSellerBanner`, tinted card with a blue left bar): avatar, `Tanlangan advokat: {name}` (name empty → `advokat`), caption: while the advocate's list loads `Endi xizmatni tanlang — buyurtma shu advokatga yuboriladi.`; when they listed services `Faqat shu advokat ko'rsatadigan xizmatlar ({n} ta) ko'rsatilmoqda — buyurtma shu advokatga yuboriladi.`; when their list is empty `Bu advokat hali xizmatlar ro'yxatini belgilamagan — butun katalog ko'rsatilmoqda.`; outside business hours ` Hozir ish vaqti emas. Mutaxassisning javob muddati ish vaqti boshlanganda hisoblanadi.` is appended. `×` button (a11y `Boshqa advokat`) drops the advocate and shows mode A.
3. **Services hub** (`src/widgets/services-hub/ServicesHub.tsx`): search row — `SearchBox` placeholder `Xizmatlarni qidirish` + icon button `Filtrlar` (dot badge when filters active); applied filters as removable chips underneath (category name, `Tayyor hujjat bor`/`Advokat tayyorlaydi`, executor, price band, sort — each with `×`). Then caption `{n} ta xizmat` and a two-column grid of **ServiceCard**s (12 at a time; button `Yana ko'rsatish · {left}`): gradient art area in the direction's tone with the subcategory illustration or an icon, badge `Hujjat` (service has `document_template_id`) or `Xizmat`, a shield badge when `advokat_required`; name (3 lines), category title, price (`{n} so'm` / `Bepul` when `pricing_tier=free` / `Narx kelishiladi`), arrow. Tap → `/services/{id}?lawyer={id}&name={name}`. Search ≥ 2 chars switches the source to the server search.
4. **Hub filter sheet** (`ServicesFilterSheet`, title `Filtrlar`, snaps 0.82 / 0.95): `Yo'nalish` as icon cards (`Hammasi` + each category with a live count; a category with 0 is hidden unless selected); `Hujjat` chips `Barchasi · n`, `Tayyor hujjat bor · n`, `Advokat tayyorlaydi · n`; `Kim bajaradi` chips `Barchasi`, `AI tayyorlaydi`, `Yurist`, `Advokat`; `Narx` chips `Barchasi`, `Bepul`, `300 ming so'mgacha`, `300 ming – 1 mln so'm`, `1 mln so'mdan yuqori`; `Tartib` radio `Tavsiya bo'yicha`, `Nomi bo'yicha (A–Z)`, `Arzonlari birinchi`, `Tayyor hujjatlilar birinchi`. Pinned bar: icon `Qayta o'rnatish` + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · {n}`.
5. **`Boshqa yo'llar`** (`MoreWays`, rises in after the list): two gradient cards side by side (`CatalogActions`): `Muammongizni yozing` / `AI eng mos xizmat va advokatni tanlab beradi` → `/intake`; `Shoshilinch advokat` / `Zudlik bilan yuridik yordam` (red) → `/sos`; then the dashed `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` row.

**Sheets** (`src/pages/services/CatalogSheets.tsx`, mounted in both modes and on `/services/direction`):

- **New-document sheet** (`NewDocSheet`, title `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma`, snaps 0.62 / 0.9): lead `Katalogdagi hujjatlardan mosini topmadingizmi? Navbatchi advokat siz uchun tayyorlab beradi.`, then up to three cards (round icon, title, hint, chevron): `0 dan hujjat tayyorlash` / `Vaziyatingizni yozing — navbatchi advokat hujjatni boshidan yozib beradi.` → `/documents/new?kind=custom` (mode `lawyer`); `Hujjatimni tekshirtirish` / `O'zingizdagi hujjatni yuklang — navbatchi advokat tekshirib, to'g'irlab beradi.` → `/documents/new?kind=review` (mode `review`); `Sun'iy intellekt tahlili` / `Hujjat matnini joylang — xatar va tavsiyalarni shu zahoti ko'ring.` → `/doc-analysis` (mode `analysis`). A mode the platform switched off (`document_mode_disabled`, or missing from `settings.modes`) is not listed; a mode closed for this account keeps the card with a lock icon and the reason as its hint: `Buning uchun faol obuna kerak.` / `Joriy tarifingizga bu xizmat kirmaydi.` / `Tarifingizda bu imkoniyat yo'q.` (or the backend's own `message`, else `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`). The card still navigates when tapped.
- **Service order sheet** (`OrderSheet`, title = service name, fallback `Xizmat buyurtmasi`, snaps 0.8 / 0.94): the `OrderPanel` — advocate, price, payment — documented in full under `/services/[id]`. On this tab it opens only from the `?service=` deep link for a service without a template (mode A) — the hub cards navigate to the service page instead. `?package=` rides along as `packageId`; in mode B the picked advocate rides along as `preSeller`.
- **Template-download plan gate** (`PlanGateSheet`, from `useTemplateDownload`): documented under `/services/[id]`. On this tab nothing triggers a download, so it never opens here; it is live on `/services/direction` and the service page.

**Deep links:**

- `?service=<id>` (`useDeepService.ts`, from the AI offer cards): GET `/services/{id}/passport`; a service with `document_template_id` → `router.replace("/services/{id}/fill")`; any other → the order sheet opens with it.
- `?lawyer=<userId>&name=<name>` → mode B; also forwarded to `/services/[id]` on card tap.
- `?package=<id>` → passed to the order sheet (`package_id` in POST `/orders`).
- `?cat`, `?subcat`, `?q` are declared in the page's params type but not used by this page any more (they belong to `/services/direction`).

**Actions:** search / clear; `Filtrlar` → filter sheet; category chip → filter; `AI ga yozish` → `/intake`; `Tezkor yordam` → `/sos`; row → `/templates/{id}`; `Yana {n} ta` / scroll end → +10 rows; pull-to-refresh → refetch every branch + categories; `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` → new-document sheet; (mode B) `Boshqa advokat` → clear advocate; service card → `/services/{id}?lawyer&name`; hub filters/sort; `Yana ko'rsatish · n`; the two `Boshqa yo'llar` cards. Remote-assistance ids: `documents.search` (`Hujjat qidirish`), `documents.filters` (`Kategoriya va til`), `documents.new_order` (`Yangi hujjat buyurtmasi`). Analytics: `document_catalog_opened`, `document_template_searched` (category/language only).

**States (mode A):** loading → six 76 px skeleton rows (no chips/pills rows removed; search stays). Offline → EmptyState (wifi-off) `Internetga ulanib bo'lmadi` / `Aloqa tiklangach ro'yxat yuklanadi.` + `Qayta urinish`. Other error → `ErrorView` (title from the error: `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`, `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.`, `Juda ko'p urinish…`, else `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`) + `Qayta urinish`. No search match → EmptyState (search) `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `«{q}» bo'yicha natija yo'q. So'rovni o'zgartiring.` + `Qidiruvni tozalash`. Empty category/language → EmptyState (file-text) `Bu yo'nalishda hujjat yo'q` + `Yo'nalishni tozalash` (resets both filters). Partial load: small branches appear first, `Boshqa hujjatlar yuklanmoqda…` with a spinner under the list while the big ones (civil, economic) download. The shown window resets to 10 whenever category, language or search changes.
**States (mode B):** loading → three `ServiceCardSkeleton`s; error with no rows → `ErrorView` + `Qayta urinish`; no match → EmptyState `Xizmat topilmadi` / `Boshqa yo'nalishni tanlang yoki qidiruvni o'zgartiring.`.

**Data:**
| Endpoint | Used for | Key fields |
|---|---|---|
| GET `/document-template-categories` | chips, filter sheet, counts, languages (mode A) | `categories[]`/`items[]`/`data[]`: `key`, `title`, `templates_count`, `languages[]` |
| GET `/document-templates?category=<key>` (one request per category, in parallel; whole answer, no paging; parsed on a worker runtime; 12 h cache) | the rows (mode A) | `id`, `title`/`name`, `category`, `language`, `description`, `fields[]` (count only), `sections[]` (count), `price`, `updated_at`/`published_at`, `source_file_name`, `has_source_file` |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime` | which new-document modes are offered / locked | `settings.modes[]`, `settings.download_policy.formats[]`, `access.{manual,lawyer,review,analysis}.{allowed,code,message}` (access only with a token) |
| GET `/services/{id}/passport` | `?service=` deep link | `service.id`, `service.document_template_id`, `service.title_uz_latn`/`title_ru`/`title` |
| GET `/lawyers/{id}/services` | mode B: narrow to the advocate's services | `services[].id` / `service_id` |
| GET `/service-categories` (+ public GET `/platform/policies` → `order.catalog.categories[id].{hidden,title}` overlay; last good answer cached on device for offline) | mode B filter-sheet directions | `id`, `title`/`name`, `slug`, `description`, `services_count`, `subcategories_count`, `subcategories[].{title,services_count,sample_services[].title}` |
| GET `/services?catalog_only=true&limit=200` | mode B: the hub's rows | `id`, `title_uz_latn`/`title_ru`/`title`, `category_id`, `category_title`, `subcategory`, `catalog_code`, `standard_price`/`base_price`/`price`, `currency`, `pricing_tier`, `delivery_minutes`, `description`, `is_active`, `advokat_required`, `executor_type`, `document_template_id` |
| GET `/services/search?q=&limit=50` + GET `/services?q=&catalog_only=false&limit=50` (merged, ranked first) | mode B search ≥ 2 chars | `results[]`/`items[]` (each `service` or the row itself), same fields |
| GET `/calendar/business-hours`, GET `/marketplace/runtime-capabilities` | mode B banner `afterHours` note | `schedule.{days,start,end}`, `holidays[]`; `marketplace_assignment.seller_response_minutes` |

**UX note:** On a phone the search field, the four colour chips and the first two or three rows must be above the fold; the two pills already compete with the chips for that space and could become one line or move under the list. Mode B is a different page wearing the same tab (different title, hub cards, its own filter sheet) — confusing when the client arrives from the lawyers list and sees no documents; the dashed `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` row at the very bottom is easy to miss.

### `/services/direction` — `{subcat name}` (fallback `Xizmat buyurtma qilish`)

- **Who:** guest and client; browsing is open. Every card button goes through `useServiceOpen.guard` — a guest is pushed to `/auth/login` on `Hujjatni ko'rish`, `Yuklab olish`, `Advokatga yo'llash`, `Hujjatni to'ldirish`. `Yuklab olish` is also plan-gated (backend feature `template_download`), and the fill / advocate ways follow the document service settings (switched off → hidden; locked → lock icon + reason).
- **Purpose:** see every service of one direction (or a search across the whole catalogue) as cards with the four ways forward, and order one.
- **Route:** `/services/direction?cat=<categoryId>&subcat=<name>[&lawyer=<userId>]` (`src/pages/services/DirectionPage.tsx`) → `AdminPage` (back arrow = `router.back()`, title = `subcat` or `Xizmat buyurtma qilish`) wrapping `ServicesCatalog` with `pinned` (in-page back row hidden) and `CatalogSheets`. `?lawyer=` narrows the rows to that advocate's services (GET `/lawyers/{id}/services`); no banner is shown here.

**Layout, top → bottom (`src/widgets/services-catalog/ServicesCatalog.tsx`; which screen depends on `cat`/`subcat`/search):**

1. **Catalogue header** (`CatalogHeader`): h3 title + count pill, then the dashed row `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` (opens the new-document sheet). Title/count by screen: services screen (`cat`+`subcat`, or searching) → subcategory name (searching: `Xizmat buyurtma qilish`) and `{n} ta xizmat`; one category without `subcat` → category name and `{n} ta yo'nalish`, plus the category's `description` under the header; no `cat` → `Yo'nalishni tanlang` and `{n} ta yo'nalish`. Count is empty while loading.
2. **Filter bar** (`FilterBar`): `Input` with search icon, placeholder `Xizmatlarni qidirish` (max 120, `x-circle` clears), then a horizontal row of **FilterSelect** pills (label while unset, chosen option in blue once set; each opens an `OptionSheet` radio drawer titled with the label) and a `Filtrlarni tozalash` chip while any is dirty. Services screens: `Hujjat` (`Barchasi (n)`, `Tayyor hujjat bor (n)`, `Advokat tayyorlaydi (n)`), `Kim bajaradi` (`Barchasi`, `AI tayyorlaydi`, `Yurist`, `Advokat`, with counts), `Narx` (`Barchasi`, `Bepul`, `300 ming so'mgacha`, `300 ming – 1 mln so'm`, `1 mln so'mdan yuqori`, with counts), `Tartib` (`Tavsiya bo'yicha`, `Nomi bo'yicha (A–Z)`, `Arzonlari birinchi`, `Tayyor hujjatlilar birinchi`). Direction screens: `Tartib` (`Xizmatlari ko'pi birinchi`, `Xizmatlari kami birinchi`, `Nomi bo'yicha (A–Z)`), `Yo'nalish hajmi` (`Barchasi (n)`, `Yirik: 20+ xizmat`, `O'rtacha: 5–19 xizmat`, `Kichik: 1–4 xizmat`, with counts). Counts show how many rows each option would leave against the other current choices.
3. **Back row** (`BackRow`, `‹ Orqaga`, one step up: subcategory → category → areas) — never drawn on this page (`pinned`); only the header arrow leads out.
4. **Body** by screen:
   - Loading (categories pending or services pending or search settling) → three `ServiceCardSkeleton`s.
   - Error → `ErrorView` compact + `Qayta urinish`.
   - **Services screen** (`ServiceResults`): `ServiceTile` cards, 10 at a time with the `Yana {n} ta ko'rsatish · {n} ta qoldi` / `Kamroq ko'rsatish` footer. Empty → EmptyState (search) `Xizmat topilmadi` / `Boshqa kategoriya yoki qidiruvni sinang.`. Rows of a subcategory are sorted by name; a service with no `subcategory` is grouped under `Boshqa`.
   - **Subcategory screen** (one `cat`): `CardGrid` two per row of `SubcatCard` (tinted stage with the subcategory illustration or an icon, arrow badge; name (3 lines); pill `{n} ta xizmat`; `Masalan: {a · b · c}` from `sample_services`). Tap drills in-page to the services screen (no in-page way back).
   - **Areas screen** (no `cat`): `FamilyStrip` pills `Barcha sohalar · {n}` + each category with its subcategory count (one tap narrows), then the flat `SubcatCard` grid of every direction (largest first; each card also shows its area name in accent). Older backend without subcategories → `CategoryCards` (one card per area). Empty → EmptyState `Xizmat topilmadi` / `Boshqa kategoriya yoki qidiruvni sinang.`.
5. **ServiceTile** (`ui/ServiceTile.tsx`, one service): card with a warn-coloured border when `advokat_required`; 54 px tile with the subcategory illustration or a file/briefcase icon; name (up to 6 lines); pills `Hujjat shabloni` (has template) or `Xizmat`, and `Advokat bilan` (warn, shield) when `advokat_required`; then **DocActions** (`src/entities/service/ui/DocActions.tsx`):
   - With a template: a row of three square tiles — `Hujjatni ko'rish` (eye); `Yuklab olish` (download icon; while busy a spinner and `Yuklanmoqda…`; when the plan locks downloads a lock icon and a `PRO` corner badge); `Advokatga yo'llash` (users icon, lock icon when the lawyer mode is locked for this account; `TOP` warn badge when `advokat_required`; the whole tile is hidden when the lawyer mode is switched off). Under the row one caption: locked → `Shablonni yuklab olish pulli tarifda ochiladi`, else a limited plan's remainder `{feature}: {n} ta qoldi`; and when `advokat_required` `Bu xizmatni advokat shaxsan bajaradi — yagona yo'l shu`. Then the one gradient primary button `Hujjatni to'ldirish` (edit icon; lock icon when the manual mode is locked; hidden when switched off). Error caption under it (download refusals).
   - Without a template: a single outlined button `Advokatga yo'llash` (+ error caption).

**Actions (`model/useCatalogActions.ts` + `features/service-open`):**

- `Hujjatni ko'rish` → guard → `/services/{id}/view`.
- `Yuklab olish` → guard → `useTemplateDownload.download`: if the backend's feature state (`template_download` unavailable, or limited with 0 left) stops it → the plan gate sheet opens before any request; else GET `/services/{id}/document-fields` → if `has_source_file` is false → error `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi. Birozdan so'ng qayta urinib ko'ring.`; if the file's extension is closed by `download_policy.formats` → `Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan.`; else the clean file is downloaded with the token and handed to the share sheet. A 402 on the download opens the plan gate sheet with the backend's code; a document-service code is said in words (`dset.code.*`); anything else → `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi…`.
- `Advokatga yo'llash` → template service: guard → `/services/{id}/lawyer`; service without template: guard → the order sheet with this service (advocate picker, price, buy — see `/services/[id]`).
- `Hujjatni to'ldirish` → guard → hold check (GET `/document-requests/service-flow?status=open_pool|lawyer_review&limit=100`, matched on `service.id` and `lawyer_request_active`); if a hold asks (`prompt`) → confirm dialog title `Hujjat navbatchi advokatda`, text `Bu hujjat bo'yicha advokat ishlayapti. Baribir o'zingiz to'ldirasizmi?`, `Ha, to'ldiraman` / `Bekor qilish` (backend `prompt_title`/`prompt_text` win when sent) → `/services/{id}/fill`.
- Subcategory / direction cards → in-page drill-down; family pills → narrow; filters / sort / `Filtrlarni tozalash`; search ≥ 2 chars → catalogue-wide search (debounced 300 ms); `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` → new-document sheet (same as `/services`); header back arrow.

**States:** skeletons while loading; `ErrorView` with retry on a categories or services failure; EmptyState `Xizmat topilmadi` / `Boshqa kategoriya yoki qidiruvni sinang.`; search results show nothing (skeletons) until the term settles; per-card busy spinner on download; per-card error caption; plan gate sheet; the confirm dialog on a held document. Offline: the category summary comes from the device snapshot, services do not (error view).

**Data:**
| Endpoint | Used for | Key fields |
|---|---|---|
| GET `/service-categories` (+ GET `/platform/policies` overlay; device snapshot when offline; falls back to grouping GET `/services?limit=200&catalog_only=true` when empty) | areas, directions, counts, samples, description | `id`, `title`/`name`, `slug`, `description`, `services_count`, `subcategories_count`, `subcategories[].{title/name, services_count, sample_services[].title}` |
| GET `/services?category_id=&catalog_only=true&limit=500&offset=` (every page) | the picked category's services, grouped by `subcategory` | service fields as in `/services` mode B |
| GET `/services/search?q=&limit=50` + GET `/services?q=&catalog_only=false&limit=50` | search ≥ 2 chars | same |
| GET `/lawyers/{id}/services` | `?lawyer=` narrowing | `services[].id`/`service_id` |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime` | fill / advocate ways on every card, download formats, new-document sheet | `settings.modes[]`, `settings.download_policy.formats[]`, `access.*` |
| GET `/subscriptions/me/features` (signed in only) | `PRO` lock on download, `{n} ta qoldi` note | `items[].{feature_key, mode, allowed, limit, used, remaining, unit, resets_at}`, `plan_id` |
| GET `/services/{id}/document-fields` (on download tap) | the blank file facts | `template_id`, `title`, `has_source_file`, `clean_source_file_url` / `clean_source_file_inline_url` / `source_file_url` / `source_file_inline_url`, `source_file_name`, `source_mime_type` — then an authenticated GET of that path |
| GET `/document-requests/service-flow?status=open_pool&limit=100` and `…=lawyer_review…` (on fill tap) | hold prompt | `items[].{id, service.id, lawyer_request_active, can_send_lawyer_request, constructor_action.*, lawyer_request.work_id, document_request.work_id}` |
| Order sheet, plan gate sheet | see `/services/[id]` | — |

**UX note:** the header's `Yangi hujjatga buyurtma` row sits above the filter bar on every screen, pushing the first card down; the four-select filter row plus a search field is a lot of chrome before the first service. Because the in-page back row is hidden here, drilling from a direction grid (only `?cat`) into a subcategory leaves the client with just the header arrow, which exits the page instead of going one step up.

### `/services/[id]` — `{service name}` (fallback `Xizmat`)

- **Who:** guest and client. The page, the terms and the passport are public. A service without a template shows a guest `Buyurtma berish uchun tizimga kiring` + `Kirish` (→ `/auth/login`) in place of the order form; a template service's buttons guard sign-in on tap. `Yuklab olish` is plan-gated; the fill / advocate ways follow the document service settings.
- **Purpose:** understand one service (time, who, format, papers, review, steps, rules, guarantees, price) and order it — from an advocate, or by one of the document ways.
- **Route:** `/services/<id>?package=<packageId>&lawyer=<userId>&name=<name>` (`app/services/[id]/index.tsx` → `src/pages/service-detail/ServiceDetailPage.tsx`). `package` becomes `package_id` of the order and of the price quote; `lawyer`+`name` preselect that advocate in the picker (`preSeller`).

**Layout, top → bottom (`src/widgets/service-sheets/ServicePageView.tsx`; `AdminPage` with back arrow, title = service name; pull-to-refresh refetches passport + runtime):**

1. **Hero** (`ServiceHero`): gradient card in the direction's tone (hashed from `category_id`), decorative ring; badges `Hujjat` (has `document_template_id`) or `Xizmat`, and `Advokat bilan` when `advokat_required`; the subcategory illustration (zooms in) or a file/briefcase icon; `{category_title} · {subcategory}` line; name (h2, white); `Narx` + price in h3 (`{n} so'm` / `Bepul` / `Narx kelishiladi`).
2. **Three facts** (`ServiceFacts`, side by side, each a tile with icon, caption and bold value; a tile with no value is dropped): `Muddat` — the marketplace runtime duration put into words (`{n} daqiqa` / `{n} soat` / `{n} kun`; business calendar: `{n} ish kuni` with the business week under it, e.g. `Du–Ju`, or `{time}, ish kunlarida`); skeleton while the runtime loads; fallback the passport's `standard_duration` (`{value} kun`, or the raw text when it carries a unit) or `delivery_minutes` as `{h} h`. `Bajaruvchi` — `AI + yurist` / `Yurist` / `Advokat` / `Yurist / Advokat` / `Navbatchi advokat` / `Hujjat konstruktori` (regex over `executor_type`). `Format` — `Onlayn` / `Oflayn (yuzma-yuz)` / `Onlayn yoki oflayn` or the passport's raw `format`.
3. **Description** (grey body) when `description` exists and differs from the name.
4. **`Kerakli hujjatlar`** card: check-circle rows from the passport's `required_documents` (only when non-empty).
5. **Marketplace terms** (`ServiceTerms`, from the runtime configuration; nothing drawn while loading; an empty part is left out; 404 → nothing):
   - `Avval ko'rib chiqiladi` (tinted box): `Ko'rib chiqish muddati: {time}` when the review has a time, and the backend's `review.client_note` or `Mutaxassis so'rovingizni ko'rib chiqib, bajarish muddatini tasdiqlaydi.` Shown only when `review.value > 0`.
   - `Mutaxassis nima qiladi`: numbered steps (client title, description, pill `Majburiy` when required), 10 at a time (`Yana {n} ta ko'rsatish · {n} ta qoldi` / `Kamroq ko'rsatish`). Read-only, no checkboxes.
   - `Xizmat qoidalari`: each client rule — title, `Versiya {v}` (only numeric versions), body clamped to 4 lines when long with `To'liq o'qish` / `Yig'ish`.
   - `Xizmat kafolatlari` (green box): per guarantee an icon (shield / refund / clock / lock / star / doc / check), title, description, `Qachon: {trigger}`, `Yechim: {resolution}`, `Hal qilish muddati: {n soat | n kun | n daqiqa}`; 10 at a time.
   - Runtime failure (not 404) → one grey line `Xizmat shartlari yuklanmadi.` + `Qayta urinish`.
6. **`Buyurtma berish`** (h4) then:
   - Template service → `ServiceDocActions`: the same DocActions block as the catalogue card (`Hujjatni ko'rish` / `Yuklab olish` / `Advokatga yo'llash` tiles, captions, `Hujjatni to'ldirish`), with its own `PlanGateSheet`. Buttons: view → `/services/{id}/view`; download → plan gate or GET document-fields → file → share sheet; advocate → `/services/{id}/lawyer`; fill → hold check + confirm → `/services/{id}/fill` (all guarded by sign-in).
   - Other service, signed in → **OrderPanel inline** (below).
   - Other service, guest → warn text `Buyurtma berish uchun tizimga kiring` + gradient button `Kirish` (log-in icon) → `/auth/login`.
7. **`Xizmat pasporti`** (`PassportToggle`, collapsed glass row with a chevron; opens inline): `PassportFacts` sections, each animated in: `Xizmat` (pill `Ha, faqat advokat` when `advokat_required`; cells `Kod` (catalog_code, selectable), `Yo'nalish` (`family · group`), `Bo'lim`, `Ijrochi` (`Yurist` / `Advokat` / `Yurist yoki advokat` or raw), `Format`); `Muddat` (`Bajarish muddati`, `Shoshilinch muddat`, as `{value} kun` or raw); `Narx` (`Standart narx` `{n} so'm`, `Narx toifasi` `Asosiy` / `Standart` / `Premium`); `SLA va qaytarish` (`SLA`, `Qaytarish qoidasi` — raw codes); `Kerakli hujjatlar` (list or green `Qo'shimcha hujjat talab qilinmaydi.`); caption `Pasport versiyasi {v}`. Empty cells/sections are dropped. Loading → two skeletons; error → `Pasportni yuklab bo'lmadi.` + `Qayta urinish`. (On this page the passport is already loaded with the page — same query — so it opens instantly.)

**OrderPanel (`src/features/order-service/ui/OrderPanel.tsx`, the order form; also the body of the catalogue's order sheet):**

1. **PriceBox** (tinted card): before an advocate is picked — one row `Narx` → catalogue price `{n} so'm` or `So'rov bo'yicha`; while a quote loads `Hisoblanmoqda…`. With a quote (GET `/pricing/quote`, only once a real advocate is picked — never for the automatic choice): `Asosiy narx` (when it differs from the total), one row per modifier (`Hudud koeffitsienti`, `Tajriba koeffitsienti`, `Super advokat ustamasi`, else the backend label/key; value `+n%` / `×1.25` / `{n} so'm`), `Oraliq summa`, `Referral chegirmasi (−n%)` as `−{n} so'm` (green), `Yakuniy narx` (accent, h4).
2. **`Xizmat pasporti`** toggle (same as above; on the sheet it fetches on first open).
3. **`Advokatni tanlang`** (`SellerPicker`): a `Tartib` select when more than one advocate (`Mosligi`, `Reyting`, `Tajriba`, `Narx`); warn line `{name} bu xizmatni ko'rsatmaydi — ro'yxatdan boshqa advokatni tanlang yoki boshqa xizmatni oching.` when the preselected advocate does not offer the service; the **automatic choice** card (zap icon) `Avtomatik tanlov` / `LexGo buyurtmangizga mos mutaxassisni o'zi biriktiradi. Yakuniy narxni backend belgilaydi.` (radio, check when picked); then the sellers: skeletons while loading; `Bu xizmat uchun hozircha mutaxassis yo'q.` when none; else **SellerRow**s — avatar, name, green check (verified) or `Tasdiqlanmagan`, stats `★ 4.8` or `Hali baholanmagan` · `{n} yil` · `{n}% yutuq` · region, the match line `{n} ball moslik · Hududingizda · Shu xizmatni ko'rsatadi` (reasons map: `Tasdiqlangan`, `Hududingizda`, `Shu xizmatni ko'rsatganadi`→`Shu xizmatni ko'rsatadi`, `Shoshilinch ishga tayyor`, `Band`; two shown), price `{n} so'm` or `So'rov bo'yicha`, check when picked. Default sort `Mosligi` = backend match score, then rated/rating. The `?lawyer=` advocate is auto-picked once they appear in the list. `Avval advokatni tanlang` (red, rows outlined red) when buying without a pick.
4. **`Muammo (ixtiyoriy)`** multiline input, placeholder `Vaziyatni qisqacha yozing — mutaxassis tezroq tushunadi`, max 2000.
5. Response note (clock icon): outside business hours `Hozir ish vaqti emas. Mutaxassisning javob muddati ish vaqti boshlanganda hisoblanadi.` (warn); otherwise, once a real advocate is picked, `Mutaxassis ish vaqtida {n} daqiqa ichida javob beradi.` (green) from `seller_response_minutes`; nothing when unknown.
6. Error line (red): the backend code in words (`mf.err.*`, e.g. `Buyurtma berish uchun mijoz sifatida kiring.`, `Bu mutaxassis hozir buyurtma qabul qilmaydi. Boshqa mutaxassisni tanlang.`, `Bu mutaxassis va xizmat bo'yicha faol buyurtmangiz bor.`, `Bu paket bo'yicha faol buyurtmangiz bor.`), else `Xarid amalga oshmadi. Qayta urining.`.
7. Gradient button `Sotib olish va boshlash` (`Amalga oshirilmoqda…` while busy) → POST `/orders`; caption `Xariddan so'ng mutaxassis bilan maxfiy, himoyalangan chat ochiladi.`.
8. **After a successful POST:** marketplace order (`source = "marketplace"`) → tinted card: check icon + `Buyurtma yuborildi` + status pill (see enum) + `To'lov tasdiqlandi. Buyurtma mutaxassisga yuborildi — u belgilangan vaqt ichida javob beradi. Qabul qilgach himoyalangan chat ochiladi.` + `work_id` caption + button `Buyurtmani ochish` → `/orders/{id}`.
