An order the backend did not run as marketplace and not yet paid → **PayBlock**: `Umumiy summa`, `Oldindan to'lov ({pct}%)`, `To'langan`, progress bar, `Maxfiy chatni ochish uchun yana {amount} to'lang.` (lock, warn) or `To'lov yetarli — maxfiy chat ochildi.` (green); button `{pct}% oldindan to'lash` (`Kutilmoqda…`) → POST `/payments` → opens the provider URL (`To'lov sahifasi ochildi.`) or `To'lov tizimi hali ulanmagan. Buyurtma saqlandi, to'lovni keyinroq amalga oshirasiz.`; error `To'lovni amalga oshirib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; once unlocked `Maxfiy chatni ochish` → `/(tabs)/orders`. Policy load failure → `To'lovni amalga oshirib bo'lmadi…`.

**Order status enum (`mk6.status`):** `new` `Yangi`, `waiting_info` `Ma'lumot kutilmoqda`, `waiting_docs` `Hujjat kutilmoqda`, `waiting_payment`/`pending_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `seller_selection` `Buyurtma qabul qilindi`, `sent_to_seller` `Mutaxassisga yuborildi`, `paid` `To'langan`, `accepted` `Qabul qilindi`, `started` `Boshlandi`, `in_progress` `Jarayonda`, `result_ready` `Natija tayyor`, `quality_check` `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `delivered` `Topshirildi`, `client_confirmation` `Tasdiqlashingiz kutilmoqda`, `completed` `Yakunlandi`, `rated` `Baholandi`, `lost` `Yo'qotildi`, `cancelled` `Bekor qilindi`, `declined` `Rad etildi`, `refunded` `Qaytarildi`; unknown → humanised slug.

**Plan gate sheet (`src/features/plan-gate/ui/PlanGateSheet.tsx`, raised by `Yuklab olish` here and on the direction page / template page):** `FormSheet` titled by the gate code — `Limit tugadi` (`feature_limit_exhausted`), `Obuna kerak` (`active_subscription_required`), `Tarifingizda yo'q` (`feature_not_in_plan`), `AI limiti tugadi` (`ai_subscription_required`), `Tarif kerak` (`plan_required`). Body: **GateNotice** (warn box) with the reason naming the feature — `«{feature}» limiti shu davr uchun tugadi. Yangilanishini kuting yoki kattaroq tarif tanlang.` / `«{feature}» faqat faol obuna bilan ishlaydi. Tarif tanlang.` / `«{feature}» joriy tarifingizga kirmaydi. Shu imkoniyati bor tarifni tanlang.` (without a feature name the `any.*` variants; `plan_required` keeps the server's own `message`), a usage meter and `{used} / {limit} ishlatildi · Yangilanadi: {date}` for a used-up limit; then `Paketni tanlang` + the tariffs that lift the gate as `PlanCard`s (`Shu tarifni tanlash` / `Tanlandi`; the client's own plans, gift plans and other audiences excluded), `Muddatni tanlang` period chips, the Telegram notice; submit `So'rov yuborish` (`Amalga oshirilmoqda…`) → POST `/subscription-plans/{id}/telegram-purchase-request`; secondary `Tariflar` → `/subscription`. No fitting plan → `Bu imkoniyatni ochadigan tarifni tariflar sahifasidan tanlang.` + `Tariflarni ko'rish`. After sending → green `So'rovingiz yuborildi. Tasdiqlangach tarif faollashadi.` + `Summa: {n} so'm` (+ campaign line) + `Yopish`. Opening the sheet re-reads `/subscriptions/me/features`.

**Actions:** back arrow; pull-to-refresh; DocActions tiles (template service); `Xizmat pasporti` toggle; sort select; advocate / `Avtomatik tanlov` pick; problem text; `Sotib olish va boshlash`; `Buyurtmani ochish` → `/orders/{id}`; `{pct}% oldindan to'lash`; `Maxfiy chatni ochish` → `/(tabs)/orders`; `Kirish` (guest) → `/auth/login`; `To'liq o'qish`/`Yig'ish` on rules; show-more footers; plan sheet actions.

**States:** page loading → three skeletons (30 / 80 / 160 px); passport error → `ErrorView` + `Qayta urinish`; facts tile skeleton while the runtime loads; runtime 404 → no terms section; runtime error → `Xizmat shartlari yuklanmadi.` + retry; sellers skeletons / `Bu xizmat uchun hozircha mutaxassis yo'q.`; quote `Hisoblanmoqda…`; `Avval advokatni tanlang`; POST error line; success card / PayBlock; payment notes; plan gate sheet; per-button download busy / error captions; hold confirm dialog.

**Data:**
| Endpoint | Used for | Key fields |
|---|---|---|
| GET `/services/{id}/passport` (shared by page, facts, passport toggle) | hero, facts fallback, papers, passport | `service.{id,title_uz_latn,title_ru,title,slug,category_id,category_title,subcategory,catalog_code,standard_price/base_price/price,currency,pricing_tier,delivery_minutes,description,is_active,advokat_required,executor_type,document_template_id}`; `passport.{catalog_code,family,group,subcategory,executor_type,advokat_required,format,standard_duration,urgent_duration,required_documents[],pricing_tier,standard_price,refund_code,sla_code,ai_category,version}` (`seller_income`, `platform_fee` never mapped) |
| GET `/marketplace/services/{id}/runtime-configuration?placement=service_detail` (public; one request per opened service; locale in the cache key) | `Muddat` tile, review, steps, rules, guarantees | `service_id`, `placement`, `duration.{value,unit,calendar,business_weekdays[],business_days_label}`, `review.{value,unit,calendar,client_note}`, `checklist_preview[].{id/key/item_key,client_title,title,client_description,description,required/is_required,order/sort_order/position,executor_title…}`, `client_rules[].{id/rule_id/rule_key,title/name,version/rule_version,body/content/text/description,audiences[]/audience}`, `guarantees[].{id,icon,title/name,description/text/body,trigger,resolution,resolution_minutes,is_active,audiences[]}` (`rules` for the executor is never read) |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime` | fill / advocate ways, download formats (template service) | `settings.modes[]`, `settings.download_policy.formats[]`, `access.*` |
| GET `/subscriptions/me/features` (signed in) | download lock / remainder; plan sheet | `items[].{feature_key,mode,allowed,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}`, `plan_id`, `subscription_id`, `active` |
| GET `/services/{id}/document-fields` (download tap) | blank file facts → authenticated file GET | see `/services/direction` |
| GET `/document-requests/service-flow?status=…` (fill tap) | hold prompt | see `/services/direction` |
| GET `/lawyers?service_id={id}` | advocate list | `lawyers[]`/`items[]`: `user_id`/`id`, `lawyer_name`/`name`/`full_name`, `region`, `experience_years`, `rating`, `rating_status`, `reviews`, `base_hourly_price`/`price` (+ service-specific price), `is_verified`/`lexgo_verified`, `verification_badge.label`, `success_rate` |
| GET `/matching/candidates?service_id={id}&urgency=normal[&region=]` | match score + reasons, default sort | `items[]`/`candidates[]`: `lawyer_user_id`, `score`, `reasons[]` |
| GET `/pricing/quote?service_id=&seller_user_id=[&package_id=][&region=]` (only with a real advocate picked) | PriceBox | `base_amount`/`subtotal`/`base`, `subtotal`, `discount_amount`, `total_amount`/`total`/`price`, `currency`, `discount_percent` / `referral_discount.discount_percent`, `modifiers[].{code/key/type,label/name,percent,amount,multiplier}` |
| POST `/orders` | buy | body `{service_id, package_id|null, lawyer_user_id? (omitted for the automatic choice), source:"marketplace", details:{region, problem, selected_option_ids:[], service_title}}`; reads `id`, `status`, `payment_status`, `source`, `work_id` |
| GET `/orders/{id}/payment-policy` (non-marketplace unpaid order) | PayBlock | `price`/`total_amount`, `advance_percent`/`upfront_percent`, `advance_amount`/`upfront_amount`, `paid_amount`, `remaining_to_unlock`, `contact_unlocked`, `currency` |
| POST `/payments` | advance | body `{provider, currency:"UZS", amount, order_id, target_type:"order", target_id, provider_payload:{purpose:"order_advance"}}`; reads `payment_url` / `invoice.payment_url`, `id`, `status` |
| GET `/calendar/business-hours`, GET `/marketplace/runtime-capabilities` | response note | `schedule.{days,start,end}`, `holidays[]`; `marketplace_assignment.seller_response_minutes` |
| GET `/subscription-plans` (public), GET `/clients/me/entitlements`, POST `/subscription-plans/{id}/telegram-purchase-request` | plan gate sheet | plans `id`, `is_active`, `monthly_price`, `functions[]`, contract terms; entitlements `active_subscriptions[].plan_id`; request answer `pricing.{amount,discount…}` |
| (also) GET `/auth/me` session `user.region` | region for quote / order / candidates | `region` |

**UX note:** the hero with price and the three fact tiles are what must be visible first; on a non-template service the order form (price, advocate list, buy) is below description, papers, review, steps, rules and guarantees — a long scroll before the buy button, and the passport toggle duplicates most of the facts above it. The automatic-choice card looks like a seller row and competes with the real advocates; the success card appears in place of the form inside the same scroll, easy to miss after a long page.

### `/services/[id]/view` — `{template title}` (fallback `Hujjat namunasi`)

- **Who:** reached only through `useServiceOpen.guard` (signed-in clients); the page itself does not check sign-in. Plan-gated by the backend: a 402 on the file facts shows the gate (`GateNotice`) with a way to the tariffs.
- **Purpose:** look at the blank sample of a document service, read-only — looking is free, getting the file is the paid `Yuklab olish`.
- **Layout, top → bottom (`src/pages/service-view/ServiceViewPage.tsx`):** `AdminPage` (back arrow, title = document-fields `title` or `Hujjat namunasi`); then one of: skeletons (26 px + 300 px); plan gate → `GateNotice` titled (code title + reason + meter/facts) + gradient button `Tanlash` (award icon) → `/subscription`; failed → EmptyState (alert-circle) `Hujjatni yuklab bo'lmadi. Birozdan so'ng qayta urinib ko'ring.` + soft `Qayta urinish`; no file → EmptyState (file) `Bu xizmat uchun hujjat namunasi mavjud emas.`; else the full-height `PreviewBody` file viewer (DOCX/PDF by name/mime). No share, save or print control.
- **Actions:** back; `Tanlash` → `/subscription`; `Qayta urinish` (refetches the facts, or restarts the download).
- **States:** loading (facts pending, or file not yet `done`); gate (402, any `plan` code); failed (facts error that is not a gate, or download error); empty (`has_source_file` false or no URL); viewer.
- **Data:**
  | Endpoint | Used for | Key fields |
  |---|---|---|
  | GET `/services/{id}/document-fields` | which file, title, gate | `template_id`, `title`, `has_source_file`, `clean_source_file_url` → `clean_source_file_inline_url` → `source_file_url` → `source_file_inline_url` (first non-empty wins), `source_file_name`, `source_mime_type`; 402 `detail.{code,feature_key,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}` |
  | GET `<that path>` (authenticated download to a local file) | the viewer | binary |
- **UX note:** the title is the backend's template title, often long; the viewer needs the whole screen, so nothing else should be added here. The page explains nothing about why there is no download — the catalogue hint `Faqat ko'rish uchun — hujjatni bu yerdan yuklab olib bo'lmaydi.` (`csv.docViewHint`) exists in the locale but is not rendered.

### `/templates/[id]` — `{template name}` (fallback `Hujjat`)

- **Who:** guest and client. A guest reads everything (title, pills, full preview); tapping any of the three action rows sends a guest to `/auth/login`. Signed in: `O'zim to'ldiraman` / `Navbatchi advokat bilan tayyorlash` follow the document service settings (switched off → row missing; locked → lock icon + reason); `Toza shablonni yuklab olish` is plan-gated (`template_download`) and only offered while DOCX downloads are open and the template has a source file.
- **Purpose:** read one ready document as a reader sees it and choose how to get it: fill it yourself, hand it to the duty advocate, or download the blank DOCX.
- **Route:** `/templates/<id>` (`app/templates/[id].tsx` → `src/pages/template-detail/TemplateDetailPage.tsx`). The template is re-read from the server on opening (never only the list row).

**Layout, top → bottom:**

1. `AdminPage` header: back arrow, title = name (1 line), subtitle = category label (`Fuqarolik` / `Iqtisodiy` / `Ma'muriy` / `Jinoiy` / server title / humanised key).
2. Name (h2, selectable); description (grey, selectable) when it differs from the name.
3. **Pills**: category (blue), language (grey: `O'zbekcha (kirill)` etc.), `{price} so'm` (green, only when `price > 0`), `{n} ta maydon` (grey, when the template has fields).
4. **Plan note card** (warn border) only after the plan stopped the download once (`planSeen`): `Tarif talab qilinadi` / `Qo'lda to'ldirish va yuklab olish uchun mos tarif kerak. Tanlagan hujjatingiz va kiritganlaringiz saqlanadi.` + gradient `Tarifni tanlash` (award) → `/subscription`.
5. `Nima qilmoqchisiz?` (h4, only when at least one action is offered) + **TemplateActions** — one big row each (never side by side), 44 px icon tile, title, hint, chevron:
   - `O'zim to'ldiraman` / `Savollarga javob bering, hujjat tayyor bo'ladi` — primary (filled accent) — → `/services/{serviceId}/fill` when a linked service exists, else `/templates/{id}/fill`. Mode `manual`.
   - `Navbatchi advokat bilan tayyorlash` / `Advokatni siz tanlamaysiz: so'rov navbatchilarga tushadi` (headphones) → `/services/{serviceId}/lawyer` or `/templates/{id}/lawyer`. Mode `lawyer`.
   - Locked mode → lock icon and the hint becomes the reason (`Buning uchun faol obuna kerak.` / `Joriy tarifingizga bu xizmat kirmaydi.` / `Tarifingizda bu imkoniyat yo'q.` / backend `message` / `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`); the row still navigates. Mode `document_mode_disabled` → row absent.
   - `Toza shablonni yuklab olish` / `Maydonlarsiz bo'sh DOCX fayl` (download icon; loader icon and disabled while busy) — only when `has_source_file` and `download_policy.formats` allows `docx` (or settings not loaded). Linked service → the service's clean file via `useTemplateDownload` (GET `/services/{sid}/document-fields` → file → share sheet, with its plan gate / format refusal); unlinked → GET `/document-templates/{id}/clean-source-file` → share sheet as `{name}.docx`. A plan stop (feature state or 402) opens the plan gate sheet and sets the plan note card; a document-service refusal or other failure → toast (`dset.code.*` text or `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi. Birozdan so'ng qayta urinib ko'ring.`).
6. **Preview** (`TemplatePreview`): h4 `Hujjat ko'rinishi`; skeleton (220 px) while the server render loads and there is no `template_text` yet; else `DocPaper`: a legend `Ko'k maydonlar: {n} tasini siz to'ldirasiz` when the text has fill spots, a letterhead kicker (category, upper-case), the document text with the server's line breaks, heading, recipient block, signatures and highlighted empty spots; the first ~900 characters inline with a fade and a soft button `To'liq ko'rish` (maximize icon) when longer; render error with no fallback text → `ErrorView` compact + retry. `To'liq ko'rish` opens a bottom sheet (title = name, snap 0.94) with the whole text in larger type.
7. Pull-to-refresh refetches the template and the render. `PlanGateSheet` mounted (see `/services/[id]`).

**Linked service vs none:** GET `/services?catalog_only=false&summary=false&limit=500` is mapped `document_template_id → id` (rows with `is_active === false` skipped; a failure of this request simply means "no linked service"). With a linked service the fill / advocate / download go through the service's own routes and endpoints (`/services/{sid}/fill`, `/services/{sid}/lawyer`, `/services/{sid}/document-fields`, the service's plan gates); without one they go through the generic template routes (`/templates/{id}/fill`, `/templates/{id}/lawyer`, `/document-templates/{id}/clean-source-file`).

**Actions:** back; the three action rows (guest → `/auth/login`); `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription`; `To'liq ko'rish` → full-text sheet; sheet close; pull-to-refresh; 404 → `Katalogga qaytish` → `/(tabs)/services`; error → `Qayta urinish`. Analytics: `document_template_opened`, `document_preview_opened`, `document_clean_template_downloaded` (category/language only).

**States:** loading → skeletons (34 / 70 / 210 px); 404 → EmptyState (file-minus) `Hujjat topilmadi` / `Bu hujjat olib tashlangan yoki mavjud emas.` + `Katalogga qaytish`; other error → `ErrorView` + `Qayta urinish`; render loading / error (falls back to `template_text`); download busy; plan note card; plan gate sheet; toast on refusal; offline → the generic error text `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…` (no dedicated offline state here).

**Data:**
| Endpoint | Used for | Key fields |
|---|---|---|
| GET `/document-templates/{id}` | title, description, pills, fallback text | `id`, `title`/`name`, `category`, `language`, `description`, `fields[]` (count), `sections[]` (count), `price`, `updated_at`/`published_at`, `source_file_name`, `has_source_file`, `template_text` |
| POST `/document-templates/{id}/render` body `{answers:{}}` (public) | the preview as a reader sees it | `preview_text`/`final_text`, `missing_required_fields`, `can_generate`, `completion_percent`, `template_id`, `language`, section schema |
| GET `/services?catalog_only=false&summary=false&limit=500` | template → linked service id | `services[]`/`items[]`: `id`, `document_template_id`, `is_active` |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime` | which rows are offered / locked; DOCX download open | `settings.modes[]`, `settings.download_policy.formats[]`, `access.{manual,lawyer}.{allowed,code,message}` |
| GET `/subscriptions/me/features` (signed in) | `template_download` stop before any request | `items[].{feature_key,mode,allowed,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}` |
| GET `/document-templates/{id}/clean-source-file` (unlinked; authenticated binary) | the blank DOCX | binary; 402 `detail.{code,…}` |
| GET `/services/{sid}/document-fields` → file (linked) | the blank via the service | see `/services/direction` |
| plan gate sheet endpoints | see `/services/[id]` | — |

**UX note:** the title, the four pills and the first action row must be visible at once; the inline preview is the real value of the page and today it sits under three tall action rows — on long templates the client scrolls past 250 px of buttons before the paper. The locked rows still navigate (to a page that then explains the gate), which reads as a dead tap; and the `≈ n daqiqa` estimate shown in the catalogue row is absent here.

### Template / service entities used by these routes

- **`src/entities/doc-template`** (ready documents): `DocTemplate = { id, name (title → source file name without extension), category, language, description, fields (count), sections (count), price, updatedAt, sourceFile, hasSource }`. The four known branches `TEMPLATE_CATEGORIES`: `legal-doc-civil` `Fuqarolik` (blue), `legal-doc-economic` `Iqtisodiy` (teal), `legal-doc-administrative` `Ma'muriy` (amber), `legal-doc-criminal` `Jinoiy` (red); any other key → server `title` or humanised slug, grey. `TemplateCategoryInfo = { key, title, count (templates_count), languages[] }` from GET `/document-template-categories`. `TemplateDetail = DocTemplate + text (template_text)`. Language labels `dcat.lang.*`: `uz-cyrl` `O'zbekcha (kirill)`, `uz-latn` `O'zbekcha (lotin)`, `ru` `Ruscha`, `en` `Inglizcha`. Catalogue is public, one request per branch, ≈14 MB for all ~989 rows, parsed on a worker runtime and slimmed; 12 h stale time. Search folds Cyrillic → Latin and strips Uzbek apostrophes. `useLinkedServices` maps template id → active service id. Also `shareTemplate` (GET `/document-templates/{id}/file` as PDF) exists in the entity but is not used by these routes.
- **`src/entities/service`** (catalogue services): `Service = { id, name (title_uz_latn / title_ru by locale → title/name, cleaned), slug, categoryId, categoryTitle, subcategory, catalogCode, price (standard_price/base_price/price, > 0 only), currency, free (pricing_tier = "free"), deliveryMinutes, description, isActive, advokatRequired, executorType, documentTemplateId }`; `hasTemplate(s)` = has `document_template_id`. `ServiceCategory = { id, name, slug, description, servicesCount, subcategoriesCount, subcategories[].{title, servicesCount, samples (≤3 sample_services titles)} }` with the admin overlay from `/platform/policies` (hidden / renamed). `ServicePassport` from GET `/services/{id}/passport` (fields listed under `/services/[id]`; money internals never mapped). Filters: `DocFilter` all/template/lawyer (`Barchasi` / `Tayyor hujjat bor` / `Advokat tayyorlaydi`); `PriceFilter` all/free/low (≤ 300 000)/mid (≤ 1 000 000)/high (`Barchasi` / `Bepul` / `300 ming so'mgacha` / `300 ming – 1 mln so'm` / `1 mln so'mdan yuqori`); `ExecFilter` all/ai/yurist/advokat (`AI tayyorlaydi` / `Yurist` / `Advokat`; `advokat_required` wins, then regexes over `executor_type`, unknown → ai); `SvcSort` rel/az/cheap/doc; `DirSize` all/big (≥ 20)/mid (≥ 5)/small; `SubSort` count/countAsc/name. `NO_SUBCAT = "Boshqa"` for services without a subcategory. Executor words (`dc1.executor`): `duty` `Navbatchi advokat`, `ai` `AI + yurist`, `constructor` `Hujjat konstruktori`, `both` `Yurist / Advokat`, `advokat` `Advokat`, `yurist` `Yurist`. Price label: `{n} so'm` / `Bepul` / `Narx kelishiladi`.
- **`src/entities/service-runtime`**: `ServiceRuntime = { serviceId, placement, duration {value, unit minute|hour|day, calendar calendar|business_days, weekdays[]}, review {time, clientNote} | null, clientRules[] {id, title, version, body (HTML → text)}, guarantees[] {id, icon shield|refund|clock|lock|star|doc|check, title, description, trigger, resolution, resolutionMinutes}, checklist[] {id, title, description, required} }`; items for other audiences and inactive guarantees are dropped; the business week defaults to Mon–Fri.
- **`src/entities/doc-settings`** (GET `/document-service-settings/runtime`): modes `manual` (fill yourself), `lawyer` (duty advocate), `review` (check my document), `analysis` (AI analysis); gate states `open` / `off` (`document_mode_disabled` → not offered) / `locked` (`document_subscription_required` `Buning uchun faol obuna kerak.`, `document_plan_not_allowed` `Joriy tarifingizga bu xizmat kirmaydi.`, `document_entitlement_required` `Tarifingizda bu imkoniyat yo'q.`); `document_download_format_disabled` `Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan.`; generic `Hozircha o'chirilgan` / `Hozir mavjud emas` / `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`. `download_policy.formats` polices pdf/doc/docx only.
- **`src/entities/plan` gates** (402 or feature state): codes `feature_limit_exhausted` `Limit tugadi`, `active_subscription_required` `Obuna kerak`, `feature_not_in_plan` `Tarifingizda yo'q`, `ai_subscription_required` `AI limiti tugadi`, `plan_required` `Tarif kerak`; document codes map onto them (`document_subscription_required` → active subscription, `document_plan_not_allowed` / `document_entitlement_required` → not in plan). Feature used here: `template_download`; remainder note `{feature}: {n} ta qoldi`.
- **`src/entities/order`**: `createOrder` (POST `/orders`), `getPriceQuote` (GET `/pricing/quote`), `getPaymentPolicy`, `createOrderPayment`; order statuses as listed under `/services/[id]`.
- **`src/entities/lawyer`** (order picker): `Lawyer { userId, name, region, experienceYears, rating, rated, basePrice, verified, badge, successRate … }`, `MatchCandidate { lawyerUserId, score, reasons[] }`, `useOfferedServiceIds` (GET `/lawyers/{id}/services`).
- **`src/entities/doc-form`** (used here only for): the hold check (GET `/document-requests/service-flow`), the template render (POST `/document-templates/{id}/render`) and the `DocPaper` preview with its `Ko'k maydonlar: {n} tasini siz to'ldirasiz` legend.

### `/services/[id]/fill` — `Hujjatni to'ldirish` (header shows the service's own document title from the schema; `Hujjatni to'ldirish` is the fallback)

- **Who:** signed-in client only. Order of gates in code: (1) the document service's **manual mode** (`GET /document-service-settings/runtime`, `access.manual` / `settings.modes`) — checked even before the sign-in check, so a guest with the mode switched off sees the mode notice, not the login prompt; (2) guest → `LoginGate` (`Tizimga kiring` / `Hujjatni to'ldirish va yaskratish uchun hisobingiz kerak.` → button `Kirish` → `/auth/login`); (3) plan: a 402 on the schema or on generate opens the plan sheet. Note: the LoginGate text is exactly `Hujjatni to'ldirish va yaratish uchun hisobingiz kerak.`
- **Purpose:** fill a catalogue service's document template section by section myself and get the server-built file.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AdminPage` header: back chip, title = schema `title` (cleaned), subtitle `{i}-qadam, jami {n}` (step counter; sections + review) — after generation the subtitle becomes `Hujjat tayyor`. Right icons (hidden after generation): trash `Qoralamani tozalash` (opens confirm dialog), eye `Hujjat ko'rinishi` (opens the preview sheet).
  2. `DraftBanner` (tinted card, only when a local draft with at least one answer was restored; shown once until dismissed): `Qoralama tiklandi`, time ago (or `hozirgina`), buttons `Qoralamani tozalash` (soft, clears all answers + go to step 0) and `Davom ettirish` (ghost, hides the banner).
  3. `SectionTabs` — one horizontally scrolling chip per section: `{section title | Bo'lim {n}} · {filled}/{total}`, a check icon when every field of the section is filled; last chip `Tekshirish` (eye icon). Tap = jump (fields of the target section become "touched", so their errors show).
  4. Current section (`SectionForm`): h3 section title (from backend `sections[].title`; fields the backend lists outside any section go into a trailing `Qo'shimcha ma'lumotlar` section), then every field in backend `order`. Field widgets by backend `type`: text/textarea (`Kiriting` placeholder unless the backend gives a real example), number/int/money (numeric keyboard), email, phone/tel (`+998 90 123 45 67` placeholder, auto-formatted), date (masked input `KK.OO.YYYY` + calendar icon `Kalendar` → `CalendarSheet`: ‹ month/year chip › , year grid when the chip is tapped, weekday row `Du Se Ch Pa Ju Sh Ya`, button `Bugun`, caption `Sana KK.OO.YYYY ko'rinishida yoziladi.`), select/radio (SelectField), multiselect/multi (chips, joined `a, b`), checkbox/boolean (switch, sends `true`). Required fields get ` *` after the label; `hint` from backend under the field.
  5. Review step (after the last section): h3 `Tekshirib chiqing`, line `To'ldirilmagan yoki noto'g'ri maydonlar bor — ularni tuzating.` (problems) or `Hammasi to'ldirilgan. Hujjatni yaratishingiz mumkin.`; `ReviewSummary`: warn box `Tuzatish kerak: {n} ta maydon` listing each problem field (tap → jump to its section), then one bordered card per section with `label → value` rows (`to'ldirilmagan` in grey when empty); h4 `Hujjat ko'rinishi` with progress bar (`completion_percent`) and `PreviewText`/`DocPaper` (the server's `preview_text` laid out as a white A4-like sheet: `LEXGO` letterhead, title, numbered clauses, every unfilled spot as a numbered blue field ①②…, legend `Ko'k maydonlar: {n} tasini siz to'ldirasiz`), or `Ko'rinishni yuklab bo'lmadi.`, or a skeleton while loading.
  6. Sticky footer (hidden while the keyboard is open and after generation): `Orqaga` (line, not on the first step) + `Davom etish` (gradient, arrow) — on the review step `Hujjatni yaratish` (gradient, file icon; disabled while any problem remains; spinner while generating). `Davom etish` refuses (error haptic, errors shown) when the current section has a required/invalid field.
  7. After generation — `ResultView`: `GeneratedCard` (tinted: green check badge, `Hujjat tayyor`, document title, pills `work_id` (blue, #), file format e.g. `DOCX`, `To'langan`/`Bepul` (price > 0 ? paid : free)); buttons `Ko'rish` (in-app `FilePreview` of the file) and `Ulashish / saqlash` (download + share sheet), or `Fayl hali tayyor emas.` when no file; then `Mening hujjatlarim` (line, only when `request.id` exists → `/documents/{id}`), `Boshqa hujjat tanlash` (ghost → `/(tabs)/services`).
- **Sheets / dialogs:**
  - `PreviewSheet` (eye icon; snaps 0.7/0.94): title `Hujjat ko'rinishi`, subtitle `Server shablonni javoblaringiz bilan to'ldirdi`; `To'ldirilganlik: {n}%` + progress bar + `DocPaper`; empty → `Ko'rinish hozircha bo'sh.`; error → compact ErrorView with `Qayta urinish`.
  - Clear dialog: `Qoralama tozalansinmi?` / `Kiritilgan barcha javoblar o'chiriladi.` / `Qoralamani tozalash` (danger) / `Bekor qilish`.
  - `PlanGateSheet` (402 on generate; also auto-opened once on a 402 schema failure): title = gate code title (`Limit tugadi` feature_limit_exhausted · `Obuna kerak` active_subscription_required · `Tarifingizda yo'q` feature_not_in_plan · `AI limiti tugadi` ai_subscription_required · `Tarif kerak` plan_required; document codes map: document_subscription_required→active_subscription_required, document_plan_not_allowed / document_entitlement_required→feature_not_in_plan); `GateNotice` (warn box): text naming the feature, e.g. `«{feature}» limiti shu davr uchun tugadi. Yangilanishini kuting yoki kattaroq tarif tanlang.` / `«{feature}» faqat faol obuna bilan ishlaydi. Tarif tanlang.` / `«{feature}» joriy tarifingizga kirmaydi. Shu imkoniyati bor tarifni tanlang.` (or the feature-less variants `Shu davr uchun limit tugadi…`, `Faol obunangiz yo'q. Davom etish uchun tarif tanlang.`, `Bu imkoniyat joriy tarifingizga kirmaydi.`, `Umumiy AI limitingiz tugadi…`, `Bu imkoniyat uchun mos tarif kerak.` — plan_required keeps the server's own sentence when sent); meter bar + `{used} / {limit} ishlatildi · Yangilanadi: {date}` when the 402 carries `used`/`limit`/`resets_at`; then `Paketni tanlang` + `PlanCard` list of tariffs whose contract lifts the gate (own tariffs excluded; card CTA `Shu tarifni tanlash` / `Tanlandi`), `Muddatni tanlang` chips (`1 oy` / `6 oy` / `12 oy` / `Oldindan`), `TelegramNotice` errors (`Bu paket tanlangan muddatni qabul qilmaydi. Mavjud muddatlar qoldirildi.` 422 / `So'rov yaratildi, lekin Telegramga yetkazib bo'lmadi. Administrator bilan bog'laning.` / `So'rovni yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`); submit `So'rov yuborish` (`Amalga oshirilmoqda…` while pending), secondary `Tariflar` → `/subscription`; no fitting tariff → `Bu imkoniyatni ochadigan tarifni tariflar sahifasidan tanlang.` + `Tariflarni ko'rish`; after a send → green notice `So'rovingiz yuborildi. Tasdiqlangach tarif faollashadi.`, `Summa: {amount} so'm` (+ campaign line), button `Yopish`.
- **Actions:** section chip → jump; `Davom etish` → next section (validates current); `Orqaga` → previous; `Hujjatni yaratish` → `POST /services/{id}/document-generate`; eye → preview sheet (`POST /services/{id}/document-preview`); trash → clear dialog; `Ko'rish` → in-app file preview; `Ulashish / saqlash` → download file + share; `Mening hujjatlarim` → `/documents/{request.id}`; `Boshqa hujjat tanlash` → services tab; plan sheet → Telegram purchase request / `/subscription`. Draft autosaves to the device (AsyncStorage, key `user:manual:serviceId`, ~600 ms after a change and on leave; max 30 drafts; wiped on logout) and is dropped on a successful generate.
- **States:**
  - loading schema/drafts → `FlowLoading` (4 skeleton rows under the header).
  - manual mode off → `Hozircha o'chirilgan` / `Platforma bu xizmatni vaqtincha o'chirgan.` + `Orqaga`; locked by a plan code → `Tarif kerak` / code text (`Buning uchun faol obuna kerak.` · `Joriy tarifingizga bu xizmat kirmaydi.` · `Tarifingizda bu imkoniyat yo'q.`) + `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription`; locked, other → `Hozir mavjud emas` / backend message or `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`. While this notice is on screen the settings are re-read on every return to the screen.
  - guest → LoginGate (above).
  - schema 404 → `Bu xizmatda hujjat shabloni yo'q` / `Bu xizmat buyurtma orqali bajariladi — xizmat kartasidan buyurtma bering.` + `Orqaga`.
  - schema 402 → titled `GateNotice` + `Tariflarni ko'rish` (→ `/subscription`) + the plan sheet opened once on top.
  - schema refused with a document code (not plan) → the mode notice above.
  - other schema error → ErrorView (`Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.` offline · `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.` 403 · `Ma'lumot topilmadi.` 404 · `Juda ko'p urinish. Bir ozdan so'ng qayta urining.` 429 · server `detail` · `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`) + `Qayta urinish`.
  - field errors (after touch): `Majburiy maydon`, `Email noto'g'ri`, `Telefon raqami to'liq emas`, `Sana noto'g'ri (KK.OO.YYYY)`, `Format noto'g'ri`, `Qiymat juda kichik`, `Qiymat juda katta`, `Juda qisqa`, `Juda uzun`.
  - generate failures: 402 → plan sheet; document-service refusal code → toast with its text (and settings re-read); 422 `missing_required_fields` → jump to the first missing field's section + toast `Server {n} ta majburiy maydon yetishmasligini bildirdi.`; offline → `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`; else server `detail` or `Hujjat yaratilmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`
  - share failure → `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi.` or, for a closed download format, `Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Used for                                               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /document-service-settings/runtime`                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | manual-mode gate, download policy                      | `settings.modes[]`, `access.manual.{allowed,code,message}`, `settings.download_policy.formats[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GET /services/{id}/document-fields`                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | section schema (404 = no template; 402 = plan)         | `title`, `service_id`, `template_id`, `sections[]{id,title,order,fields[]}`, `fields[]` (flat: `key/name,label,type,required,placeholder,hint,options,pattern,min,max,min_length,max_length,order,section_id,section_title,section_order`), `required_count`, `has_source_file`, `source_file_name`, `ai_flow`, `lawyer_flow` |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-preview` body `{answers}`                                                                                                                                                                                                        | review-step preview and the preview sheet              | `preview_text`/`final_text`, `completion_percent`, `can_generate`, `missing_required_fields[]{key,label}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-generate` body `{answers}`                                                                                                                                                                                                       | create the document                                    | `document_request{id,work_id,title,status,paid,price,mode}`, `file{download_url                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | inline_url,file_name,mime_type,format}`; 402 `detail{code,feature_key,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}`; 422 `detail.missing_required_fields[]` |
| `GET {file.download_url}` (authorised download)                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `Ko'rish` / `Ulashish / saqlash`                       | binary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Plan sheet: `GET /subscription-plans` (+ `GET /platform/policies` overlay), `GET /subscription-plans/{id}/quote?billing_period=…`, `GET /clients/me/entitlements`, `GET /subscriptions/me/features`, `POST /subscription-plans/{id}/telegram-purchase-request` | tariffs that lift the gate, prices, own plans, request | plans `id,is_active,name,monthly price,allowed_billing_periods,entitlement_contract`; quote `amount,base,discount,campaign`; entitlements `active_subscriptions[].plan_id`; features `plan_id,items[]`; request → `work_id (SUB-…)`, `pricing`, `sent`; 422 lists the sellable periods                                        |

- **UX note:** the first screen must show the section chips and the first section's fields with the sticky `Davom etish`; the draft banner and the preview sheet can be secondary. Confusing today: the mode gate shows before the sign-in prompt (a guest may see a "plan" notice), and the review step repeats the whole preview that the eye icon also opens.

### `/services/[id]/ai` — `AI bilan hujjat` (header = the service's document title; `AI bilan hujjat` is the fallback)

- **Who:** signed-in client (guest → the same `LoginGate` as above). No document-service mode gate here; the plan decides through 402s (schema / questions / generate → plan sheet). A service whose schema has no `ai_flow` shows an empty state instead.
- **Purpose:** describe my situation, answer the questions the server asks, and let the AI build the document from the service's original template.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Header: back, title, subtitle `AI yordamida tayyorlash` (after generation `Hujjat tayyor`). No header icons.
  2. **Need step** (`NeedStep`): tinted card h4 `Qanday ishlaydi` + `Vaziyatni yozing. Server savollar beradi, siz javob berasiz, AI esa asl shablon asosida hujjatni tayyorlaydi.` + small soft button `Bo'sh shablonni ko'rish` (in-app preview of the clean DOCX `GET /services/{id}/document-template/clean-source-file`); multiline input `Hujjat nima uchun kerak?` placeholder `Masalan: ish beruvchi 3 oy ish haqini bermadi, sudga ariza yozmoqchiman`, hint `Kamida 10 ta belgi. Qancha aniq yozsangiz, hujjat shuncha yaxshi chiqadi.`; SelectField `Hujjat tili` (`O'zbekcha (lotin)` uz-latn · `O'zbekcha (kirill)` uz-cyrl · `Ruscha` ru; default uz-latn, ru when the app is in Russian); multiline input `Qo'shimcha ko'rsatmalar (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Uslub, talablar, e'tibor berish kerak bo'lgan jihatlar`. Footer: full-width gradient `Savollarni olish` (zap icon; disabled under 10 characters; spinner while loading).
  3. **Questions step** (`AiQuestions`, after `POST …/document-ai/questions`): the same `SectionTabs` + `SectionForm` as the manual fill, with the template's sections plus the AI's own questions in a trailing section `AI uchun ma'lumotlar`. Non-strict: required fields are not enforced (the AI fills them), only formats (email/phone/date/pattern/length). Footer: `Orqaga` (on the first section returns to the need step) + `Davom etish`.
  4. **Review** (last chip `Tekshirish`): while generating a card with spinner `AI hujjatni tayyorlamoqda…`; h3 `Tayyor`; tinted card `Javob berilmagan maydonlarni AI o'zi to'ldiradi. Hujjat serverda asl shablon asosida yaratiladi.`; `ReviewSummary` (format problems box `Tuzatish kerak: {n} ta maydon` + per-section values, `to'ldirilmagan`). Footer: `Orqaga` + gradient `Hujjatni tayyorlash` (zap).
  5. **Result**: `ResultView` exactly as the manual flow (`Hujjat tayyor`, work id, format, `To'langan`/`Bepul`, `Ko'rish`, `Ulashish / saqlash`, `Mening hujjatlarim`, `Boshqa hujjat tanlash`).
  6. `PlanGateSheet` over any 402.
- **Actions:** `Bo'sh shablonni ko'rish` → file preview; `Savollarni olish` → `POST /services/{id}/document-ai/questions {need, language}`; chips/`Davom etish`/`Orqaga` → wizard; `Hujjatni tayyorlash` → `POST /services/{id}/document-ai/generate {need, answers, language, extra_instructions?}`; result buttons as above. Local draft (key `user:ai:serviceId`) keeps `need` and the answers (not the fetched sections: reopening restores the need text and restarts at the need step).
- **States:** guest → LoginGate; schema 404/402/error → as the manual fill (`FlowFailure`); loading → skeletons; no `ai_flow` → empty state (zap-off) `Bu hujjat uchun AI mavjud emas` / `Hujjatni o'zingiz to'ldiring yoki navbatchi advokatga topshiring.`; questions failure → toast `Savollarni olib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` (offline `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`, 429 `Juda ko'p urinish…`, 402 → plan sheet, else server `detail`); generate failure → toast `AI hujjatni tayyorlay olmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` (same mapping); share failure as above.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                    | Used for                    | Key fields                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /services/{id}/document-fields`                                                        | title + `ai_flow` check     | `title`, `ai_flow`, sections (not shown on the need step)                                                                   |
| `GET /services/{id}/document-template/clean-source-file`                                    | `Bo'sh shablonni ko'rish`   | DOCX binary                                                                                                                 |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-ai/questions` `{need, language}`                              | the questions               | `sections[]{id,title,order,fields[]}`, `questions[]` (flat, go to `AI uchun ma'lumotlar`), `note`                           |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-ai/generate` `{need, answers, language, extra_instructions?}` | the document                | `document_request{id,work_id,title,status,paid,price,mode}`, `file{download_url,file_name,mime_type,format}`; 402 plan gate |
| `GET {file.download_url}`                                                                   | view / share                | binary                                                                                                                      |
| Plan sheet endpoints                                                                        | as in `/services/[id]/fill` | —                                                                                                                           |

- **UX note:** the need textarea and the `Savollarni olish` button are the whole first screen — keep the "how it works" card short or collapsible. Confusing today: there is no step indicator (subtitle stays `AI yordamida tayyorlash`), and `Orqaga` on the first question section silently drops the fetched questions.

### `/templates/[id]/fill` — `Hujjatni o'zim to'ldiraman` (header = template `name`; fallback the render's title, then `Hujjatni o'zim to'ldiraman`)

- **Who:** signed-in client; manual-mode gate first (same `FlowGate`), then `LoginGate`; plan via 402 (plan sheet). Query `?request=<document_request_id>` resumes one specific request (used by the lawyer flow's "Ha, o'zim to'ldiraman").
- **Purpose:** fill a ready legal template that has no linked service through my own document request, saved to the server as I type.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** the same `FillFlow` as `/services/[id]/fill` with these differences:
  1. Title = template name; the schema is the template's sections from `POST /document-templates/{id}/render` (empty answers), or — when the render has no sections — built from the request's `questionnaire` (fields grouped by `section`, nameless ones under `Qo'shimcha ma'lumotlar`).
  2. `SaveStatus` row (right-aligned, above the chips) for the server autosave: `Saqlanmoqda…` (loader) / `Saqlandi` (check) / red `Saqlanmadi — qayta urinish` (tappable, refresh icon). Full answers are `PUT` ~800 ms after the last change, once more on leaving and when the app goes to the background; never two saves at once.
  3. Answers = the server's `answers` for the schema's fields with the newer local draft (key `user:tpl:templateId`, which also remembers `requestId`) on top.
  4. If the request already has a ready file (`status` file_ready/rated or `contract_file.status = ready`) the page opens straight on `ResultView` (file `GET /document-requests/{id}/file`).
  5. Preview sheet / review preview come from `POST /document-requests/{id}/preview`; `Hujjatni yaratish` first `PUT`s the full answers, then `POST …/generate`; after a successful generate the autosaver stops and the local draft is dropped. `Mening hujjatlarim` → `/documents/{id}`.
- **Actions:** as the service fill; plus the request resolution on open: `?request=` → `GET /document-requests/{id}`; else the draft's `requestId`; a request that answers 403/404/410 (or none) → `POST /document-requests` (started at most once; its id is written into the draft at once so leaving and returning resumes it).
- **States:** gate off/locked → mode notice (as above); guest → LoginGate; template detail error (and nothing cached) → `FlowFailure` (note: its 404 copy is the service wording `Bu xizmatda hujjat shabloni yo'q` / `Bu xizmat buyurtma orqali bajariladi — xizmat kartasidan buyurtma bering.`); loading template/render/drafts/request → skeletons; request create/read failure → `FlowFailure` with `Qayta urinish`; save failure → `Saqlanmadi — qayta urinish` (typed text kept); generate: 402 → plan sheet, document refusal → toast, 422 missing → jump + `Server {n} ta majburiy maydon yetishmasligini bildirdi.`, 409 (nothing to build) / other → server `detail` or `Hujjat yaratilmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                          | Used for                                                       | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /document-service-settings/runtime`                                                                                          | manual-mode gate                                               | `access.manual`, `settings.modes`                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /document-templates/{id}`                                                                                                    | title, category, language                                      | `name`, `category`, `language`, `template_text`                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /document-templates/{id}/render` `{answers:{}}` (public)                                                                    | section schema + reader preview                                | `sections[]`, `fields[]`, `template_id`, `language`, `preview_text`, `completion_percent`                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /document-requests/{id}`                                                                                                     | resume a request                                               | `id`, `work_id`, `title`, `status`, `questionnaire[]{name,label,type,required,placeholder,options,order,section,section_order}`, `answers{}`, `paid`, `requires_payment`, `price`, `mode`, `contract_file{status,file_name,mime_type}`, `template_id` |
| `POST /document-requests` `{template_id, document_type:"document", title, questionnaire:[], answers:{}, price:0, currency:"UZS"}` | start a request                                                | same shape → `id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PUT /document-requests/{id}/answers` `{answers}`                                                                                 | server autosave (full answers)                                 | request                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `POST /document-requests/{id}/preview` `{answers}`                                                                                | preview                                                        | `preview_text`, `completion_percent`, `can_generate`, `missing_required_fields[]{name,label}`                                                                                                                                                         |
| `POST /document-requests/{id}/generate`                                                                                           | build the file (402 unpaid, 409 nothing to build, 422 missing) | request with `contract_file`                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /document-requests/{id}/file` (token)                                                                                        | view / share                                                   | binary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Plan sheet endpoints                                                                                                              | 402                                                            | as above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

- **UX note:** phone-first the save status and the section chips must sit together under the header; the draft banner can go. Confusing today: a 404 on the template shows service-flavoured copy, and nothing tells the person which document request they are editing (the work id appears only on the result card).

### `/services/[id]/lawyer` — `Navbatchi advokat bilan`

- **Who:** `RoleGate roles=["any"]`: a guest is redirected straight to `/auth/login` (no gate screen); any signed-in role passes. Document-service **lawyer mode** gate (`access.lawyer` / `settings.modes`) shows the mode notice instead of the form; a 402 on send shows the plan card. A service whose schema has no `lawyer_flow` shows an empty state. Duplicate rule: a request already active on the same template blocks the form.
- **Purpose:** hand this catalogue document to the duty-advocate pool (no advocate is chosen) with my situation, files and voice notes.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`FormFrame`: scrolling body + sticky footer that rises above the keyboard):
  1. Header: back, title `Navbatchi advokat bilan`.
  2. `DutyHero` gradient card: briefcase icon, h3 `Navbatchi advokat bilan`, sub = the service's document `title`, progress bar + `{done}/{total}` of required parts (total 2: request text ≥ 10 chars, consent; 3 for `review` with the main file). Bar eases to the new share as each part is met.
  3. _(kind `review` only)_ `MainFilePicker` card (before the steps): upload icon, `Tekshiriladigan hujjatni yuklang`, caption `{types} · {mb} MB gacha` (formats = settings `allowed_formats` as `PDF, DOCX…`, else the phone list `PDF, DOCX, TXT, XLSX, JPG, PNG`; mb = `max_file_mb` else 25); after a pick: file name + `Boshqa fayl tanlash uchun bosing`; refused submit → red border + `Tekshiriladigan hujjatni yuklang.`
  4. Step card `1` `Hujjat turi` — only when `GET /document-services/request-document-types` lists items and applies to this flow (`template_lawyer_assisted` for a service, `custom_from_scratch` for custom): a sideways row of chips (backend `items`), chip `Boshqa — o'zim yozaman` (edit icon, when `custom_allowed`) → input `Hujjat turini yozing` (max 120), caption `Ixtiyoriy — tanlasangiz, advokat nima kerakligini oldindan biladi.` (optional) / `Advokat nima tayyorlashini biladi.`. For `custom`/`review`-less kinds that are not a service or template the same step also carries `Hujjat nomi (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Masalan: Ijara shartnomasi` (max 120). Not shown for `review`; for a service only when the backend offers types. Numbering continues from whatever step is shown.
  5. Step `So'rov matni` (required): multiline placeholder `Vaziyatingizni qisqa yozing: qaysi hujjat kerak, kimga qarshi, qanday holat bo'lgan, qaysi ma'lumotlar muhim. Navbatchi advokat ishni tezroq boshlaydi.`; counter `{n}/5000`; validation 10–5000 chars (`Bu maydonni to'ldiring` · `Kamida 10 ta belgi kiriting` · `Eng ko'pi bilan 5000 ta belgi`); input `Qo'shimcha izoh` placeholder `Ixtiyoriy — qo'shimcha ma'lumot bo'lsa yozing.` (max 2000; appended to the request text as `\n\nQo'shimcha izoh: …`). Step number turns into a green tick at 10 chars; a refused submit outlines it red and scrolls to it.
  6. Step `Qo'shimcha fayl va ovozli xabar` (not for `template`): three `AttachTile`s `Fayl` (paperclip, document picker, multiple), `Rasm` (image, photo picker, multiple), `Ovozli xabar` (mic, spinner while starting); caption `{types} · har biri {mb} MB gacha` or a red rule error: `Bu fayl turi qabul qilinmaydi. Ruxsat etilgan: {types}.` / `Fayl {mb} MB dan katta bo'lmasin.` / `Ko'pi bilan 10 ta fayl biriktirish mumkin.`; limits 10 files, 3 voice notes (tiles disable when full). `AttachmentList`: file rows (file/image icon, name, size `KB`/`MB`, x `O'chirish`), voice rows `Ovozli xabar {n}` with play/pause, progress line, `m:ss`, x. Live recorder (`VoiceLive`, red card replacing the tiles): `Tayyorlanmoqda…` → `Yozilmoqda` with live dot, timer `m:ss / 5:00`, level bars, progress line, buttons `Bekor qilish` (discard) and round red `To'xtatish` (disabled until recording really started); caption `Gapiring, tugatgach to'xtating`; stops by itself at 5:00; m4a `audio/mp4`; permission refused → red `Mikrofonga ruxsat berilmadi. Sozlamalarda yoqing.` (tap opens Settings). Step ticks when at least one attachment exists.
  7. Step `Hujjat tili`: field with globe icon showing the current language (`O'zbekcha (lotin)` default; `Ruscha` when the app is Russian) → bottom sheet `Hujjat tili` with radio rows `O'zbekcha (lotin)` / `O'zbekcha (kirill)` / `Ruscha` (closes on pick).
  8. `ConsentRow` pledge card: shield icon, h4 `Advokat bilan aloqa uchun rozilik`, toggle; text (2 lines, `Matnni o'qish` / `Yig'ish`): `Advokat shu hujjat bo'yicha men bilan bog'lanishiga, telefon qo'ng'iroqlari va onlayn uchrashuvlar o'tkazishiga hamda hujjat uchun zarur ma'lumotlarni men bilan muhokama qilishiga roziman.`; opened hint `Rozilik faqat shu so'rov doirasida amal qiladi va hujjat tayyor bo'lgandan keyin tugaydi.`; on → green card, `Rozilik berildi`; refused submit → red, shakes, scrolls into view, `So'rovni yuborish uchun roziligingiz kerak — tugmani yoqing.`
  9. Sticky footer: `So'rovni yuborish` (lg, send icon; spinner while sending; for `template` disabled until the template title has loaded).
  10. `CtorPrompt` bottom sheet (after a successful send, only when the backend's `constructor_action.available && prompt_required`, once per request): title (backend `title` or `Ishingiz Navbatchi advokatga berildi`), text (backend `message` or `Konstruktor orqali hujjatni o'zingiz ham to'ldirishni davom ettirasizmi?`), `Ha, o'zim to'ldiraman` → `/templates/{templateId}/fill?request={id}` (replace), `Yo'q, advokatni kutaman` → `/documents/{id}`.
- **Submit gate (walked top to bottom on every press):** main file (review) → request text (react-hook-form messages) → consent; the first missing one is outlined and scrolled to. Payload: `need` (+ note), `language`, `requested_document_type` (if chosen), `answers = {contact_consent: true, contact_consent_text: <consent text>}` (service: as `answers_json`), `main_file`, `files[]`, `voice_files[]`; `title` only for custom/review. JSON when nothing is attached, multipart otherwise.
- **Outcomes (replace the form):**
  - Created → `SentCard` (ok, check): `So'rovingiz yuborildi` / backend `message` or `Navbatchi advokatlar ko'rib chiqadi. Kimdir ishni olishi bilan xabar beramiz.`; with `payment_required` → clock/warn `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda` / `So'rov hali advokatlarga yuborilmadi. To'lov tasdiqlangach advokatlar navbatiga tushadi.` + `{amount} so'm · {n} sahifa`; `work_id`; buttons `Hujjatni ochish` (→ `/documents/{id}`, replace) and `Mening hujjatlarim` (→ `/documents`).
  - 409 `document_lawyer_request_already_open` (or `already_exists` on 2xx, or the pre-check from the light list) → `ExistingCard` (info): `So'rov allaqachon yuborilgan` / server message or `Bu hujjat bo'yicha Navbatchi advokatga so'rov yuborilgan. Ish yakunlanmaguncha yangi so'rov yubora olmaysiz.`; work id + `StatusBadge` (statuses: questionnaire `To'ldirilmoqda`, draft `Qoralama`, ready_to_generate `Yaratishga tayyor`, payment_pending/awaiting_payment/pending_payment `To'lov kutilmoqda`, payment_required `Qo'shimcha to'lov kutilmoqda`, lawyer_review_requested `Advokatga yuborildi`, open_pool `Navbatchi advokat kutilmoqda`, claimed `Advokat ishni oldi`, lawyer_review `Advokat tayyorlamoqda`, file_ready `Hujjat tayyor`, rated `Baholangan`, closed `Yopilgan`, payment_cancelled `To'lov bekor qilindi`, payment_rejected `To'lov rad etildi`, cancelled `Bekor qilingan`, rejected `Rad etilgan`); `Mavjud so'rovni ochish`, `Mening hujjatlarim`.
  - 402 → `PlanCard` (lock, warn): `Tarif kerak` / gate text (+ `{used} / {limit} ishlatildi · Yangilanadi: {date}`), or the server sentence, or `Advokat so'rovi uchun mos tarif kerak.`; `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription`, `Formaga qaytish`.
  - document-service refusal code → toast (`Platforma bu xizmatni vaqtincha o'chirgan.` etc.); 422 → toast `So'rov matnini yozing — advokat nimadan boshlashini bilishi kerak.`; other → toast with server/common error text.
- **States:** service schema loading → tall skeleton; schema error → ErrorView + `Qayta urinish`; no `lawyer_flow` → `Advokat so'rovi mavjud emas` / `Bu xizmat uchun advokat so'rovi hozircha qabul qilinmayapti.`; lawyer mode off/locked → `ModeNotice` (`Hozircha o'chirilgan` / `Tarif kerak` + `Tarifni tanlash` / `Hozir mavjud emas` + `Orqaga`); offline during send → toast `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`. Until the settings arrive the phone's own file list and 25 MB apply.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                                                | Used for                                                      | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /document-service-settings/runtime`                                                                                                                | lawyer/review mode gate, file rules                           | `access.lawyer`, `access.review`, `settings.modes`, `settings.allowed_formats[]`, `settings.max_file_mb`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /services/{id}/document-fields`                                                                                                                    | service header, template id, flow check                       | `title`, `template_id`, `lawyer_flow` (non-empty object)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /document-requests` (light list)                                                                                                                   | duplicate rule before the form                                | `items[]{id,work_id,status,template_id,lawyer_request_active,can_send_lawyer_request,lawyer_request_block_reason}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GET /document-services/request-document-types`                                                                                                         | `Hujjat turi` chips (optional; a failure just hides the step) | `items[]`, `optional`, `custom_allowed`, `applies_to[]`, `not_applies_to[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-lawyer/request` `{need, answers, language, requested_document_type?}`                                                     | send without attachments                                      | `request`/`document_request`: `id`, `work_id`, `status`, `already_exists`, `payment_required`, `payment_gate{amount,currency,page_count,included_pages,extra_pages,telegram_sent}`, `message`/`detail`, `constructor_action{available,prompt_required,title,message}`; 409 `detail.request{id,work_id,status}`; 402 `detail{code,feature_key,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}` |
| `POST /services/{id}/document-lawyer/request-with-files` (multipart `need,title?,language,answers_json,requested_document_type?,files[],voice_files[]`) | send with files / voice                                       | same                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

- **UX note:** on a phone the hero, the request-text step and the sticky send button are what matters; the type chips, language and consent can stay compact. Confusing today: the consent card sits last and is the most common reason a send is refused; and for a service request the `Ha, o'zim to'ldiraman` path builds `/templates/{templateId}/fill` with no `templateId` (only a template kind has one), so it should not be offered or must route by service.

### `/documents/new` — `0 dan hujjat` (`?kind=custom`, default) / `Hujjatni tekshirtirish` (`?kind=review`)

- **Who / purpose:** same `DutyRequestPage`; a document from scratch, or my own finished document for the duty advocate to check. Guest → `/auth/login`.
- **What differs from `/services/[id]/lawyer`:**
  - No service lookup, no `lawyer_flow` empty state, no duplicate pre-check (only the backend's 409 / `already_exists`).
  - **custom:** hero edit-3 icon `0 dan hujjat tayyorlash` / `Vaziyatingizni yozing — navbatchi advokat hujjatni boshidan yozib beradi.`; step 1 always present: type chips when the backend offers them for `custom_from_scratch`, plus `Hujjat nomi (ixtiyoriy)` (`Masalan: Ijara shartnomasi`) sent as `title`; gate = **lawyer** mode; endpoint `POST /document-services/custom-draft/request` (always multipart: `need, title?, language, requested_document_type?, files[], voice_files[]`).
  - **review:** hero clipboard icon `Hujjatimni tekshirtirish` / `O'zingizdagi hujjatni yuklang — navbatchi advokat tekshirib, to'g'irlab beradi.`; `MainFilePicker` required (formats = settings `allowed_formats` when given, else the phone list; progress total 3); no type/title step; gate = **review** mode (`access.review`); endpoint `POST /document-services/review-existing/request` (multipart with `main_file` + optional `files[]`, `voice_files[]`, `page_count` when known).
  - Everything else (text step, attachments, language, consent, outcomes, `CtorPrompt`) is identical. Data: `GET /document-service-settings/runtime`, `GET /document-services/request-document-types`, the one POST above (response shape as in the service entry).

### `/templates/[id]/lawyer` — `Advokat bilan tayyorlash` (kind `template`)

- **Who / purpose:** same page for a ready template that has **no linked service**: ask the duty advocate to prepare it; the request is a document request sent to the pool through `…/lawyer-review`. Guest → `/auth/login`.
- **What differs:**
  - Loads `GET /document-templates/{id}` first (tall skeleton while loading); hero headphones icon `Advokat bilan tayyorlash`, sub = template `name`; send button disabled until the name is in.
  - No title field (the template's own name is the title); **no attachments step** (the endpoint is JSON only); under the request text an extra guide line `Hujjatni tayyorlash uchun zarur bo‘ladigan shaxsiy ma’lumotlar, kompaniya rekvizitlari, hudud, davlat idoralari va boshqa tegishli ma’lumotlarni yozishingiz yoki fayl ko‘rinishida ilova qilishingiz mumkin.`; type step only if the backend offers types for `template_lawyer_assisted`; progress total 2.
  - Duplicate rule: the light list (same `template_id`, `lawyer_request_active && !can_send_lawyer_request`) **or** the constructor draft's own request (`GET /document-requests/{draft.requestId}` flags) → `ExistingCard` instead of the form.
  - Send = (1) reuse the draft's request id (or one started earlier this session), else `POST /document-requests {template_id, document_type:"document", title, questionnaire:[], answers:{}, price:0, currency:"UZS"}`; (2) `PUT /document-requests/{id}/answers` with the constructor draft's non-empty answers (skipped when empty, so the server's answers are never wiped); (3) `POST /document-requests/{id}/lawyer-review {need, requested_document_type?, page_count: 0, urgency: "normal", language}`. The request is created at most once even on a retry. Consent is not written into `answers` for this kind.
  - `CtorPrompt` `Ha, o'zim to'ldiraman` → `/templates/{id}/fill?request={docId}` (the same request, resumed in the constructor). Gate = **lawyer** mode.

### `/doc-analysis` — `Hujjat tahlili`

- **Who:** open to everyone; a guest sees the subtitle, then `Hujjat tahlili uchun tizimga kiring.` + gradient `Kirish` (→ `/auth/login`). Document-service **analysis** mode gate (`access.analysis` / `settings.modes`) replaces the form with the mode notice (checked before the sign-in check; a result already on screen stays). Plan: `FeatureUsage` counter for `ai_document_analysis` and the plan sheet on any 402 (analysis or advocate handoff).
- **Purpose:** upload a document (or paste its text), get the AI's risks and advice, and optionally send the same file to the duty advocate.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `Header` (back) `Hujjat tahlili`; grey line `Hujjatni yuklang — AI xato va xavflarni topadi.`
  2. **Input stage** (`AnalysisInput`):
     - `FeatureUsage` card (only when `GET /subscriptions/me/features` lists `ai_document_analysis` and it is not unlimited): label `AI hujjat tahlili`, `{used} / {limit} ishlatildi` (or `Mavjud emas` when locked), bar (red when full), `{n} ta qoldi · Yangilanadi: {date}`; when locked/used up a soft `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription`.
     - `PickArea` (dashed accent card): upload-cloud, h4 `Fayl tanlash`, hint `{types} · {mb} MB gacha` (defaults `PDF, DOC, DOCX, TXT · 20 MB`; settings `allowed_formats`/`max_file_mb` override). After a pick → `FileCard`: file icon, name, size, x `Olib tashlash`.
     - (no file) `yoki matnni joylang` + multiline `Hujjat matnini shu yerga joylang…`, hint `Kamida 20 ta belgi.`
     - red error line when set (`Fayl {mb} MB dan katta.` / `Faqat {types} fayl.` / run errors below).
     - `OptionsPanel`: input `Sahifalar soni` (digits; default = ceil(text chars / 2500), 1..500; error `Sahifalar soni 1 dan 500 gacha bo'lishi kerak.`), switches `Skanerlangan hujjat (OCR)`, `Shoshilinch`, `Advokat tekshiruvi` (only while the review mode is not switched off), `Yozma xulosa`; warn `Dastlabki {n} sahifa asosiy narxga kiradi, undan keyingi har bir sahifa alohida hisoblanadi.` when pages > included (10, or settings `included_pages`).
     - `QuotePanel`: line button `Narxni hisoblash` (`Hisoblanmoqda…`; disabled when pages invalid) → `FactRows`: `Sun'iy intellekt tahlili ({n} sahifa)` = `Obunaga kiradi` (when `ai_included_in_subscription`) or `Tahlil ({n} sahifa)` = sum; `Narxga kiritilgan: {n} sahifa` = `+{n} qo'shimcha sahifa` / `Qo'shimcha sahifa yo'q`; `Skanerni o'qish (OCR)`, `Advokat tekshiruvi`, `Shoshilinchlik`, `Yozma xulosa` (only non-zero, `… so'm`); `Jami` bold accent. Error: refusal text or `Narxni hisoblab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.` A quote is "fresh" only while the options match it.
     - `RuleCard` (tinted, from the rules quote): `Advokat tekshiruvi narxi`, `Server qoidasi: {n} betgacha bazaviy narx, undan ortiq betlar uchun qo'shimcha to'lov.`, rows `{a}–{b} bet` = `… so'm`, `Har bir qo'shimcha bet` + `… so'm`, `Shoshilinch ish` + `{n}%`.
     - while running: three skeletons.
     - gradient lg `Tahlil qilish` (`Tahlil qilinmoqda…`; enabled only when the mode is open, a file or ≥ 20 chars is there, pages valid, not running). Run = quote first if not fresh (a failed quote never blocks), then analyse.
     - caption `Sahifalar sonini server hisoblaydi — fayl yuklangach aniq chiqadi.`
  3. **Result stage** (`status = done`):
     - `AnalysisResult`: pills — file name (file only), `{n} bet · PDF betlari` / `{n} bet · matn bo'yicha taxmin` (by `page_count_source`), `AI tahlili tarifga kiradi` / `AI tahlili pullik` (from `pricing`); tinted card `Qisqacha` + `summary` (or `Qisqacha ma'lumot yo'q`); row `Xatarlar` + `Yuqori {h} · O'rta {m} · Past {l}`; `RiskRow`s sorted high→low with pills `Yuqori` / `O'rta` / `Past` and the text, or `Xatar topilmadi.`; `Tavsiyalar` with check rows; `To'liq tahlilni ko'rsatish` / `Yashirish` toggling a card with `analysis_text` (markdown marks stripped).
     - `UpsellCard` (only when `upsell_offer.available`): shield, title (backend or `Advokat tomonidan tekshiruv`), reason (or `Sun'iy intellekt tahlilidan keyin hujjatni mutaxassis ko'rib chiqishi mumkin.`), `Advokat tekshiruvi: {price} so'm` when the fresh quote prices it, the quote rows when the review option is on, `Advokat tekshiruvini qo'shish` (turns the option on and re-quotes; only while the review mode is open and the option is off), line `Advokat tanlash` → `/lawyers`.
     - `HandoffCard` (only for an analysed **file** with `page_count > 0` and the review mode not switched off): h4 `Advokat ko'rigi`, `Navbatchi advokat hujjatni tekshirib, yozma xulosa beradi. Narx server hisoblagan sahifalar soniga qarab belgilanadi.`; if the review mode is locked by the plan → inline notice (`Tarif kerak` + code text + `Tarifni tanlash`); else `QuoteRows` (`Sahifalar`, `Bazaviy narxga {n} bet kiradi` = `+{n} qo'shimcha bet` / `qo'shimcha bet yo'q`, `Advokat tekshiruvi`, `Shoshilinch ustama` `+ …`, `Jami`) or `Narxni hisoblab bo'lmadi.` or skeletons; switch `Shoshilinch` hint `Narxga ustama qo'shiladi`; input `Advokatga izoh` placeholder `Sun'iy intellekt tahlil qilgan hujjatni advokat tekshirib chiqsin.` (sent as `need` when empty); gradient `Advokatga yuborish` (disabled without a quote).
     - after sending → `HandoffStatus` card (replaces the handoff card, updates itself): kind **sent** (check, green) `Navbatchi advokatga yuborildi` / `So'rov advokatlar navbatiga tushdi. Advokat ishni olgach, sizga xabar beramiz.`; **wait** (clock, warn) `Telegram tasdig'i kutilmoqda` / `To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanmaguncha ish advokatga yuborilmaydi. Holat o'zi yangilanadi.` with pills `{amount} so'm`, `{n} bet`, `+{n} qo'shimcha bet`, red `Telegramga yuborilmadi` when `telegram_sent` is false; **stopped** (x, red) `To'lov tasdiqlanmadi` / `So'rov bekor qilindi. Kerak bo'lsa, qaytadan yuboring.`; **already** `So'rov allaqachon yuborilgan` / server message or `Bu hujjat bo'yicha advokat so'rovi mavjud. U yakunlangach yangisini yuborish mumkin.`; `work_id` pill. Phase: `payment_required`/`pending_payment` (or `awaiting_payment` + `payment_required`) = wait; `payment_cancelled`/`payment_rejected` or gate `rejected`/`cancelled` = stopped; else sent.
     - line `Yangi hujjat` (refresh icon) → reset to the input stage.
  4. `PlanGateSheet` over a 402 from the analysis or the handoff (contents as in `/services/[id]/fill`).
- **Actions:** `Fayl tanlash` → document picker (types/extensions from the rule); x → remove file; `Narxni hisoblash` → `POST /ai/document-analysis/quote`; `Tahlil qilish` → `POST /ai/document-analysis/file` (multipart `file`) or `POST /ai/document-analysis {text}`; `Advokat tekshiruvini qo'shish` → re-quote with `lawyer_review: true`; `Advokat tanlash` → `/lawyers`; `Advokatga yuborish` → `POST /document-services/review-existing/request` (multipart `need, language, urgency, page_count, main_file`); `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription`; `Kirish` → `/auth/login`; `Yangi hujjat` → reset.
- **States:** analysis mode off → `Hozircha o'chirilgan` / `Platforma bu xizmatni vaqtincha o'chirgan.`; locked → `Tarif kerak` + `Tarifni tanlash` (or `Hozir mavjud emas` / `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`); guest → login prompt; pick errors → `Fayl {mb} MB dan katta.` / `Faqat {types} fayl.`; run errors: 402 → plan sheet; document refusal code → its text; file 413/415/422 → server `detail` or `Fayldan matnni o'qib bo'lmadi.`; offline → `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`; 429 → `Juda ko'p urinish…`; else `Tahlil qilib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; quote error → `Narxni hisoblab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; advocate quote error → `Narxni hisoblab bo'lmadi.`; handoff error → toast server `detail` or `Yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.` (402 → plan sheet, refusal → toast). Realtime: while the handoff waits for Telegram approval the request is polled every 20 s and refreshed on user-socket events `document_request.*`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                       | Used for                                         | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /document-service-settings/runtime`                                                                       | analysis/review gates, file rule, included pages | `access.analysis`, `access.review`, `settings.modes`, `settings.allowed_formats`, `settings.max_file_mb`, `settings.included_pages`                                                                              |
