| `GET /subscriptions/me/features`                                                                               | `FeatureUsage` counter                           | `items[]{feature_key:"ai_document_analysis",mode,allowed,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}`                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /ai/document-analysis/quote` `{page_count, ocr, lawyer_review, urgency:"normal"                          | "urgent", written_opinion}`                      | price for the options; price rules (page_count 1, lawyer_review true); advocate quote for the measured pages                                                                                                     | `page_count`, `included_pages`, `extra_pages`, `currency`, `base_amount`, `ocr_amount`, `lawyer_review_amount`, `urgency_amount`, `written_opinion_amount`, `total_amount`, `payment_required`, `ai_included_in_subscription`, `pricing_range.amount`, `pricing_rule{review_fee_ranges[]{min_pages,max_pages,amount},extra_page_amount,urgent_percent,written_opinion_amount}` |
| `POST /ai/document-analysis` `{text}`                                                                          | analyse pasted text                              | `summary`, `analysis_text`, `risks[]{level,text                                                                                                                                                                  | risk}`, `recommendations[]`, `points_count`, `page_count`, `page_count_source` (`pdf_pages`/`text_estimate`), `file_name`, `pricing{…quote}`, `lawyer_review_pricing{…}`, `upsell_offer{available,title,reason}`, `requires_extra_payment`; 402 plan gate                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /ai/document-analysis/file` (multipart `file`)                                                           | analyse a file (413/415/422 unreadable)          | same                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /document-services/review-existing/request` (multipart `need, language, urgency, page_count, main_file`) | advocate handoff                                 | `request`/`document_request`: `id`, `work_id`, `status`, `payment_required`, `already_exists`, `payment_gate{id,status,amount,currency,page_count,included_pages,extra_pages,telegram_sent}`, `message`/`detail` |
| `GET /document-requests/{id}` (poll 20 s while waiting; on socket `document_request.*`)                        | live handoff status                              | `id`, `work_id`, `status`, `payment_required`, `payment_gate{…}`                                                                                                                                                 |
| user socket events `document_request.*`                                                                        | refresh the handoff status                       | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Plan sheet endpoints                                                                                           | 402                                              | as in `/services/[id]/fill`                                                                                                                                                                                      |

- **UX note:** phone-first: the pick area, the text fallback and `Tahlil qilish` must fit the first screen; the options, quote and price-rule card are long and could collapse behind `Narxni hisoblash`. Confusing today: two different price tables (the quote rows before the run and `QuoteRows` under the result) use different wording for the same numbers, and the result page stacks three cards (upsell, handoff, status) about the same advocate review.

### `/documents` — `Mening hujjatlarim`

- **Who:** signed-in user of any role (`RoleGate roles=["any"]`); a guest is redirected to `/auth/login` before anything renders. No plan gate on the list itself. The "new document" menu is gated by the document-service runtime settings: a mode switched off by the platform is dropped from the menu, a mode outside the account's plan stays with a lock icon (its own page then explains why).
- **Purpose:** every document the client has started — filled in by hand, with AI or by an advocate — with its live status, grouped by whether the ball is with the client, searchable, and openable into a detail sheet or the full page.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Header (AdminPage): back button · title `Mening hujjatlarim` · subtitle `Barcha hujjatlaringizni bir joyda boshqaring` while counts load, then `Hujjatlar: {n}` (n = size of the light list) · right: round "+" button labelled `Yangi hujjat` → opens the "new" action list.
  2. Mode chip bar (single-select, horizontal): `Barchasi · n`, `O'zim to'ldirganlar · n`, `AI bilan · n`, `Advokat bilan · n` (counts from the light list; no count while it loads). Mode is sent to the server (`?mode=`) when no other filter is on.
  3. Scope pills (DocScope, horizontal, 44 px, selected one filled with the brand gradient): `Jami hujjatlar`, `Sizdan kutilmoqda` (carries a warn dot while its count > 0 and it is not selected), `Advokatda`, `Tayyor`. Each shows a count badge (skeleton while loading). Tapping the selected pill deselects it.
  4. Search row: SearchBox placeholder `Hujjat nomi yoki ish raqami…` (searches title, work id, requested type, status label; debounced 200 ms, max 200 chars) + sliders icon button `Filtr` with a dot badge while a mode or an exact status is set.
  5. Applied-filter chips (only when a mode or exact status is set): mode short name `Qo'lda` / `AI bilan` / `Advokat`, status label; each has an "x" and removes itself.
  6. List body (FlatList, 10 rows a page). When at least one document waits on the client the rows are split by two section heads: `Sizdan harakat kutilmoqda` (warn dot, count badge, hint line `To'lov yoki ma'lumot sizda turibdi — shulardan boshlang.`) then `Qolgan hujjatlar` (gray dot, count). Without such documents there are no heads.
  7. Card (DocListItem): 44 px tile with the mode icon (edit-3 / cpu / briefcase) tinted by status tone · title, 2 lines (`Nomsiz hujjat` when empty) · status pill (pulsing "live" while draft/payment/review) · meta line `{date} · {at}/{total} qadam` (step position among the steps this document really has) · chevron. Under it at most ONE quick-action button chosen in this order: `Chat` (chat room exists and file not ready) → `Yuklab olish` (file ready and format allowed by download policy) → `Davom etish` (`constructor_action.available`; with `prompt_required` it first opens CtorSheet) → `Chat` → `Advokatga` (mode manual, not closed/cancelled/rejected, not waiting for payment, `can_send_lawyer_request`, review mode not switched off).
  8. Footer under the last card: counter `{a} / {b}` and, while there is more, soft button `Yana {n} ta` (n = min(10, remaining)); scroll-end also loads the next ten.
  - **Sheet — filters** (`Filtrlar`, snaps 0.84/0.94): block `Qanday tayyorlangan` as four icon cards (`Barchasi`, `O'zim to'ldirganlar`, `AI bilan`, `Advokat bilan`); block `Holat` (only when more than one distinct status exists in the archive): list `Barcha holatlar` + every status seen, each with a tone-coloured dot, count and radio check. Pinned bar: rotate icon button `Filtrlarni tozalash` + gradient `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · {total}`.
  - **Sheet — document detail** (DocDetailSheet, snaps 0.72/0.94, reads the document by id from the live query; the list row paints the head at once): head = tile + title (3 lines) + work id (selectable) + close button `Yopish`; pills: status (large) + mode `Qo'lda`/`AI bilan`/`Advokat`. Then: skeleton while loading / ErrorView; block `Jarayon` with the next-step line by group (`draft` → `Ma'lumotlarni to'ldirib, hujjatni yarating`, `payment` → `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda`, `review` → `Navbatchi advokat ustida ishlamoqda — shu yerda kuzating`, `ready` → `Tayyor hujjatni yuklab oling`, none when closed) and the four-step timeline `Ma'lumot` → `To'lov` → `Advokat` → `Tayyor` (step states: `Bajarildi`, `Hozir shu yerda`, `Keyingi`, `Kerak emas` (dashed, skipped), `To'xtadi` (red), pay step done+paid → `To'landi`); block `Asosiy ma'lumotlar` = two-per-row fact tiles, only facts the backend gave: `Turi`, `Yaratilgan` (date-time), `Yangilangan` (only if > 1 min after created), `To'lov` (`{amount} so'm · ` + `Bepul`/`Xizmat narxi`/`To'langan`/`Kutilmoqda`/`Bekor qilingan`), `Til` (upper-cased), `Sahifalar`, `Versiyalar` (`{n} ta`), `Advokat`, `Baholash muddati` (while the rating window is open); block `Kiritilgan ma'lumotlar` (hidden when the questionnaire has no fields): progress bar + `{n} / {total} to'ldirilgan` or `Hali ma'lumot kiritilmagan`, first 3 answers (label + value cut at 90 chars); AdvocateCard (when an advocate holds it and a name is known); RatingCard (see `/documents/[id]`); the backend's `lawyer_request_block_reason` as a gray caption when a review is not offered. Sticky bar: info line when nothing can be downloaded (`To'lov tasdiqlangach fayl tayyor bo'ladi` / `Advokat tayyorlagach yuklab olasiz` / `Fayl hali tayyor emas` / `Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan`); one contextual button above (`To'lash` gradient → POST payment; `Baholash` → rating sheet; `Advokatga` → review sheet; in that priority); row `Yuklab olish` (download icon, disabled without a file) + `Batafsil` (line) → `/documents/{id}`.
  - **Sheet — send for review** (ReviewSheet `Advokat tekshiruviga yuborish`, sub `Navbatchi advokat hujjatingizni ko'rib chiqadi`): when the review mode is not open → inline ModeNotice (`Hozircha o'chirilgan` / `Tarif kerak` + button `Tarifni tanlash` → `/subscription` / `Hozir mavjud emas`; text = `Platforma bu xizmatni vaqtincha o'chirgan.` / `Buning uchun faol obuna kerak.` / `Joriy tarifingizga bu xizmat kirmaydi.` / `Tarifingizda bu imkoniyat yo'q.` / backend message / `Bu xizmat hozir siz uchun yopiq.`) and submit disabled. Otherwise: document-type chip row `Hujjat turi` (items from the backend, + `Boshqa — o'zim yozaman` with input `Hujjat turini yozing`; hint `Ixtiyoriy — tanlasangiz, advokat nima kerakligini oldindan biladi.` or `Advokat nima tayyorlashini biladi.`; hidden when the backend does not offer the field for flow `constructor_review`), textarea `Nimani tekshirish kerak?` placeholder `Hujjatimni tekshirib bering` (max 2000, optional — the placeholder text is sent when empty), note `Advokat tanlanmaydi — so'rov call-center advokatlar navbatiga tushadi.`, submit `Yuborish`.
  - **Sheet — rating** (RatingSheet `Hujjatni baholang`, snaps 0.62/0.92): five 40 px stars (a11y `{n} yulduz`), hint `Yulduzni tanlang` → `Yomon` / `O'rtacha` / `Yaxshi` / `Juda yaxshi` / `A'lo`; `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Fikringizni yozing` (500); at 1–2 stars an extra field `Nima noto'g'ri bo'ldi?` placeholder `Qisqacha yozing — operator uchun.` hint `Bu baho bilan shikoyat call-centerga boradi va operator ko'rib chiqadi.` (2000); submit `Bahoni yuborish`.
  - **Sheet — constructor prompt** (CtorSheet, from `Davom etish` when `prompt_required`): gradient shield badge, title (backend `constructor_action.title` or `Ishingiz Navbatchi advokatga berildi`), note (backend message or `Konstruktor orqali hujjatni o'zingiz ham to'ldirishni davom ettirasizmi?`), `Ha, to'ldiraman` (gradient, lg) → `/templates/{template_id}/fill?request={id}` (or `/documents/{id}` without a template), `Yo'q, advokatni kutaman` (soft).
  - **Action list — new** (`Yangi hujjat`): `Xizmatlardan hujjat tanlash` → `/services`; `0 dan hujjat (advokat tayyorlaydi)` → `/documents/new?kind=custom` (mode `lawyer`); `Mavjud hujjatni tekshirtirish` → `/documents/new?kind=review` (mode `review`); `Hujjatni AI tahlil qilish` → `/doc-analysis` (mode `analysis`). Off modes are dropped; locked ones show a lock icon instead of their own.
- **Enums (Uzbek labels):**
  - Document mode (`mode`): `manual` = `O'zim to'ldirganlar` (short `Qo'lda`, icon edit-3) · `ai` = `AI bilan` (short `AI bilan`, icon cpu) · `lawyer` = `Advokat bilan` (short `Advokat`, icon briefcase). Anything else normalises to `manual`.
  - Document status (`status`, `dm2.status`), with its group / tone: `questionnaire` `To'ldirilmoqda`, `draft` `Qoralama`, `ready_to_generate` `Yaratishga tayyor` (group draft, warn) · `payment_pending` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `payment_required` `Qo'shimcha to'lov kutilmoqda`, `awaiting_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `pending_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda` (group payment, warn) · `lawyer_review_requested` `Advokatga yuborildi`, `open_pool` `Navbatchi advokat kutilmoqda`, `claimed` `Advokat ishni oldi`, `lawyer_review` `Advokat tayyorlamoqda` (group review, blue) · `file_ready` `Hujjat tayyor`, `rated` `Baholangan` (group ready, green) · `closed` `Yopilgan` (gray) · `payment_cancelled` `To'lov bekor qilindi`, `payment_rejected` `To'lov rad etildi`, `cancelled` `Bekor qilingan`, `rejected` `Rad etilgan` (group closed, red). Unknown statuses fall into "draft" and show the backend's `status_label` (or a tidied slug). Scope buckets: `Sizdan kutilmoqda` = draft + payment; `Advokatda` = review; `Tayyor` = ready.
- **Actions:** card tap → detail sheet; quick action (see 7); `Batafsil` → `/documents/{id}` (sheet closes first, 320 ms hand-off); `To'lash` → POST `/document-requests/{id}/payments` then opens the returned https payment URL (toast `To'lov so'rovi yaratildi. Tasdiqlanishini kuting.` / `To'lov so'rovini yaratib bo'lmadi.`); `Yuklab olish` → downloads with the bearer token and opens the system share sheet (toast `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.` or the document-service refusal text on failure); `Chat` → `/chat/{room_id}`; `Advokatga` → review sheet → POST `…/lawyer-review` (toasts `Hujjat advokat tekshiruviga yuborildi. Navbatchi advokatlar ko'rib chiqadi.` / payment gate `So'rov hali advokatlarga yetmadi — to'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda.` / already open (409 or `already_exists`) `Bu hujjat bo'yicha Navbatchi advokatga so'rov yuborilgan. Ish yakunlanmaguncha yangi so'rov yubora olmaysiz.`); `Baholash` → rating sheet → POST `…/rating` (toasts `Bahoyingiz uchun rahmat` / `Baho yuborildi. Shikoyatingiz call-centerga yuborildi.` when a complaint work came back / 409 `Baholash muddati tugagan` / `Bahoni yuborib bo'lmadi`); `Davom etish` → template fill screen; pull to refresh; scope / mode / status / search each reset the window to the first ten; `Filtrlarni tozalash` clears everything.
- **States:** loading = five 104 px skeleton cards (header still shown); error = ErrorView with `Qayta urinish` (`Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, 404 `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, 403 `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.`, 429 `Juda ko'p urinish. Bir ozdan so'ng qayta urining.`); offline = same view with wifi-off icon and `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.`; empty = `Hozircha hujjat yo'q` / `Xizmat tanlab hujjat to'ldirganingizda yoki advokatga so'rov yuborganingizda, ular shu yerda ko'rinadi.` + `Hujjat tanlash` → `/services`; filtered-empty = `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Boshqa so'z yoki filtrni sinab ko'ring.` + `Filtrlarni tozalash`; detail sheet loading = three skeleton blocks, error = ErrorView inside the sheet. Realtime toasts (only on the focused screen, once per event): `Hujjatingiz tayyor. Yuklab olishingiz mumkin.`, `Advokat ishni oldi`, `Advokat uchrashuv boshladi. Qo'ng'iroqni qabul qiling.`, `So'rov advokatlar navbatiga tushdi`, `To'lov bekor qilindi — so'rov yuborilmadi` (red), `Operator siz uchun hujjat so'rovi yaratdi`, `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                     | Used for                                                                                                        | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/document-requests/service-flow?limit=10&offset=&mode=` | the unfiltered / mode-filtered list, 10 rows a page (rich rows)                                                 | `items[]` (or `data[]`), `total`; per item `document_request{…}` merged with the item: `id`, `work_id`, `title`, `status`, `status_label`, `mode`, `price`, `currency`, `requested_document_type`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `template_id`, `contract_file.status/file_name`, `assigned_lawyer.name`, `rating{available,submitted,deadline_at,remaining_seconds,closed_reason,value}`, `lawyer_request_active`, `can_send_lawyer_request`, `lawyer_request_block_reason`, `constructor_action{available,prompt_required,title,message}`, `payment_required`, `payment_gate{status,…}`, `file{ready,format}`, `next_action`, `meeting`/`active_meeting`/`meeting_status` |
| GET `/document-requests`                                     | the light list: counts of every pill/chip, exact search, status/group filtering on the phone, work ids          | same flat row fields as above (no `file`/`next_action`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}`                                | the detail sheet                                                                                                | row fields + `document_type`, `questionnaire[]`/`fields[]` (`name`,`label`,`type`,`required`,`placeholder`,`options`,`section_title`,`section_order`,`order`), `answers{}`, `paid`, `requires_payment`, `payment_status`, `payment_id`, `payment_gate{status,amount,currency,page_count,included_pages,extra_pages,telegram_sent}`, `page_count`, `contract_file.mime_type`, `updated_at`, `language`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/unlock-policy`                  | can it pay / generate a DOCX, missing fields (sheet, after the document loaded)                                 | `paid`, `requires_payment`, `can_generate`, `amount`, `currency`, `formats[]`, `missing_required_fields[]{name,label}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/versions`                       | the `Versiyalar` count in the facts grid                                                                        | list of `{id, created_at, payload{contract_id, answers}}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/chat`                           | fallback only, when the chat-room list is not in cache: the `Chat` quick action for documents an advocate holds | `available`, `room.id`/`room_id`, `message`; 404 = no chat yet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime`                     | which "new" modes to offer, whether review may be sent, which download formats are allowed                      | `settings{allowed_formats[], max_file_mb, included_pages, modes[], download_policy.formats[]}`, `access{<mode>{allowed, code, message}}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| GET `/document-services/request-document-types`              | the document-type chips of the review sheet                                                                     | `items[]`, `optional`, `custom_allowed`, `applies_to[]`, `not_applies_to[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/file` (or `…/docx`)             | download / share of the finished file (or a DOCX of the current answers when the policy allows)                 | binary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/payments`                      | `To'lash`                                                                                                       | body `{provider:"payme", amount, currency:"UZS", provider_payload:{}}`; reads `payment_url` (or `payment.payment_url` / `invoice.payment_url`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/lawyer-review`                 | `Advokatga` (send for review)                                                                                   | body `{need, requested_document_type?, page_count:0, urgency:"normal"}`; reads `request`/`document_request`, `already_exists`, `payment_required`, `payment_gate`, `message`/`detail`; 409 = already open                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/rating`                        | `Bahoni yuborish`                                                                                               | body `{rating, comment, complaint?}`; reads `quality_complaint.work_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| WS `/ws/users/me?token=`                                     | realtime refresh + toasts                                                                                       | events `document_request.*`, `document_constructor_continue_prompt`, `support.assist_document_request_created`, `document_review_payment_*`, `notification.created`; ids from `document_request_id` / `record_id` / `request_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

- **UX note:** on a phone the scope pills and the first "waiting on you" card must be visible above the fold; the mode chip bar duplicates the filter sheet's mode cards and could be folded into the sheet. The card's single quick action plus a full detail sheet plus a separate full page is three depths for one document — the sheet's `Batafsil` and the page overlap heavily.

### `/documents/[id]` — `Hujjat`

- **Who:** signed-in user of any role (`RoleGate roles=["any"]`, guest → `/auth/login`). Review and download actions respect the document-service settings (review mode off → no review card; closed download format → explanation instead of buttons).
- **Purpose:** one document request end to end: where it stands, pay, open/download the file, fill or read the answers, reach the advocate, send for review, keep versions and rate it.
- **Layout, top → bottom (ScrollView with pull-to-refresh, 16 px padding):**
  1. Header: back · `Hujjat` · subtitle = `work_id`.
  2. Summary card (DocSummary): 46 px mode-icon tile tinted by status · title (4 lines, `Nomsiz hujjat` fallback) · work id (selectable). Inside a tinted status well: status pill (large) + mode pill (`O'zim to'ldirganlar` / `AI bilan` / `Advokat bilan`) · next-step sentence by group (same `dm2.next.*` texts as the sheet; none when closed) · four-step timeline `Ma'lumot` / `To'lov` / `Advokat` / `Tayyor` (hidden when closed without a file). Then fact rows: `Hujjat turi`, `Advokat`, `Narx` (`{amount} so'm` or `Bepul`), `Sahifalar` (if > 0), `Yaratilgan` (date-time). Empty values are omitted.
  3. Payment card (PaymentCard, only when `can.pay` or `can.payWait`): icon clock/credit-card · title `To'lov kerak` or `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda` · amount `{amount} so'm` (gate amount or price) · text `To'lov so'rovini yarating — to'langanini faqat server tasdiqlaydi.` or `To'lov tasdiqlangach hujjat keyingi bosqichga o'tadi. Holat o'zgarsa, xabar beramiz.` · while waiting with a gate: `Hujjat: {n} sahifa · to'lovsiz: {free} · qo'shimcha: {extra}` and `Tasdiqlash so'rovi Telegramga yuborildi.` (green) / `Tasdiqlash so'rovi Telegramga yuborilmadi — qo'llab-quvvatlashga murojaat qiling.` (red) · buttons: `To'lovni boshlash` (when payable) · while waiting: `Holatni yangilash` (soft, refetches) + `To'lovni ko'rish` (line, when `payment_id`) → `/payments/{payment_id}`.
  4. File card (FileActions; hidden when there is nothing at all): title `Tayyor hujjat fayli` (finished file) or `Hujjat nusxasi` (DOCX of the current answers, offered only when `can_generate`, `docx` in `formats`, download policy allows and not waiting for payment) · row `Ochish` (in-app FilePreview) + `Yuklab olish` (share) · caption `Hozirgi ma'lumotlar bilan to'ldirilgan DOCX — yakuniy hujjat emas.` for the draft, or `Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan.` when the finished file's format is closed by policy (no buttons then) · small line button `Bo'sh shablon (DOCX)` when the document came from a template (opens `…/clean-template-file`, named `{title} (bo'sh shablon).docx`).
  5. Answers card (AnswersSection; hidden when no fields, or when not editable and nothing filled): title `Ma'lumotlar` · progress bar · `{n} / {total} to'ldirilgan` · warn `Majburiy maydonlar to'ldirilmagan: {n}` (from unlock-policy, only when editable) · button `Ma'lumotlarni to'ldirish` (nothing filled) / `Ma'lumotlarni tahrirlash` (editable) / `Kiritilgan ma'lumotlarni ko'rish` (read-only). Editable = has fields, not stopped, not delivered/closed, and status in draft group or `constructor_action.available`.
  6. Advocate card (AdvocateCard; when `lawyer_request_active`, mode lawyer, or an assigned lawyer): title `Advokat: {name}` or `Advokat bilan aloqa` · skeleton while the chat room loads · with a room: buttons `Chat` → `/chat/{room_id}` and `Qo'ng'iroq` (soft; POST `…/calls/audio` → `/call/{room_id}?callId=`) + caption `Har bir hujjat uchun 3 martagacha qo'ng'iroq qilish mumkin.` or `Yana {n} marta qo'ng'iroq qilishingiz mumkin` after a call · without a room: backend `message` or `Advokat ishni olgach, shu yerda u bilan yozishasiz.` Errors: 429 → `Bu hujjat bo'yicha qo'ng'iroq limiti tugadi (3 marta). Chat orqali yozing.`, else backend detail or `Qo'ng'iroqni boshlab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`
  7. Review card (ReviewSection; when review is allowed: review mode on, `can_send_lawyer_request`, has `template_id`, not stopped, not waiting for payment): briefcase icon · `Advokat tekshiruvi` · `Tayyor yoki to'ldirilayotgan hujjatni navbatchi advokatga yuboring — u tekshirib chiqadi.` · button `Advokatga yuborish` → ReviewSheet (same sheet as the list page). When not allowed but an advocate request is active, the backend's `lawyer_request_block_reason` shows as a gray caption instead.
  8. Versions row (VersionsRow; hidden when no versions and none can be created): clock icon · `Versiyalar` · `{n} ta saqlangan versiya` / `Hali versiya yo'q` → VersionsSheet.
  9. Rating card (RatingCard; only when the window is open, a rating was given, or a window existed): star icon · title `Hujjatni baholang` (open) / `Bahoyingiz` (given) / `Baholash muddati tugagan` (closed) · countdown `{m:ss} qoldi` (red under 2 min; counts from `remaining_seconds`, else `deadline_at`) · given: five stars + comment (2 lines) · button `Baholash` (open) → RatingSheet · link `Shikoyat qoldirish` → `/rate/{work_id}` (when a work id exists and the window is open or rated).
  - **Sheet — answers** (AnswersSheet, FormSheet `Hujjat ma'lumotlari`, subtitle `{n} / {total} to'ldirilgan`, snaps 0.82/0.95): when editable, a tinted preview card first: `Hujjat qanday ko'rinishini ko'rish` / `Ko'rinishni yangilash` (POST `…/preview`), then progress bar, `{n}% to'ldirilgan`, warn `To'ldirilmagan majburiy maydonlar: {list}`, the preview text; error `Ko'rinishni olib bo'lmadi.`. Fields grouped under the backend's section titles; each field typed by the backend (text / textarea / number (decimal pad, grouped) / date (DateField, saved as dd.mm.yyyy) / phone (`+998 90 123 45 67` placeholder) / email), required ones marked `*`. Read-only mode shows label/value rows (`—` when empty). Submit: `Saqlash va yaratish` when every required field is filled (PUT answers then POST generate; toast `Hujjat yaratildi`) else `Saqlash` (toast `Saqlandi`); a secondary `Saqlash` is offered beside "save and create".
  - **Sheet — versions** (VersionsSheet `Versiyalar`, snaps 0.6/0.92): button `Joriy holatdan yangi versiya` (when allowed: has fields, not stopped, draft or ready) → POST `…/versions` (toast `Versiya yaratildi`); rows `Versiya {n}` · date-time · `{n} ta maydon`, tap opens the version's DOCX (`/contracts/{contract_id}/file`) in FilePreview; empty `Versiyalar yo'q` / `Hujjatning joriy holatini versiya sifatida saqlab qo'yishingiz mumkin.`
  - **Sheet — review**, **Sheet — rating**: identical to `/documents` above.
  - **Sheet — constructor prompt** (auto): when the backend sends `constructor_action{available:true, prompt_required:true}` and the question was not asked yet this session, CtorSheet opens by itself; `Ha, to'ldiraman` → `/templates/{template_id}/fill?request={id}` or the answers sheet when there is no template.
  - **Full-screen file preview** (FilePreview) for `Ochish`, the blank template and version files; a sheet closes first (320 ms) when one is open.
- **Actions:** all buttons above; pull to refresh invalidates every `my-docs` query; back from the header.
- **States:** loading = one 320 px skeleton; error = ErrorView with `Qayta urinish` (common texts as above; offline wifi-off variant); there is no empty state (an unknown id yields the 404 text `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`). Download failure toast `Faylni yuklab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.` or the document-service refusal (`Bu formatda yuklab olish yopilgan.`, plan codes). Realtime: same socket events as the list refresh this page; focused-screen toasts as listed on `/documents`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                      | Used for                                                                            | Key fields                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}`                                                                                 | the whole page                                                                      | as on `/documents` (detail fields)                                                                                  |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/unlock-policy`                                                                   | pay/generate flags, missing fields, draft-DOCX offer                                | `paid`, `requires_payment`, `can_generate`, `amount`, `currency`, `formats[]`, `missing_required_fields[]`          |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/versions`                                                                        | versions row + sheet                                                                | `[{id, created_at, payload{contract_id, answers{}}}]`                                                               |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/chat`                                                                            | advocate card (fallback when the room list is not cached)                           | `available`, `room.id`/`room_id`, `message`                                                                         |
| GET `/document-service-settings/runtime`                                                                      | review-off gate, download formats                                                   | `settings.download_policy.formats[]`, `settings.modes[]`, `access.review{allowed,code,message}`                     |
| GET `/document-services/request-document-types`                                                               | review sheet chips                                                                  | `items[]`, `optional`, `custom_allowed`, `applies_to[]`, `not_applies_to[]`                                         |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/file` · `…/docx` · `…/clean-template-file` · GET `/contracts/{contract_id}/file` | open / download of the finished file, the draft DOCX, the blank template, a version | binary (DOCX unless `contract_file.mime_type` says otherwise)                                                       |
| PUT `/document-requests/{id}/answers`                                                                         | `Saqlash`                                                                           | body `{answers{}}` → the updated request                                                                            |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/preview`                                                                        | preview in the answers sheet                                                        | body `{answers{}}` → `preview_text`/`final_text`, `can_generate`, `completion_percent`, `missing_required_fields[]` |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/generate`                                                                       | `Saqlash va yaratish`                                                               | 402 not paid · 409 nothing to build · 422 required missing (server text shown)                                      |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/versions`                                                                       | `Joriy holatdan yangi versiya`                                                      | body `{}`                                                                                                           |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/calls/audio`                                                                    | `Qo'ng'iroq`                                                                        | body `{}` → `id`, `room_id`, `client_call_usage.remaining`; 429 = limit                                             |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/payments`                                                                       | `To'lovni boshlash`                                                                 | as on `/documents`                                                                                                  |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/lawyer-review`                                                                  | `Advokatga yuborish`                                                                | as on `/documents`                                                                                                  |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/rating`                                                                         | `Bahoni yuborish`                                                                   | as on `/documents`                                                                                                  |
| WS `/ws/users/me`                                                                                             | refresh on `document_request.*` etc.                                                | as on `/documents`                                                                                                  |

- **UX note:** the status well with the next-step sentence and the timeline must be the first thing on screen, followed by the one card that needs the client now (payment, answers or file); eight stacked cards is long for a phone — versions and the blank template could move under a "more" row. The two ways to rate (card button → sheet, `Shikoyat qoldirish` → `/rate/{work_id}`) are confusing.

### `/works` — `Mening ishlarim` (tab `Ishlar`)

- **Who:** the tab is open to everyone; a guest sees only the sign-in prompt (`Ishlaringizni ko'rish uchun kiring` / `Hujjat so'rovlari, tezkor xizmatlar va buyurtmalar akkauntingizga bog'langan.` / button `Kirish` → `/auth/login`). No role or plan gate; the tab is mounted lazily on iOS (renders nothing until first focused).
- **Purpose:** every business process of the client in one list — document works with an advocate, urgent services, marketplace orders, legal cases and complaints — with live status, the next step, and a sheet per work.
- **Layout, top → bottom (FlashList, 16 px padding):**
  1. TabPage header: the shared round header buttons, big title `Mening ishlarim`, subtitle `{n} ta ish` (server total) or `Advokat bilan ishlar, tezkor xizmatlar va buyurtmalar` while 0.
  2. Summary (WorksSummary, `lead`): three counter cells — activity icon `Jarayonda` (open = queue + active + review), check-circle `Yakunlangan` (group done), layers `Jami` (all loaded) — then a tinted link row `Hujjatlar arxivi` → `/documents`.
  3. Filter bar: SearchBox placeholder `Ish nomi yoki ID bo'yicha qidirish` (title, work id, assignee, kind and status words; 200 ms debounce; diacritic-insensitive) + sliders button `Filtrlar` with a dot while a kind or group is set. Applied chips under it (removable): the kind name, the group name.
  4. Rows (WorkRow card): 46 px tinted kind tile + small-caps kind name + title (2 lines, falls back to the kind name) + chevron · status pill (pulsing for active/review) with small marks for `has_chat` (message icon) and `has_meeting` (video icon) · next-step strip (arrow + `next_action`, translated when known) · footer: assignee avatar + name, `{work_id} · {time ago / date}` (relative within 7 days).
  5. Footer: `Qolgan ishlar yuklanmoqda…` with a spinner while later pages fill in the background; `Barcha ishlar yuklanmadi.` + `Qayta urinish` when a later page failed.
  - **Sheet — filters** (`Filtrlar`, snaps 0.82/0.94): `Turi` as icon cards (`Barcha turlar` + only kinds with a count or the selected one, each with its count) · `Holat` list with coloured dot, count and radio: `Barchasi`, `Navbatda`, `Ijroda`, `Tekshiruvda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Yopilgan` · pinned bar: reset icon `Filtrlarni tozalash` + gradient `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · {n}`. Counts ignore their own dimension.
  - **Sheet — work** (WorkSheet, snaps 0.7/0.93, opens on row tap; reads the detail by work id): kind chip + status pill (large) · title (h3) · id row `Ish ID` + `{work_id}` + copy icon (tap copies, toast `Nusxa olindi`) · four fact tiles: `Turi`, `Holat`, `Advokat` (avatar + name or `Hali biriktirilmagan`), `Ochilgan` (date-time) · `Keyingi qadam` strip when `next_action` · skeleton while the detail loads · complaint boxes (`Shikoyat matni`: text, `Operator izohi: …`) when the detail carries complaints · link `To'liq tafsilotlar` → `/works/{work_id}` · pinned bar: round chat button (`Chatni ochish`, when the detail has a room) + gradient `Hujjatni ochish` / `So'rovni ochish` / `Buyurtmani ochish` / `Ishga o'tish` (per kind) → the module screen (`/documents/{id}`, `/urgent/{id}`, `/orders/{id}`, `/cases`, `/complaints`) when the detail's `related` id is known, else `/works/{work_id}`.
- **Enums (Uzbek labels):**
  - Work kind (`type` → kind): `document_lawyer_work` → `document` `Advokat bilan hujjat` (file-text, blue #1668F0) · `urgent_advokat` → `urgent` `Tezkor xizmat` (zap, pink #E0457F) · `service_order` → `order` `Buyurtma` (shopping-bag, green #00A870) · `legal_case` → `case` `Yuridik ish` (briefcase, violet #7A5CFF) · `quality_complaint`/`complaint` → `complaint` `Shikoyat` (alert-triangle, orange #C2680A) · anything else → `other` `Boshqa` (layers, gray #5C6E85).
  - Status group (filter): `all` `Barchasi` · `open` `Faol` (queue+active+review; used on the dashboard list) · `queue` `Navbatda` (warn) · `active` `Ijroda` (blue; also every unknown status) · `review` `Tekshiruvda` (cyan) · `done` `Yakunlangan` (green) · `closed` `Yopilgan` (gray; rejected/declined/failed/payment_rejected/lost in red).
  - Group membership: queue = new, draft, pending, submitted, available, open_pool, questionnaire, ready_to_generate, pending_payment, payment_pending, payment_required, awaiting_payment, waiting_payment, waiting_info, waiting_docs, seller_selection, sent_to_seller · review = in_review, lawyer_review, lawyer_review_requested, under_review, pending_review, review, quality_check, result_ready, delivered, client_confirmation · done = completed, fulfilled, done, file_ready, resolved, rated, closed · closed = cancelled, canceled, rejected, expired, archived, failed, declined, lost, payment_rejected, payment_cancelled.
  - Status labels (`cl2.s` / `cl3.s`; unknown → backend `status_label` → tidied slug): `new` `Yangi`, `draft` `Qoralama`, `pending` `Kutilmoqda`, `submitted` `Yuborilgan`, `available` `Mavjud`, `open_pool` `Mutaxassis kutilmoqda`, `questionnaire` `Ma'lumotlar to'ldirilmoqda`, `ready_to_generate` `Hujjat yaratishga tayyor`, `pending_payment`/`payment_pending`/`awaiting_payment`/`waiting_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `payment_required` `To'lov kerak`, `waiting_info` `Ma'lumot kutilmoqda`, `waiting_docs` `Hujjatlar kutilmoqda`, `seller_selection` `Buyurtma qabul qilindi`, `sent_to_seller` `Ijrochiga yuborildi`, `in_review` `Tayyorlanmoqda`, `lawyer_review` `Advokat ko'rib chiqmoqda`, `lawyer_review_requested` `Advokat tekshiruviga yuborildi`, `under_review` `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda`, `pending_review` `Tekshiruv kutilmoqda`, `review` `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda`, `quality_check` `Sifat tekshiruvida`, `result_ready` `Natija tayyor`, `delivered` `Topshirildi`, `client_confirmation` `Tasdiqlashingiz kutilmoqda`, `completed` `Yakunlandi`, `fulfilled`/`done` `Bajarildi`, `file_ready` `Hujjat tayyor`, `resolved` `Hal qilindi`, `rated` `Baholandi`, `closed` `Yopildi`, `cancelled`/`canceled` `Bekor qilindi`, `rejected`/`declined` `Rad etildi`, `expired` `Muddati o'tdi`, `archived` `Arxivlangan`, `failed` `Muvaffaqiyatsiz`, `lost` `Bekor bo'ldi`, `payment_rejected` `To'lov rad etildi`, `payment_cancelled` `To'lov bekor qilindi`, `claimed` `Mutaxassis ishni oldi`, `assigned` `Tayinlandi`, `in_progress` `Jarayonda`, `started` `Ish boshlandi`, `accepted` `Qabul qilindi`, `paid` `To'langan`, `scheduled` `Rejalashtirilgan`, `meeting_active` `Uchrashuv ketyapti`, `open` `Ochiq`, `active` `Faol`, `rework_required` `Qayta ishlash kerak`, `investigation` `Tergov`, `court` `Sud`, `appeal` `Apellyatsiya`, `unpaid` `To'lanmagan`, `processing` `Amalga oshirilmoqda`, `refunded` `Qaytarilgan`.
  - Next-action sentences the backend sends in Uzbek and the app recognises: `Xulosa bilan tanishing`, `Natijani ko'ring va baholang`, `So'rov holatini kuzating`, `Tayyor hujjatni yuklab oling`, `Advokat ish jarayonini kuzating`, `Callcenter operator shikoyatni tekshiradi` (shown as `Call-markaz operatori shikoyatni tekshiradi`); any other text is shown verbatim.
- **Actions:** row tap → WorkSheet; sheet: copy id, chat → `/chat/{room_id}`, module button (see above), `To'liq tafsilotlar` → `/works/{work_id}`; `Hujjatlar arxivi` → `/documents`; empty-state `Xizmatlarni ko'rish` → services tab; `Filtrlarni tozalash`; pull to refresh; kind / group / search are applied on the phone (the backend ignores search).
- **States:** guest prompt (above); loading = four 132 px skeleton rows; error with no rows = ErrorView + `Qayta urinish`; error after some rows = footer `Barcha ishlar yuklanmadi.` + `Qayta urinish`; background paging footer `Qolgan ishlar yuklanmoqda…`; empty = `Hozircha ish yo'q` / `Xizmat buyurtma qilsangiz yoki advokatga murojaat qilsangiz, ish shu yerda paydo bo'ladi.` + `Xizmatlarni ko'rish`; filtered-empty = `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Boshqa so'z yoki filtrni sinab ko'ring.` + `Filtrlarni tozalash`; offline = ErrorView offline text. Realtime: socket events `document_request.*`, `urgent_advokat.*`, `quality_complaint.*`, `marketplace.*`, `order.*`, `case.*`, `rating.*`, `complaint.*`, `payment.*`, `notification.created` refetch the list (700 ms debounce); stale queries refetch on focus.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                  | Used for                                                                                                                      | Key fields                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/clients/me/works?limit=100&offset=` | the list; first page at once, the rest in the background up to 1000 rows, merged unique by id and sorted by `updated_at` desc | `items[]`/`data[]`, `total`; per row `id`, `work_id`, `type`, `title`/`document_title`, `status`, `status_label`, `service_kind`, `assigned_lawyer{name | first_name,last_name, role}`, `operator{…}`, `has_chat`, `has_meeting`, `next_action`, `document_request_id`, `created_at`, `updated_at` (phones are never read) |
| GET `/clients/me/works/{work_id}`         | WorkSheet (room id, complaints, related module id)                                                                            | `summary{…}` + per type: `chat{available,room_id}`, `complaints[]{id,work_id,status,payload{description                                                 | complaint                                                                                                                                                        | comment | text, operator_note, resolved_action}}`, `document.id`, `urgent_request.id`, `order.id`(see`/works/[id]`) |
| WS `/ws/users/me`                         | list refresh                                                                                                                  | event names above                                                                                                                                       |

- **UX note:** the three counters + archive link + search + chips push the first row below the fold; the counters could become one line. The kind name is repeated three times on a row (tile colour, caps label, sheet chip).

### `/works/[id]` — `Ish tafsilotlari`

- **Who:** no route gate; a guest sees the same sign-in prompt as `/works` (`Ishlaringizni ko'rish uchun kiring` … `Kirish`). `id` is the public work id (ADV-…, DOC-…, ORD-…).
- **Purpose:** one work opened out: who is on it, what happens next, payment, files, history, complaints and the server-driven rating window, with hand-offs to chat, call, rating and the module screen.
- **Layout, top → bottom (ScrollView, pull to refresh; refetches on every return to the screen):**
  1. Header: back · `Ish tafsilotlari` · subtitle = `work_id`.
  2. Head card (WorkHead): 4 px kind-coloured top stripe · kind chip + status pill · title (h3; kind name fallback) · CopyId chip `{work_id}` (tap copies, check icon for 1.6 s, a11y `Nusxalash`) · `Ochilgan: {date}`.
  3. Next step (WorkNext, when `next_action`): accent box, caps `Keyingi qadam`, the sentence (translated when known).
  4. Rating card (WorkRatingCard, when the detail carries a `rating` block and the window is open / rated / expired): title `Baholash va shikoyat` · open: clock + `Qolgan vaqt: {mm:ss}` (counts down from `remaining_seconds` since fetch; at 0 refetches), `Ish yakunlangandan keyin belgilangan vaqt ichida baholash yoki shikoyat qilish mumkin.`, gradient `Baholash` → `/rate/{work_id}` · rated: `★★★★☆` + comment + `Baholangan: {date-time}` · expired: lock + `Baholash va shikoyat muddati tugagan.`
  5. Segmented tabs: `Umumiy` · `Fayllar · {n}` · `Tarix · {n}` (counts only when > 0).
  6. Tab `Umumiy`: People card `Mutaxassis` (hidden when nobody and no room and not waiting): assignee avatar + name + role (`Advokat` / `Yurist` / else `Mutaxassis`), `Mutaxassis hali tayinlanmagan.` while unassigned in the queue group, operator row labelled `Call-markaz operatori`, and — when a chat room exists — the gradient ChatCard `Chatni ochish` (+ person name) with a round video button `Uchrashuvga qo'shilish` beside it when `meeting.call_id` is live → `/call/{room_id}?callId=`. Payment card `To'lov` (when the detail has a payment block): `{amount} so'm` or `Bepul` + status pill (`paid` green, unpaid/pending/processing warn, failed red, cancelled/refunded gray) · when required and unpaid: `Holat to'lov serverda tasdiqlangach yangilanadi.` + soft `To'lovlarni ochish` → `/payments`. Info card `Asosiy ma'lumotlar`: `Ish ID`, `Ochilgan`, `Yangilangan` (when different) then only the facts the backend sent, in this order: `Murojaat matni`, `Xizmat`, `Hujjat turi`, `Asosiy fayl`, `Aloqa usuli` (`Chat` / `Audio qo'ng'iroq` / `Video qo'ng'iroq` / `Qo'ng'iroq`), `Qulay vaqt`, `Hudud` (region name), `Davomiyligi` (`{n} daq`), `Natija xulosasi`, `Izoh`, `Ish raqami`, `Bosqich` (status label), `Tavsif`, `Muddat` (date). Complaints card `Shikoyat` (when any): per complaint `{work_id} · {date-time}` + status pill, text, resolved action in green, `Operator izohi: …`. Line button `Hujjatni ochish` / `So'rovni ochish` / `Buyurtmani ochish` / `Ishga o'tish` when the module id is known.
  7. Tab `Fayllar`: card `Fayllar` with rows: type icon, name (2 lines), `Tayyor natija` / `Yuklangan fayl` + size, eye icon → FilePreview; empty `Hozircha fayl yo'q`.
  8. Tab `Tarix`: card `Holatlar tarixi` as a rail timeline: `Ish ochildi` first, then each status (translated) with date-time and note, the last one bold with a bigger dot; empty `Tarix hali bo'sh`.
- **Actions:** copy id; `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/{room_id}`; `Uchrashuvga qo'shilish` → `/call/{room_id}?callId=…`; `Baholash` → `/rate/{work_id}`; `To'lovlarni ochish` → `/payments`; module button → `/documents/{id}` / `/urgent/{id}` / `/orders/{id}` / `/cases` / `/complaints`; file row → in-app preview; pull to refresh.
- **States:** guest prompt; loading = four skeleton blocks (150/110/190/140 px); error = ErrorView with `Qayta urinish` (404 → `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, offline text); no empty state beyond the per-tab ones. Realtime: same socket events as `/works` invalidate the detail.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                          | Used for         | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/clients/me/works/{work_id}` | the whole screen | `summary{id, work_id, type, title, status, status_label, assigned_lawyer, operator, has_chat, has_meeting, next_action, document_request_id, created_at, updated_at}` plus, by type — document: `document{id, price, currency, requires_payment/payment_required, payment_status, paid, contract_file{id, file_name, contract_type, mime_type, download_url}, requested_document_type, answers.need, rating_available, rating_submitted, rating_deadline_at, rating_remaining_seconds, rating_closed_reason, rating}`, `lawyer_request{need, requested_document_type, main_file_name, attachments[]{id, title, record_type, download_url, payload{file_name, mime_type, size}}}`, `chat{available, room_id}`, `meeting{call_id}`, `rating{…}`, `complaints[]`; urgent: `urgent_request{id, price, currency, chat{available, room_id}, payload{need, channel, region, meeting_minutes, result_summary, status_note, status_history[]{from,to,changed_at}, files[], voice_messages[], secure_chat_room_id}, rating{available, submitted, deadline_at, remaining_seconds, closed_reason, value{rating, comment, rated_at}}}`; order: `order{id, price, currency, payment_status, service_title, details{secure_chat_room_id, service_title, preferred_channel, preferred_time, client_note}}`, `status_history[]{record_type, created_at, payload{status, note}}`; case: `case{case_number, stage, description, deadline_at}`, `timeline[]{created_at, status, payload{status, stage, next_action}}`; complaint: `complaint{id, work_id, status, created_at, payload{…, resolved_at, resolved_action}}` |
| GET `{download_url}` of a file    | file preview     | binary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| WS `/ws/users/me`                 | refresh          | as `/works`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

- **UX note:** head + next step + rating window are the right first screen; the `Umumiy` tab is still four cards deep, and the module button at its bottom ("open the document / order") is the action most clients want — it should sit beside the chat card near the top.

### `/cases` — `So'rovlarim`

- **Who:** no route gate; a guest sees `So'rovlaringizni ko'rish uchun kiring` + `Kirish` → `/auth/login`. Signed-in clients only in practice (the endpoints are the client's own cases/orders).
- **Purpose:** the client's legal cases joined with the orders behind them (plus orders without a case), with the simplified 5-step progress, milestone payments, status history, attached documents and refund / lawyer-replacement requests; unfinished document work is pinned on top.
- **Layout, top → bottom (FlatList, pull to refresh):**
  1. Header: back · `So'rovlarim` · subtitle `Jami {n}` when signed in and there are items.
  2. Block `Tugallanmagan hujjatlar` (OpenDocuments; hidden when none or when the fetch failed): header action `Barchasini ko'rish` → `/documents`; lead `Boshlangan va hali yetkazilmagan hujjat ishlari.`; rows (OpenDocRow, tap → `/documents/{id}`): title (fallback `Tugallanmagan hujjatlar`) + document status pill · `{advocate} · {created date-time}` · next-action in accent · `Uchrashuv faol` with a video icon when a meeting is live · button `Chatni ochish` when a room exists, else `Advokat kutilmoqda` (clock) when an advocate holds it. Rows = first page (10) of service-flow where the file is not ready and the group is neither ready nor closed.
  3. Case cards (CaseCard) — one per legal case (with its order) then one per order that has no case: title = `case_type` / case title / `So'rovlarim` (case) or service name / order title / `Buyurtma` (order) · pill = order status (`ccs.os.*`) or case status (green when the order stage is done, else blue) · `{work_id}` caption for a bare order · sub-line `{stage} · {status}` of the case (translated with the works status map) or the order title · StageTrack: five segments, `{n}/5-bosqich · {label}` (`So'rov`, `Tayyorlanmoqda`, `To'lov`, `Ish jarayoni`, `Yakun`) or the plain text `Yopilgan` for a lost/cancelled/declined order · CaseMeta: arrow + `next_action` (accent) and `Advokatni almashtirish: {status}` for the latest replacement request (`Ko'rib chiqilmoqda` for active/pending/in_review, `Tasdiqlandi`, `Rad etildi`, `Almashtirildi`) · expandable panels (one at a time) · action row of small line buttons: `To'lov bosqichlari` / `Bosqichlarni yopish`, `Tarix` / `Tarixni yopish` (both only with an order id), `Hujjatlar` and `So'rov` (only for a case).
  4. Milestones panel (`To'lov bosqichlari`; hidden when the list is empty): rows `{title}` or `{n}-bosqich` with `{percent}% · {amount} so'm · {status}` (`To'lov kutilmoqda`, `To'lov jarayonda`, `Kutilmoqda`, `To'langan`, `Ushlab turilgan`, `Ijrochiga o'tkazildi`); the first unpaid row gets `To'lash` (POST pay → opens the provider URL; toast `To'lov sahifasi ochildi` / `To'lov havolasi olinmadi.`); paid/held rows get `Ishni tasdiqlash` → confirm dialog (`Tasdiqlasangiz, shu bosqich uchun to'lov ijrochiga o'tkaziladi. Buni ish bajarilgach qiling.`, `Ha, tasdiqlayman` / `Bekor qilish`) → release (toast `Bosqich tasdiqlandi, to'lov ijrochiga o'tkazildi.`). A 403 on the list makes the panel read-only. Errors: 409 `Bosqich holati o'zgargan — ro'yxat yangilandi.`, 403 `Bu amal uchun ruxsatingiz yo'q.`, else `Amalni bajarib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.` (The 30/40/30 split is the backend's; the app only renders `percent`.)
  5. History panel (`Buyurtma tarixi`): rows newest first (clock icon on the first, check on the rest): status label, `{date-time} · {note}`; `Tarixni yuklab bo'lmadi.` / `Hali status o'zgarishlari yo'z.` → actual copy `Hali status o'zgarishlari yo'q.`; then `Keyingi qadam` with note input `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` (300) and the moves the client may make: `Qabul qilish va yakunlash` (from delivered / client_confirmation) or `Bekor qilish` (line; from new / waiting_info / waiting_docs / waiting_payment / seller_selection). Toasts: `Holat yangilandi: {status}`, 409 `Bu o'tish hozir mumkin emas — holat allaqachon o'zgargan.`, 403 `Bu buyurtma holatini o'zgartirishga ruxsatingiz yo'q.`, else `Holatni o'zgartirib bo'lmadi.`
  - **Sheet — request** (RequestSheet `Qaytarish yoki almashtirish so'rovi`): lead `Qaytarish va advokat almashtirish admin + menejer tomonidan ko'rib chiqiladi.` · select `So'rov turi`: `Qaytarish` / `Advokatni almashtirish` · `Sabab` textarea placeholder `So'rovingizni qisqacha yozing` (10–1000 chars) · caption `Faqat admin va menejer tasdig'idan so'ng qabul qilinadi.` · submit `So'rov yuborish` (`Yuborilmoqda…` while busy). Toasts `So'rov ko'rib chiqishga yuborildi.` / backend detail or `Yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`
  - **Sheet — documents** (DocsSheet `Ish hujjatlari`): skeleton → rows (title + status) or empty `Hujjatlar yo‘q` / `Bu ish uchun birinchi hujjatni qo‘shing.` · label `Hujjat qo‘shish` + input `Hujjat nomi` (2–200) · submit `Hujjat qo‘shish` (toast `Hujjat qo‘shildi.` / `Yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`).
- **Enums (Uzbek labels):**
  - Client stage (order status → 5 steps): `request` `So'rov` = new, waiting_info, waiting_docs · `matching` `Tayyorlanmoqda` = seller_selection, sent_to_seller, accepted · `payment` `To'lov` = waiting_payment · `work` `Ish jarayoni` = paid, started, in_progress, result_ready, quality_check · `done` `Yakun` = delivered, client_confirmation, completed, rated · `closed` `Yopilgan` = lost, cancelled, canceled, declined. Unknown statuses show no track.
  - Order status (`ccs.os`): `new` `Yangi`, `waiting_info` `Ma'lumot kutilmoqda`, `waiting_docs` `Hujjatlar kutilmoqda`, `waiting_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `seller_selection` `Buyurtma qabul qilindi`, `sent_to_seller` `Ijrochiga yuborildi`, `paid` `To'langan`, `accepted` `Qabul qilindi`, `started` `Ish boshlandi`, `in_progress` `Jarayonda`, `result_ready` `Natija tayyor`, `quality_check` `Sifat tekshiruvida`, `delivered` `Topshirildi`, `client_confirmation` `Tasdiqlashingiz kutilmoqda`, `completed` `Yakunlandi`, `rated` `Baholandi`, `lost` `Bekor bo'ldi`, `cancelled` `Bekor qilindi`, `declined` `Rad etildi`.
  - Milestone status: `waiting_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `payment_pending` `To'lov jarayonda`, `pending` `Kutilmoqda`, `paid` `To'langan`, `held` `Ushlab turilgan`, `released` `Ijrochiga o'tkazildi`.
- **Actions:** listed above; open-document row → `/documents/{id}`; `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/{room_id}`; `Barchasini ko'rish` → `/documents`; pull to refresh refetches cases and orders.
- **States:** guest prompt; loading = two 130 px skeletons; error (both lists failed) = ErrorView + `Qayta urinish`; empty = `Hozircha so'rov yo'q` / `Birinchi so'rovni yaratish uchun muammoni yozing.` (no action); panels have their own skeleton / `Tarixni yuklab bo'lmadi.` / `Hali status o'zgarishlari yo'q.`; the open-documents block disappears silently on error. Realtime: socket events `case.*`, `order.*`, `marketplace.*`, `payment.*` invalidate the lists.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                               | Used for                                                                      | Key fields                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| GET `/cases`                                                                                                           | legal cases                                                                   | `cases[]`/`items[]`/`data[]`: `id`, `case_number` (or `work_id`), `case_type`, `title`, `stage`, `status`, `next_action`, `order_id`             |
| GET `/orders`                                                                                                          | the orders joined to cases, and orders without a case                         | `orders[]`/…: `id`, `work_id`, `title`/`details.title`/`service.name`, `service.name                                                             | title`/`service_name`/`service_title`/`details.service_title | service_name`, `status`, `created_at` |
| GET `/document-requests/service-flow?limit=10&offset=0`                                                                | the `Tugallanmagan hujjatlar` block (first page only)                         | as on `/documents` (`file.ready`, `status`, `next_action`, `meeting`, `assigned_lawyer.name`, `created_at`)                                      |
| GET `/document-requests/{id}/chat`                                                                                     | chat button of an open document (fallback when the room list is not cached)   | `available`, `room.id`/`room_id`                                                                                                                 |
| GET `/replacement-requests/me` → GET `/replacement-requests/{id}/history`                                              | the latest replacement status per case (request matched by `payload.case_id`) | `items[]{id, status, title, payload{case_id}}`; history `items[]{id, status                                                                      | payload.status, created_at}`                                 |
| GET `/orders/{order_id}/milestones`                                                                                    | milestones panel                                                              | `items[]`/`milestones[]`: `id`, `index`, `title`, `percent`, `amount`, `status`                                                                  |
| POST `/orders/{order_id}/milestones/{id}/pay?provider=payme` (+ POST `/payments/{id}/demo-confirm` on a demo provider) | `To'lash`                                                                     | → `payment{id, payment_url}`                                                                                                                     |
| POST `/orders/{order_id}/milestones/{id}/release`                                                                      | `Ishni tasdiqlash`                                                            | —                                                                                                                                                |
| GET `/orders/{order_id}/status-history`                                                                                | history panel                                                                 | `items[]`/`history[]`: `status`/`stage`, `note`, `created_at`/`at`                                                                               |
| PATCH `/orders/{order_id}/status`                                                                                      | `Qabul qilish va yakunlash` / `Bekor qilish`                                  | body `{status, note?}`                                                                                                                           |
| POST `/refund-requests` · POST `/replacement-requests`                                                                 | request sheet                                                                 | body `{record_type:""                                                                                                                            | "replacement", title:"{label} — refund                       | replacement", status:"active"         | "pending", price:0, currency:"UZS", payload{case_id, order_id, reason}}` |
| GET `/case-documents` (filtered by `payload.case_id` on the phone) · POST `/case-documents`                            | documents sheet                                                               | rows `id`, `title`, `status`, `payload.case_id`; POST body `{record_type:"", title, status:"active", price:0, currency:"UZS", payload{case_id}}` |
| WS `/ws/users/me`                                                                                                      | refresh on `case.*`, `order.*`, `marketplace.*`, `payment.*`                  | —                                                                                                                                                |

- **UX note:** the stage track and the next action are what a client needs first; the four small line buttons per card plus two inline panels make each card tall and busy — milestones and history belong in sheets like documents and requests. The open-documents block duplicates `/documents` and is only the first ten.

### `/dashboard-lists/[kind]` — the list behind a Home counter

- **Who:** no route gate; a guest sees the works sign-in prompt (`Ishlaringizni ko'rish uchun kiring` … `Kirish`). Unknown `kind` → title `Bo'lim topilmadi` and empty state `Bo'lim topilmadi` / `Bunday ro'yxat mavjud emas.`
- **Purpose:** one filtered list per Home counter — active works, the document archive, marketplace orders, payment requests, chats with unread messages — each opening the row's own screen.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** header (back · kind title · kind subtitle), then a chip bar of the kind's filters, then the rows; every kind except works uses PagedList (skeleton of five 84 px rows → error → rows; `Yana ko'rsatish` footer when the server has more; pull to refresh).
  - **`works` — `Faol ishlar`, sub `Hali yakunlanmagan ishlaringiz`:** the same WorksList as `/works` without the summary block, preset to group `open`, group options `Faol`, `Navbatda`, `Ijroda`, `Tekshiruvda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Barchasi` (no `Yopilgan`). Filter bar, chips, filter sheet, footer and empty states as on `/works` (the reset returns to `Faol`). Row tap → `/works/{work_id}` directly (no sheet).
  - **`documents` — `Mening hujjatlarim`, sub `Hujjatlar arxivi`:** chips `Barchasi` / `Tayyor` (server filter `status=file_ready`); rows (DocRow): file icon tinted #00A6E6, title, next action (translated), work status pill (works status map).
