Tap → `/documents/{id}`. Empty `Hujjat yo'q` / `Tayyorlagan hujjatlaringiz shu yerda paydo bo'ladi.` Paged 20 at a time by the server.
  - **`orders` — `Buyurtmalar`, sub `Marketplace buyurtmalaringiz`:** chips `Faol · n` (status not in completed/rated/cancelled/canceled/declined/rejected/lost/closed) / `Barchasi · n`; rows (OrderRow card): title (`details.question` / title / service name / work id), status pill (`profile.status.*`: `new` `Yangi`, `pending_payment` `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `sent_to_seller` `Mutaxassisga yuborildi`, `accepted` `Qabul qilindi`, `started` `Boshlandi`, `result_ready` `Natija tayyor`, `quality_check` `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `delivered` `Topshirildi`, `declined`/`rejected` `Rad etildi`, `pending` `Kutilmoqda`, `active` `Faol`, `in_progress` `Jarayonda`, `completed` `Yakunlangan`, `cancelled` `Bekor qilingan`, `paid` `To'langan`, `unpaid` `To'lanmagan`; unknown → raw status), service name line, `{date} · {lawyer}`, amount `{n} so'm`. Tap → `/orders/{id}`. Empty `Buyurtma yo'q` / `Marketplace'da xizmat buyurtma qilsangiz, u shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  - **`payments` — `To'lovlar`, sub `To'lov so'rovlaringiz`:** chips `Kutilmoqda · n` (status pending/processing/created/awaiting/awaiting_payment/waiting/unpaid) / `Barchasi · n`; rows (PaymentRow): credit-card icon #C2680A, title by kind (`order` `Buyurtma to'lovi`, `subscription_plan`/`subscription_plan_telegram_approval` `Obuna to'lovi`, `document_request` `Hujjat to'lovi`, `urgent_advokat` `Tezkor xizmat to'lovi`, `document_analysis` `Hujjat tahlili to'lovi`, else tidied kind/description), `{amount} so'm`, date, admin StatusPill (`pending` `Kutilmoqda`, `processing` `Jarayonda`, `paid` `To'langan`, `unpaid` `To'lanmagan`, `approved` `Tasdiqlangan`, `rejected` `Rad etilgan`, `cancelled` `Bekor qilingan`, `completed` `Yakunlangan`, `failed` `Xato`). Tap → `/payments/{id}`. Empty `To'lov yo'q` / `To'lov so'rovlari shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  - **`chats` — `Xabarlar`, sub `Xavfsiz suhbatlar`:** chips `O'qilmagan · n` / `Barchasi · n` (archived rooms excluded); rows (ChatRow): avatar + peer name (fallback `Mutaxassis`), last message text, time ago, unread badge. Tap → `/chat/{room_id}`. Empty `Xabar yo'q` / `Yangi xabarlar bo'lmaganda bu ro'yxat bo'sh.`
- **Actions:** chip taps filter on the phone (documents: on the server); row taps as above; `Yana ko'rsatish`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** guest prompt; loading skeletons; error = ErrorView + `Qayta urinish` (offline text when status 0); empty per kind as above; works kind inherits `/works` states.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                           | Used for                                                                              | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/clients/me/works?limit=100&offset=` (+ GET `/clients/me/works/{work_id}` is NOT called here) | `works`                                                                               | as `/works`                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GET `/document-requests/service-flow?limit=20&offset=&status=file_ready?`                          | `documents`                                                                           | `items[]`, `total`; `id`, `title`, `status`, `status_label`, `next_action`, `updated_at`, `created_at`                                                                                                         |
| GET `/orders`                                                                                      | `orders`                                                                              | `id`, `work_id`, `details.question`/`title`/`service.name`, `service.*`, `status`, `payment_status`, `region`, `price`/`amount`, `created_at`, `lawyer_name`/`lawyer.name`, `source`, `acceptance_deadline_at` |
| GET `/payments`                                                                                    | `payments`                                                                            | `payments[]`/`items[]`: `id`, `amount`, `currency`, `status`, `kind`, `description`, `order_id`, `created_at`                                                                                                  |
| GET `/secure-chats`                                                                                | `chats` (the app's one cached chat list, kept live by the user socket)                | `rooms[]`/`items[]`: `id`, `status`, `participants[]{user_id,name,role}`, `last_message.content`, `last_message_at`/`updated_at`, `unread_count`                                                               |
| WS `/ws/users/me`                                                                                  | works kind refresh (`useWorksRealtime`); the chat list is kept live by the chat slice | —                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

- **UX note:** each kind is a one-chip-bar list, which is right for a phone; the mismatch to fix is that `documents` here uses the works status words and a different row than `/documents`, and `orders` uses a third status vocabulary (`profile.status`) — the three lists for the same records should share one status map and one row.

### `/urgent` — `Tezkor advokat`

- **Who:** client only (`RoleGate roles=["client"]` in `app/urgent/index.tsx`). A guest is redirected to `/auth/login`; a signed-in advokat/yurist gets the gate screen `Ruxsat yo'q` / `Bu bo'lim sizning rolingiz yoki ruxsatlaringiz uchun ochiq emas.` with `Orqaga` (advocates have their own `/pro/urgent`). No plan or entitlement gating on the page; the backend gates the group second opinion with a 402 (see `/urgent/new`).
- **Purpose:** pick a Tezkor advokat service (video, chat, road accident, second opinion, document work) and see my own urgent requests.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (`src/pages/urgent/UrgentPage.tsx` → `ClientHeader` + `MineSection`, one ScrollView)
  1. Header (`AdminPage`) title `Tezkor advokat`.
  2. **Hero** (`UrgentHero`, navy gradient, yellow bolt icon): title = catalog `title` (fallback `Tezkor advokat`); lead `Advokat bilan hoziroq bog'laning: video yoki chat orqali konsultatsiya, yoki boshqa advokatning ikkinchi fikri.`; a ruled strip of three numbers from the catalog: `Uchrashuv` → `{n} daqiqa` (`meeting_default_minutes`, default 30), `Bepul uzaytirish` → `{n} daqiqa` (`free_extension_once_minutes`, default 3), `Pullik uzaytirish` → `{price} so'm / daqiqa` (`paid_extension_price_per_minute`, column only when > 0). While the catalog loads the strip is a skeleton.
  3. **Flash banner** (`UrgentNote`, only right after an order was sent from `/urgent/new`): green `So'rov yuborildi. Advokat uni olgach xabar beramiz.` + `So‘rovingiz navbatga tushdi. Navbatchi advokat qabul qilishi bilan xabar keladi va xavfsiz chat ochiladi.`; for an "immediate" service when the backend opened no call: the backend's own `message` or `Hozir bo‘sh navbatchi advokat yo‘q. So‘rovingiz navbatga qo‘yildi — advokat bo‘shashi bilan qo‘ng‘iroq qilamiz.`; X dismisses. Not shown otherwise.
  4. **Service list** (`ServiceCards withDocs`): one full-width card per catalog card, in this order: video consultation card, `chat_consultation`, `traffic_accident_consultation`, then the rest in catalog order. Cards are built from `GET /urgent-advokat/catalog`: the catalog's own `groups` (e.g. `second_opinion` = `Ikkinchi fikr` holding `second_opinion_single` + `second_opinion_group`) and an app-made group `video_consultation_kind` = `Advokat bilan videokonsultatsiya` when both `video_consultation` and `express_video_consultation` exist and are not already grouped. Two card looks: `ServiceTile` (whole card lit in the kind's colour, white type, big watermark icon) when every item of the card has `immediate_call`; otherwise `FeatureTile` (a "ticket": coloured stub with the icon on the left, text in the middle, tinted delivery strip underneath). Each card shows: name (group: `ug1.groups.<key>` else backend group `title`; single: `ug4.kind.<key>` else backend `title`), one sentence `ug1.about.<key>`:
     - `video_consultation_kind`: `Advokat bilan video uchrashuv — ikki xil: oddiy (navbat bo‘yicha) va shoshilinch (navbatchi advokatga darhol).`
     - `second_opinion`: `Boshqa advokatning mustaqil fikri — yakka advokatdan yoki advokatlar guruhidan.`
     - `video_consultation` (`Advokat bilan videokonsultatsiya`): `Advokat bilan 30 daqiqalik video uchrashuv. So‘rovingiz navbatga tushadi va bo‘sh navbatchi advokat oladi.`
     - `express_video_consultation` (`Shoshilinch videokonsultatsiya`): `O‘sha zahoti navbatchi advokatga boradi — umumiy navbatni kutmaysiz. Shoshilinch holatlar uchun.`
     - `traffic_accident_consultation` (`YTH bo‘yicha konsultatsiya`): `Yo‘l-transport hodisasi bo‘yicha ixtisoslashgan navbatchi advokat bilan darhol bog‘lanish.`
     - `chat_consultation` (`Chat orqali konsultatsiya`): `Yozma savol-javob: advokat bilan xavfsiz chatda yozishasiz, uchrashuv kerak emas.`
     - `second_opinion_single` (`Ikkinchi fikr — yakka advokat`): `Boshqa advokat ishingizga mustaqil baho beradi. Hamma mijozlar uchun ochiq.`
     - `second_opinion_group` (`Ikkinchi fikr — advokatlar guruhi`): `2 dan 7 gacha advokat birgalikda fikr bildiradi. Avval LexGo orqali advokat yoki yurist xizmatidan foydalangan bo‘lish kerak.`
       Pills: `Video` / `Chat` (the channels of the card's `variants`, or video when `meeting_minutes` > 0 else chat), `{n} daqiqa` (largest meeting length), `Darhol` (all items `immediate_call`), `{n} xil` (group card), `Avvalgi xizmat talab qilinadi` (first item `requires_prior_lexgo_purchase`). Delivery strip: `Navbatchi advokatga to‘g‘ridan-to‘g‘ri boradi.` (immediate), `Navbatga tushadi, bo‘sh navbatchi advokat oladi.` (pool), `Qaysi xilni tanlashingizga qarab: navbat yoki darhol.` (group with mixed items). Kind colours: video `#1668F0`, express `#E0457F`, YTH `#F59E0B`, chat `#00A870`, second opinion `#7A5CFF`. No price is shown on the cards.
  5. **Kind sheet** (`KindSheet`, opens for a grouped card): title = group name, lead = group about sentence, then one Card per kind (icon, name, about sentence, pills `Darhol` / `Avvalgi xizmat talab qilinadi`, chevron). Picking a kind opens the order form.
  6. **Documents card** (`DocsCard`, always the last tile): `Huquqiy hujjatlar bo'yicha ishlash` / `Navbatchi advokat hujjatni noldan yozib beradi yoki sizdagi hujjatni tekshirib to'g'irlaydi.`, pill `2 xil`, strip `Navbatga tushadi, bo‘sh navbatchi advokat oladi.`. Opens a sheet titled `Huquqiy hujjatlar bo'yicha ishlash`, lead `Katalogdagi hujjatlardan mosini topmadingizmi? Navbatchi advokat siz uchun tayyorlab beradi.`, two cards: `0 dan hujjat tayyorlash` / `Vaziyatingizni yozing — navbatchi advokat hujjatni boshidan yozib beradi.` → `/documents/new?kind=custom`; `Hujjatimni tekshirtirish` / `O'zingizdagi hujjatni yuklang — navbatchi advokat tekshirib, to'g'irlab beradi.` → `/documents/new?kind=review`.
  7. **`Mening so'rovlarim`** (`MineSection`): clock icon, title, a count badge with the number of requests (when any), `Barchasini ko'rish` (when more than 3) → `/urgent/requests`. Shows the first 3 rows as the backend lists them (`ClientRow`); the row just created is outlined green. `ClientRow`: a 5 pt rail in the status colour, kind icon, kind name, `work_id` (accent), created date-time, `StatusBadge`; the request text in a quote block (3 lines); optional lines `Belgilangan vaqt: {when}` (`scheduled_at`), `Guruh: {names}` (`group_lawyers`), call line `Qo‘ng‘iroq qilinmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq davom etmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq tugadi` + ` · Video|Chat|Audio` (`call_status` · `call_channel`, green while `calling`), `Operator yaratgan` (source is an operator channel or `support_ticket_id` / `created_by_operator_user_id`); buttons `Chatni ochish` (only with a `room_id`) and `Batafsil`.
- **Actions:** service card → `/urgent/new?service=<key>&from=urgent` (group card → kind sheet first); documents tile → sheet → `/documents/new?kind=custom|review`; `Barchasini ko'rish` → `/urgent/requests`; row / `Batafsil` → `/urgent/<id>`; `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/<room_id>`; banner X dismisses; error views `Qayta urinish`.
- **States:** catalog loading: hero strip skeleton + three 150 pt skeleton cards; catalog error: inline `ErrorView` (offline `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.`, 403 `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.`, 404 `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, 429 `Juda ko'p urinish. Bir ozdan so'ng qayta urining.`, else the backend detail or `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`) with `Qayta urinish`. Requests loading: one 120 pt skeleton; error: compact ErrorView; empty: `So'rovlar yo'q` / `Yuqoridan xizmat tanlab, birinchi so'rovni yuboring.`. Status badge values (`ug4.status`, pulsing dot on the first five): `open_pool` `Navbatda` (blue), `claimed` `Olingan` (cyan), `scheduled` `Rejalashtirilgan` (amber), `in_progress` `Jarayonda` (cyan), `meeting_active` `Uchrashuvda` (green), `completed` `Yakunlangan` (green), `cancelled` `Bekor qilingan` (red), `expired` `Muddati o'tgan` (grey); an unknown status is humanised. Realtime: every `urgent_advokat.*` / `support.assist_urgent_advokat_request_created` user-socket frame refetches the list (app-root `UrgentWatcher`) and toasts one of: `Navbatchi advokat siz bilan bog'lanmoqda`, `Advokat so'rovingizni qabul qildi`, `Uchrashuv vaqti belgilandi`, `Uchrashuv boshlandi — qo'shiling`, `So'rov yakunlandi. Ishni baholashingiz mumkin`, `So'rov bekor qilindi`, `Ish boshqa advokatga o‘tkazildi`, `Yangi tezkor so'rov tushdi`, `Operator siz uchun tezkor advokat so'rovi yaratdi`; the list polls every 30 s only while the socket is down.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                    | Used for                                                                                                                                                     | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/catalog`               | hero numbers, service cards, kind sheet                                                                                                                      | `title`, `meeting_default_minutes`, `free_extension_once_minutes`, `paid_extension_price_per_minute`, `services[]` (`key`, `title`, `delivery`, `price`, `meeting_minutes`, `variants[]{channel, price, price_per_lawyer, meeting_minutes}`, `requires_prior_lexgo_purchase`, `lawyer_count_min`, `lawyer_count_max`, `variant`, `immediate_call`), `groups[]{key, title, items[]}` |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/me`           | `Mening so'rovlarim` rows                                                                                                                                    | list in `items`/`data`/`requests`; per record: `id`, `work_id`, `service_kind`, `title`/`service_title`, `channel`, `status`, `source`, `need`, `created_at`, `scheduled_at`, `group_lawyers[]{id,name}`, `chat.room_id`/`secure_chat_room_id`, `payload.call_status`, `payload.call_channel`, `payload.support_ticket_id`, `payload.created_by_operator_user_id`                   |
| user socket                                 | refresh + toasts                                                                                                                                             | events `urgent_advokat.*`, `support.assist_urgent_advokat_request_created` (`status`, `record_id`/`id`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST <promotion events url>` (conditional) | only for a request that was born from a second-opinion promotion (origin kept on the phone): sends `event: "purchased"` once the request appears in the list | `event`, `placement`, `session_id`, `idempotency_key`, `metadata{surface:"urgent_request", request_id}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

- **UX note:** the hero, its three numbers and the first (video) service card must be visible before scrolling; the document tile and the request preview can sit below the fold. Confusing today: no price appears anywhere on the list (only on the order form's footer), the flash banner and the "fresh" green row only exist right after ordering, and the hero's three numbers describe extension rules the client has not yet bought.

### `/urgent/new` — order form (`Xizmatni tanlang` when no service is known, else the service name e.g. `Advokat bilan videokonsultatsiya`)

- **Who:** client only (same `RoleGate`); guest → `/auth/login`. The group second opinion is gated by the backend (402 `previous_lexgo_advokat_service_required`) — explained in a sheet, never a silent failure.
- **Purpose:** answer the two questions (practice areas, what happened), confirm channel/kind/panel size and send the request; the price is read from the catalog.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (`UrgentNewPage` → `OrderScreen` → `OrderForm`; `?service=<key>&from=urgent`)
  1. Header title = kind label (`label.kind`) or `Xizmatni tanlang`.
  2. If the service key is missing/unknown: the same service list as `/urgent` **without** the documents tile (`ServiceCards`), each card routing back into this form.
  3. **Service banner** (`OrderBanner`, gradient in the kind's colour): icon, name, about sentence (`ug1.about.<key>`), pills: channel `Video`/`Chat` (the one in effect), `{n} daqiqa`, `Darhol` (`immediate_call`).
  4. **Kind switch** (`KindSwitch`, only when the service belongs to a group with 2+ kinds): field `Ikkinchi fikr turi` (`second_opinion`) or `Konsultatsiya turi` (`video_consultation_kind`), radio cards each with icon, name and about sentence; picking swaps the whole form to that kind.
  5. **Channel** (`ChannelPick`, only when the service sells 2+ channels): `Aloqa turi` segmented `Video` / `Chat` (default video).
  6. **Panel size** (`LawyerCount`, only `second_opinion_group`): `Advokatlar soni`, hint `{min} dan {max} tagacha` (`lawyer_count_min`..`lawyer_count_max`, defaults 2..7), `−` `n` `+` (starts at 3, clamped).
  7. **Prior-purchase note** (`PriorNote`, when `requires_prior_lexgo_purchase`): amber `Advokatlar guruhi faqat oldin LexGo orqali advokat yoki yurist xizmatidan foydalangan mijozlar uchun ochiladi. Yakka advokat fikri hammaga ochiq.`
  8. **Step 1 card** `Yo'nalish` (`DirectionPick`, number turns into a green tick once answered): hint `Bir nechta yo‘nalishni tanlashingiz mumkin — shu bo‘yicha mos navbatchi advokat tanlanadi.`; multi-select chips: `Jinoiy ishlar` (jinoiy), `Fuqarolik ishlari` (fuqarolik), `Oila ishlari` (oila), `Mehnat nizolari` (mehnat), `Ma'muriy ishlar` (mamuriy), `Iqtisodiy ishlar` (iqtisodiy), `Soliq nizolari` (soliq). Error `Yo‘nalishni tanlang — advokat shunga qarab tanlanadi`.
  9. **Step 2 card** `Vaziyatni tavsiflang` with a `{len}/10` counter: multiline input, placeholder `Nima bo'ldi va sizga qanday yordam kerak?`, hint `Kamida 10 ta belgi. Batafsil yozsangiz, advokat tezroq tayyorlanadi.`, error `Muammoni kamida 10 ta harf bilan yozing`.
  10. **What happens next** (`OrderSummary`, a small timeline of bubbles): `Uchrashuv davomiyligi: {n} daqiqa` (when the channel has minutes); `Har bir advokat uchun {price} so'm × {n} ta` (group, `price_per_lawyer`); `So'rov yuborilgach, advokat uni oladi va siz bilan maxfiy chat ochiladi.` or for the group `Navbatchi advokat advokatlar guruhini va uchrashuv vaqtini belgilaydi, so'ng sizga xabar beriladi.`; for an immediate service `Navbatchi advokatga to‘g‘ridan-to‘g‘ri boradi — umumiy navbatni kutmaysiz.` and `Nomida video bo‘lsa ham, ulanish audio qo‘ng‘iroq sifatida ochiladi — kamera yoqilmaydi.`; always `Fayl va ovozli xabarlarni advokat so'rovni olgandan keyin chatda yuborasiz.`
  11. **Pinned footer** (above the keyboard): `Jami` + `{price} so'm` (price of the chosen channel variant; group = `price_per_lawyer × count`; else the service `price`), gradient button `So'rov yuborish`.
      There is **no region field** (keys `Hudud` / `Barcha hududlar` exist in `ug2` but the form never sets `region`), **no attachments and no voice recorder**: `files: []` and `voice_messages: []` are sent empty and the client is told to send them in the chat later.
- **Actions:** `So'rov yuborish` → validates areas then text (≥ 10 chars) → `POST /urgent-advokat/requests`. Success: a flash note is stored for `/urgent`, the new request id is highlighted there, and the screen goes back (`router.back()` when it came from `/urgent`, else `replace("/urgent")`); when the answer says `immediate_call` and carries `call_session.room_id` + `call_session.id`, the call screen `/call/<room_id>?callId=<id>` is pushed on top (no banner); immediate without a call → banner with the backend `message` or `Hozir bo‘sh navbatchi advokat yo‘q…`. 402 `previous_lexgo_advokat_service_required` (old spelling `previous_lexgo_purchase_required`) → **Gate sheet** `Bu variant hali ochilmagan`: lock icon, the backend's sentence or `Advokatlar guruhi bo‘yicha ikkinchi fikr LexGo orqali avval advokat yoki yurist xizmatidan foydalangan mijozlarga ochiladi.`, `Yakka advokat fikrini hoziroq olishingiz mumkin, yoki quyidagilardan birini bajaring — keyin guruh varianti ham ochiladi.`, buttons `Yakka advokat fikrini olish` (switches the form to `second_opinion_single`), `Advokat bilan online konsultatsiya` (→ `video_consultation`), `Chat konsultatsiya boshlash` (→ `chat_consultation`) — each only if that service has no prior requirement — `Hujjatni advokat bilan tayyorlash` → `/documents`, `Xizmatlarni ko'rish` → `/services`. Any other failure: red toast with the backend `detail` or `Amalni bajarib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` Kind radio → re-renders the form for that kind; `−`/`+` change the panel size; chips toggle areas.
- **States:** catalog loading: one 320 pt skeleton; catalog error: `ErrorView` + `Qayta urinish`; sending: button spinner; validation errors inline (above); gate sheet (402); success leaves the screen.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                        | Used for                                                                            | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/catalog`   | the service, its kinds, channels, minutes, price, panel bounds, prior-purchase flag | as on `/urgent`: `services[].key/title/price/meeting_minutes/variants[]{channel,price,price_per_lawyer,meeting_minutes}/requires_prior_lexgo_purchase/lawyer_count_min/lawyer_count_max/immediate_call`, `groups[]`                                                                                                              |
| `POST /urgent-advokat/requests` | send the order                                                                      | request `service_kind`, `channel`, `need`, `directions[]`, `files: []`, `voice_messages: []`, `lawyer_count` (group only); response: the record (`id`, `work_id`, `status`, … or `urgent_request{…}`), `immediate_call`, `call_session{id, room_id}`, `message`; errors 402 `previous_lexgo_advokat_service_required` (`detail`) |

- **UX note:** the banner, kind switch and the two numbered cards are the whole decision; keep the total + `So'rov yuborish` pinned. Confusing today: the price only appears in the footer (never on the list), "video" services that ring as audio need the small note to explain it, and the absence of a region/attachments step contradicts the detail page which has `Hudud` and `Biriktirilgan fayllar` sections.

### `/urgent/[id]` — request detail, client view (title = kind name, e.g. `Chat orqali konsultatsiya`; subtitle = `work_id`)

- **Who:** any signed-in user (`RoleGate roles=["any"]`, this is where a push lands); guest → `/auth/login`. An advokat/yurist (`seller`) gets the advocate view (`scope="pro"`, not documented here); everyone else the client view below. Staff-only blocks (client card, `Qabul qilgan`, sources) are hidden for the client; action buttons follow the backend's `lifecycle.next_statuses` and `can_manage`.
- **Purpose:** follow one request — status, call/chat entry, what I asked, who is on it, the result — and rate or cancel it.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (`UrgentDetailPage.ClientDetailPage` → `DetailScreen` → `RequestDetail`; ScrollView with pull-to-refresh)
  1. **Head card** (`DetailHead`): large `StatusBadge` (enum as on `/urgent`), pills `work_id` (blue), channel `Video`/`Chat` (`Audio` when `call_channel` is audio), `{n} daqiqa` (`meeting_minutes`), `{amount} so'm` (`price`/`amount`), `SLA buzildi` (`sla_breached`), `Operator yaratgan` (via operator); call line `Qo‘ng‘iroq qilinmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq davom etmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq tugadi` (`call_status`); `Belgilangan vaqt: {when}` (`scheduled_at`); `Meeting hali boshlanmadi — navbatchi advokat uni boshlaganda tugma aktiv bo‘ladi.` (status scheduled/meeting_active with a time but no joinable call yet); `meeting_note`; **payment step** (`PaymentNote`, only when the record carries `payment`/`payment_status`): pill `To'lov kutilmoqda` (pending / awaiting_payment), `Tasdiq kutilmoqda` (pending_confirmation), `To'langan` (paid), `Tasdiqlangan` (approved), `To'lov amalga oshmadi` (failed), `To'lov bekor qilindi` (cancelled), unknown status shown raw, + ` · {amount} so'm`; green for paid/approved/confirmed/completed, amber for open ones, and under an open one `Telegram orqali tasdiqlash kutilmoqda — tasdiqlanishi bilan holat avtomatik yangilanadi.`; finally `Yaratilgan: {when}`.
  2. **Rating** (client only): while `rating.available && !submitted` → `RateCard`: `Ishni baholang`, amber pill `{mm:ss} qoldi` counting down to `rating.deadline_at`, `Yakunlangandan keyin 15 daqiqa ichida baho qoldirishingiz mumkin.`, gradient `Baholash` → `/rate/<work_id>` (shared rating screen). Otherwise `RateResult`: submitted → `Bahoyingiz uchun rahmat` + `Siz {n} yulduz qo‘ydingiz`; completed with `closed_reason: expired` → `Baholash oynasi yopildi` / `15 daqiqalik muddat tugadi — bu ish uchun endi baho qoldirib bo‘lmaydi.`; nothing otherwise.
  3. **Action bar** (`ActionBar`): primary full-width gradient `Meetingga kirish` (video icon, phone icon when `call_channel` = audio) when `call_id` + `room_id` exist and the record is not final; teal **chat card** (`ChatEntry`) `Chatga o‘tish` with the assigned advocate's avatar/name (or the operator's) when `room_id` exists; small soft button `So'rovni bekor qilish` when `next_statuses` contains `cancelled`. (If the backend sends `can_manage: true` the staff buttons `Ishni olish`, `Uchrashuv boshlash`, group/transfer/status/complete sheets would also appear — normally not for a client.) Bar hidden when there is nothing to do.
  4. **`So'rov mazmuni`** (`DetailBody`): the text or `Mijoz izoh qoldirmagan`; area pills (`ug4.dir`: `Jinoiy ishlar`, `Fuqarolik ishlari`, `Oila ishlari`, `Mehnat nizolari`, `Ma'muriy ishlar`, `Iqtisodiy ishlar`, `Soliq nizolari`, `Shartnoma`, `YTH`, `Avtoavariya`); fact rows `Hudud` (region translated via `lawyers.regions`, raw slug otherwise; "—" when empty) and `So'ralgan advokatlar` (group kind only, `requested_lawyer_count`).
  5. **`Biriktirilgan fayllar`** (only when `payload.files` / `payload.voice_messages` exist): rows with a file or mic icon and the name; tap → in-app `FilePreview`.
  6. **`Mas'ullar`** (`DetailPeople`, only when someone is set): `Biriktirilgan advokat`, `Operator` (`Qabul qilgan` is staff-only). For `second_opinion_group`: card `Advokatlar guruhi` listing the names or `Guruh hali tanlanmagan`.
  7. **Outcome** (`DetailOutcome`): `Natija` (`result_summary`, `Keyingi qadam: {next_action}`, `completed_at`) when set; `Bekor qilingan` (`cancel_reason`, `cancelled_at`) when set; `Status tarixi` rows (status label → `changed_at`) when `status_history` exists. `Ikkinchi fikr asosi` is staff-only.
  8. **Cancel sheet** (`CancelOwnSheet`): title `So'rovni bekor qilish`, lead `Sabab ixtiyoriy — u advokat va operatorga ko'rinadi.`, input `Sabab` placeholder `Sabab (ixtiyoriy)` (≤ 500), red submit `Ha, bekor qilinsin` → confirm dialog `So'rovni bekor qilish` / `So'rov bekor qilinsinmi? Bu amalni qaytarib bo'lmaydi.` with `Ha, bekor qilinsin` / `Yo'q`.
- **Actions:** `Meetingga kirish` → `/call/<room_id>?callId=<call_id>`; chat card → `/chat/<room_id>?ua=<request id>&wid=<work_id>`; `Baholash` → `/rate/<work_id>`; `So'rovni bekor qilish` → sheet → `POST /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/cancel` with `reason` (default `Mijoz bekor qildi`), toast `So'rov bekor qilindi`; file row → preview; pull to refresh → refetch. Payment is never actioned here (no pay button — the Telegram approval drives it).
- **States:** loading: three 84 pt skeletons; error: `ErrorView` (403 `Bu amal uchun ruxsat yo'q.` with a lock icon, 404 `Ma'lumot topilmadi.`, offline `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi…`) + `Qayta urinish`; refreshing spinner; cancel pending spinner; mutation failure toast (backend detail or `Amalni bajarib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`). Realtime: socket frames refetch the record; it polls every 20 s only while the socket is down. Toasts as on `/urgent`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                | Used for                                                       | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/{id}`                     | the whole record                                               | top level or `payload.*`: `id`/`record_id`, `work_id`, `service_kind`, `title`/`service_title`, `channel`, `status`, `source`, `region`, `need`/`description`, `directions[]`, `requested_lawyer_count`/`lawyer_count`, `price`/`amount`, `meeting_minutes`, `scheduled_at`, `sla_breached`, `created_at`, `can_manage`, `can_view`; `assigned_lawyer{id,name,phone,role,lexgo_id}`, `claimed_by`, `operator`, `group_lawyers[]`; `lifecycle.next_statuses[]`; `chat.room_id` / `secure_chat_room_id` / `room_id`; `payload.meeting_call_id`/`call_id`, `payload.call_status`, `payload.call_channel`, `payload.meeting_note`/`note`, `payload.status_history[]{from,to,changed_at}`, `payload.files[]` / `payload.voice_messages[]` (`id,name,url,size,content_type,duration_seconds` or a bare path), `payload.result_summary`/`summary`, `payload.next_action`, `payload.completed_at`, `payload.cancel_reason`, `payload.cancelled_at`, `payload.immediate_call`, `payload.support_ticket_id`, `payload.created_by_operator_user_id`; `rating{deadline_at, available, submitted, remaining_seconds, closed_reason, value}` (or flat `rating_*`); `payment{status, amount, provider}` / `payment_status` |
| `POST /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/cancel` | the client cancels their own request                           | body `reason`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| user socket                                             | refresh                                                        | `urgent_advokat.*`, `support.assist_urgent_advokat_request_created`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `POST <promotion events url>` (conditional)             | `purchased` for a request born from a second-opinion promotion | as on `/urgent`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

- **UX note:** status + the one primary action (join / chat) must be the first screen; the request text, people and history can follow. Confusing today: the head card stacks up to seven pills plus three text lines, the 15-minute rating window is easy to miss under the head, and a scheduled meeting shows a disabled-state sentence instead of a disabled button.

### `/urgent/requests` — `Mening so'rovlarim`

- **Who:** client only (`RoleGate roles=["client"]`); guest → `/auth/login`.
- **Purpose:** the full list of my Tezkor advokat requests (the preview on `/urgent` shows three).
- **Layout, top → bottom:** header `Mening so'rovlarim`; a `PagedList` of `ClientRow` cards (same card as on `/urgent`: rail colour, kind, `work_id`, date, status badge, request text, time / panel / call / operator lines, `Chatni ochish` + `Batafsil`). The list is whatever `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/me` returns (no server paging in the app; `Yana ko'rsatish` never appears for this query).
- **Actions:** row / `Batafsil` → `/urgent/<id>`; `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/<room_id>`; empty-state button `Tezkor advokat` → `/urgent`; `Qayta urinish` on error.
- **States:** loading: 120 pt skeleton rows; empty: zap icon `So'rovlar yo'q` / `Yuqoridan xizmat tanlab, birinchi so'rovni yuboring.` + `Tezkor advokat`; error: `ErrorView`; 30 s poll only while the socket is down; socket frames refetch.
- **Data:** `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/me` — same fields as the `/urgent` rows. Socket `urgent_advokat.*`.
- **UX note:** a plain list; differs from `/urgent` only by having no hero/services. The empty text says "from above" (`Yuqoridan`) although nothing is above on this page.

### `/lawyers` — `Advokat va yuristlar`

- **Who:** guest and client (the tab page is reachable for both; `src/widgets/tab-bar/tabConfig.ts`: the client's bar is `index · services · ai · works · messages` — **`lawyers` has no slot in the client tab bar**; it is only in the `ALWAYS` list, so it is pushed from Home chips, `/marketplace` redirects, notifications and deep links). Guests see the whole directory; only the AI search needs sign-in. Also the operator's remote-assist map (`client.marketplace`) can focus the search pill and open the filter sheet.
- **Purpose:** find a verified advokat/yurist — by describing the problem (AI), by name, or by filters — and open their page.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (`LawyersPage`, one list with a header; header shrinks in AI mode or when a search/filter is on)
  1. `TabPage` title `Advokat va yuristlar`, subtitle `Tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar, real reyting va shaffof narxlar.`
  2. **Promo rail** (`PromoRail`, only in the "calm" state: no name query, no filter, no area, not AI): one full-width banner at a time, swipe for the next, max 6, expired (`ends_at` passed) dropped, one per seller+service. Source: `GET /promotions/active?placement=banner&limit=6`, else the list's own `banners`. `PromoCard`: eyebrow `Top` (megaphone) + `{n} kun qoldi`; **image mode** (`banner_mode: image` / a picture that loads): photo under a dark left fade, title (`service_title` → `title` → `seller_name` → `LexGo tavsiya qiladi`), sub (seller name for a service banner; else the seller's own `subtitle` line, else `Advokat|Yurist · <area> · <region>`), white CTA (backend `cta_label`, but the default "Profilni ko'rish" is replaced by the app's `Profilni ko'rish`); **profile-card mode** (`profile_card`, no picture, or a 404 picture): dark brand gradient, title, sub, `Monogram` with rating ring, facts: star + rating + `{count} ta baho` or `Hali baholanmagan`, years + `yil tajriba`, CTA.
  3. **Hero card** (`MarketHero`, blue gradient): kicker `Marketplace`; headline `Muammoingizni yozing — ` + cyan `AI mos mutaxassisni` + ` topib beradi`; lead `Vaziyatingizni oddiy so'zlar bilan tushuntiring yoki ism, yo'nalish, xizmat nomini yozing — tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar orasidan eng moslari saralanadi.`; **search pill** (white, blue border): gradient star tile, input placeholder `Muammo yoki mutaxassis nomi…` (max 200 chars), x-circle clear, gradient arrow button (`Topish`); `Ko'p tanlanadi:` + up to three example chips (real service titles from `/marketplace/meta`, one per category, ≤ 30 chars); **counters strip** (`HeroStats`): `mutaxassis` (`total`), `xizmat` (distinct service ids among loaded cards), `eng arzon xizmat` (lowest `basePrice` among loaded cards, else "—"); "—" while loading; **showcase** inside the hero (calm state only, `MarketShowcase`): label `Tavsiya etilgan mutaxassislar`, a snapping carousel of up to 10 loaded specialists (rated first, then rating, reviews, years), `ShowCard` 224 pt: a **static demo portrait** per kind (`assets/market/demo-advokat-card.webp` / `demo-yurist-card.webp`, not the seller's photo), rating badge (only when rated), verified shield seal, eyebrow `ADVOKAT · Toshkent`, name, first area or first service title, `{n} yil tajriba` or `Hali baholanmagan`, `{price} so'mdan` or `Narx kelishiladi`, round arrow; page dots.
  4. **Filter button** `Filtrlar` (sliders icon, badge = number of active filters).
  5. **Active filter chips** (`ActiveFilters`, horizontal, tap removes): `Advokatlar`/`Yuristlar`, region name, service title (or `Xizmat`), category title (or `Kategoriya`, hidden when a service is set), `{n}+ yil`, `★ {n}+`, `{n} gacha`, then `Tozalash`.
  6. **Count** `{n} ta mutaxassis` (server `total`).
  7. **Result cards** (`MarketCard`, 10 per page, FlashList): 3 pt brand line (gold when promoted, plus a gold `Top` badge top-right), `Monogram` with rating ring, name + blue shield (`is_verified`), chip `Advokat` (blue) / `Yurist` (violet) + map-pin place (region, district); three counters between hairlines: gold star rating + `{count} ta baho` or `Hali baholanmagan` / `Baholar to'planmoqda`, years + `yil tajriba`, `{n}%` + `muvaffaqiyat` (only when `total_cases` ≥ 5, else `—` and `muvaffaqiyat · to'planmoqda`); first service title + `+{n} ta`; dashed footer: `{price} so'mdan` / `Narx kelishiladi`, `Hozir band` (availability offline), pill `Batafsil`. Only `is_verified` sellers with a name are shown.
  8. Footer `Yana ko'rsatish · {remaining}` while the server has more.
  9. **Filter sheet** (`FilterSheet`, title `Filtrlar`, draft applied on tap): `Turi` tiles `Hammasi` / `Advokatlar` / `Yuristlar`; `Reyting` tiles `Farqi yo'q` / `4.0+` / `4.5+`; `Tajriba` stepped track `Farqi yo'q` / `3+ yil` / `5+ yil` / `10+ yil` (applied on the device — the server has no `min_experience`); `Narx (gacha)` stepped track `Farqi yo'q` / `{200 000} gacha` / `{300 000} gacha` / `{500 000} gacha` (compact number format); collapsible pickers `Hudud` (`Farqi yo'q`, `Toshkent`, `Samarqand`, `Farg'ona`, `Buxoro`, `Namangan`, `Andijon`, `Qashqadaryo`, `Xorazm`, `Jizzax`, `Navoiy`), `Yo'nalish` (`Farqi yo'q`, `Jinoiy ishlar`, `Fuqarolik ishlari`, `Oila ishlari`, `Mehnat nizolari`, `Soliq nizolari`, `Iqtisodiy ishlar`, `Ma'muriy ishlar`, `Ko'chmas mulk`, `Migratsiya`, `Intellektual mulk`), `Kategoriya` (meta categories, shown only when any), `Xizmat` (meta services, narrowed by the picked category), `Saralash` (`Tavsiya`, `Reyting` — sorted on the device because the server 500s on `sort=rating`, `Tajriba`, `Arzonroq`); pinned bar: reset icon button (`Tozalash`, keeps the name query) and gradient `Ko'rsatish · {live count}`.
  10. **AI mode** (`AiResults`, after `Topish` / an example chip / `?ai=1&q=`): same header; then (a) **intro** when fewer than 3 chars: `Muammoingizni yozing` / `AI vaziyatingizni tushunib, tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar orasidan eng mosini topadi. Bu yuridik maslahat emas.`, `Ko'p tanlanadi:` chips (meta service titles ≤ 34 chars, one per category, else `Ajrashish va aliment`, `Ijara shartnomasi`, `Biznes uchun yuridik yordam`); (b) **guest**: lock `AI qidiruv uchun tizimga kiring` + `Kirish` → `/auth/login`; (c) **searching**: banner `AI mos mutaxassislarni qidirmoqda…` + three card skeletons; (d) **results**: banner `AI {n} ta mos mutaxassis topdi`, the model's `summary`, disclaimer (backend `disclaimer` when the app is in Uzbek, else `Bu qidiruv faqat mos advokat/yuristlarni topishga yordam beradi, yuridik maslahat emas.`), cards with an **AI strip** (`{pct}% mos` or `AI tanlovi`, a progress bar, up to three reasons), footer `Oddiy qidiruvga qaytish`; (e) **none**: `AI bu so'rov bo'yicha mos mutaxassis topmadi` / `Oddiy qidiruv yoki filtrlar bilan urinib ko'ring.` + `Oddiy qidiruvga qaytish`; (f) error: `ErrorView`. Region and type filters are passed to the AI search.
- **Actions:** typing in the pill (not in AI mode) filters by name after 400 ms (`q`); arrow / return key / example chip → AI search with that sentence; x clears both and leaves AI mode; banner tap → `/specialist/<seller_user_id>` (or an https `cta_url` in the browser); showcase card / result card → `/specialist/<user_id>`; AI result card → `/specialist/<user_id>?tab=services`; `Filtrlar` → sheet; chip x removes one filter; `Tozalash` resets filters and the text; `Yana ko'rsatish` → next page; `Operatorga murojaat` → `/support?new=1&topic=marketplace`; pull to refresh. Deep links `/marketplace?q&area&region&service&ai` are applied once (`useMarketParams`), region only if it is one of the 10 keys.
- **States:** list loading / any new filter: three `LawyerCardSkeleton`s (list is cleared, scroll resets to top); error: `ErrorView` under the header with `Qayta urinish`; empty: users icon `Bu yo'nalishda hozircha advokat yo'q. Filtrni o'zgartirib ko'ring.` (+ `Oddiy filtrlar bilan qidirib ko'ring yoki operatordan yordam so'rang.` and the `Operatorga murojaat` button when a name query is set) with action `Tozalash`; counters "—" while loading; filter count button spinner while the count loads; AI states as above. Filters survive tab switches (zustand store).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                       | Used for                                               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/lawyers?limit=10&offset=&sort=recommended    | experience                                             | price_asc&seller_type=advokat                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | yurist&region=&specialization=&category_id=&service_id=&min_rating=&price_max=&q=` | the result pages, `total`, fallback banners, showcase, counters (for `Reyting` sort or a min-experience filter the app reads up to 500 rows in 100s and filters/sorts locally) | `items[]`/`data[]`/`lawyers[]`: `id`, `user_id`, `public_id`/`lexgo_id`, `lawyer_name`/`name`, `region`, `district`, `specializations[]`, `languages[]`, `experience_years`, `rating`, `reviews_count`, `rating_status`, `price_from`, `base_hourly_price`/`price`, `price_to`, `bio`, `is_verified`/`lexgo_verified`, `verification_badge.label`, `seller_type`, `total_cases`, `wins_count`, `success_rate`, `completed_orders`, `organization_name`, `created_at`, `promotion{active, service_title, boost_score}`, `is_sponsored`, `availability.status`, `services[]{id, title, base_price, selected_price, category_title, category_id, is_sponsored}`; `total`; `banners[]` |

| `GET /promotions/active?placement=banner&limit=6`              | the promo rail                                         | `items[]`: `id`/`promotion_id`, `placement`, `seller_user_id`, `seller_name`, `seller_profile{seller_type, region, headline, rating, reviews_count, rating_status, experience_years, is_verified}`, `service_id`, `service_title`, `title`, `subtitle`, `cta_label`, `cta_url`, `image_url`/`banner_image_url`, `banner_mode`, `ends_at` |

| `GET /marketplace/lawyers/{user_id}` (→ `GET /lawyers` on 404) | profile of an AI hit that came without one             | as the list row                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

- **UX note:** on a phone the search pill and the first result card are what matter; the promo rail, the three counters and the in-hero showcase push the first card below two screens. Confusing today: one input does two different things (name filter vs AI search on submit), the showcase uses stock portraits instead of real photos, and the tab is not in the client's tab bar although the page is built as a tab.

### `/lawyer/[id]` — redirect

- **Who:** anyone. **Purpose:** legacy address. **Layout/Actions:** `app/lawyer/[id].tsx` renders nothing: `<Redirect>` to `/specialist/<id>` keeping `?tab=` when present. **States/Data:** none.

### `/marketplace` — redirect

- **Who:** anyone. **Purpose:** the stack-level address of the directory used by other roles, push notifications and Home chips. **Layout/Actions:** `app/marketplace.tsx` redirects to `/(tabs)/lawyers` forwarding every query param (`q`, `area`, `region`, `service`, `ai`), which `useMarketParams` applies once. **States/Data:** none.

### `/specialist/[id]` — specialist page (no page title; a transparent back header over the cover)

- **Who:** guest and client. A guest sees everything; `Sotib olish` sends a guest to `/auth/login`. A signed-in user may be blocked from buying with a toast/inline reason (`useBuyGate`): own profile `Bu sizning profilingiz.`, a professional account `Xizmatni faqat mijoz akkaunti sotib oladi.`, unverified seller `Mutaxassis hali tasdiqlanmagan — xarid yopiq.`, busy `Mutaxassis hozir band — keyinroq urinib ko'ring.` The chat button exists only while the client has a running paid order with this specialist whose chat the backend opened.
- **Purpose:** read a specialist's public profile, pick one of their services and send a purchase request.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** (`SpecialistPage` → `SpecialistView`; `?tab=services|about|reviews`, default services)
  1. **Cover** (`ProfileCover`): dark brand gradient band with glows; transparent light `Header` (back).
  2. **Identity** (`SpecialistIdentity`): 92 pt avatar (initials) on the cover's edge with a blue check seal when verified; name (h2); map-pin `Region · district`; globe languages (`O'zbek`, `Русский`, `English`…); five stars + `4,8 · {n} ta baho` or the backend's `unrated_display` text / `Hali baholanmagan`; pills: `Advokat` / `Yurist`, `public_id` (LGP-…), `Tasdiqlangan profil`, `Top` (promoted or any running promotion), `Yangi` (fewer than 5 reviews and ≤ 60 days old), `Hozir band` (availability offline).
  3. **Six numbers** (`SpecialistStats`, 2-column tiles, unset = "—"): `Reyting`, `Baho` (reviews), `Yil tajriba`, `Ish` (`total_cases`), `Muvaffaqiyat` (`{n}%` when `total_cases` > 0), `Bajarilgan buyurtma` (`completed_orders`).
  4. **Tabs** (`ProfileTabs`, sliding): `Xizmatlar` · `Haqida` · `Fikrlar`.
  5. **`Xizmatlar va narxlar`** (services tab, `ServicesPanel`): link pill `Narx qanday hisoblanadi?`; lead `Xizmatni tanlang — narx mutaxassis tomonidan belgilangan.`; radio cards (`ServiceOption`): `Tavsiya etilgan` pill (server `is_sponsored` / a running `service_boost` naming it; those first), title, category, the seller's note, dashed line, price or `Narx kelishiladi`; empty `Hozircha sotuvdagi xizmat yo'q.`; the blocked reason in amber when any; then **`SafeDeal`** box: `Xizmat kafolatlari` with rows (`Qachon: {text}`, `Yechim: {text}`, `Hal qilish muddati: {time}`) from the picked service's runtime configuration, or when none `LexGo orqali xarid` / `To'lov, chat va qo'ng'iroqlar LexGo orqali amalga oshadi.`
  6. **`Mutaxassis haqida`** (about tab, `AboutPanel`): bio or `Mutaxassis o'zi haqida hali yozmagan.`; `Yo'nalishlar` chips (humanised slugs); `Tashkilot` block when set (education, licence, bar association are never shown); then **`Yutuqlar`** (`AchievementsSection`, badge = total; hidden while loading, when empty or on error): rows with the type label (`Sud nizosi natijasi`, `Sudgacha hal etish`, `Mediatsiya kelishuvi`, `Korporativ bitim`, `Soliq va bojxona`, `Intellektual mulk`, `Davlat organiga murojaat`, `Mehnat va kadrlar`, `Komplayens va audit`, `Nashr va tadqiqot`, `Malaka va sertifikat`, `Mukofot va e'tirof`, `Bepul yordam`, `Boshqa`), title, `date · category`; show-more for the next 10.
  7. **`Mijozlar fikri`** (reviews tab, `ReviewsPanel` → `ReviewsList`): per review author (or `Mijoz`), five stars, date, comment, `Mutaxassis javobi` block when the seller replied; empty `Hali sharh yo'q` / `Birinchi buyurtmadan keyin sharhlar shu yerda chiqadi.` (reviews come embedded in the profile; no separate call).
  8. **Bottom bar** (`BuyBar`, pinned): picked service title or `Xizmatni tanlang`, price `{n} so'm` (picked service, else the profile's "from" price, else "—"), round chat icon button (`Chat`, only with a running order's room), gradient `Sotib olish` (or `Xizmat tanlash` when nothing priced is on sale).
  9. **Price explainer sheet** (`PriceExplainer`, from `Narx qanday hisoblanadi?`): title `Narx qanday hisoblanadi?`, lead `Narxni mutaxassis o'zi qo'yadi, lekin LexGo belgilagan oraliqdan chiqa olmaydi.`, `«{service}»: katalog narxi {base} so'm, mutaxassis narxi {price} so'm.`, five steps `Katalog narxi` / `Hisob xizmatning LexGo katalogidagi asosiy narxidan boshlanadi.`, `Hudud` / region coefficients (`Toshkent ×1.15 · …`) or `Hududga qarab narx biroz farq qiladi.`, `Tajriba` / `{min}–{max} yil +{n}% · {min}+ yil +{n}%` or `Tajribali mutaxassis uchun ustama qo'shiladi.`, `Reyting va natijalar` / `Reyting {rating}+ ({reviews}+ sharh): +{percent}%`, `Yuqori natijalar: +{percent}%`, `Sohada {years}+ yil tajriba: +{percent}%` or `Yuqori reyting va yaxshi natijalar uchun ustama qo'shiladi.`, `Ruxsat etilgan oraliq` / `Mutaxassis narxi tavsiya narxning {min}–{max}% oralig'ida bo'ladi.`; foot `Narx o'zgarsa, faqat yangi buyurtmalarga ta'sir qiladi.`; skeletons while the policy loads.
  10. **Purchase sheet** (`PurchaseFlow`, from `Sotib olish`, snaps 72/93 %):
      - `PurchaseHead`: avatar, seller name, service title, X; gradient card `Xizmatni sotib olish` + the big total (`so'm`) — the backend quote's `total_price` (else the service price).
      - `PurchaseSteps`: `1 · So'rov`, `2 · To'lov tasdig'i`, `3 · Chat va ish` (done green, current blue, cancelled red).
      - `PurchaseForm`: `1 · Bog'lanish usuli` cards `Chat` / `Audio` / `Video` (default chat); `2 · Vaziyatingizni qisqacha yozing` textarea placeholder `Masalan: ijara shartnomasi bo'yicha maslahat kerak`, counter `n/1000`; `3 · Qulay vaqt` chips `Bugun`, `Bugun kechqurun`, `Ertaga`, `Shu hafta ichida` (tap again clears) + input placeholder `Masalan: bugun 18:00 dan keyin` (≤ 120); `4 · Qanchalik tez`: `Qachon bog'lanishsin?` chips `15 daqiqa ichida`, `1 soat ichida`, `Bugun`, `Ertaga`; `Ish qachon kerak?` chips `Bugun`, `Ertaga`, `Keyinroq` (both optional, toggle off); `5 · To'lov usuli` only when the platform enables online providers: `Telegram (operator tasdiqlaydi)`, `Payme`, `Click`; **options** `Qo'shimcha shartlar` / `Hammasi ixtiyoriy: hech narsa tanlamasangiz asosiy narx qoladi. Bir nechtasini tanlash mumkin.` — the server's sections as checkbox rows with `+{n} so'm` / `+{n}%`; **quote panel** `Asosiy narx`, one line per picked option, `Jami to'lov` (bold), `Narx hisoblanmoqda…` while re-quoting, error text + `Qayta hisoblash`; note `Narxni LexGo belgilaydi. To'liq to'lov tasdiqlangach buyurtma mutaxassisga yuboriladi.`; red error line; `Telegram orqali yuborish` after an online provider refused; the blocked reason.
      - Pinned bar: `Jami to'lov` + amount, gradient `So'rov yuborish` (`Yuborilmoqda…` while sending; disabled while blocked or until the quote matches the current selection).
      - `PurchaseReceipt` (replaces the form once sent): tinted box with icon, title per phase — `pending` `So'rovingiz qabul qilindi`, `paid` `To'lov tasdiqlandi`, `chat` `Xizmat faollashtirildi`, `completed` `Bu xizmat yakunlangan`, `cancelled` `So'rov bekor qilindi`; for an already-active order (409): `Bu mutaxassis va xizmat bo'yicha faol so'rovingiz bor.` / `Bu xizmat sizda allaqachon faol`; `work_id`; lead per phase — `To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanmoqda. Tasdiqlangach buyurtma mutaxassisga yuboriladi.`, `Buyurtma mutaxassisga yuborildi. U qabul qilgach xavfsiz chat ochiladi.`, `Mutaxassis buyurtmani qabul qildi, xavfsiz chat ochildi. Unga yozishingiz mumkin.`, `Ish yakunlandi. Natijani buyurtmalaringizda ko'rasiz.`, `To'lov tasdiqlanmadi. Pul yechilmagan.`; `Tasdiqlovchilarga xabar yetib bormadi. So'rov saqlandi — biz uni qo'lda tekshiramiz.` when `telegram_sent` is false; `Holat avtomatik yangilanadi` (pending/paid); buttons `To'lash` (pending with a `payment_url`: in-app browser, re-read on return), `Chatni ochish` (chat phase with a room), `Qayta yuborish` (cancelled), `Hozir tekshirish` (pending/paid), `Buyurtmalarimga o'tish`, `Yopish` (cancelled). Phase: before the order is in the list — `cancelled|canceled|declined|rejected|lost|refunded|refund_pending|expired` → cancelled, `completed|rated|done|closed` → completed, `payment.status == paid` or `in_progress|started` → paid, else pending; once found in `/marketplace/me/orders` its own stage decides (chat when `can_start_chat` + room).
- **Actions:** tabs; service card → picks it (bar price updates; one runtime-configuration read per picked service); `Narx qanday hisoblanadi?` → explainer sheet; `Sotib olish` → login / blocked toast / `Xizmat tanlash` → `/services?lawyer=<id>&name=<name>` / purchase sheet; chat icon → `/chat/<room_id>` of the running order; in the sheet: option checkboxes → `POST …/quote`; `So'rov yuborish` → `POST …/purchase-request`; `To'lash` → opens `payment_url`; `Hozir tekshirish` → re-reads the order; `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/<room>`; `Buyurtmalarimga o'tish` → `/(tabs)/orders`; `Qayta yuborish` → back to the form; show-more in `Yutuqlar`.
- **States:** profile loading: `SpecialistSkeleton` (cover, avatar ring, name/chips, tab bar, one card); profile error: `Header` + `ErrorView` (`Qayta urinish`); not found (null after the `/lawyers` fallback): `Mutaxassis topilmadi` / `Profil marketplace'dan olib tashlangan yoki manzil noto'g'ri.`; services fall back to the profile's embedded `services[]` until `/services` answers (404 → none); rating 404 → profile's own rating fields; achievements hidden on error/empty; explainer skeletons; quote pending/error; purchase errors: 401 → login, 409 `marketplace_order_already_active` → receipt in "existing" wording, provider codes `payment_provider_not_configured|payment_provider_unavailable|payment_invoice_rejected|marketplace_payment_provider_invalid` → `Telegram orqali yuborish`, other → `ErrorView` text inline; after sending, the order is re-read on socket events (`marketplace.*`, `marketplace_*`, `payment.*`, `order.*`, `order_*`, `notification.created`) and on `Hozir tekshirish` — nothing polls.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                    | Used for                                                                                       | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/lawyers/{user_id}` (404 → `GET /lawyers`, matched by `user_id`/`id`)      | the profile                                                                                    | `id`, `user_id`, `public_id`/`lexgo_id`, `lawyer_name`/`name`, `region`, `district`, `specializations[]`, `languages[]`, `experience_years`, `rating`, `reviews_count`, `rating_status`, `price_from`, `base_hourly_price`, `price_to`, `bio`, `is_verified`/`lexgo_verified`, `verification_badge.label`, `seller_type`, `total_cases`, `wins_count`, `partial_wins_count`, `success_rate`, `completed_orders`, `organization_name`, `created_at`, `promotion{active,…}`, `is_sponsored`, `availability.status`, `services[]`, `reviews[]{id, rating, comment, author_name | client_name                                               | reviewer_name | author.name, created_at, reply | seller_reply}` |
| `GET /marketplace/lawyers/{user_id}/rating` (404 → none)                                    | stars + review count (rating v3)                                                               | `public_rating`/`rating`, `reviews_count`, `rating_status`, `unrated_display`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `GET /platform/policies` (no auth)                                                          | price explainer                                                                                | `items.marketplace_pricing{default_min_percent, default_max_percent, experience_multipliers[]{min_years,max_years,percent}, region_multipliers{}, client_rating_premium{rating_min,reviews_min,percent}, seller_premium.percent, sector_experience_premium{min_years,percent}}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
