- **UX note:** name + stars + the pinned `Sotib olish` bar with the price are the first screen; the six tiles and the explainer can be cut or collapsed. Confusing today: the page loads up to eight requests before the client taps anything, the "Top" pill and the gold "Tavsiya etilgan" mean paid placement but read like a recommendation, and the purchase sheet asks five numbered questions plus options before showing the only real total.

### `/orders` — `Buyurtmalarim`

- **Who:** client (a hidden tab: `app/(tabs)/orders.tsx` → `lazyTab(OrdersPage)`, reached from Profil and from a purchase receipt). A guest gets a lock empty-state `Buyurtmalarni ko'rish uchun tizimga kiring` / `Xaridlaringiz hisobingizga bog'langan.` with the button `Kirish` → `/auth/login`. No plan or entitlement gating.
- **Purpose:** see every marketplace service I bought, where each one stands, and open one to pay, chat, call or rate.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Tab header (`TabPage`): title `Buyurtmalarim`, subtitle `Marketplace orqali sotib olgan xizmatlaringiz: holati, chat va qo'ng'iroq.`
  2. Filter bar (`OrdersFilterBar`, full-bleed): search box placeholder `Xizmat, ADV raqam yoki mutaxassis` (matches work number, service title, specialist name) + one `sliders` icon button labelled `Filtrlar` that carries a dot while a status or a sort other than the default is on.
  3. Applied chips (only while something is applied): the stage label (`To'lov kutilmoqda` / `Faol` / `Yakunlangan` / `Bekor qilingan`) and/or the sort label, each with an `x` that clears it.
  4. One `MarketOrderCard` per purchase (10 px gaps): top row = work number `ADV-…` (accent, bold; `—` if missing) + `StageBadge` pill; service title (h4, 2 lines); `OrderTrack` — four slim bars (request → payment → work → done, green done / blue current / grey ahead; hidden entirely for a cancelled or refunded order); footer = `specialist name · date` and the price `N so'm` (accent) when > 0.
  5. Filter sheet (`OrdersFilterSheet`, title `Filtrlar`, snaps 70/90 %): group `Holat` with five `SheetOption` rows, each with a coloured dot and a live count — `Hammasi` (grey), `To'lov kutilmoqda` (warn), `Faol` (accent), `Yakunlangan` (ok), `Bekor qilingan` (grey2); group `Saralash` — `Avval yangilari`, `Avval eskilari`, `Narx: yuqoridan pastga`, `Narx: pastdan yuqoriga`; pinned bottom bar = `rotate-ccw` icon button `Filtrlarni tozalash` + gradient button `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N`.
- **Stage enum (list filter, `marketStageOf` from `status` + `payment_status`):** `pending` = `To'lov kutilmoqda` (pending_payment / payment_pending / pending_confirmation / waiting_payment / `pending` unpaid, or a before-payment status — seller_selection, sent_to_seller, new, created, assigned, awaiting_acceptance, pending_acceptance, accepted, awaiting_advance, advance_pending, awaiting_payment — while payment_status is not paid/held/partially_paid/advance_paid); `active` = `Faol` (everything else); `completed` = `Yakunlangan` (completed, rated, done, closed); `cancelled` = `Bekor qilingan` (cancelled, canceled, declined, rejected, lost, refunded, refund_pending, or payment_status refunded).
- **Badge enum (`FlowPhase`, `mf.phase.*`, same words on the detail lead):** `received` `Qabul qilindi` · `assigned` `Mutaxassis javobi kutilmoqda` · `advance` `To'lov kutilmoqda` · `pending` `To'lov kutilmoqda` · `paid` `To'langan` · `reviewing` `Materiallar o'rganilmoqda` · `in_progress` `Ish jarayonida` · `rework` `Qayta bajarishda` · `cancelReview` `Bekor qilish ko'rib chiqilmoqda` · `resolution` `Qaror kutilmoqda` · `replacing` `Mutaxassis almashtirilmoqda` · `refund` `Pul qaytarilmoqda` · `refunded` `Pul qaytarildi` · `completed` `Yakunlandi` · `rated` `Baholandi` · `cancelled` `Bekor qilindi` · `attention` `Operator ko'rib chiqmoqda`. Tone: cancelled = err, completed/rated = ok, refunded = grey, waiting phases (received, assigned, advance, pending, cancelReview, resolution, replacing, refund, rework, attention) = warn, paid/reviewing/in_progress = blue; the pill pulses ("live") for paid, reviewing, in_progress, rework.
- **Actions:** search (client-side); filter button → sheet; chips clear a filter; tap a card → `/orders/[id]` with the order id; empty-state button `Mutaxassis tanlash` → `/(tabs)/lawyers`; pull to refresh (spinner for 0.8 s while the list refetches).
- **States:** loading = three `MarketOrderSkeleton` cards; empty (no purchases) = `shopping-bag` empty-state `Hali marketplace xizmati sotib olinmagan` / `Mutaxassislar ro'yxatidan xizmat tanlang — buyurtma shu yerda chiqadi.` + `Mutaxassis tanlash`; filter empty = compact `filter` empty-state `Filtrga mos buyurtma topilmadi`; error with no cached rows = `ErrorView` with retry; offline = the generic ErrorView / last cached list (React Query cache); socket events re-read the list after 0.5 s.
- **Data:** only the marketplace list is read — `GET /orders` is wired into `mergeOrders` but the page passes an empty service list, so catalogue orders are not listed here.

| Endpoint                                      | Used for                                            | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders?role_view=client` | the list (sorted by `created_at` desc on the phone) | items[]: `id`/`order_id`, `work_id`, `status`, `payment_status` (or `payment.status`), `service_title`, `price` (or `payment.amount`), `created_at`, `lawyer_name`/`lawyer.name`, `details.*`; top-level `client_actions` (keys → which actions are offered) |
| user socket (`useMarketSync`)                 | refetch the list/history                            | events `marketplace.*`, `marketplace_*`, `order.*`, `order_*`, `payment.*`, `notification.created` (inner `data.event` order*\*/marketplace*\*/payment_paid with `target_type: marketplace_order`); fields read: `order_id`, `work_id`, `room_id`/`chat_url` |

- **UX note:** the first screen must show the search, the filter button and the first two cards (number + badge + title are what the client scans for); the four-bar track and the price can go small. Confusing today: the badge says `To'lov kutilmoqda` for two different phases (`advance` and `pending`) and the filter tab also uses the same words, so "waiting" cannot be told apart from the list.

### `/orders/[id]` — the order number (`ADV-…`), fallback `Buyurtma`

- **Who:** client. Unauthenticated → lock empty-state `Buyurtmalarni ko'rish uchun tizimga kiring` + `Kirish` → `/auth/login`. The same route also renders a catalogue service order (`GET /orders`) when the key is not a marketplace order, and a yurist/advokat looking at an open service order gets accept/decline (`SellerActions`) — those are not client UI and are only mentioned.
- **Purpose:** follow one purchase from payment to result: what to pay now, who is doing it, the live checklist, the deadline, the result files, the 15-minute rating/complaint window, the warranty, the money and the history; chat and call from the footer when the backend has unlocked contact.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`AdminPage` with title = `work_id`, subtitle = service title; body is a `ScrollView` with pull-to-refresh; every card below renders only when its condition holds):
  1. `DetailHeader` card: `ADV-…` (accent) + `StageBadge` (same phase enum as the list); service title (h3); price `N so'm` (h2 accent) when > 0; `OrderTrack` with labels `So'rov` · `To'lov` · `Ish` · `Yakun` (hidden for cancelled/refund/refunded).
  2. Lead. Phase `pending` → `PendingNote` (warn box): `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda` / `To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanmoqda. Tasdiqlangach chat ochiladi.` / `Holat avtomatik yangilanadi` + soft button `Hozir tekshirish` (refetches). Otherwise, unless the resolution offer is open (`resolution.can_choose`), `FlowLead`: phase icon + phase word + the backend's own `client_status_message` if present, else the client lead text: `received` `Buyurtmangiz qabul qilindi. To'lov tasdiqlangach mutaxassisga yuboriladi.` · `assigned` `To'lov tasdiqlandi. Buyurtma mutaxassisga yuborildi — u belgilangan vaqt ichida javob beradi. Qabul qilgach himoyalangan chat ochiladi.` · `advance` `Mutaxassis buyurtmani qabul qildi. To'lov tasdiqlangach himoyalangan chat va qo'ng'iroq ochiladi.` · `pending` `To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanmoqda. Tasdiqlangach buyurtma mutaxassisga yuboriladi.` · `paid` `To'lov tasdiqlandi. Mutaxassis ishni boshlagach, muddat shu buyurtmada ko'rinadi.` · `reviewing` `Mutaxassis so'rovingiz va materiallaringizni o'rganmoqda. Shundan keyin ish muddatini belgilaydi.` · `in_progress` `Ijrochi ishlamoqda. Bajarilgan bandlar nazorat ro'yxatida darhol ko'rinadi.` · `rework` `Shikoyatingiz asosli deb topildi. Mutaxassis ishni qayta bajarmoqda.` · `cancelReview` `Bekor qilish so'rovi operatorda. Qaror chiqquncha muddat to'xtatilgan.` · `resolution` `Buyurtmangizni operator ko'rib chiqmoqda. Natija shu yerda ko'rinadi.` · `replacing` `Buyurtmangiz boshqa mutaxassisga taklif qilindi. U qabul qilgach, yangi himoyalangan chat ochiladi. Qo'shimcha to'lov yo'q.` · `refund` `Pulni qaytarish tasdiqlanmoqda. Chat va qo'ng'iroqlar yopilgan.` · `refunded` `Buyurtma bekor qilindi va to'lov qaytarildi.` · `completed` `Ish yakunlandi. Natijani ko'ring va belgilangan vaqt ichida baholang.` (or, when the rating window is already closed, `completedClosed` `Ish yakunlandi. Natija va fayllar shu buyurtmada saqlanadi.`) · `rated` `Ish yakunlandi va baholandi. Rahmat!` · `cancelled` `Buyurtma bekor qilingan.` · `attention` `Buyurtmangizni operator ko'rib chiqmoqda. Natija shu yerda ko'rinadi.` Box tint: err for cancelled, warn for waiting phases, ok for completed/rated/refunded, accent otherwise.
  3. `ActiveCallBar` (only while chat mode is `open` and a live call exists in the room): call icon + call title or `Qo'ng'iroq davom etmoqda` + button `Qo'shilish` → `/call/{roomId}?callId=…`.
  4. `ResolutionCard` (only while `resolution.can_choose` and the offer lists options): warn box, title `Qanday davom etamiz?`, `HH:MM:SS` countdown to `offer.deadline_at` (red under 5 min), lead `Buyurtmani hozircha hech bir mutaxassis qabul qilmadi. Davom etish yo'lini o'zingiz tanlang.`, quoted `Operator xabari` + `offer.message`, one button per option — `Boshqa mutaxassis` (gradient, first) / `Pulni to'liq qaytarish` (line). Expired → grey box `Tanlash muddati tugadi. Buyurtmani operator hal qiladi, natijasi shu yerda ko'rinadi.` and the order is re-read. Tapping opens `ResolutionSheet` (title = the choice, subtitle `Buyurtma boshqa mos mutaxassisga taklif qilinadi. Oldingi mutaxassis unda qatnashmaydi.` / `Buyurtma bekor qilinadi. To'lov qilingan bo'lsa, pul to'liq qaytariladi va holati shu buyurtmada ko'rinadi.`, field `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Operatorga qo'shimcha ma'lumot`, submit `Tasdiqlash`, red for refund). Toasts: `Tanlovingiz qabul qilindi.` / `Pulni qaytarish so'rovi yaratildi. Holati shu buyurtmada ko'rinadi.` / `Buyurtma bekor qilindi. Sizdan to'lov olinmagan edi.`
  5. `WorkTimeCard` (one countdown box, only when there is something to count): phase `reviewing` → `Materiallarni o'rganish` with `{time} qoldi`, ticking every second, warn under 3 h with `3 soatdan kam vaqt qoldi`, expired `O'rganish muddati o'tdi. Call-markaz bu kechikishni hal qilmoqda.`, the client's review note under `Izoh`; phase `rework` → `Qayta bajarish`, line `{n}-marta qayta bajarish` when iteration > 1, `Qayta bajarish muddati: {date}`, note titled `Call-markaz izohi`, expired `Muddat o'tdi`; phase `in_progress` → `Bajarish muddati`, `Muddat: {date}`, `Belgilangan muddat: {d}` (duration), ticks every 30 s, expired `Muddat o'tdi`; phase `assigned`/`replacing` → `Mutaxassis javobi` counting to `acceptance_deadline_at`, expired `Javob vaqti tugadi — call-markaz buyurtmani davom ettiradi.` At zero the order is re-read.
  6. `RefundCard` (phase `refund` or `refunded`): section `Pulni qaytarish` — amount `N so'm` + status pill (`Tasdiq kutilmoqda` pending / `Jarayonda` processing / `Operator qaytaradi` operator_required / `Qaytarildi` succeeded, refunded / `Rad etildi` failed / `To'lov olinmagan` not_charged); while manual and not finished a warn note `Qo'lda ko'rib chiqilmoqda` / `Qaytarishni to'lov tizimi emas, operator tasdiqlaydi. Natija shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; `Yangilangan: {date}`. Loading skeleton; error `Qaytarish holatini yuklab bo'lmadi.` + `Qayta urinish`. Hidden when the backend answers no refund.
  7. `PaymentRequestCard` (phase `advance` with an advance part, or `completed`/`rated` with a `final_payment`): warn box (ok once paid) — title `Oldindan to'lov so'raldi` / `Oldindan to'lov tasdiqlandi` or `Yakuniy to'lov so'raldi` / `Yakuniy to'lov tasdiqlandi`; status pill from `mf.pay.status.*` (`To'langan`, `Kutilmoqda`, `Amalga oshmadi`, `Bekor qilingan`, `Qaytarilgan`, `Muddati o'tgan`, `Yaratildi`, `To'lashga tayyor`, `Ushlab turilgan`, `Qaytarish kerak`); stage line `Oldindan to'lov ({pct}%)` / `Yakuniy to'lov ({pct}%)` (or without percent); amount h3; unpaid text `Chat va qo'ng'iroq oldindan to'lov tasdiqlangach ochiladi.` (advance) or `Ish yakunlandi. Qolgan summani to'lang — to'lov tasdiqlangach buyurtma hisob-kitobi yopiladi.` (final); then either gradient `To'lash` opening the provider `payment_url` in the browser, or the caption `To'lov so'rovi Telegram orqali yuboriladi. Tasdiqlangach holat shu yerda o'zi yangilanadi.`
  8. `AddPayNote` (status `waiting_payment` with an unpaid additional payment): tinted card `Qo'shimcha to'lov so'raldi` / `Mutaxassis ish hajmi uchun qo'shimcha to'lov so'radi. To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanguncha ish to'xtatilgan.` + amount + `Muddat: {when} gacha` (24 h after the payment's creation).
  9. `SellerCard` (when a specialist name is known; hidden while `waiting_replacement_acceptance`): square avatar, caption `Mutaxassis`, name + verified check, line `Advokat`/`Yurist` · public id · `★ 4.8` or `Hali baholanmagan`; chevron and tap → `/specialist/[id]` only when the public profile loaded.
  10. `RatingBanner`: already rated → one grey line `Siz baholadingiz: {n} ★` (+ comment). Window open → tinted card `Ishni baholang` with `mm:ss` countdown from `rating.remaining_seconds`, text `Baholash vaqti cheklangan. 1–2 yulduz avtomatik ravishda sifat shikoyatini ochadi.`, gradient `Baholash yoki shikoyat` → `/rate/{work_id}`. Window expired → `Baholash vaqti tugagan.` Otherwise nothing.
  11. `ComplaintCard` (TZ §10): open window → card `Xizmat sifati bo'yicha muammo bormi?` with `HH:MM:SS` + `Shikoyat uchun {time} qoldi` and line button `Shikoyat qilish` → `OrderComplaintSheet` (title `Shikoyat sababi`, subtitle `Xizmat sifati bo'yicha muammo bormi?`, textarea placeholder `Nima noto'g'ri bo'ldi? Masalan: natija kelishilganidek emas, muddat buzildi`, hint `Kamida 5 ta belgi. Shikoyatni call-markaz ko'rib chiqadi.`, submit `Shikoyatni yuborish`; success toast `Shikoyat yuborildi — call-markaz ko'rib chiqmoqda.`). Submitted → grey/tint note: `Shikoyat yuborildi — call-markaz ko'rib chiqmoqda.` until the ruling, then `Shikoyat asosli topildi — ish qayta bajarilmoqda.` (rework_required) / `Shikoyat bo'yicha pulni qaytarish qarori qabul qilindi.` (refund_required) / `Shikoyat rad etildi.` (rejected) / `Shikoyat ko'rib chiqildi va hal qilindi.` (resolved), with the `resolution_note` under it. Closed or never offered → nothing.
  12. `ResultCard` (when `result_text` or `result_files` exist): `Natija`, the text, one `file-text` row per file → in-app `FilePreview`.
  13. `ChecklistCard` (read-only; from the review stage on — reviewing, in_progress, rework, cancelReview, attention, completed, rated — or `paid` with `checklist_progress`; hidden when the list is empty): section `Nazorat ro'yxati`, lead `Ijrochi bajargan ishlar shu yerda darhol ko'rinadi.`, progress bar + `{done} / {total} bajarildi · N%` and `Majburiy: {done} / {total}`; one row per item: icon (done check / `skipped_with_reason` skip / pending circle), `client_title` or title, description, `Sabab: …` for a skipped item or the comment of a done one, shared files/images as paperclip links (open `FilePreview`), pill `Majburiy` on a pending required item, right-hand status word `Kutilmoqda` / `Bajarildi` / `Sabab bilan o'tkazildi`. Loading skeleton; error `Nazorat ro'yxatini yuklab bo'lmadi.`; empty `Bu xizmatda nazorat ro'yxatida yo'q.` is only shown to sellers (clients get no card).
  14. `PaymentsCard` (when `financials_url` exists and the phase is not cancelled, or the order was paid): section `To'lov bosqichlari` — a row per backend payment: label `Oldindan to'lov ({pct}%)` / `Yakuniy to'lov ({pct}%)` / `Xizmat to'lovi` / `Qo'shimcha to'lov` / `Qaytarish` / `To'lov` by stage word, amount (+ `To'langan: N` when partially paid) and a status pill; planned-but-absent split parts with the pill `Rejalashtirilgan`; `Hali to'lov yo'q` when empty; fact row `Yakuniy narx`; `To'lov rejasi: {a}% oldindan, {b}% yakunda` under a split; footer `Narx buyurtma berilganda qayd etilgan va keyin narx qoidalari o'zgarsa ham o'zgarmaydi.` or `Tarixiy hisob-kitob`. Loading skeleton; error `To'lov ma'lumotlarini yuklab bo'lmadi` + `Qayta urinish`. (Seller-only payout/commission/settlement rows are never drawn for the client.)
  15. `WarrantyCard` (when `warranty_url` exists and the phase is paid/reviewing/in_progress/rework/cancelReview/attention/completed/rated, or a claim exists): section `LexGo kafolati`, shield lead `Ijrochi vaqtida javob bermasa, muddatni buzsa, ishni tashlab ketsa yoki xizmatni bajarmasa, kafolat so'rovini yuboring. Call-markaz ko'rib chiqib, boshqa mutaxassis yoki pulni qaytarish haqida qaror qabul qiladi.`; no claim → soft button `Kafolatdan foydalanish` → `OrderWarrantySheet` (title `Kafolat so'rovi`, subtitle `Call-markaz so'rovni ko'rib chiqadi va qaror haqida xabar beradi. Qarorgacha hech narsa o'zgarmaydi.`; select `Nima bo'ldi?` placeholder `Sababni tanlang` with `Ijrochi vaqtida javob bermadi` / `Ish muddati buzildi` / `Ish asossiz tashlab ketildi` / `Kelishilgan xizmat bajarilmadi` / `Ijrochi faoliyati cheklangan`; textarea `Batafsil` placeholder `Nima bo'lganini qisqacha yozing`, help `Call-markaz shu izohni ko'radi.`; switch `Materiallarimni yangi mutaxassisga berishga roziman` with hint `Call-markaz boshqa mutaxassis tayinlasa, chatdagi fayllaringiz unga beriladi. Rozilik bermasangiz, yangi mutaxassis ularni ko'rmaydi.`; submit `So'rov yuborish`; a 409 `warranty_already_open` is treated as sent). Pending → warn note `So'rov call-markazda ko'rib chiqilmoqda. Qaror bo'yicha xabar beramiz.` Decided → `Call-markaz qaror qabul qildi.` + status pill (`Yangi` / `Hal qilingan` / `Rad etilgan`), resolution (`Boshqa mutaxassis` / `Pulni to'liq qaytarish` / `Rad etildi`) and `Qaror: …`.
  16. `SecondOpinionCard` (only when the order carries `second_opinion_promotion_url` / `actions.second_opinion_promotion` and the backend answers `visible: true`): accent-bordered card with the backend's `title`, `description`, `benefits[]` (check rows), an `x` (`Taklifni yopish`, only when `dismissible`) and a gradient CTA (`cta_label` or `Advokatlar guruhidan ikkinchi fikr`). Impression is posted once when half the card is on screen; tap posts `click` and opens `SecondOpinionSheet`: `Aloqa usuli` segmented (backend channels), `Advokatlar soni` stepper with `{min}–{max} ta advokat`, `Yo'nalishlar` chips (`Ixtiyoriy: bir nechtasini tanlang yoki o'zingiz yozing`, input placeholder `Masalan: Shartnomalar`), button `Narxni ko'rish` (`Hisoblanmoqda…`) → price box `Xizmat narxi` + amount + `Narx tanlovingiz bo'yicha hisoblandi. Tanlovni o'zgartirsangiz, narx qayta hisoblanadi.` + field `Nimani tekshirtirmoqchisiz?` (`Qisqacha yozing (ixtiyoriy)`) → `So'rov yuborish`; toast `So'rov yuborildi` and navigation to the created urgent request. Errors: `Tanlov yuklanmadi` + `Qayta urinish`, `Avval aloqa usulini tanlang`, `Aloqa usulini tanlang`, `Advokatlar soni {min} dan {max} gacha bo'lishi kerak`, `Advokatlar guruhidan ikkinchi fikr olish uchun avval LexGo advokat xizmatidan foydalanishingiz kerak`, `Internet bilan aloqa yo'q`, `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qaytadan urinib ko'ring`, `Bu buyurtma uchun ikkinchi fikr endi mavjud emas`, `Buyurtma topilmadi`.
  17. `PackageCard` (package orders, from `details.package_snapshot`): package icon + title, code, custom items (`*` when required) with descriptions, included/excluded list, footer `Paket xarid paytidagi holatda saqlangan.`
  18. Price: `PriceCard` when `details.pricing_snapshot` exists — section `Narx nimadan iborat`, one row per component (the backend's localized `explanation`, else `Xizmatning asosiy narxi` / `Tanlangan qo'shimcha` / `Chegirma`, else the code), `Jami` = `final_price`, `Tarixiy hisob-kitob` when `historical_policy_exact` is false. Otherwise `OptionsCard` from `details.option_quote`: `Tanlangan qo'shimcha shartlar` — `Asosiy narx`, each option line, `Jami to'lov` (bold accent).
  19. `OrderFacts` rows: `To'lov` pill (`To'langan` / `Bekor qilindi` / `Kutilmoqda`), `Bog'lanish usuli` (`Chat` / `Audio` / `Video`), `Qulay vaqt`, `Yaratilgan`, `Ish boshlangan`, `Topshirish muddati`, `Yakunlangan` (the last three only when the backend sent them); tinted card `Sizning izohingiz` with the client's note.
  20. `OrderTimeline` under `Holat tarixi`: oldest first, a rail of 22 px dots (green check done / blue clock current / red x failed) with label, date-time and note. Source = the unified `…/timeline` (`status` steps named from `mk6.status.*`: `Yangi`, `Ma'lumot kutilmoqda`, `Hujjat kutilmoqda`, `To'lov kutilmoqda` (waiting_payment, pending_payment), `Buyurtma qabul qilindi` (seller_selection), `Mutaxassisga yuborildi`, `To'langan`, `Qabul qilindi`, `Boshlandi`, `Jarayonda`, `Natija tayyor`, `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `Topshirildi`, `Tasdiqlashingiz kutilmoqda`, `Yakunlandi`, `Baholandi`, `Yo'qotildi`, `Bekor qilindi`, `Rad etildi`, `Qaytarildi`; other types: `Mutaxassis call-markaz tomonidan almashtirildi` + `Sabab: …`, `Pulni qaytarish: {status}`, `Call-markaz davom etish yo'lini tanlashni taklif qildi` / `Tanlov muddati tugadi` / `Tanlandi: boshqa mutaxassis` / `Tanlandi: pulni to'liq qaytarish`, `Bekor qilish so'raldi` / `Bekor qilish so'rovi ko'rib chiqildi: buyurtma davom etadi | yangi narx | bekor qilindi | mutaxassis almashtirildi | pul qaytariladi`; actor appended). Without a timeline: `GET /orders/{id}/status-history` or the steps built from the order (`So'rov yuborildi`, `To'lov tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda`, `To'lov tasdiqlandi`, `To'lov tasdiqlandi, chat ochildi`, `Mutaxassis ishlamoqda`, `Ish yakunlandi`, `Buyurtma bekor qilindi`), plus `reassignment_history` rows.
  21. Footer `DetailActions` (sticky, only when the server allows something): chat `open` → gradient `Chatni ochish` → `/chat/[roomId]`; chat `readOnly` → line `Chatni ko'rish` + lock note `Chat faqat o'qish uchun — buyurtma yakunlangan.` (finished) or `Chat hozir faqat o'qish uchun.`; chat `locked` → note `Chat va qo'ng'iroq oldindan to'lov tasdiqlangach ochiladi.` (or `Yangi chat mutaxassis buyurtmani qabul qilgach ochiladi.` while replacing); `CallButtons` soft `Audio` / `Video` (`Ulanmoqda…` while starting; only the `allowed_call_types` while `can_create_call`, contact unlocked, `calls_enabled` and the phase is not closed / resolution / replacing / assigned); row: `Yakunlash` (soft; only when `client_actions` lists `complete` and the order is active) · `Baholash yoki shikoyat` (soft; rating window open) → `/rate/{work_id}` · `Bekor qilish` (line; `can_cancel`, or `cancel_url` while the payment waits) → `CancelOrderSheet`; full-width line `Bekor qilishni so'rash` (`can_request_cancellation` and not plain-cancellable) → `CancelRequestSheet`.
  22. Sheets of the footer: `CancelOrderSheet` — `Xaridni bekor qilish`, lead `So'rov va to'lov bekor qilinadi. Pul yechilmagan.`, field `Sabab (ixtiyoriy)` placeholder `Masalan: fikrimdan qaytdim`, red `Ha, bekor qilish`, secondary `Ortga`, toast `Buyurtma bekor qilindi`. `CompleteOrderSheet` — `Buyurtmani yakunlash`, `Ish topshirilgan bo'lsa tasdiqlang. Mutaxassisga xabar boradi.`, `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` / `Masalan: maslahat oldim`, `Yakunlash`, toast `Buyurtma yakunlandi`. `CancelRequestSheet` — `Bekor qilishni so'rash`, subtitle `So'rov call-markazga boradi. Operator ko'rib chiqquncha buyurtma muddati to'xtatiladi.`, field `Sabab` placeholder `Nima uchun bekor qilmoqchisiz?`, hint `Kamida 3 ta belgi`, red `Yuborish`, toast `So'rov yuborildi — operator ko'rib chiqadi.`
  23. Service-order fallback (`ServiceOrderDetail`, when the key is an `/orders` row): `OrderInfo` hairline rows `Holat` (pill from `profile.status.*`: `Yangi`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Mutaxassisga yuborildi`, `Qabul qilindi`, `Boshlandi`, `Natija tayyor`, `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `Topshirildi`, `Rad etildi`, `Kutilmoqda`, `Faol`, `Jarayonda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Bekor qilingan`, `To'langan`, `To'lanmagan`), `To'lov` (`To'langan` / `To'lanmagan`), `Narx`, `Mutaxassis`, `Yaratilgan`; then `To'langan` when paid, nothing when cancelled, else `PayBlock`: `Umumiy summa`, `Oldindan to'lov ({pct}%)`, `To'langan`, progress bar, `To'lov yetarli — maxfiy chat ochildi.` / `Maxfiy chatni ochish uchun yana {amount} to'lang.`, button `{pct}% oldindan to'lash` (`Kutilmoqda…`), notes `To'lov sahifasi ochildi.` / `To'lov tizimi hali ulanmagan. Buyurtma saqlandi, to'lovni keyinroq amalga oshirasiz.` / `To'lovni amalga oshirib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`
- **Order status enum (raw `status` → phase):** `refunded` or payment refunded → refunded; `refund_pending` → refund; cancelled/canceled/declined/rejected/lost/expired → cancelled; `rated` (or completed/done/closed with a rating) → rated; completed/done/closed → completed; `cancellation_review` → cancelReview; `resolution_required` → resolution; `waiting_replacement_acceptance` → replacing; paid + sent_to_seller/assigned/seller_assigned/awaiting_acceptance/pending_acceptance/awaiting_seller/waiting_seller_response (or `assignment_status: waiting_seller_response`, or an `acceptance_deadline_at` on a new/created/pending/seller_selection order) → assigned; accepted/awaiting_advance/advance_pending/awaiting_payment unpaid and contact not unlocked → advance; pending_payment/payment_pending/pending_confirmation/waiting_payment (or unpaid `pending`) → pending; `complaint.rework_required` → rework; `work_phase: reviewing` → reviewing; in_progress/started/result_ready/quality_check/delivered/client_confirmation or a `started_at` → in_progress; unpaid new/created/pending/seller_selection/sent_to_seller… → received; `paid`/accepted/awaiting → paid; anything else → attention (`Operator ko'rib chiqmoqda`).
- **Actions:** see 3, 4, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13 (file preview), 15, 16, 21, 22; pull to refresh re-reads the order and its history.
- **States:** loading = three skeletons (150/80/180 px); not signed in = lock empty-state; not found (neither marketplace nor service order) = `search` empty-state `Buyurtma topilmadi` + button `Buyurtmalarim` → `/(tabs)/orders`; error = `ErrorView` with retry; each sub-card has its own skeleton / `…yuklab bo'lmadi` + `Qayta urinish`; offline = cached order, mutations fail at once with a toast (never queued). Every write re-reads the order on a 409 or a known business code; business errors are shown in words (`mf.err.*`, e.g. `Buyurtma holati o'zgargan — ma'lumot yangilandi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, `Baholash muddati tugagan.`, `Shikoyat muddati tugadi.`, `Bu buyurtma bo'yicha kafolat so'rovi allaqachon ko'rib chiqilmoqda.`, `Bekor qilish so'rovi allaqachon operatorda.`).

- **UX note:** the phone must show the number + badge, the lead sentence and the one thing to do now (pay / choose / rate / chat button) above the fold; the price breakdown, package snapshot, facts and timeline can sit at the bottom. Confusing today: the page is a very long single scroll of up to twenty cards with no grouping, the same countdown pattern appears in four cards (resolution, work time, rating, complaint), and the lead and the resolution card are mutually exclusive, which the client cannot know.

### `/packages` — `Xizmat paketlari`

- **Who:** signed-in client (route redirects a guest to `/auth/login`). Buying inside the purchase sheet also requires sign-in (a guest tapping a seller is sent to login). No plan gating.
- **Purpose:** browse the service packages (several services for one fixed price, in Basic/Standard/Premium tiers), compare against buying the services one by one, read what is included, and order one from a specialist who enabled it.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`AdminPage` title `Xizmat paketlari`; `PackageList` — note: the client page uses `PackageList`; `PackagesPanel`/`PackagesHero`/the seller `TierCard` in the same folder are the advokat's "Paketlar" tab (`entities/seller-package`, `pkgSeller.*`) and are not rendered here):
  1. Lead line `Paket — bir necha xizmat bitta narxda. Nima kiradi, nima kirmaydi va muddat aniq yozilgan.` + ` · {n} ta paket` (count of distinct package codes; omitted while loading).
  2. `SearchBox` placeholder `Paket yoki toifa nomi…` (matches title, category, code).
  3. `ChipBar` of categories (only when more than one): `Barcha toifalar`, then each `category_title` with its code count.
  4. One `PackageGroup` per package code (sorted by code), 22 px apart: package icon + title (h4), `category · code` caption, then one `TierCard` per tariff in BASIC → STANDARD → PREMIUM order. The middle card (index 1) is `best`: accent border + pill `Eng ko‘p tanlanadi`. Card: tier icon (package/star/award) + tier name (`Basic` / `Standard` / `Premium`), price h2 `N so'm`, green `Tejaysiz: N` when the separate services cost more, caption `Alohida: {sep} · Paketda: {pkg}` when the separate price is known, clock + duration, `IncludeList` (first 3 included with checks, first 2 excluded with crosses), two small buttons `Batafsil` (line) and `Buyurtma berish` (gradient on the best card).
  5. `PackageSheet` (`Batafsil`): title `{title} · {tariff}`, fact rows `Narx` / `Muddat` / `Toifa` / `Xizmat kodlari`, block `Natija`, `Paketga kiradi` list, `Kirmaydi` list, full-width `Buyurtma berish`.
  6. `PackagePurchaseSheet` (`Buyurtma berish`; title `{title} · {tariff}`, fallback `Paketga buyurtma`, snaps 85/95 %). Step 1 `SellerStep`: lead `{tier} paketini bajaradigan mutaxassisni tanlang. Tartib backenddan keladi.`, the backend's `matching_policy[tier]` rule line in accent, one `SellerCard` per eligible seller (avatar, name, `★ 4.8` or `—`, `· {n} ta bajarilgan`, pill `Onlayn`, pill `LexGo tasdiqlagan` or the backend badge, red reason lines when not eligible — then the row is disabled and dimmed), `Yana ko'rsatish` while `has_more`. Step 2 `PackageCheckout`: back chevron, `PurchaseSteps` (`1 · So'rov`, `2 · To'lov tasdig'i`, `3 · Chat va ish`), ticket card `{seller} · {tariff}` / title / amount, then `PurchaseForm`: `1 · Bog'lanish usuli` cards `Chat` / `Audio` / `Video`; `2 · Vaziyatingizni qisqacha yozing` textarea (`Masalan: ijara shartnomasi bo'yicha maslahat kerak`, counter /1000); `3 · Qulay vaqt` chips `Bugun` / `Bugun kechqurun` / `Ertaga` / `Shu hafta ichida` + input `Masalan: bugun 18:00 dan keyin`; `4 · Qanchalik tez` — `Qachon bog'lanishsin?` chips `15 daqiqa ichida` / `1 soat ichida` / `Bugun` / `Ertaga`, `Ish qachon kerak?` chips `Bugun` / `Ertaga` / `Keyinroq`; `5 · To'lov usuli` chips `Telegram (operator tasdiqlaydi)` / `Payme` / `Click` only while the runtime caps enable online providers; `OptionSections` (price options: `Qo'shimcha shartlar`, hint `Hammasi ixtiyoriy: hech narsa tanlamasangiz asosiy narx qoladi. Bir nechtasini tanlash mumkin.`, `Qo'shimcha shartlar yo'q`) and `QuotePanel` (`Asosiy narx`, `Qo'shimcha`, `Jami to'lov`, `Narx hisoblanmoqda…`, `Qayta hisoblash`, `Narx hisoblangach tugma yoqiladi`); note `Narxni LexGo belgilaydi. To'liq to'lov tasdiqlangach buyurtma mutaxassisga yuboriladi.`; gradient `So'rov yuborish` (`Yuborilmoqda…`); after an online provider refusal a soft `Telegram orqali yuborish`. `PurchaseReceipt` after sending: tinted box with the phase title `So'rovingiz qabul qilindi` / `To'lov tasdiqlandi` / `Xizmat faollashtirildi` / `Bu xizmat yakunlangan` / `So'rov bekor qilindi` (or, for an already-active order, `Bu mutaxassis va xizmat bo'yicha faol so'rovingiz bor.` / `Bu xizmat sizda allaqachon faol`), the `ADV-…` number, lead `To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanmoqda. Tasdiqlangach buyurtma mutaxassisga yuboriladi.` / `Buyurtma mutaxassisga yuborildi. U qabul qilgach xavfsiz chat ochiladi.` / `Mutaxassis buyurtmani qabul qildi, xavfsiz chat ochildi. Unga yozishingiz mumkin.` / `Ish yakunlandi. Natijani buyurtmalaringizda ko'rasiz.` / `To'lov tasdiqlanmadi. Pul yechilmagan.`, `Tasdiqlovchilarga xabar yetib bormadi. So'rov saqlandi — biz uni qo'lda tekshiramiz.` when Telegram did not deliver, `Holat avtomatik yangilanadi`; buttons `To'lash` (provider link), `Chatni ochish`, `Qayta yuborish`, `Hozir tekshirish`, `Buyurtmalarimga o'tish` → `/(tabs)/orders`, `Yopish`.
- **Actions:** search, category chip, `Batafsil` → sheet, `Buyurtma berish` → purchase sheet (both on the card and in the detail sheet), seller pick, send request, receipt buttons. `usePackageList.order()` (a jump into `/services/[id]?package=` or `/(tabs)/services?q=`) exists in the hook but `PackageList` does not call it — ordering goes through the sheet.
- **States:** loading = one 260 px skeleton; error without data = `ErrorView` + retry; empty (nothing after search/category) = compact `briefcase` empty-state `Paketlar topilmadi` / `Qidiruvni o'zgartiring.`; pull to refresh. Sheet: sellers skeletons ×3, `ErrorView` with retry, empty `Hozircha mutaxassis yo'q` / `Bu paketni yoqqan mutaxassis topilmadi. Keyinroq urinib ko'ring.`; business errors in words (`Bu paket bo'yicha faol buyurtma allaqachon bor`, `Bu mutaxassis ushbu paketni bajara olmaydi`, `Paket mutaxassis uchun yoqilmagan`, …). Test/seed packages (code or title containing "test"/"t1b") are hidden.
- **Data:**

| `GET /services?limit=500` (+`offset`, paged until short)                                                                                                                                                                               | the "separately" price and the catalogue code → service map | `services                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | items[{id, catalog code, price}]`                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /service-packages/{id}/eligible-sellers?limit=30&offset=N`                                                                                                                                                                        | seller step                                                 | `items[{user_id, display_name                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | name, public_id, rating, reviews_count, completed_orders, online_now, verification_badge.label, package_eligibility{eligible, reasons[], extended_verified, provider_tier}, package_purchase_url}]`, `has_more`, `matching_policy{BASIC | STANDARD    | PREMIUM}`           |
