- **UX note:** first screen = lead, search and the first package group with its best-value card; the "separately vs package" line is the one selling argument and should stay on the card. Confusing today: every tier is a tall card, so one package code already fills more than a screen, and the purchase flow stacks a five-step form inside a bottom sheet.

### `/subscription` — `Obuna`

- **Who:** anyone. A guest sees the tariff cards, benefits, comparison and FAQ; `TopCards`, `MyPlanCard`, `PendingRequests`, `AutopayPanel` and `BillingHistory` render only when signed in, and `Tarifni tanlash` sends a guest to `/auth/login`. Plans are filtered by audience for the viewer (client/guest → client plans; the gift plan keeps its own card).
- **Purpose:** see my current tariff, what it gives and when it renews, compare the LexGo.AI and other tariffs for a term, and ask for one (approved by an operator in Telegram).
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`Header` title `Obuna`; `Screen` with pull-to-refresh; 14 px gaps):
  1. `TopCards` (signed in): three tinted cells — `Joriy tarif` = `subscription.plan_name` or `Faol tarif yo'q` + hint `Hozirda hech qanday tarif ulanmagan.`; `Keyingi to'lov sanasi` = `renews_at` or `—` + `Tarif ulanganidan so'ng ko'rsatiladi.`; `Ulangan karta` = `BRAND •••• 1234` (default card) or `Karta ulanmagan` + `To'lovlar uchun karta qo'shing.`
  2. `MyPlanCard` (signed in; only when `/subscriptions/me/features` names a subscription or lists items): caption `Mening tarifim`, plan name (contract snapshot name → live plan name → `Tarif`), pill `{n}-versiya`, note `Shartlar joriy davr uchun qayd etilgan: tarif keyin o'zgarsa ham, ular yangilanishgacha o'zgarmaydi.`; consent box when `renewal_consent_required` / terms changed and not yet accepted: `Tarif shartlari o'zgardi` / `Keyingi to'lovdan boshlab yangi shartlar amal qiladi. Davom etish uchun ularga roziligingizni bering.`, a `TermList` under `Yangi shartlar` / `Yangi shartlar · {n}-versiya` (changed rows say `Hozir: {v}`, dropped features show locked), `Keyingi to'lov: {sum}`, buttons `Yangilamaslik` (line) and `Yangi shartlarga roziman` (gradient); toasts `Rahmat! Yangi shartlar keyingi davrdan qo'llanadi.` / `Avtomatik yangilanish o'chirildi: obuna joriy davr oxirigacha eski shartlarda ishlaydi.` / `Rozilikni yuborib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` / `O'zgartirib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`; then one `FeatureRow` per feature: name (plan function title → `AI so‘rovlari`, `Advokat bilan hujjat to‘ldirish`, `Eksport`, `Hujjat shablonlari`, `Hujjat tahlili`, `Qo‘lda tayyorlanadigan hujjatlar`, `Shablonni yuklab olish`, `Sud amaliyotini qidirish`, `Tarixni saqlash`, `Hujjat konstruktori`, `AI hujjat tahlili`) with `Mavjud emas` / `Cheksiz` / `{used} / {limit} ishlatildi` + bar / `{n} ta qoldi` / `{n} kun` etc., facts `{n} ta qoldi · Yangilanadi: {date}`. (No `Tarifni tanlash` button here — it is only passed on `/entitlements`.)
  3. `PendingRequests` (signed in, non-empty): section `Tasdiq kutilayotgan so'rovlar`, a `RowCard` per request: `send` icon, plan title or `Tarif so'rovi`, `N so'm · 1 oy|6 oy|12 oy|Oldindan`, meta `SUB-… · date time`, `StatusPill` (`Kutilmoqda`, `Tasdiqlangan`, `Rad etilgan` …), tap → `/payments/{payment_id}` when there is one.
  4. `AutopayPanel` (signed in): `Avtomatik to'lov (ATMOS)`, switch `Avtomatik to'lov · Yoqilgan|O'chirilgan` (PATCH the first active subscription's auto-renew; with no subscription the choice is kept on the device, hint `Sozlama shu qurilmada saqlangan` and the info line `Tugmani bossangiz, tanlovingiz saqlanadi. Joriy holat hozircha faqat shu qurilmada ko'rsatiladi.`), soft `Kartani ulash (ATMOS)` → `MethodSheet` titled `Kartani ulash` with note `Hozircha karta ma'lumoti profilingizda saqlanadi; ATMOS orqali haqiqiy ulash backend ulangach ishga tushadi.`; toasts `Avtomatik uzaytirish yoqildi` / `Avtomatik uzaytirish o'chirildi` / `Sozlama saqlandi.`
  5. `AiPlansSection`: h2 `LexGo.AI tariflari`, sub `Free — oyiga 5 savol; Lite — 100 savol, 5 hujjat tahlili; Pro — cheksiz (fair-use 1000), 20 tahlil, sud amaliyoti, eksport.`, `AiTermBar`: caption `Muddat`, segmented `1 oy` / `6 oy` / `12 oy`, and for 12 months the switch `Bir yo'la to'lash (oldindan)`; one `AiPlanCard` per lexgo-ai-\* plan: name, pill `Joriy tarif` (ok) or `Tavsiya etiladi` (cyan, the `lexgo-ai-pro` card, which also gets the accent border), `PlanPrice` = the backend quote for the term (`Bepul` for a free plan; big amount + `so'm / oy` / `so'm · 6 oy` / `so'm · 12 oy` / `so'm · 12 oy, oldindan`; under a campaign the base struck through, pill `−{sum} so'm` and `{campaign} · {date} gacha` or `Chegirma`; failure `Narxni olib bo'lmadi` + `Qayta urinish`), `PlanMeta` caption (`Mijozlar uchun` / `Mutaxassislar uchun` · `{n}-versiya` · `{date} dan`), `Bu oy: {limit} tadan {used} tasi ishlatildi` on the current plan, up to 6 feature lines, button `Joriy tarif` (disabled) / `Hozirgi tarif` (free, disabled) / `Tarifni tanlash`; footer `Tarifni oshirish darhol — farq qolgan kunlarga proporsional; tushirish — joriy davr oxirida. Har oyning 1-sanasida limit yangilanadi.` With no AI plans: empty-state `Tariflar mavjud emas` / `Paket tariflar backenddan yuklanadi.`
  6. `Benefits`: three tinted rows `Xavfsiz to'lovlar` / `To'lovlar ATMOS orqali amalga oshiriladi. Sizning kartangiz ma'lumotlari bizda saqlanmaydi.`, `Istalgan paytda bekor qilish` / `Xohlagan vaqtda tarifni bekor qilishingiz mumkin.`, `Doimiy qo'llab-quvvatlash` / `Savollaringiz bormi? Biz har doim yordam berishga tayyormiz.`, line button `Savol berish` → `/assistant`.
  7. `CompareTable` (AI plans): `Tariflar taqqoslanishi` — rows `Narx` (`N so'm / oy` or `Bepul`), `AI so'rovlar (oyiga)` (`entitlements.ai_requests` or 5/100/1000 by slug), `Imkoniyatlar soni`, one column per plan.
  8. `FaqList` (AI plans): `Tez-tez beriladigan savollar` — five question rows, no answers: `To'lov qanday amalga oshiriladi?`, `Tarifni istalgan vaqtda bekor qila olamanmi?`, `Karta ma'lumotlarim xavfsizmi?`, `Free tarifda nimalar mavjud?`, `Pro tarif qanday afzallik beradi?`
  9. `OtherPlansSection`: h2 `Boshqa tariflar`, sub `Shaxsiy advokat obunasi — doimiy yuridik yordam, konsultatsiyalar va chegirmalar.`, `OtherTermBar` segmented `6 oy` / `1 yil` + switch `Yillikni to‘liq to‘lang — yana −10%` for the year; one `OtherPlanCard` per non-AI plan (second card featured), same price/meta/features, `Tarifni tanlash`; `GiftPlanCard`: gift icon + the gift plan's name or `Sovg'a qilish`, `To'lovni to'liq qilasiz`, its features or `QR kod va tushuntirilgan rasmli flayer olasiz` / `Muddat sovg'a qabul qilingandan keyin boshlanadi` / `Qabul qiluvchi havola orqali ro'yxatdan o'tadi` / `Sovg'a holatini profilingizdan kuzatasiz`, line `Sovg'a qilish` → `/profile/gifts`; footer `Har bir xizmatdan so'ng advokatni baholaysiz — bu uning reytingini shakllantiradi va keyingi mijozlarga to'g'ri tanlov qilishga yordam beradi.`
  10. `BillingHistory` (signed in): h3 `To'lovlar tarixi`, subscription payments (`kind` subscription / subscription_plan / none) as `PaymentRowCard` rows ten at a time (`ShowMore`), tap → `/payments/{id}`; empty `To'lovlar tarixi yo'q` / `Hisob-fakturalaringiz shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  11. Checkout sheet `TelegramRequestSheet` (opened by any `Tarifni tanlash`): icon `send`, title `Telegram orqali tasdiqlash`, subtitle = plan name. Before sending: `RequestPlanCard` (navy gradient: plan name, `Telegram` badge, caption `To'lov · {period}`, the quote amount big + `so'm`, campaign line; `Bepul` for a free plan; failure `Narxni olib bo'lmadi` + `Qayta urinish`), label `Muddat` + `PeriodTiles` (two-column tiles for the periods the plan sells: `1 oy` / `6 oy` / `12 oy` / `Oldindan`, each with its own quote, crossed base + `−{sum}` badge under a campaign, chosen tile with blue border and check), `RequestSteps` (`So'rov yuboriladi` → `Admin Telegramda tasdiqlaydi` → `Tarif faollashadi`), caption `«{plan}» paketi uchun so'rov yuboriladi. To'lov tizimi hozircha ulanmagan — so'rov admin Telegramiga boradi va tasdiqlangach paket avtomatik faollashadi.`, `TelegramNotice` errors; sticky submit `So'rov yuborish` (`Yuborilmoqda...`). After sending: `RequestSent` — green check pop-in, `Paket so'rovingiz yuborildi.`, `Tasdiqlangandan keyin paket avtomatik faollashadi.`, pill `ID SUB-…`, box `Summa` + amount + campaign line; notice; gradient `Yopish`. Notices: 422 → the tiles narrow to `allowed_billing_periods` and `Bu paket tanlangan muddatni qabul qilmaydi. Mavjud muddatlar qoldirildi.`; created but not delivered → `So'rov yaratildi, lekin Telegramga yetkazib bo'lmadi. Administrator bilan bog'laning.`; other → the server detail or `So'rovni yuborib bo'lmadi`. The invoice path (`usePlanCheckout`, `POST /subscription-plans/{id}/purchase` with the provider ladder, outcome `invoice` vs `unavailable`) exists in `features/choose-plan` but this page only wires the Telegram request sheet.
- **Actions:** term segmented / up-front switches (also on the remote-assistance map), `Tarifni tanlash` → sheet, period tiles, `So'rov yuborish`, consent / `Yangilamaslik`, autopay switch, `Kartani ulash (ATMOS)`, `Savol berish`, `Sovg'a qilish`, pending request row → payment, billing row → payment, pull to refresh (plans + entitlements).
- **States:** plans loading = two `PlanCardSkeleton`; plans error = `ErrorView` + retry (the signed-in cards above still show); no AI plans = empty-state (see 5); autopay loading = skeleton card; quotes load per card/tile with skeletons and retry on failure; billing loading skeleton / empty; guest = public plans only.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                           | Used for                                                                     | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /subscription-plans` (no auth) + `GET /platform/policies` (overlay; failure ignored)          | the tariffs                                                                  | plans: `id`, `name` (localized), `slug`, `monthly_price                                                                                                                                                                                        | price`, `six_month_price`, `yearly_price`, `prepaid_yearly_price`, `audience`/`audiences`/`target_roles`, `description`, `features`/`benefits`, `functions[{key,title,description,enabled,limit,unit}]`, `entitlements`, `entitlement_contract[]`, `current_version` (+`effective_from`), `is_giftable`, `is_active`, `sort_order`, `billing_type`, `auto_charge_provider`, `allowed_billing_periods[]`, `allowed_gift_durations[]`; policies: `payment.plans.overrides[slug]{hidden,title,description,audience,\*\_price,benefits,is_giftable,is_active}` |
| `GET /subscription-plans/{id}/quote?billing_period=monthly                                         | six_month                                                                    | yearly                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | prepaid_yearly`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | every price shown (cards, tiles, request card) | `amount`, `base_amount`, `discount_amount`, `currency`, `months`, `campaign{id,title,discount_type,discount_value,ends_at}` |
| `GET /clients/me`                                                                                  | `TopCards` current plan / renew date, autopay fallback, "current" card match | `subscription{plan_name,status,renews_at,auto_renew                                                                                                                                                                                            | autopay                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | auto_pay}`                                     |
| `GET /clients/me/payment-methods`                                                                  | linked card                                                                  | `items[{id,brand,last4,expires,is_default}]`                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /clients/me/entitlements`                                                                     | pending requests, the subscription id for autopay                            | `active_subscriptions[{id,plan_id,starts_at,ends_at,family_members[]}]`, `pending_manual_document_plan_requests[{id,work_id,status,created_at,price,payload{plan_title,plan_id,billing_period,amount,payment_id}}]`, `has_active_subscription` |
| `GET /subscriptions/me/features`                                                                   | `MyPlanCard`                                                                 | `subscription_id`, `plan_id`, `plan_version`, `active`, `renewal_consent_required`, `items[{feature_key,mode,allowed,limit,used,remaining,unit,resets_at}]`                                                                                    |
| `GET /subscriptions/{id}/contract`                                                                 | snapshot name/terms and the next version                                     | `subscription_id`, `plan_id`, `status`, `billing_period`, `snapshot{title,meta.name{uz,ru,en},version,entitlement_contract                                                                                                                     | entitlements,prices}`, `entitlements`, `plan_version`, `current_plan_version{version,entitlement_contract,prices}`, `renewal_terms_changed`, `renewal_consent_required`, `renewal_consent{accepted}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /subscriptions/{id}/renewal-consent` (body `accepted: true`)                                 | `Yangi shartlarga roziman`                                                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PATCH /subscriptions/{id}/auto-renew` (body `auto_renew`, `payment_method_id?`)                   | autopay switch / `Yangilamaslik`                                             | `id`, `auto_renew`                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /clients/me/payment-methods` (body `brand,last4,expires,is_default`)                         | `Kartani ulash` sheet                                                        | `id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /subscription-plans/{id}/telegram-purchase-request` (body `billing_period`, `currency: UZS`) | the request                                                                  | `id`, `work_id`, `status`, `payment_id`, `telegram_sent`, `telegram_results[{ok}]`, `pricing{amount,base_amount,discount_amount,campaign}`; 422 `detail.allowed_billing_periods[]`                                                             |
| `GET /payments`                                                                                    | billing history                                                              | `items[{id,kind,amount,status,method,created_at,work_id,…}]`                                                                                                                                                                                   |

- **UX note:** on a phone the current-tariff cell and the AI cards with the term switch must be first; the FAQ (questions only), the comparison and the rating footnote can be cut. Confusing today: the page mixes "my plan" (consent, autopay, pending requests) with the shop, prices appear in three different places with per-card network quotes, and the autopay switch silently becomes a device-only toggle when there is no subscription.

### `/subscriptions` — `Obuna`

- Alias of `/subscription`: `app/subscriptions.tsx` renders the same `SubscriptionPage`; nothing differs.

### `/entitlements` — `Tarifim imkoniyatlari`

- **Who:** signed-in client (guest → `/auth/login`).
- **Purpose:** see exactly what my current paid period gives (features, limits, resets), the active subscriptions with the terms they were bought with, document access, family members who share the plan, and tariff requests still waiting for an operator.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`AdminPage` title `Tarifim imkoniyatlari`; `QueryBoundary` around `GET /clients/me/entitlements`; `PayScroll` with pull-to-refresh):
  1. `MyPlanCard` as on `/subscription`, here with `onUpgrade` → `/subscription`, so a locked or used-up feature adds the soft button `Tarifni tanlash`.
  2. One `ActiveSubCard` per `active_subscriptions` row: name (contract snapshot → plan name → `Tarif`), pill `{n} kun qoldi` (warn at ≤ 5 days), `{start} → {end}` + ` · oila a'zolari: {n}`, progress bar of the term used, `Shu davr shartlari` (+ ` · {n}-versiya`) with a `TermList` of the contract snapshot (only when the backend sent one), switch `Avtomatik uzaytirish` with hint `Joriy holat serverda ko'rsatilmaydi; oxirgi tanlovingiz saqlanadi.` until a PATCH answered. No subscriptions → section card with compact empty-state `Faol tarif yo'q` / `Tarif tanlang — imkoniyatlar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  3. `DocsAccess`: section `Hujjatlar` with check/cross rows `Tayyor hujjat shablonlari`, `Shablonni yuklab olish`, `Hujjatni qo'lda to'ldirish`; `AI so'rovlari: {v}` (`cheksiz` for unlimited); `Manba: {s}`.
  4. `SharedMembers`: section `Tarifdan foydalanuvchi oila a'zolari` — avatar rows `name|phone` / `phone · relation`, or `Hozircha hech kimga ruxsat berilmagan.`; soft `Oila a'zolarini boshqarish` → `/profile/family`.
  5. `PendingRequests` (same as on `/subscription`; hidden when empty).
  6. Gradient button `Tariflarni ko'rish` → `/subscription`.
- **Actions:** consent / stop renewal, auto-renew switch per subscription, `Tarifni tanlash`, `Oila a'zolarini boshqarish`, pending row → `/payments/{id}`, `Tariflarni ko'rish`, pull to refresh.
- **States:** `QueryBoundary` loading/error/retry for the entitlements call; feature card hidden until `/subscriptions/me/features` answers; contract rows absent when the contract call fails (`retry: false`).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                           | Used for                              | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /clients/me/entitlements`                                                     | subs, docs, family, pending, AI limit | `has_active_subscription`, `active_subscriptions[{id,plan_id,starts_at,ends_at,family_members[]}]`, `manual_documents{manual_document_templates,template_download,manual_document_fill,source}`, `ai_limit`, `shared_family_members[{id,name,phone,relation}]`, `pending_manual_document_plan_requests[…]` |
| `GET /subscription-plans` (+ `GET /platform/policies`)                             | plan names and function titles        | as above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /subscriptions/me/features`                                                   | feature rows                          | as above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /subscriptions/{id}/contract` (per active sub and for the feature card)       | snapshot terms, next version          | as above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /subscriptions/{id}/renewal-consent`, `PATCH /subscriptions/{id}/auto-renew` | consent, auto-renew                   | as above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

- **UX note:** the feature rows with their counters are the reason to open this page and must be first; the family block and the documents block are secondary. Confusing today: the auto-renew switch has no server read, so the hint about "last choice saved" appears on every fresh install.

### `/academy` — `Akademiya`

- **Who:** signed-in client (guest → `/auth/login`). No plan gating.
- **Purpose:** browse the short legal-literacy courses of LexGo Akademiya (not yet openable).
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`Header` title `Akademiya`; `Screen` with pull-to-refresh):
  1. h2 `LexGo Akademiya`, sub `Huquqiy savodxonlik uchun qisqa kurslar.`
  2. `AcademyFilters` (only when more than one category or ≥ 7 courses): search input placeholder `Kurs nomi bo'yicha qidirish` (from 7 courses), horizontal chips `Barchasi (N)` + one per category with counts (`Qarz undirish`, `Biznes`, `Oila`, `Mehnat`, `Soliq`, `Shartnomalar`, `Sud`, `Ko'chmas mulk`, else the slug humanized; only when more than one category).
  3. One `CourseCard` per course: 84 px coloured cover (brand blue picked by title length) with a glass pill for the level `Boshlang'ich` / `O'rta` / `Yuqori`; accent category caption; title (h4); meta `{n} dars` · `{n} daq`; progress bar when `progress` > 0; disabled soft button `Tez orada`.
- **Actions:** search, category chip, pull to refresh. The course button is disabled — nothing opens.
- **States:** loading = three `SkeletonCard`; error = `ErrorView` + retry; no match = `search` empty-state `Mos kurs topilmadi` / `Boshqa so'z bilan qidiring yoki filtrni tozalang.`; no courses = `book-open` empty-state `Kurslar yo'q` / `Kurslar tez orada qo'shiladi.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                       | Used for    | Key fields |
| ------------------------------ | ----------- | ---------- | ---- | ------------------ | ----------------------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------ | -------- |
| `GET /academy/courses/catalog` | the courses | `items     | data | courses[{id, title | name, category, lessons_count | lessons, duration_min | duration, level, progress, cover_url | cover}]` |

- **UX note:** a card list with a disabled CTA; on a phone the cover + title + meta is enough, the progress bar is dead weight until courses open. Confusing today: every button says `Tez orada`, so the page promises nothing it can do.

### `/rate/[workId]` — `Baholash`

- **Who:** signed-in client (guest → `/auth/login`). Rating is allowed only while the backend's window is open (`available && !submitted`, and seconds left).
- **Purpose:** rate a finished work (document work, urgent advokat request, marketplace order or a plain review) with 1–5 stars and a comment inside the backend's 15-minute window; a 1–2 star rating carries a complaint text.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`AdminPage` title `Baholash`, subtitle = the work's title or number; `PayScroll` keyboard-aware):
  1. `Mutaxassis: {name}` (when known).
  2. While open: `RateForm` card — h4 `Ishni baholang` + pill `{time} qoldi` (`mm:ss`, warn under 2 min; no pill for a `review` kind), `StarInput`, word under the stars `Yulduzni tanlang` → `Yomon` / `O'rtacha` / `Yaxshi` / `Juda yaxshi` / `A'lo` (or the validation error), input `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)` (500), at 1–2 stars a warn box `Nima noto'g'ri bo'ldi?` with textarea `Qisqacha yozing — operator uchun.` and `Bu baho bilan shikoyat call-centerga boradi va operator ko'rib chiqadi.`, gradient `Bahoni yuborish`.
  3. After sending / when closed: `RateOutcome` card — green check `Bahoyingiz uchun rahmat` / `Baho mutaxassis reytingiga ta'sir qiladi.` with the stars (and `Shikoyatingiz call-centerga yuborildi ({id}).` + button `Shikoyatni ko'rish` → `/complaints` when a quality complaint was opened), or grey clock `Baholash muddati tugagan` with `Baholash va shikoyat uchun berilgan vaqt tugadi.` or `Baholash ish yakunlangach ochiladi.` (`closed_reason` not_ready / deadline_missing); an existing comment is quoted; button `Orqaga`.
  4. Line button `Alohida shikoyat yozish` (only while open and the kind is not `market`) → `ComplaintSheet` (the same new-complaint sheet as `/complaints`, pre-linked to this work).
- **Actions:** stars, comment, complaint text, `Bahoni yuborish`, `Alohida shikoyat yozing`, `Shikoyatni ko'rish`, `Orqaga` (back or `/`). When the countdown hits zero the target is re-read and the form gives way to the outcome.
- **States:** loading = one 260 px skeleton; 404 = `search` empty-state `Ish topilmadi`; other error = `ErrorView` + retry; a 409 `rating_window_closed` on send = info toast `Baholash muddati tugagan` and a re-read (no error toast); other send errors via the payment error handler.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                              | Used for                                            | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /clients/me/works/{workId}`                                                                                                      | the rate target and its window                      | `type`/`summary.type` (urgent_advokat, document_lawyer_work, else order), `summary{work_id,title,status,assigned_lawyer{id,name}}`, `urgent_request{id,rating{available,submitted,deadline_at,remaining_seconds,closed_reason,value{rating,comment}},payload}`, `document{id,rating…}` / flat `rating_available`, `rating_submitted`, `rating_deadline_at`, `rating_remaining_seconds`, `rating_closed_reason`, `order{id,lawyer_name,lawyer_user_id}` |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders?role_view=client` (only for an order target)                                                              | the marketplace order's own window and `rating_url` | `rating{available,deadline_at,remaining_seconds,reason}`, `rating_url`, `details.rating_submitted`, `lawyer_name`, `lawyer_user_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `POST /document-requests/{id}/rating` / `POST /urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/rating` (body `rating, comment, complaint?`)              | document / urgent rating                            | answer `quality_complaint{id,work_id,status}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `POST {rating_url}` (default `/marketplace/me/orders/{id}/rating`; body `rating, comment` (comment + complaint joined), `complaint?`) | marketplace rating                                  | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /reviews` (body `lawyer_user_id, case_id, rating, comment`)                                                                     | plain review                                        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GET /clients/me/works?limit=100&offset=N` (all pages), `POST /complaints`                                                            | the separate complaint sheet                        | see `/complaints`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

- **UX note:** stars, the countdown and the send button must fit one screen with the keyboard closed; the lawyer line and subtitle can be merged. Confusing today: the countdown pill and the `Alohida shikoyat yozing` button both live on the same screen as the 1–2-star complaint box, so two complaint channels exist for non-marketplace works.

### `/complaints` — `Shikoyatlar`

- **Who:** signed-in client (guest → `/auth/login`). Query params: `?tab=ratings|warranty` switches the variant, `?work=<id|ADV>` opens that complaint's detail sheet, `?new=1` or `?about=<work>` opens the new-complaint form pre-linked.
- **Purpose:** write a formal complaint about a service, a lawyer's work or a payment, and follow every complaint (written ones and those a 1–2 star rating opened) to the call centre's ruling.
- **Layout, top → bottom** (`ComplaintsPage` is one page with three variants; `AdminPage` title `Shikoyatlar`; `PagedList` flat list with a header):
  1. `ComplaintsHead` hero (gradient): h3 `Shikoyatlaringiz`, lead `Xizmat, advokat ishi yoki to'lovdan norozi bo'lsangiz — shu yerda rasmiy shikoyat yozing.`, three glass counters `Jami` / `Jarayonda` (stages new, review, rework) / `Hal bo'lgan`, white button `+ Yangi shikoyat`.
  2. Flow row of three connected nodes: `Yuborasiz` → `Mas'ul xodim ko'rib chiqadi` → `Qaror shu yerda ko'rinadi`.
  3. Note `Ishga 1–2 yulduz qo'ysangiz, sifat bo'yicha shikoyat o'zi ochiladi va shu ro'yxatga tushadi.` and the link row `Tezkor savol bo'lsa — operator bilan chat` → `/support`.
  4. `ComplaintNotes` (conditional): green `Shikoyatingiz qabul qilindi` + `Raqami: {workId}` + `Mas'ul xodim ko'rib chiqadi — har bir o'zgarish haqida bildirishnoma olasiz.` (dismissable, after sending); blue `Havoladagi shikoyat topilmadi.` (a `?work=` that matched nothing; dismissable); warn `Siz yozgan shikoyatlar yuklanmadi — ro'yxat to'liq emas.` / `Sifat bo'yicha shikoyatlar yuklanmadi — ro'yxat to'liq emas.` with `Qayta urinish` when one of the two sources failed.
  5. `ComplaintFilters` (only when the list has more than one item and can be narrowed): search box `Mavzu, raqam yoki matn` (from 5 items; matches subject, text, number, id, linked work, category, source) + `sliders` button `Filtrlar` with a dot (only when both kinds exist or more than one stage); applied chips `Siz yozgan` / `Sifat bo'yicha` and the stage label, each removable.
  6. `ComplaintFilterSheet` (`Filtrlar`): group `Turi` (only when both kinds exist) — `Barchasi` / `Siz yozgan` / `Sifat bo'yicha` with counts; group `Holat` — `Barcha holatlar` then each present stage with a coloured dot (new warn, review accent, rework cyan, resolved ok, rejected err, closed grey) and count; bottom bar `Filtrni tozalash` + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N`.
  7. Rows (`ComplaintRowCard`, 46 px tile tinted by stage, star icon for a quality complaint else the category icon briefcase/user/credit-card/star/clock): title = subject, else category/source, else kind; stage pill (pulsing while new/review/rework); meta `facet · work number · Ish: {id}|Ishga bog'langan · date`; first line of the text or the stage hint. Ten at a time (`useSlicedList`).
  8. `ComplaintDetailSheet` (tap a row): title = subject or kind, subtitle `Sifat bo'yicha shikoyat|category|Siz yozgan shikoyat · work number`; `ComplaintTimeline` (`Ko'rib chiqish jarayoni`): `Qabul qilindi` (date) → `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda` (`Navbatda` while new, `Jarayonda` while in review; blinking) → `Qaror` (the tag, or `Qayta ishlanmoqda`; green check / red x / grey minus); `ComplaintStateBox` (tinted, stage icon): bold tag when decided or rework, hint (`Shikoyatingiz navbatda — mas'ul xodim ko'rib chiqadi, qaror shu yerda ko'rinadi.` new; `Callcenter operatori ish va bahoingizni tekshirmoqda.` quality in review / `Mas'ul xodim shikoyatingizni ko'rib chiqmoqda. Qaror shu yerda ko'rinadi.` manual in review; `Shikoyat asosli deb topildi — advokat ishni bepul qayta bajaradi.`; `Shikoyatingiz ko'rib chiqildi va hal qilindi.`; `Tekshiruvda shikoyat asosli deb topilmadi.`; `Bu shikoyat yopilgan.`), `Qarorga rozi bo'lmasangiz, operator bilan chatda yozing.` after a rejection, `Ko'rib chiqdi: {name} · date` when decided, `Oxirgi o'zgarish: {date}` while open; card `Xodim javobi · {operator|Mas'ul xodim}` + note; for a quality complaint a card `Baho bilan qoldirgan izohingiz` with the stars and comment (`Baho bilan izoh qoldirilmagan.`), loading skeleton, error `Tafsilotlarni yuklab bo'lmadi. · Qayta urinish`; for a manual one `Siz yozgan matn`; `ComplaintRelated` (`Bog'liq ish`): work card with title/number and `Advokat: {name}`, button `Ishni ochish` → `/works/{workId}` (or `/documents` / `/urgent` / `/works`), `Ishga bog'langan` or `Ish raqami: {id}`; line button `Operator bilan chat` → `/support`.
  9. `ComplaintSheet` (new complaint; title `Yangi shikoyat`, secondary `Bekor qilish`, submit `Yuborish`): `RelatedWorkField` `Qaysi ish bo'yicha? (ixtiyoriy)` searchable select with `Muayyan ishga bog'liq emas` + my works (`Ishlaringiz yuklanmoqda…`, `Ishlar ro'yxati yuklanmadi — shikoyatni ishga bog'lamasdan yuborishingiz mumkin. Qayta urinish`; hidden when there are none); `CategoryCards` `Nima bo'ldi?` radio cards `Xizmat` / `Xizmat va'da qilinganidek bajarilmadi`, `Advokat/Yurist` / `Mutaxassisning muomalasi yoki ishi`, `To'lov` / `Pul yechilishi, summa yoki qaytarish`, `Sifat` / `Natija sifatsiz yoki xato`, `Muddat buzildi` / `Ish o'z vaqtida tayyor bo'lmadi`, `Boshqa` / `Yuqoridagilarga to'g'ri kelmaydi`; input `Mavzu` placeholder `Masalan: hujjat va'da qilingan muddatda tayyor bo'lmadi` (3–150); `Batafsil` with hint `Nima bo'ldi, qachon va qaysi ish bo'yicha — shularni yozing.`, textarea `Nima bo'lganini yozing…` (15–4000) and counter `{n}/4000`; red card `Platformadan tashqarida bog'lanish ma'lumotini yozish mumkin emas. Muloqotni tizim ichida davom ettiring.` when the backend refuses contact details; bell note `Har bir o'zgarish haqida bildirishnoma olasiz; javob shu sahifada ko'rinadi.` Validation words: `Toifani tanlang.`, `Mavzuni yozing.`, `Mavzu juda qisqa — kamida {min} ta belgi yozing.`, `Batafsilroq yozing — kamida {min} ta belgi.`
- **Complaint status enum (`complaintStage` → label / tag):** `new` → `Yangi`; `rework_required`, `rework_in_progress` → `Qayta ishlanmoqda` (tags `Qayta ishlashga yuborildi` / `Qayta ishlanmoqda`); `resolved` → `Hal qilindi`; `rejected` → `Rad etildi`; `closed`, `cancelled` → `Yopildi` (tags `Yopildi` / `Bekor qilindi`); everything else (`pending`, `under_review`, …) → `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda` (tags `Kutilmoqda`, `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda`). Kinds: `manual` `Siz yozgan shikoyat`, `quality` `Sifat bo'yicha shikoyat`; sources `Hujjat bo'yicha ish` / `Tezkor advokat`.
- **Actions:** `Yangi shikoyat` (hero), `Yuborish`, filters, row → detail sheet, `Ishni ochish`, `Operator bilat chat` → `/support`, notes dismiss / `Qayta urinish`, pull to refresh; socket events `quality_complaint.*`, `complaint.*` (and `notification.created` carrying them) refetch the list.
- **States:** loading via `PagedList`; empty (no complaints) = `message-square` `Hozircha shikoyat yo'q` / `Xizmat, advokat ishi yoki to'lovdan norozi bo'lsangiz, shikoyat yozing. Mas'ul xodim ko'rib chiqadi, qaror shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; filter empty = `filter` `Bu filtrga mos shikoyat yo'q.`; both sources failed = the list error with retry; one failed = the partial note.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                            | Used for                                                   | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| `GET /complaints`                                                                   | complaints the client wrote                                | `items                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | data[{id, work_id, record_type                                                                                                    | category, title | subject, status, created_at, updated_at, payload{description, case_id, operator_name, operator_note | resolution_note, resolved_at, work_id}}]` |
| `GET /clients/me/works?type=quality_complaint&limit=100&offset=N` (all pages, ≤ 10) | complaints a low rating opened                             | `items[{id, work_id, title, status, type                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | source, assigned_lawyer{name}, operator{name}, urgent_advokat_request_id, document_request_id, created_at, updated_at}]`, `total` |
| `GET /clients/me/works/{workId}`                                                    | quality complaint detail; the related work of a manual one | `summary{status,updated_at,assigned_lawyer{name},operator{name},work_id,title,type}`, `complaint{status,payload{rating,comment,operator_name,operator_note,resolved_at,document_title,service_title}}`, `review{rating,comment,lawyer_name}`, `document.title` |
| `GET /clients/me/works?limit=100&offset=N` (all pages)                              | the works a new complaint can point at                     | `items[{id, work_id, type, title                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | document_title}]`                                                                                                                 |
| `POST /complaints` (body `category, subject, description, case_id?`)                | new complaint                                              | the created row (as `GET /complaints`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| user socket                                                                         | refetch                                                    | `quality_complaint.*`, `complaint.*`, `notification.created` (`notification.data.event`/`meta.event`)                                                                                                                                                          |

- **UX note:** on a phone the hero with the counters and the `Yangi shikoyat` button plus the first rows must be visible; the three-step flow row and the auto-complaint note can collapse. Confusing today: the hero, the flow row, the notes and the filter bar push the first complaint below the fold, and a quality complaint's detail needs a second request before its text shows.

### `/ratings` — `Baholar`

- Same page, `initial="ratings"` (`RatingsTab`); nothing of the complaints chrome is shown. Layout: `RatingsHero` gradient — `O'rtacha baho` + the average of my given stars (`—` with none), stars + `{n} ta baho`, amber band `{n} ta baho kutmoqda` when works wait; h4 `Baholanmagan ishlar` + warn count pill; `PendingRow` per waiting work (open 15-minute windows first — avatar, lawyer name or `Mutaxassis`, title/number, pulsing pill `{time} qoldi` — then finished marketplace jobs from `/reviews/pending` without a countdown), each with a `Baholash` button; empty `Baholanmagan ish yo'q` / `Ish yakunlangach shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; h4 `Mening sharhlarim` with `GivenRow`s (avatar, lawyer, comment, date, stars, moderation `StatusPill`), ten at a time; empty `Sharh yo'q` / `Qoldirgan sharhlaringiz shu yerda.` Tap a pending row → `RateSheet` (title `Ishni baholang`, `Kimga baho berasiz` + avatar/name/title, the same `RateForm` without its card; success toast `Sharhingiz uchun rahmat!`). Data: `GET /reviews/me` (`items[{id,lawyer_name,rating,comment,status,created_at}]`), `GET /reviews/pending` (`items[{id|case_id, lawyer_user_id, lawyer_name|lawyer|name, service|title|category}]`), `GET /clients/me/works?limit=12` then `GET /clients/me/works/{work_id}` for up to four finished document/urgent works (open windows; skipped for sellers), and the rating POSTs listed under `/rate/[workId]`. The app-wide `RatePromptBanner` (`Suhbat qanday o'tdi?` / `{name} ga baho bering`) uses the same queries but is hidden on `/complaints*` routes.

### `/warranty` — `Kafolat`

- Same page, `initial="warranty"` (`WarrantyTab`). Layout: `WarrantyHero` — shield tile, `LexGo kafolati` / `Buyurtma bo'yicha muammo bo'lsa, shu yerdan so'rov yuboring.`; tinted facts `Xizmat kafolatlari uning sahifasida ko'rsatiladi.`, `Qaror so'rov ko'rib chiqilgandan keyin chiqadi.`, `So'rov holati pastdagi ro'yxatda yangilanadi.`; tinted card `Marketplace buyurtmasi bo'yicha kafolat so'rovi o'sha buyurtma sahifasidan yuboriladi.` + soft `Buyurtmalarim` → `/(tabs)/orders`; card `Advokatni almashtirish` / `Advokat ishidan ko'nglingiz to'lmadimi? Almashtirishni so'rang — menejer 24 soat ichida ko'rib chiqadi.` + gradient `So'rov yuborish`; h4 `So'rovlarim`; then `RowCard`s (shield, reason or `Almashtirish so'rovi`, subtitle `Almashtirish so'rovi` for replacement records, meta `work number · date`, pill `Yangi` / `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda` / `Tasdiqlandi` / `Rad etildi`), ten at a time; empty `Hali so'rov yo'q.` `WarrantySheet`: title `Advokatni almashtirish`, subtitle `Sababini yozing — biz ko'rib chiqamiz.`, select `Ish (ixtiyoriy)` (`Muayyan ishga bog'liq emas` + my works), textarea `Sabab` placeholder `Nega almashtirishni xohlaysiz?`, submit `So'rov yuborish`. `WarrantyResultSheet` after sending: `So'rov holati`, green check `So'rovingiz qabul qilindi`, `Holat: {status}`, `Menejer 24 soat ichida bog'lanadi. Holatni pastdagi ro'yxatdan kuzatib borishingiz mumkin.`, button `Tushunarli`. Data: `GET /warranty/claims` and `GET /replacement-requests/me` (`items[{id, work_id, status, record_type, title, created_at, payload.reason}]`; the second failing is ignored), `GET /clients/me/works?limit=100&offset=N` (targets), `POST /warranty/claims` (body `reason, kind: replacement, case_id?`). The per-order LexGo warranty (`POST …/orders/{id}/warranty`) is not on this page.

## Cross-cutting for the client

### Bottom tab bar (`src/widgets/tab-bar/tabConfig.ts`)

- Roles are decided from the backend role only (`useTabRole`): signed out → `guest`; `client` → client; advokat / yurist → `seller`. Guest and client share the same bar; a professional has a different one (Ish maydoni first) and never sees the client Home.
- Client bar, left → right: `Bosh sahifa` (route `index`, icon home) · `Hujjatlar` (`services`, file-text) · `AI` (centre raised button, icon zap — the tab bar is hidden while it is open) · `Ishlar` (`works`, briefcase) · `Xabarlar` (`messages`, message-circle, red counter badge `messages` fed by the secure-chat unread count, capped `99+`).
- Guest bar is the same five; `Ishlar` and `Xabarlar` are marked `auth` and a tap pushes `/auth/login` instead of the tab.
- Routes that stay reachable without a bar slot (`ALWAYS`): `profile`, `services`, `lawyers`, `orders`, `messages` — so `/lawyers` (Advokat tab page) and `/orders` are pushed screens for the client, not tabs. Profil has no tab: it lives behind the avatar in the header.
- iOS uses the system (Liquid Glass) tab bar; Android a flat custom bar. A route outside the role's list (after sign-out / role switch) is replaced by the role's first tab.

### Page header (`src/widgets/tab-page/TopBar.tsx` + `HeaderActions.tsx`)

Every tab page and most stack pages share the same header row: menu button (`Menyuni ochish`, opens the drawer) · greeting `Assalomu alaykum` over the person's name (or `Mehmon` for a guest) · page-specific tools (`right`) · then the global actions:

1. `Demo` switch (`DemoPill`, icon layers): toggles sample data on/off for the app session and refetches every query; a green dot while on. Off by default, not persisted.
2. Support headset (client and seller only) — see "Support sheet" below; two soft rings pulse once every 7 s.
3. Bell (badge dot while unread notifications > 0) → `/notifications`; a guest is sent to `/auth/login`.
4. Avatar (initials from the display name) → `/(tabs)/profile`; a guest gets a round person button → `/auth/login`.
   Under the row: the page title (h2, max 2 lines) and optional subtitle, then the "header extra" slot (the upsell nudge). The header scrolls away with the page (not pinned).

### Drawer (`src/widgets/client-sidebar/navItems.ts` — `NAV`, same order as the web client sidebar; labels `cpd.menu.*`)

| #   | Row (Uzbek)                                    | Route               | Guest may open? |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------- | --------------- |
| 1   | `Boshqaruv paneli`                             | `/`                 | yes             |
| 2   | `SOS — Shoshilinch`                            | `/sos`              | no → sign-in    |
| 3   | `Tezkor Advokat`                               | `/urgent`           | no              |
| 4   | `Mening so'rovlarim` (`ug1.mine`)              | `/urgent/requests`  | no              |
| 5   | `Huquqiy hujjatlar`                            | `/services`         | yes             |
| 6   | `Mening hujjatlarim`                           | `/documents`        | no              |
| 7   | `Advokat va yuristlar`                         | `/lawyers`          | yes             |
| 8   | `Mening ishlarim`                              | `/works`            | no              |
| 9   | `Xabarlar`                                     | `/messages`         | no              |
| 10  | `Sun'iy Intellekt`                             | `/ai`               | yes             |
| 11  | `Muammo tahlili`                               | `/intake`           | no              |
| 12  | `Akademiya`                                    | `/academy`          | yes             |
| 13  | `Paketlar`                                     | `/packages`         | yes             |
| 14  | `Xizmat buyurtmalarim`                         | `/orders`           | no              |
| 15  | `Mos advokatlar`                               | `/matches`          | no              |
| 16  | `Tariflar`                                     | `/subscription`     | yes             |
| 17  | `To'lovlar`                                    | `/payments`         | no              |
| 18  | `Sovg'alar`                                    | `/profile/gifts`    | no              |
| 19  | `Do'stni taklif qilish`                        | `/profile/referral` | no              |
| 20  | `Baholash va sharhlar`                         | `/ratings`          | no              |
| 21  | `Kafolat`                                      | `/warranty`         | no              |
| 22  | `Shikoyatlar`                                  | `/complaints`       | no              |
| 23  | `Yordam markazi` (badge: unread support total) | `/support`          | no              |

Then a `QO'SHIMCHA` kicker with the two mobile-only rows (`EXTRA`, labels `nav1.menu.*`): `Tarif huquqlari` → `/entitlements`, `Roziliklar` → `/profile/consents`. A `Jamoa vazifalari` row (`/team-tasks`) is prepended only for accounts holding `tasks.view`. Footer: `Chiqish` (sign-out confirm) or, for a guest, `Kirish` → `/auth/login`. `GUEST_OPEN` = `/`, `/services`, `/lawyers`, `/ai`, `/academy`, `/packages`, `/subscription`; every other row sends a visitor to `/auth/login`. Drawer header shows the brand, the name and the role (`Mijoz`) or `Mehmon`. The drawer is 84% of the width (max 340 pt), glass, closes by scrim tap, drag left or hardware back; on a wide tablet it is docked permanently.

### Upsell nudge (`src/features/gift-nudge`)

- Rendered in the header-extra slot of every page for signed-in **clients only** (`useTabRole() === "client"`), once the plans (`GET /subscription-plans`) and entitlements (`GET /clients/me/entitlements` → `active_subscriptions[].plan_id`) have loaded. Hidden on screens that need the whole height: `/support/*` (except new), `/chat/*`, `/sos`, `/urgent`, `/urgent/new`, `/services`, `/services/direction`.
- One of four variants is picked at random per app session and can be dismissed once per session (`Yopish`); the session reset clears it. Variants whose plan the client already holds are skipped (`lexgo-ai-lite` hides `lite`; `lexgo-ai-pro` hides `lite` and `pro`):
  - `gift` (orange→rose gift icon): `Yaqinlaringizga g'amxo'rlik qiling` / `Yuridik xizmatni emas — yuridik xotirjamlikni sovg'a qiling.` / CTA `Sovg'a qilish` → `/profile/gifts`
  - `pro` (gold crown): `LexGo Pro bilan cheksiz imkoniyat` / `AI yordamchidan cheklovsiz foydalaning, shablonlarni erkin yuklab oling va ustuvor yordam oling.` / `Pro'ga o'tish` → `/subscription`
  - `lite` (teal gem): `LexGo Lite — arzonroq, qulayroq` / `Ko'proq AI so'rovlari va hujjat shablonlarini yuklab olish imkoniyati — Lite tarifda.` / `Lite'ni ko'rish` → `/subscription`
  - `lawyers` (blue group): `Eng yaxshi advokatlarni toping` / `Reytingi yuqori, tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar bilan bevosita bog'laning.` / `Advokatlarni ko'rish` → `/lawyers`
- Visual: a warm pastel gradient card (dark variant in dark mode) with a shine sweep, icon tile, title + text (tappable), an × and a full-width gradient CTA with an arrow.

### Support sheet (`src/features/support-menu`)

- Header headset button (label `Qo'llab-quvvatlash`) opens a bottom sheet titled `Yordam kerakmi?` / `Biz shu yerdamiz — qulay yo'lni tanlang.` with, top → bottom:
  1. `Operatorga ulanish` — `Jonli chat — operator shu yerda javob beradi` (blue gradient card, big faded headset) → `/support`.
  2. `Shikoyat yuborish` — `Xizmat yoki mutaxassis ustidan rasmiy shikoyat` (amber card; **clients only**) → opens the complaint sheet (below) after the menu closes.
  3. `Qo'ng'iroq qilish` — the support phone in bold (green card) with the business-hours badge (`GET /calendar/business-hours`) → dials the number.
  4. `Yordam markazini ochish` (outlined pill) → `/support`.
- Complaint sheet (`src/features/rating/ui/ComplaintSheet.tsx`, title `Yangi shikoyat`, submit `Yuborish`, secondary `Bekor qilish`): related-work picker `Qaysi ish bo'yicha?` `(ixtiyoriy)` with `Muayyan ishga bog'liq emas` / `Ishlaringiz yuklanmoqda…` / `Ishlar ro'yxati yuklanmadi — shikoyatni ishga bog'lamasdan yuborishingiz mumkin.`; category cards `Nima bo'ldi?` — `Xizmat` (Xizmat va'da qilinganidek bajarilmadi), `Advokat/Yurist` (Mutaxassisning muomalasi yoki ishi), `To'lov` (Pul yechilishi, summa yoki qaytarish), `Sifat` (Natija sifatsiz yoki xato), `Muddat buzildi` (Ish o'z vaqtida tayyor bo'lmadi), `Boshqa` (Yuqoridagilarga to'g'ri kelmaydi); `Mavzu` (placeholder `Masalan: hujjat va'da qilingan muddatda tayyor bo'lmadi`); `Batafsil` with hint `Nima bo'ldi, qachon va qaysi ish bo'yicha — shularni yozing.`, placeholder `Nima bo'lganini yozing…` and a `{n}/{max}` counter; a reassurance line `Har bir o'zgarish haqida bildirishnoma olasiz; javob shu sahifada ko'rinadi.`; validation `Toifani tanlang.` / `Mavzuni yozing.` / `Mavzu juda qisqa — kamida {min} ta belgi yozing.` / `Batafsilroq yozing — kamida {min} ta belgi.`; a refused contact detail shows `Platformadan tashqarida bog'lanish ma'lumotini yozish mumkin emas. Muloqotni tizim ichida davom ettiring.` inside the form. Success toast `Shikoyatingiz qabul qilindi`.
- Data: `POST /complaints` (category, subject, description, optional work ref); the work picker reads `GET /clients/me/works` and `GET /clients/me/works/{ref}`.

### App-wide rating prompt (`src/widgets/rating/RatePromptBanner.tsx`, mounted globally)

For a signed-in client, a floating glass card under the status bar (hidden on `/auth/*` and `/complaints`) while there is a finished work with an open rating window or a finished marketplace job awaiting its review: the specialist's avatar, `Suhbat qanday o'tdi?` over `{name} ga baho bering` (or the work title) and the window countdown `{time} qoldi` (or `Yakunlangan ishni baholang`), five empty stars, and an ×. A tap opens the rate sheet in place (not `/rate/[workId]`). Open windows are found by scanning `GET /clients/me/works?limit=12` for finished `document_lawyer_work` / `urgent_advokat` rows (status `completed`, `file_ready`, `fulfilled`, `done`, `delivered`, `result_ready`, `ready`) and reading up to four of them via `GET /clients/me/works/{ref}` (the detail's rating window with `remaining_seconds` decides); pending reviews come from `GET /reviews/pending`. Dismissals are remembered per work for the session.
