# PART B — ADVOKAT and YURIST: every screen

## LexGo mobile — professional roles (advokat / yurist): screen-by-screen spec

Source: `/Users/projects/lexgo-mobile` (expo-router routes in `app/`, pages in `src/pages/*`, widgets `src/widgets/*`, features `src/features/*`, entities `src/entities/*`, Uzbek copy `src/shared/i18n/locales/uz/*.ts`). Everything below was read from the code; nothing is invented. Backend kinds: advokat = `advocate`, yurist = `lawyer` (`session.user.role`). Both roles share the `seller` tab role and the same pages; what differs is listed per route under **Who**.

Terms used throughout:

- **limited** = `GET /lawyers/me/cabinet` answered `limited_access: true` (account still under review). The drawer keeps only a few rows, the tab bar keeps only `Ish maydoni` and the AI button.
- **actions** = `available_actions` of the cabinet: `accept_orders`, `secure_chat`, `calls` (a missing flag follows the overall access level).
- **AdminPage** = the shared frame of every non-tab screen: back arrow, title (+ one-line subtitle), header-right slot (default: the registered support headset), optional sticky footer above the home indicator.
- **TabPage** = the frame of the three tab screens: the same header row as Home (menu, demo pill, support, bell, avatar), then a big title and subtitle.

---

### `/workspace` — `Ish maydoni` (tab)

- **Who:** both. Differences: hero accent and badge — advokat gets gold and a shield badge `Advokat · <license_number>` (licence shown when the cabinet has it), yurist gets violet and a briefcase badge `Yurist`. The LexGo AI card sends an advokat to `/pro/assistant` and a yurist to the AI chat tab `/(tabs)/ai`. The last block is `Obro' va reyting` (promotion/plan card) for an advokat and `Hisob xavfsizligi` (2FA / Telegram / notification cards) for a yurist. Gating: while `limited_access` the page shows only the brief hero (no counters, no plan line), the verification `StatusBanner`, the profile-completeness ring and the two info cards (plan, referral).
- **Purpose:** the professional's home: what needs an answer now (new orders), today's plan, quick ways to add a client / case / document / meeting, and the money and rating picture.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Dark gradient hero (`SellerHero`, carries the header row itself — not wrapped in TabPage): row with menu button, greeting `home.greeting` + name, demo pill, support headset, bell (unread badge), avatar → `/(tabs)/profile`; line `Xush kelibsiz, {name}` + role badge; title `Adolat yo'lida, har kuni.`; sub `Sizning professional platformangiz — mijozlar, ishlar, hujjatlar va imkoniyatlar bir joyda.`; plan line `Bugun {meetings} ta uchrashuv va {tasks} ta vazifa kutmoqda.` (meetings = `stats.workload.courts_today`, tasks = active cases whose `deadline_at` is today) — hidden while loading or brief; 2×2 gradient counter tiles: `Mijozlar` (distinct `client_user_id` across `secure_chats`) → `/(tabs)/clients`, `Faol ishlar` (`active_cases.length`) → `/pro/cases`, `Uchrashuvlar` (`courts_today`) → `/pro/calendar`, `Hujjatlar` (`workload.documents_to_review`) → `/pro/files`. Values show `—` while the cabinet loads.
  2. `HeaderExtra` slot (app-registered banner, if any).
  3. `NewAlert` — only when `new_orders` > 0: amber card with live dot, `{n} ta yangi so'rov kutmoqda` / `Javob muddati ketmoqda — ochish uchun bosing` → `/pro/orders`.
  4. `ProfileStrengthCard` `Profil to'liqligi`: ring with percent (halo until 100 %), hint `Yuqori o'rin uchun profilni to'ldiring` or `Profilingiz to'liq — mijozlar sizni ishonch bilan tanlaydi`, chips `Qolgan: {n}` + missing parts (`Ma'lumotlar`, `Rasm`, `Turi`, `Litsenziya` (yurist: `Diplom`), `Tajriba`, `Hudud`, `Tuzilma`), each → `/pro/profile?section=…`; button `Profilni to'ldirish` / `Profilni ko'rish`. Hidden until profile + account have loaded. (Percent is the same hook as `/pro/profile`.)
  5. `Tezkor amallar` 2×2 gradient tiles: `Yangi mijoz qo'shish` (ClientFormSheet in place), `Yangi ish ochish` (CaseFormSheet), `Hujjat yuklash` → `/pro/files`, `Uchrashuv belgilash` (EventFormSheet, today preselected).
  6. `Mening vazifalarim` panel: segmented `Bugun ({n})` / `Kelingusi ({n})` / `Bajarilgan ({n})` over the cabinet's `active_cases` (today = `deadline_at` is today and not completed/archived; done = completed/archived); card = coloured edge (amber today, blue upcoming, green done), `next_action` or title, `case_number`, deadline date, stage pill. Empty: `Bu yerda vazifa yo'q.`
  7. `Kalendar` panel: next 4 non-cancelled events from `GET /calendar-events` (`EventRow` with date) → `/pro/calendar`. Not drawn when there are none.
  8. `So'nggi faollik` (folded by default, header shows the newest title): 5 latest `notifications` of the cabinet → `/notifications`. Not drawn when none.
  9. `Do'stlarni taklif qiling` gradient card (needs `GET /referrals/me`): `hozir {pct}%` commission, one dot per colleague needed, line `{joined} ta qo'shildi ({invited} taklif) — yana {remaining} tadan keyin komissiya {pct}%` or `Maqsadga erishdingiz: eng past komissiya`. Ladder in code: base 18 %, 5 → 16 %, 10 → 15 %, 20 → 13 %. Tap → `/pro/referrals`.
  10. `LexGo AI` dark card with orb, chip `Beta`, `Hujjatlarni tahlil qiling, maslahat oling, qonunlarni tez toping.`, white button `AI yordamchisini ochish` (advokat → `/pro/assistant`, yurist → `/(tabs)/ai`).
  11. `Yangi keyslar` panel, sub `{n} ta yangi so'rov`: one card per offered order (cabinet `new_orders`, unpaid marketplace ones filtered out): service name + `RespondTimer` (`Javob uchun {time}` / `Javob muddati tugadi`, from `confirmation_deadline_at`), client's words (`details.question`, tap to expand), chips region / budget `so'm` / created; buttons `Qabul qilish` (POST accept), eye `Ko'rib chiqish` (expands text or → `/pro/orders`), x `Rad etish` (DeclineSheet). Empty: `Yangi so'rov yo'q` / `Yangi mijoz so'rovlari shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  12. `Ko'rsatkichlarim` (folded; fold line `{response}% · {avg} daq · {rating|Yangi}`): vacation row `Ta'til rejimi` / `Ta'tildaman` toggle (stored only on the phone, key `lexgo_vacation_<uid>`; note `Ta'til rejimi yoqilgan: yangi buyurtmalar sizga taklif qilinmaydi, javob muddati hisoblanmaydi.`), 4 tiles `Javob foizi`, `Qabul foizi`, `O'rtacha javob vaqti` (`{n} daq`), `Reyting` (`Yangi` when `reviews_count` < 5, hint `5 tadan kam baho — reyting raqami hali ko'rsatilmaydi`), coaching tip (`tipSpeed` / `tipRate` / `tipGood`), text `Reyting hisoblanishi: mijoz bahosi (50%) …`, `Super advokat mezonlari` + lead + 4 steps (`Reyting 4,5 dan yuqori`, `Javob foizi 90% dan yuqori`, `Kamida 3 yillik ish staji ({n} yil)`, `Kamida 10 ta yakunlangan buyurtma ({n} ta)`).
  13. `Mening reytingim` (folded; fold `★ 4.7` or `Hali baholanmagan`): `MyRatingView` of `GET /marketplace/me/rating` (components as bars: `Mijoz baholari`, `Yakunlangan buyurtmalar`, `Javob tezligi`, `Qabul qilish darajasi`, `Tasdiqlangan tajriba`, `Tasdiqlangan yutuqlar`; metrics; `Qoidalar v{v}`). Not drawn on 404.
  14. `Hisob-kitob (payout)` (folded; sub `Keyingi to'lov: {date} (payshanba)`): tiles `Kutilmoqda` / `To'langan` (sums of `GET /payouts/me` by status), rule `Haftada bir — payshanba. Minimal summa 100 000 so'm; kam bo'lsa keyingi haftaga o'tadi…` or `Balans {min} so'mdan kam — keyingi haftaga o'tadi.`, up to 12 rows (amount, date · period, status pill `Kutilmoqda` / `Rejalashtirilgan` / `To'langan` / `Ushlab turilgan` / `Xato`). Empty: `Hali to'lovlar yo'q` / `Yakunlangan buyurtmalar bo'yicha ulushingiz shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  15. advokat: `Obro' va reyting` panel — `Ko'rinuvchanligingizni oshiring va ko'proq mijozga yeting.`, gradient `Ko'rinuvchanlikni oshirish` → `/pro/promotions`, line `Tarifni yangilash` → `/subscription`. yurist: `Hisob xavfsizligi` fold (`2FA, Telegram va xabarnoma sozlamalari`) that unfolds the TwoFactor, Telegram and NotifPrefs cards.
- **Actions:** every tile/panel tap above; accept = `POST /orders/{id}/accept`; decline = DeclineSheet → `POST /orders/{id}/decline`; sheets create a client (`POST /lawyers/me/clients` after `POST /conflicts/check`), a case (`POST /cases`), an event (`POST /calendar-events`); pull-to-refresh and a socket resync invalidate `pro-workspace`, `pro-profile`, `seller-rating`, `pro-requests`, `pro-calendar`, `pro-tasks`, `pro-cases`, `pro-clients`, `pro-referral`, `pay-referral`.
- **States:** cabinet not loaded yet → hero with `—` counters + 3 skeleton cards; cabinet error → hero + `ErrorView` with retry (never the pending layout). Limited → brief hero + `StatusBanner`: title `Profilingiz hali tasdiqlanmagan`, pill = `verification` / `account_status` / `pending`, text = `limited_reason` or `Profil va hujjatlar tekshiruvdan o'tgach barcha imkoniyatlar ochiladi.`, progress `Profil to'liqligi: {done}/{total}` (from `/seller-onboarding/progress`), `Hozircha cheklangan:` + locked pills `Buyurtma qabul qilish` / `Maxfiy chat` / `Qo'ng'iroqlar`, button `Tasdiqlashni davom ettirish` → `/pro/onboarding`; then `InfoCards`: `Tarif` (`Faol · {date} gacha` / `Faol tarif yo'q — imkoniyatlarni kengaytiring`) → `/subscription`, `Hamkasblarni taklif qiling` / `Komissiyangiz kamayadi` → `/pro/referrals`. Offline: cached cabinet is shown; mutations toast errors.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                    | Used for                          | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cabinet`                                                                   | whole home                        | `limited_access`, `limited_reason`, `account_status`, `verification{status,is_verified}`, `seller_type`, `available_actions{accept_orders,secure_chat,calls}`, `profile{lawyer_name,region,license_number,reviews_count,experience_years,total_cases}`, `stats.workload{courts_today,documents_to_review,unread_messages}`, `stats.performance{response_rate,acceptance_rate,avg_response_minutes,rating}`, `stats.finance`, `new_orders[]{id,status,source,created_at,confirmation_deadline_at,region,price | amount,details{question,service_title,budget,region},service{name}}`, `active_cases[]{id,case_number,title | case_type,stage,status,next_action,deadline_at}`, `notifications[]{id,title,body,created_at}`, `secure_chats[]{client_user_id}` |
| `GET /calendar-events`                                                                      | `Kalendar` panel                  | `id,type,title,starts_at,ends_at,status,case_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /referrals/me`                                                                         | referral card                     | `code`, `invited`, `joined`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /marketplace/me/rating`                                                                | rating panel                      | `rating`, `rated`, components `client_reviews,completion_rate,response_score,acceptance_rate,verified_experience,verified_achievements`, metrics, `policy_version`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /payouts/me`                                                                           | payouts panel                     | `items[]{id,amount                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | seller_share,status,period                                                                                 | month,created_at}`                                                                                                              |
| `GET /lawyers/me`, `GET /clients/me`                                                        | completeness ring                 | profile fields, `name`, `email`, `avatar_url`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GET /seller-onboarding/progress`                                                           | limited banner                    | `steps[]{completed}`, `completed_count`, `total_count`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /clients/me/entitlements`                                                              | limited `Tarif` card              | `active`, `ends_at`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `POST /orders/{id}/accept`, `POST /orders/{id}/decline`                                     | new-case cards                    | decline body `{reason_code, reason_text, reason, note}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /lawyers/me/clients`, `POST /conflicts/check`, `POST /cases`, `POST /calendar-events` | quick-action sheets               | see `/clients`, `/pro/cases`, `/pro/calendar`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| user socket (`useResync`)                                                                   | refresh on reconnect / foreground | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** the first screen must show the new-order alert (if any), the plan line and the four counters; the completeness ring is fine high while the profile is incomplete but should drop below the quick actions once it is 100 %. The vacation switch is local-only and misleading (it never reaches the backend); the metrics/rating/payouts folds repeat `/pro/stats` and could be cut to one "stats" link.

---

### `/requests` — `So'rovlar` (tab)

- **Who:** both; the tab is hidden while limited. Subtitle: `{n} ta ish e'tibor talab qiladi` when something needs the professional, else `Sizga kelgan va biriktirilgan ishlar`. The tab badge = items that need action (also kept live by the core `ReqMounts`).
- **Purpose:** one inbox over the four work modules so the professional sees what arrived and what waits on them.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. TabPage header + title.
  2. `ChipBar` with live active counts: `Hujjatlar`, `Tezkor`, `Buyurtmalar`, `Uchrashuvlar` (default `Hujjatlar`).
  3. Segmented `Faol` / `Barchasi` (active = status not final for that module).
  4. Soft button `To'liq bo'limni ochish` → `/pro/docs`, `/pro/urgent`, `/pro/orders`, `/meetings` for the chosen chip.
  5. Rows (`RowCard`, 10 at a time): kind icon (amber tint when `action`, blue otherwise), title or fallback `Hujjat so'rovi` / `Tezkor so'rov` / `Buyurtma` / `Uchrashuv`, subtitle `client · extra` (orders: price `so'm`; meetings: `Audio` / `Video`; urgent: channel `Chat`/`Audio`/`Video` · scheduled time; docs: requested document type or template title), meta = time ago, status pill (aw2.st: `Rejalashtirilgan`, `Uchrashuv ketyapti`, `Muddati o'tgan`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Qabul qilingan`, `Boshlangan`, `Natija tayyor`, `Topshirilgan`, `Mijoz tasdig'i`, `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `Rad etilgan`, `Tugagan`, `O'tkazib yuborilgan`, `Yuborilgan`, `Baholangan`, `Qaytarilgan`, `Hujjat kutilmoqda`; else `admSt.*`; else humanised).
- **Actions:** row tap → docs `/pro/docs/{id}`, urgent `/pro/urgent/{id}`, orders `/pro/orders/{id}`, meetings: live call → `/call/{room_id}`, else `/meetings`. Pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton rows (84 pt); error view; empty per chip `Hujjat so'rovlari yo'q` / `Tezkor ishlar yo'q` / `Marketplace buyurtmalari yo'q` / `Uchrashuvlar yo'q` + `Yangi ishlar shu yerda paydo bo'ladi.`. "Needs action" statuses: docs `claimed,new,open_pool` or `can_claim`; urgent `open_pool,claimed,scheduled,meeting_active,in_progress`; orders `new,paid,sent_to_seller,waiting_seller`; meetings `active,scheduled`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                          | Used for         | Key fields       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------- | --------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/document-requests`                                                               | `Hujjatlar`      | `id,status,title | need                                                                                               | service.title,client.name,requested_document_type   | template.title,updated_at | created_at,can_claim` |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/assigned`                                                           | `Tezkor`         | `id,status,title | payload.need,client.name,payload.channel,payload.scheduled_at,lifecycle.final_statuses,updated_at` |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders?role_view=seller`                                                     | `Buyurtmalar`    | `id              | order_id,status,service_title                                                                      | details.service_title,client.name,price,updated_at` |
| `GET /calls/invited`                                                                              | `Uchrashuvlar`   | `call_id         | id,room_id,title,caller_name,call_type,call_status                                                 | status,started_at`                                  |
| user socket events `document_request.*`, `urgent_advokat.*`, `marketplace.*`, `order.*`, `call.*` | refetch all four | —                |

- **UX note:** the attention count and the first chip with work should be visible without scrolling; the "open full module" button could become a trailing link in the chip row. Today the four lists are fetched at once even when the chip is not chosen.

---

### `/clients` — `Mijozlar` (tab)

- **Who:** both (drawer `Mijozlar`); hidden while limited (tab bar rule). No role differences.
- **Purpose:** the professional's client base (derived by the backend from cases, orders and documents) plus own-base clients and the conflict-of-interest check.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. TabPage header, title `Mijozlar`, subtitle `Siz ishlagan mijozlar`.
  2. `ClientsHero` dark gradient: numbers `Jami`, `Faol ishli`, `To'qnashuv`; buttons `Yangi mijoz` (white) and `Ziddiyat tekshiruvi` (glass).
  3. Search `Ism yoki telefon bo'yicha qidirish`.
  4. Chips with counts: `Hammasi`, `Faol ishli` (has `active_case_ids`), `Qo'lda qo'shilgan` (no orders), `To'qnashuv` (`has_conflict`) — a chip with 0 is hidden unless chosen.
  5. `ClientRow` cards, newest `last_active_at` first, 10 at a time: gradient monogram with green dot (active cases) or amber (conflict), name, phone, last-active date, three counters `ish` / `buyurtma` / `faol ish`, conflict strip `Ziddiyat`.
- **Actions:**
  - Card → `ClientSheet` `Mijoz kartasi` (`GET /lawyers/me/clients/{id}`): hero (avatar, name, company · region, pill `qo'lda kiritilgan`, `Mijoz: {date} dan`); quick tiles `Qo'ng'iroq` (tel:) and `Maxfiy chat` (existing room → `/chat/{id}`; none and not manual → `POST /secure-chats` then open); contact card (`Telefon`, `Kompaniya`, `Hudud`, `Izoh`); `Tarix va hujjatlar` chips `Tarix` / `Ishlar` / `Buyurtmalar` / `To'lovlar` / `Hujjatlar` with counts; rows (`Buyurtma:` / `Ish:` / `Maxfiy chat:` / `Mijoz qo'shildi:` prefixes on the timeline); a case row opens `CaseSheet` on top, an order row → `/pro/orders/{id}`.
  - `Yangi mijoz` → `ClientFormSheet` `LexGo'dan tashqari mijoz` (`Ism majburiy. Telefon, JShShIR va qarshi tomon keyingi ziddiyat tekshiruvlarida ishlatiladi.`): `F.I.Sh.`, `Telefon`, `JShShIR` (14 digits), `Kompaniya`, `Qarshi tomon(lar)`, `Vakillar` (comma lists), `Izoh`; secondary `Ziddiyat tekshiruvi` runs the check in place; Save runs the check first (a failure never blocks), then `POST /lawyers/me/clients`; toast `Mijoz qo'shildi`; after a potential conflict the sheet stays 2.5 s.
  - `Ziddiyat tekshiruvi` → `ConflictSheet` `Manfaatlar to'qnashuvi tekshiruvi`: `Telefon`, `JShShIR`, `Qarshi tomon`, `Vakillar`, button `Tekshirish`; result `To'qnashuv topilmadi` or `Manfaatlar to'qnashuvi ehtimoli — qabul qilishdan oldin tekshiring:` + up to 8 `• title — reason` (reasons: `qarshi tomon boshqa ishda uchraydi`, `vakil boshqa ishda uchraydi`, `telefon mijozlar bazasida`, `JShShIR mijozlar bazasida`, `qarshi tomon mijozlar bazasida`).
- **States:** skeleton cards; error; empty `Hali mijoz yo'q` (no clients at all) / `Hozircha mijoz yo'q` (search/filter) + `Ish olganingizdan so'ng mijozlar shu yerda ko'rinadi. O'z bazangizdagi mijozni ham qo'shishingiz mumkin.`; sheet 404 `Bu mijoz bilan ish topilmadi`; tab empty `Hozircha yo'q`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                       | Used for              | Key fields                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/clients`      | list                  | `id,name,phone,cases_count,orders_count,active_case_ids[],has_conflict,last_active_at`              |
| `GET /lawyers/me/clients/{id}` | client card           | `type` (`manual`), `client{id,name,phone,region,company,notes,created_at}`, `cases[]{id,title       | case_type,case_number | work_id,stage,status,updated_at}`, `orders[]{id,title | service.name | service_title,work_id,price | amount,status}`, `payments[]{id,amount,method | provider,description,status}`, `documents[]{id,title | document_type,work_id,status}`, `timeline[]{id,type,title,status,created_at}`, `chats[]{id,status,case_id,order_id}` |
| `POST /lawyers/me/clients`     | own-base client       | `{name,phone,pinfl,company,notes,opponents[],representatives[]}`                                    |
| `POST /conflicts/check`        | conflict check        | body `{phone,pinfl,opponent,representatives[]}` → `status` (`potential_conflict`), `matches[]{title | case_number,reason}`  |
| `POST /secure-chats`           | open a private room   | `{client_user_id, seller_user_id}` → `id`                                                           |
| case sheet                     | see `/pro/cases/[id]` | —                                                                                                   |

- **UX note:** a phone sees the three numbers, the two buttons and the first client; the hero could be slimmer so two cards fit. The two sheets (add client vs. conflict check) overlap in fields — one entry point with a "save as client" toggle would be simpler.

---

### `/pro/market` — `Ishlar bozori` (yurist) / `Imkoniyatlar` (advokat)

- **Who:** both; the drawer row is `Ishlar bozori` for a yurist and `Imkoniyatlar` for an advokat (same route), and is hidden when `available_actions.accept_orders` is false or the account is limited. Inside the page a limited account sees a lock note `Profil tasdiqlangach buyurtmalarni qabul qila olasiz.` and the accept button disabled.
- **Purpose:** the live feed of open orders the professional may take (accept or decline with a reason) before someone else does.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Yangi buyurtmalarni qabul qiling yoki rad eting`.
  2. (limited) amber lock note.
  3. Search `Xizmat yoki hudud qidirish` + sliders button `Filtr va saralash` (badge when a non-default choice is on).
  4. Removable chips of active filters (`Hudud`, `300 ming so'mgacha` / `300 ming – 1 mln` / `1 mln dan yuqori`, `Bugun` / `So'nggi 7 kun`, sort label).
  5. `{n} ta imkoniyat` + live dot `Jonli` (feed refetches every 30 s while focused).
  6. `JobCard` per open order: coloured edge (red < 5 min left, amber < 30 min, else accent), service name + `RespondTimer` (`Javob uchun {time}` / `Javob muddati tugadi`), the client's words in a quote (tap to expand), price pill (green) or `Byudjet ko'rsatilmagan`, region chip, created time; row of `Qabul qilish` (primary), eye `Batafsil`, x `Rad etish`.
- **Actions:** `Qabul qilish` → `POST /orders/{id}/accept` (toast `Buyurtma qabul qilindi`; 409 → `Bu buyurtmani boshqa mutaxassis oldi`; the card leaves the feed); `Batafsil` → `/pro/orders/{id}`; `Rad etish` → `DeclineSheet` `Buyurtmani rad etish` (`Sababni tanlang: mijozga boshqa mutaxassislar taklif qilinadi.`; `Sabab` select: `Manfaatlar to'qnashuvi`, `Mening yo'nalishim emas`, `Bandman`, `Hudud uzoq`, `Narx mos kelmaydi`, `Hujjatlar yetarli emas`, `Oldingi nizo`, `Kasal yoki ta'tilda`, `Boshqa`; `Izoh (ixtiyoriy)`, required for `Boshqa` — `«Boshqa» sababi uchun izoh yozing`) → `POST /orders/{id}/decline`. Filter sheet `Filtr va saralash`: `Saralash` tiles `Yangilari avval` / `Eskilari avval` / `Byudjet: yuqoridan pastga` / `Byudjet: pastdan yuqoriga` / `Javob muddati yaqinlari avval`; `Hudud` chips with counts + `Barcha hududlar`; `Byudjet` segmented `Hammasi` / `< 300k` / `300k–1M` / `> 1M`; `Qachon kelgan` `Hammasi` / `Bugun` / `So'nggi 7 kun`; pinned reset icon + `Ko'rsatish · {n}`. Pull to refresh.
- **States:** 3 skeleton cards; `ErrorView` + retry; empty `Hozircha ish yo'q` / `Yangi buyurtmalar shu yerda paydo bo'ladi. Ro'yxat har 30 soniyada yangilanadi.`; no match `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Qidiruv yoki filtrlarni o'zgartirib ko'ring.` + `Hammasini tozalash`; expired timer closes the accept in place.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                    | Used for                                                   | Key fields                                 |
| --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /orders`               | feed (only open, not-taken, not unpaid-marketplace orders) | `id,status,source,work_id,region,price     | amount,created_at,confirmation_deadline_at`(or`details.confirmation_deadline_at`), `details{question,service_title}`, `service.name` |
| `POST /orders/{id}/accept`  | take the job                                               | 409 = taken                                |
| `POST /orders/{id}/decline` | decline                                                    | `{reason_code, reason_text, reason, note}` |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cabinet`   | lock note                                                  | `limited_access`                           |

- **UX note:** the timer and the accept button are the whole screen — the first card should be fully visible with its countdown. The feed title differs by role but the content is identical; `Imkoniyatlar` hides that these are paid orders waiting for an answer.

---

### `/pro/orders` — `Xizmat buyurtmalari`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Xizmat buyurtmalari`); limited shows notice `Hisobingiz hali tasdiqlanmagan: buyurtma qabul qilish yopiq.` and the drawer hides the row.
- **Purpose:** every paid marketplace order of this seller, with the one thing to do next on each.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Faqat to'lovi tasdiqlangan buyurtmalar sotuvchiga keladi.`
  2. (limited) warn notice.
  3. Search `Xizmat, ID yoki mijoz qidirish` + `Filtr va saralash` button with badge.
  4. Active-filter chips (`Yangi`/`Jarayonda`/…, `300 ming so'mgacha`…, `Bugun`/`Oxirgi 7 kun`/`Oxirgi 30 kun`, sort).
  5. `{n} ta buyurtma` + `Jonli`.
  6. `OrderCard`: edge by stage (new = blue, active = cyan, completed = green, cancelled = red), service title (or work id) + status pill (sl12.status: `To'langan`, `Sizga yuborilgan`, `Qabul qilingan`, `Boshlangan`, `Jarayonda`, `Natija tayyor`, `Sifat tekshiruvi`, `Topshirilgan`, `Mijoz tasdig'ini kutmoqda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Baholangan`, `Bekor qilingan`, `Rad etilgan`, `Yo'qotilgan`, `Ushlab turilgan`, `Chiqarilgan`, `Kutilmoqda`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Bekor qilish ko'rib chiqilmoqda`, `Operator ko'rib chiqmoqda`, `Pul qaytarilmoqda`, `Pul qaytarilgan`, `Muddati o'tgan`), `buyer · work id`, price pill (`Narx yo'q`), created; `AnswerTimer` when the order is an assignment waiting for the answer (`Javob uchun {time}` / `Javob muddati tugadi`, ticks every second); hint box: `Qabul qilish yoki rad etish kerak` / `Ishni davom ettiring` / `Natijani topshirishingiz mumkin` / amber `Qo'shimcha to'lov kutilmoqda`.
- **Actions:** card → `/pro/orders/{id}`; filter sheet: `Holati` chips with counts (`Hammasi`, `Yangi`, `Jarayonda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Bekor qilingan`), `Saralash` tiles (`Yangilari avval`, `Eskilari avval`, `Narx: yuqoridan pastga`, `Narx: pastdan yuqoriga`), `Davr` (`Hammasi`, `Bugun`, `7 kun`, `30 kun`), `Narx` (`Hammasi`, `< 300k`, `300k–1M`, `> 1M`); reset + `Ko'rsatish · {n}`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeletons; error; empty `Hali buyurtmalar yo'q` / `To'lovi tasdiqlangan buyurtmalar shu yerda paydo bo'ladi. Ro'yxat jonli yangilanadi.`; no match + `Hammasini tozalash`. Stage rules (`stageOf`): cancelled = `cancelled,canceled,declined,rejected,lost,refunded,refund_pending`; completed = `completed,rated,done,closed`; paused (supplemental payment / `waiting_payment`) = active; an assignment with `acceptance_deadline_at` = new; accepted but not paid = active; unpaid (`pending_payment,waiting_payment,pending,new,seller_selection` or payment not paid/held) is hidden; `paid,sent_to_seller` = new; everything else active. Realtime: `marketplace.*`, `order.*`, `notification.created` refetch; supplemental-payment events toast `Qo'shimcha to'lov so'raldi` / `…tasdiqlandi, ish davom etadi` / `…rad etildi` / `…muddati o'tdi, qo'llab-quvvatlashga yuborildi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                              | Used for     | Key fields                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders?role_view=seller`                                         | list         | `id                                | order_id, work_id, status, payment_status | payment.status, service_id, service_title | details.service_title, price | payment.amount, currency, client{name,lexgo_id,id}, client_user_id, created_at, updated_at, acceptance_deadline_at | confirmation_deadline_at, can_start, start_url, can_complete, complete_url, checklist_url, financials_url, contact_unlocked, can_start_chat, can_create_call, secure_chat_room_id | chat_url, work_phase, additional_payment\* | payment.target_type=marketplace_additional_payment` |
| socket `marketplace.*`, `marketplace_*`, `order.*`, `order_*`, `notification.created` | live refresh | `event`, `notification.data.event` |

- **UX note:** the hint line is the most valuable element and should be the first thing on the card; status pills with 22 values are hard to scan — the four stage colours already carry the meaning.

### `/pro/orders/[id]` — title = `work_id` (fallback `Marketplace buyurtmalari`)

- **Who:** both. Gating: `available_actions.accept_orders === false` removes every action except chat and shows `Bu buyurtma bilan ishlashga ruxsatingiz yo'q` on an assignment; chat/calls follow `can_start_chat` / `can_create_call` / `contact_unlocked`. Opened fresh by id on every mount (a push lands here).
- **Purpose:** work one paid order through its flow: answer the assignment before the deadline, start, review and set the delivery time, tick the checklist, deliver the result, ask for a supplemental payment or a cancellation, and read the money.
- **Layout, top → bottom (PageScroll with pull-to-refresh):**
  1. `OrderSummaryCard`: service title + status pill, price `so'm`, 5-step track `To'landi · Qabul · Ish · Topshirildi · Yakun` (hidden when cancelled; a status after `completed` stays on the last step).
  2. `AssignmentCard` (phase `assigned` or `replacing`): amber box `Sizga yangi buyurtma biriktirildi` + big countdown (hh:mm:ss, red under 5 min), hint `Belgilangan vaqt ichida qabul qiling yoki rad eting. Muddat o'tsa, buyurtmani call-markaz boshqa mutaxassisga beradi.`, buttons `Qabul qilish` (gradient) / `Rad etish` (line → DeclineSheet). After the answer the box turns grey with `Qabul qilindi` / `Rad etildi` / `Bu buyurtmani boshqa ijrochi oldi` (409) / `Bu buyurtma bilan ishlashga ruxsatingiz yo'q` (403); when the deadline passes: `Javob berish vaqti tugadi — buyurtmani call-markaz boshqa mutaxassisga beradi` and the order is re-read.
  3. `FlowLead` (every other phase): icon + phase title + the seller's one-line explanation. Phases (mf.phase → mf.lead.seller): `received` `Qabul qilindi` → `Buyurtma hali sizga biriktirilmagan.`; `advance` / `pending` `To'lov kutilmoqda` → `Mijoz to'lovi kutilmoqda. Chat va qo'ng'iroq to'lov tasdiqlangandan keyin ochiladi.` / `Mijoz to'lovi tasdiqlanishi kutilmoqda.`; `paid` `To'langan` → `To'lov tasdiqlandi. Ishni boshlang — o'rganish bosqichi bo'lsa, muddatni u tugagach belgilaysiz.`; `reviewing` `Materiallar o'rganilmoqda` → `Mijoz materiallarini o'rganing va ish muddatini belgilang. Nazorat ro'yxati shundan keyin ochiladi.`; `in_progress` `Ish jarayonida` → `Nazorat ro'yxatini to'ldiring va muddat ichida natija bilan yakunlang.`; `rework` `Qayta bajarishda` → `Mijoz shikoyati asosli topildi: ishni qayta bajaring…`; `cancelReview` `Bekor qilish ko'rib chiqilmoqda` → `Bekor qilish so'rovi operatorda. Qaror chiqquncha muddat to'xtatilgan.`; `resolution` `Qaror kutilmoqda` → `Buyurtmani operator ko'rib chiqmoqda.`; `replacing` `Mutaxassis almashtirilmoqda` → `Buyurtma sizga taklif qilindi. To'lov allaqachon qilingan — …`; `refund` `Pul qaytarilmoqda` → `Buyurtma bo'yicha pul qaytarilmoqda. Chat va qo'ng'iroqlar yopilgan.`; `refunded` `Pul qaytarildi` → `Buyurtma bekor qilindi, mijozga pul qaytarildi.`; `completed` `Yakunlandi` → `Ish yakunlandi. Mijoz belgilangan vaqt ichida baholashi mumkin.`; `rated` `Baholandi` → `Ish yakunlandi va mijoz baholadi.`; `cancelled` `Bekor qilindi` → `Buyurtma bekor qilingan.`; `attention` `Operator ko'rib chiqmoqda` → `Buyurtmani operator ko'rib chiqmoqda.` Tone: red cancelled, amber waiting phases, green finished, accent otherwise.
  4. `WorkTimeCard` (only when a clock runs): reviewing → `Materiallarni o'rganish` + `{time} qoldi`, `3 soatdan kam vaqt qoldi` under 3 h, expired `O'rganish muddati o'tdi. Call-markaz bu kechikishni hal qilmoqda.`, note block `Izoh` = `review_seller_note`; rework → `Qayta bajarish`, `{n}-marta qayta bajarish`, `Qayta bajarish muddati: {date}`, note `Call-markaz izohi`; in_progress → `Bajarish muddati`, `{time} qoldi`, `Muddat: {date}`, `Belgilangan muddat: {d}` (`5 ish kuni` / `2 soat`), expired `Muddat o'tdi`. Reaching zero re-reads the order.
  5. `RefundCard` `Pulni qaytarish` (phase refund/refunded): amount + status pill (`Tasdiq kutilmoqda`, `Jarayonda`, `Qaytarildi`, `Rad etildi`, `To'lov olinmagan`, `Operator qaytaradi`), `Qo'lda ko'rib chiqilmoqda` / `Qaytarishni to'lov tizimi emas, operator tasdiqlaydi…` when manual, `Yangilangan: {date}`; load fail + retry.
  6. `ActiveCallBar` when the chat is open and the order is not finished: a call already running in the room, with join.
  7. `AdditionalCard` `Qo'shimcha to'lov` (when a supplemental request exists or status `waiting_payment`): notice `Mijoz to'lovini kutmoqda. Ish to'xtatilgan: so'rov 24 soat amal qiladi.` (escalated: `24 soat o'tdi: so'rov qo'llab-quvvatlashga yuborildi.`); facts `Holat` (`Kutilmoqda`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Tasdiqlandi`, `To'landi`, `Rad etildi`, `Operatorga uzatildi`, `Bekor qilindi`, `Muddati tugadi`), `Yangi jami summa`, `Qolgan vaqt` (hh:mm:ss), `Amal qilish muddati`, the seller's note.
  8. `BuyerCard` `Xaridor`: avatar, name, `LGC-…` public id (never the phone), lock line `Telefon raqami yashirin: aloqa xavfsiz chat orqali.` or `Telefon to'lovdan keyin ochiladi`; `Chatni ochish` (open) / `Chatni ko'rish` (read-only after the order closed) / locked text `Chat buyurtmani qabul qilganingizdan keyin ochiladi.` or `Mijoz to'lovi kutilmoqda. Chat va qo'ng'iroq to'lov tasdiqlangandan keyin ochiladi.`; call buttons `Audio` / `Video` (only the `allowed_call_types`, not while assigned/replacing/resolution/closed).
  9. `ChecklistCard` `Nazorat ro'yxati` (needs `checklist_url`; not before payment, not replacing/cancelled): lead `Har bir bandni bajaring yoki sababini yozib o'tkazing. Mijoz o'zgarishlarni darhol ko'radi.`, progress bar `{done} / {total} bajarildi · N%` + `Majburiy: {done} / {total}`; items: icon (done ✓ green, skipped ⏭ amber, pending ○), title, comment or `Sabab: …`, attached files (open in preview), marks `Majburiy`, `Izoh kerak`, `Fayl kerak`, `Rasm kerak`; editable only in `in_progress` / `rework` (chevron → item sheet), else status word (`Kutilmoqda` / `Bajarildi` / `Sabab bilan o'tkazildi`); locked note `Buyurtma yakunlangan — nazorat ro'yxati endi o'zgarmaydi.`; empty `Bu xizmatda nazorat ro'yxati yo'q.`; error `Nazorat ro'yxatini yuklab bo'lmadi.`; a blocking item is highlighted for 5 s when the completion was refused.
  10. `OrderQuoteCard` `Mijoz nimani sotib oldi` (package snapshot: `Paket`, `Tarif` `Basic`/`Standard`/`Premium`, `Muddat`, included list; or quote rows base / options / total) and/or `PriceCard` `Narx nimadan iborat` (pricing snapshot components with the backend's explanation, `Jami`, `Tarixiy hisob-kitob`).
  11. `OrderResultCard` `Natija` (only when there is a result, a rating or an open rating window): result text, file buttons, `Mijoz bahosi`: stars + comment, or `Mijoz baholashi uchun qoldi: {mm:ss}`, or `Baholash oynasi yopilgan, baho qo'yilmagan` / `Baho hali yo'q`.
  12. `PaymentsCard` `To'lov bosqichlari` (needs `financials_url`; not while assigned): payment rows (`Oldindan to'lov ({pct}%)`, `Yakuniy to'lov ({pct}%)`, `Xizmat to'lovi`, `Qo'shimcha to'lov`, `Qaytarish`, `To'lov`) with status pills (`To'langan`, `Kutilmoqda`, `Amalga oshmadi`, `Bekor qilingan`, `Qaytarilgan`, `Muddati o'tgan`), planned rows `Rejalashtirilgan`, `Sizga to'lovlar` payouts (`Buyurtmani qabul qilish`, `Birinchi majburiy bosqich`, `Yakun`; `LexGo komissiyasi: …`) + plan `Qabul qilganda {a}%, birinchi majburiy bosqichda {b}%, yakunda {c}% — LexGo komissiyasi ({k}%) yakuniy bosqichdan ushlanadi.`; facts `Yakuniy narx`, `LexGo komissiyasi (N%)`, `Sizga tushadi`, `Hisob-kitob` pill (`Kutilmoqda`, `Ushlab turilgan`, `Shikoyat sababli ushlab turilgan`, `O'tkazishga tayyor`, `O'tkazilgan`, `Mijozga qaytarilgan`, `Qayta bajarish tugaguncha ushlab turiladi`, `Mijozga qaytariladi`, `Chiqarishga tayyor`); `To'lov rejasi: {a}% oldindan, {b}% yakunda`; footer `Narx buyurtma berilganda qayd etilgan va keyin narx qoidalari o'zgarsa ham o'zgarmaydi.` / `Tarixiy hisob-kitob`; empty `Hali to'lov yo'q`; fail `To'lov ma'lumotlarini yuklab bo'lmadi` + retry.
  13. `OrderFactsCard` `Buyurtma tafsilotlari`: `Buyurtma ID`, `To'lov` (payment status), `Aloqa usuli` (`Chat` / `Audio qo'ng'iroq` / `Video qo'ng'iroq` / `Uchrashuv`), `Qulay vaqt`, `Yaratilgan`, `Ish boshlangan`, `Topshirish muddati`, `Yakunlangan`, `Yangilangan`, `Mijoz izohi`.
  14. `OrderTimeline` `Holatlar tarixi` (unified timeline, else status history; newest first; `Hali o'zgarishlar yo'q`) and `To'lov bosqichlari` milestones (`{index}. {title}`, `{percent}% · amount`, status pill).
  15. Sticky footer `OrderActionsBar` when any action is allowed: first action as the big button, the rest under `Yana` (action list).
- **Actions (backend flags decide, never the status alone):**
  - `Qabul qilish` (status `sent_to_seller`/`paid` without an assignment deadline) → `POST /orders/{id}/accept`.
  - `Ishni boshlash` (`can_start` + `start_url`, not paused, contact unlocked) → `StartWorkSheet` `Ishni hozir boshlaysizmi?` (`Boshlaganingizda nazorat ro'yxati qotiriladi va mijozga xabar boradi…`, `Ha, boshlash`; a note field only when there is no `start_url`) → `POST {start_url}` or `PATCH /orders/{id}/status {status:"in_progress"}`; toast `Ish boshlandi` or `Ish boshlandi. Avval mijoz materiallarini o'rganing va muddatni belgilang.`
  - `Muddatni belgilash` (`work_phase === "reviewing"`) → `ReviewDurationSheet` (`Ishni qancha vaqtda topshirasiz?`: `Muddat` number ≥ 1, `Birlik` `Daqiqa`/`Soat`/`Kun`, `Hisob` `Kalendar kunlari`/`Ish kunlari`, `Tasdiqlash`) → `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/review/complete`; done `Muddat belgilandi — endi nazorat ro'yxati bo'yicha ishlang.`; `delivery_duration_invalid` marks the field; `order_not_in_review` closes the sheet.
  - `Yakunlash` (`can_complete`, active, not paused, not reviewing) → `CompleteSheet` `Buyurtmani yakunlash` (`Natija matnini yozing va/yoki fayl biriktiring. Mijozning tasdig'i kerak emas: baholash oynasi darhol ochiladi.`; `Natija matni`, chips of files, `Fayl` / `Rasm` uploaded through the order's secure chat, `Kamida bitta narsa kerak: matn yoki fayl`, `Yakunlash`) → `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/complete-v2 {result_text, result_files[{id,name}]}` (fallback `POST /marketplace/me/orders/{id}/complete`); toast `Buyurtma yakunlandi`. If required items are open (locally or `required_checklist_incomplete`): sheet `Yakunlashdan oldin majburiy bandlarni bajaring` / `{n} ta majburiy band hali bajarilmagan yoki sababi yozilmagan.` listing the items, `Nazorat ro'yxatini ochish` scrolls to and highlights the item.
  - `Qo'shimcha to'lov so'rash` (active/new, not paused, has service or package) → `AdditionalSheet` `Qo'shimcha to'lov so'rovi` (`To'langan: {sum}`; notice `Faqat Studio belgilagan shartlarni tanlash mumkin. Yangi jami summa to'langanidan katta bo'lishi kerak…`; option sections multi-select from the Studio price options, live quote, `Summa taxminiy ko'rsatilgan; yakuniy summani server tasdiqlaydi.`, `Izoh` / `Nega bu qo'shimcha shart kerak?`, `So'rovni yuborish`; `Bu xizmat uchun qo'shimcha shartlar belgilanmagan`) → `POST /marketplace/me/orders/{id}/additional-payment-request {selected_option_ids, note}`; toast `So'rov yuborildi, mijoz to'lovi kutilmoqda`.
  - `Bekor qilishni so'rash` (`can_request_cancellation`) → `CancelRequestSheet` (`So'rov call-markazga boradi. Operator ko'rib chiqquncha buyurtma muddati to'xtatiladi.`; `Sabab` 3–2000, `Kamida 3 ta belgi`, `Yuborish`) → `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/cancellation-review {reason}`; done `So'rov yuborildi — operator ko'rib chiqadi.`
  - `Rad etish` (status `sent_to_seller`, no assignment deadline) → DeclineSheet (see `/pro/market`).
  - Checklist item → `ChecklistItemSheet` (title = item): `Holat` segmented `Bajarildi` / `O'tkazib yuborish` (only if `skip_allowed`) / `Hali bajarilmagan`; `Izoh` / `Izoh (majburiy)` / `O'tkazish sababi (majburiy)`; `Fayl biriktirish` / `Fayllar (kamida bittasi majburiy)` / `Rasm qo'shish` (uploads go through the room's `POST /secure-chats/{room}/messages/upload`, `Fayl biriktirish uchun buyurtma chati kerak.` without a room); inline rules `Bandni o'tkazish uchun sababini yozing.`, `Bu band uchun izoh majburiy.`, `…kamida bitta fayl biriktiring.`, `…kamida bitta rasm qo'shing.`, `Majburiy bandni o'tkazib yuborib bo'lmaydi — uni bajaring.`; `Saqlash` → `PATCH /marketplace/orders/{id}/checklist/{itemId} {status, comment, files[], images[]}`; toast `«{title}» saqlandi`.
  - Chat → `/chat/{room_id}`; `Audio` / `Video` → room call flow (`POST /secure-chats/{room}/calls`, joins a running call instead of starting a second one).
- **States:** QueryBoundary skeleton / error; not this seller's or 404/403 → `Buyurtma topilmadi` / `Ro'yxatni yangilab ko'ring.`; every mutation runs with `networkMode: "always"` (offline fails at once with a retry toast, never queued); a 409 or a known flow code refetches the order and toasts the `mf.err.*` text (e.g. `Buyurtma holati o'zgargan — ma'lumot yangilandi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, `Yakunlash uchun natija matnini yozing yoki fayl biriktiring.`, `Buyurtma allaqachon yakunlangan.`); sockets `marketplace.*`, `order.*`, `payment.*`, `notification.created` re-read financials / checklist / timeline (400 ms debounce), `marketplace.checklist_updated` replaces the checklist at once.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                 | Used for                            | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders/{id}` (refetch on every mount)                                                               | the order                           | as in `/pro/orders` plus `details{client_note,preferred_channel,preferred_time,pricing_snapshot,package_snapshot,quote_snapshot,selected_option_ids,result,additional_payment_request_id}`, `package_id`, `result{result_text,result_files[]}`, `rating{available,closed,reason,deadline_at,remaining_seconds,value,comment}`, `acceptance_deadline_at`, `assignment_status`, `waiting_replacement_acceptance`, `can_request_cancellation`, `complaint{rework_required,rework_deadline_at,rework_iteration,resolution_note}`, `review_deadline_at`, `review_seller_note`, `review_duration`, `duration{value,unit,calendar}`, `started_at`, `expected_completed_at`, `completed_at`, `allowed_call_types`, `chat_read_only`, `calls_enabled`, `financial_snapshot.payment{advance_*,final_*}` |
| `GET /marketplace/me/orders`                                                                                             | fallback when the single read fails | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

| `POST /orders/{id}/accept`, `POST /orders/{id}/decline`                                                                  | assignment answer                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST {start_url}` / `PATCH /orders/{id}/status`                                                                         | start                               | `{status:"in_progress", note}` → `work_phase`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/review/complete`                                                                          | set delivery time                   | `{duration_value, duration_unit, duration_calendar}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/complete-v2`                                                                              | deliver                             | `{result_text, result_files[{id,name}]}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /marketplace/orders/{id}/cancellation-review`                                                                      | cancellation                        | `{reason}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `POST /marketplace/me/orders/{id}/additional-payment-request`                                                            | supplemental payment                | `{selected_option_ids, note}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GET /marketplace/services/{id}/price-options` or `GET /marketplace/service-packages/{id}/price-options`, `POST …/quote` | option picker + quote               | option sections, `total_price`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `POST /secure-chats/{room}/messages/upload`                                                                              | result / proof files                | `id`, `file{path,name}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /secure-chats/{room}/calls`                                                                                        | calls                               | `type`, participants                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cabinet`                                                                                                | `accept_orders` gate                | `available_actions`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

- **UX note:** on a phone the first screen must hold the summary, the assignment countdown or the FlowLead, and the sticky main action; the checklist and money cards can stay below. Today 14 cards stack and the buyer card sits above the checklist even though the checklist is the work.

---

### `/pro/cases` — `Mening ishlarim`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Mening ishlarim`); hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** the professional's own case files (not marketplace orders): status, stage, next step and deadline per client.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Mijozlar bilan ishlar va muddatlar`, header-right `+`.
  2. `CasesSummary` dark gradient: `Jami`, `Faol`, `Muddati yaqin` (late, today or within 3 days), `Yopilgan`.
  3. Search `Mijoz, ish yoki raqam…` + sliders `Filtrlar` (badge when the filter is not the default `Faol`), removable chip.
  4. `CaseRow` cards (10 at a time, newest `updated_at` first): status-coloured edge and monogram, client name as headline (from `/lawyers/me/clients`), `title · case_number`, status pill (`Yangi`, `Faol`, `Tergov`, `Sud` (red), `Apellyatsiya`, `Yakunlangan`, `Arxivlangan`), box `Keyingi qadam` + `next_action`, stage chip (`Yangi`, `Qabul qilingan`, `Topshirilgan`, `Jarayonda`, `Sud`, `Apellyatsiya`, `Yakunlangan`), deadline chip (`Muddati o'tgan · {date}` red, `Bugun · {date}` amber, soon accent).
- **Actions:** `+` and empty-state → `CaseFormSheet` `Yangi ish`: `Sarlavha` (≥ 2 chars, e.g. `Masalan, Ivanov — mehnat nizosi`), `Holat` select, `Bosqich`, `Keyingi qadam`, `Muddat` (date, clearable), `Ish turi` (`general, fuqarolik, jinoyat…`), `Tavsif` → `POST /cases` (toast `Ish ochildi`); row → `/pro/cases/{id}`; filter sheet `Filtrlar` › `Holat`: `Faol` (default), `Barchasi`, then the seven statuses with coloured dots and live counts, pinned `Qayta o'rnatish` + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton; error; empty `Hozircha ish yo'q` / `Ishlaringiz shu yerda ko'rinadi. "+" bilan yangisini oching.`; filtered empty `Bu holatda ish yo'q`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                        | Used for                             | Key fields                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cases`                         | list                                 | `id,work_id,case_number,client_user_id,lawyer_user_id,order_id,case_type,stage,status,title,description,next_action,deadline_at,created_at,updated_at` |
| `GET /lawyers/me/clients`                       | client names                         | `id,name`                                                                                                                                              |
| `POST /cases`                                   | new case                             | `{title,case_type,stage,status,description,next_action,deadline_at,lawyer_user_id}`                                                                    |
| `PATCH /cases/{id}`, `POST /cases/{id}/archive` | manage (from the sheet when editing) | same keys                                                                                                                                              |

- **UX note:** the client name and the deadline chip are what a phone needs first; the four-number summary could shrink to a row of chips. "Case" and "order" are separate things in this app, which is confusing — a case card links to its order only on the detail page.

---

### `/pro/cases/[id]` — title = `case_number`, subtitle = case title

- **Who:** both; no differences.
- **Purpose:** one case in full — status and progress, next step, the client's channels, and the documents, tasks, events, history and AI helpers attached to it.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `CaseHero` dark gradient: status-coloured monogram, client (or title) headline, `title · number`, pills status / type / deadline (`{n} kun qoldi`, `Bugun`, `{n} kun kechikdi`, `Muddat yo'q`, + date), 7 progress bars with `Bosqich: {stage} n/7`, box `Keyingi qadam`.
  2. `CaseActions` round shortcuts: `Boshqarish` (always), `Qo'ng'iroq` (client phone known), `Chat` (private room found by `case_id` / `order_id` in `GET /secure-chats`), `Buyurtma` (`order_id`) → `/pro/orders/{orderId}`.
  3. `Ish haqida` description (when any).
  4. Tabs: `Hujjatlar` (n), `Vazifalar` (n), `Voqealar` (n), `Tarix`, `AI`.
  5. Tab body: **Hujjatlar** — `Fayllar` + `Yuklash` (menu `Fayllar` / `Galereya` / `Kamera`), upload progress bar, `FileRow`s (open preview; more menu = rename / move / star / details / delete), empty `Bu ishga fayl biriktirilmagan`, 403 → `Xotira administrator profilingizni tasdiqlagandan so'ng ochiladi.`; **Vazifalar** — `Vazifa qo'shish`, `TaskRow`s (tick toggles done/todo, tap edits), empty `Bu ish bo'yicha vazifa yo'q`; **Voqealar** — `Voqea qo'shish`, `EventRow`s with date, empty `Bu ish bo'yicha voqea yo'q`; **Tarix** — vertical dots: `Ish yaratildi` / `Ish yangilandi`, `stage · status`, next action, time; empty `Tarix hali bo'sh`; **AI** — lead `AI ish materiallari bo'yicha yordam beradi. Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar maskalanadi.`, tiles `Qisqa xulosa`, `Xronologiya`, `Yetishmayotgan hujjatlar`, `Mijozga savollar`, `AI tahlil qilmoqda…`, result card `Natija` (markdown) + `AI natijasini advokat tekshirishi shart.` + `Nusxalash`.
- **Actions:** `Boshqarish` → `CaseFormSheet` `Ishni boshqarish` (subtitle = number; secondary `Arxivlash` with confirm `Ishni arxivlash` / `Ish arxivga o'tadi. Davom etasizmi?`; toasts `Ish yangilandi`, `Ish arxivlandi`); upload → `POST /workspace/files/upload` with `case_id`; task sheet preset with the case; event sheet preset with the case; AI tile → `POST /ai/cases/{id}/tools`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton (hero + two cards); `ErrorView`; `Ish topilmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                              | Used for                                  | Key fields                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /cases/{id}`                                                                                     | hero                                      | as in the list                                                              |
| `GET /lawyers/me/clients`                                                                             | client name + phone                       | `id,name,phone`                                                             |
| `GET /secure-chats`                                                                                   | chat shortcut                             | rooms `id,case_id,order_id`                                                 |
| `GET /workspace/tree?case_id={id}`                                                                    | documents tab (+ quota for upload checks) | `files[]{id,name,size,mime,ext,case_id,starred,scan}`, `folders[]`, `quota` |
| `POST /workspace/files/upload`, `PATCH/DELETE /workspace/files/{id}`, `POST …/signed-url`             | file actions                              | multipart + `case_id`                                                       |
| `GET /tasks/me`, `POST /tasks`, `PATCH /tasks/{id}`, `PATCH /tasks/{id}/status`, `DELETE /tasks/{id}` | tasks tab                                 | `case_id,case_title,status`                                                 |
| `GET /calendar-events`, `POST`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`, `POST …/reminders/cascade`                         | events tab                                | `case_id`                                                                   |
| `GET /cases/{id}/timeline`                                                                            | history                                   | `id,record_type,title,payload{stage,status,next_action},created_at`         |
| `POST /ai/cases/{id}/tools`                                                                           | AI                                        | `{tool: summary                                                             | chronology | missing_docs | questions, file_version_ids: []}`→`result` |
| `PATCH /cases/{id}`, `POST /cases/{id}/archive`                                                       | manage                                    | —                                                                           |

- **UX note:** deadline + next step in the hero are right; the five tabs are too many for a phone — `Tarix` and `AI` could move under a "more" row so documents and tasks get the space.
