### `/pro/urgent` — `Mening Tezkor ishlarim`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Tezkor ishlar`); hidden while limited. The page shows only the professional's own Tezkor Advokat jobs (the open pool lives in the call-centre web panel). What a card may do comes from the record's `can_manage` flag — a plain advokat/yurist typically only joins, chats and (in a group chat) ends the chat.
- **Purpose:** urgent consultations the professional was assigned to, sorted by how urgently they wait.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Siz ishtirok etayotgan so'rovlar`.
  2. Search `Mijoz, ish raqami yoki mazmun` + sliders `Saralash va filtr` (badge).
  3. Removable chips (status label, `Bugun` / `7 kun`, sort).
  4. `{n} ta so'rov` + `Jonli`.
  5. `ProCard` per request: edge coloured by heat (under 15 min calm, under 1 h warm, then hot), kind icon + kind name (`Advokat bilan videokonsultatsiya`, `Shoshilinch videokonsultatsiya`, `YTH bo‘yicha konsultatsiya`, `Chat orqali konsultatsiya`, `Ikkinchi fikr — yakka advokat`, `Ikkinchi fikr — advokatlar guruhi`), `work id · client`, big `AgeChip` (`hozirgina`, `{n} daq`, `{n} soat {m} daq`, `{n} kun`; ticks live while open), the client's words (tap to expand; `Mijoz izoh qoldirmagan`), chips (up to 2 directions, region, channel, scheduled time, `SLA buzildi`), `StageTrack` `Biriktirildi → Qabul qilindi → Jarayonda → Bajarildi` (cancelled/expired: red `Bekor / muddati o'tdi`), full-width `Batafsil`.
- **Actions:** `Batafsil` → `/pro/urgent/{id}`; filter sheet `Saralash va filtr`: `Saralash` tiles `Avval shoshilinchlari` (default) / `Eng uzoq kutayotgan` / `Yangilari avval` / `Eskilari avval`; `Holat` chips `Barchasi · n` + present statuses (`Navbatda`, `Olingan`, `Rejalashtirilgan`, `Jarayonda`, `Uchrashuvda`, `Yakunlangan`, `Bekor qilingan`, `Muddati o'tgan`) with counts; `Davr` `Hammasi` / `Bugun` / `7 kun`; reset + `Ko'rsatish · {n}`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** 3 tall skeletons; error; empty `Hozircha ish yo'q` / `Sizni Tezkor Advokat so'roviga biriktirishsa, shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; no match `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Qidiruv yoki filtrni o'zgartirib ko'ring.` + `Hammasini tozalash`. Realtime: `urgent_advokat.*` user events refetch.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                    | Used for | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/assigned` (optional `?status=&service_kind=`) | list     | `id,work_id,service_kind,service_title,channel,status,region,payload{need,directions[],scheduled_at,channel},lawyer_count,amount,meeting_minutes,client{name,phone,lexgo_id},assigned_lawyer,claimed_by,operator,group_lawyers[],sla_breached,immediate_call,via_operator,can_manage,created_at,history[]` |

- **UX note:** the age chip and the stage track answer "how late am I" at a glance; the card is tall (quote + chips + track + button) so only one fits a phone — the `Batafsil` button could go, the whole card already opens the record.

---

### `/pro/urgent/[id]` — title = kind name, subtitle = `work_id`

- **Who:** both. Every button depends on the backend's `can_manage` / lifecycle flags; `/urgent/[id]` (notification deep link) shows this same advocate view to a seller.
- **Purpose:** one urgent request: who, what, how long it has waited, join the meeting or chat, and (when the backend lets this user manage it) move it on.
- **Layout, top → bottom (pull-to-refresh):**
  1. `DetailHero`: kind icon + name, work id, pills status (`Navbatda` … `Muddati o'tgan`), `SLA buzildi`, `Darhol`, `Operator yaratgan`; `So'rov kelganiga` + big age chip; `StageTrack`; client row with avatar, name, phone (shown to the professional) and a call button.
  2. `Mijoz so'rovi` card: need text, direction pills (`Jinoiy ishlar`, `Fuqarolik ishlari`, `Oila ishlari`, `Mehnat nizolari`, `Ma'muriy ishlar`, `Iqtisodiy ishlar`, `Soliq nizolari`, `Shartnoma`, `YTH`, `Avtoavariya`), attached files / `Ovozli xabar` (mm:ss) opening the preview.
  3. `Asosiy ma'lumotlar` grid: `Hudud`, `Aloqa turi`, `Davomiyligi` (`{n} daqiqa`), `Narxi`, `Belgilangan vaqt`, `Kelgan vaqt`, `Biriktirilgan advokat`, `Operator`, `Advokatlar soni` (group second opinion), `Qo'ng'iroq holati` (`Qo‘ng‘iroq qilinmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq davom etmoqda` / `Qo‘ng‘iroq tugadi`); `PaymentNote` pill when the backend sends a payment step (`To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Tasdiq kutilmoqda`, `To'langan`, `Tasdiqlangan`, `To'lov amalga oshmadi`, `To'lov bekor qilindi` + amount) and `Telegram orqali tasdiqlash kutilmoqda — tasdiqlanishi bilan holat avtomatik yangilanadi.`
  4. `Outcome`: `Advokatlar guruhi` (group kinds; `Guruh hali tanlanmagan`), `Natija` (`result_summary`, `Keyingi qadam: …`, completed time), `Bekor qilingan` (reason, time), `Ikkinchi fikr asosi` (sources: `Xizmat buyurtmasi` / `Hujjat so'rovi` / `Ish`).
  5. `Bosqichlar tarixi`: `So'rov kelib tushdi` + every status with time, the latest lit.
  6. Sticky `ActionDock`: primary full-width (join or claim, else chat), chat icon beside it, `Boshqa amallar` opens `Qo'shimcha amallar` sheet with the rest.
- **Actions:** `Meetingga kirish` (joinable → call screen of the room); `Ishni olish` (`can_manage` + claimable → `POST /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/claim`; taken → toast); `Chatga o‘tish` (room id → `/chat/{room}`); `Uchrashuv boshlash` (manage, live, channel not chat → `POST …/meeting`); `Guruhni belgilash` / `Guruhni o'zgartirish` (manage, group kind → GroupSheet with candidates); `Chatni yakunlash` (group chat, manage or the advocate on it → ChatCompleteSheet, `Guruh chat yopiladi, so'rov yakunlanadi va mijozga baholash oynasi ochiladi…` → `POST /urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/chat/complete {summary}`); `Yakunlash` (manage → CompleteSheet); `Boshqa advokatga` (manage → TransferSheet); `Statusni o'zgartirish` (manage, `next_statuses` → `PATCH …/status {status, note}`); `Bekor qilish` (manage → `POST /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/cancel {reason}`).
- **States:** 4 skeletons; `ErrorView`; `joinWaiting` line `Meeting hali boshlanmadi — navbatchi advokat uni boshlaganda tugma aktiv bo‘ladi.` on the client-style head (shared `DetailScreen`); payment pending copy above.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                               | Used for         | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /urgent-advokat/requests/{id}`                                                                    | the record       | all list fields plus `next_statuses[]`, `history[]{from,to,at}`, `files[]{id,name,url,size,content_type,seconds,voice}`, `sources[]{type,id,status,title,created_at}`, `room_id`, `call_id`, `call_status`, `call_channel`, `result_summary`, `next_action`, `completed_at`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_at`, `payment{status,amount,provider}`, `rating{…}` |
| `POST /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/claim`, `…/meeting`, `PATCH …/status`, `POST …/cancel` | manage actions   | `{status, note}`, `{reason}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `POST /urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/chat/complete`                                                     | end a group chat | `{summary}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /call-center/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/candidates`                                             | group picker     | candidates                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `/secure-chats/{room}/calls/…`                                                                         | join the meeting | join token                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

- **UX note:** the primary button (join) and the age chip must be above the fold; the facts grid repeats the hero pills. Most "more" actions are call-centre only and will be empty for a normal professional.

### `/pro/docs` — `Hujjat so'rovlari`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Hujjat so'rovlari` in both lists); hidden while limited. The system editor route (`claim` / `editor`) is governed by the backend flags `can_claim` / `can_open_editor` / `system_editor`.
- **Purpose:** the document-preparation requests assigned to this professional (template-assisted, from scratch, review): take, prepare in the editor, hand over, send.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `So'rovlar: {n}` (else `Menga biriktirilgan hujjat ishlari`).
  2. `SummaryStrip` three tiles `Jarayonda` / `Yangi` / `Yuborilgan` with counts (tap = tab).
  3. `ChipBar` tabs (each a server-side `?status=`): `Jarayonda` (default, `claimed`), `Yangi`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Yuborilgan`, `Hammasi`.
  4. Search `Mijoz, hujjat yoki xizmat bo'yicha qidirish` + `Filtr` button (badge) and removable chips (`Shablon bo'yicha` / `Noldan tayyorlash` / `Hujjatni tekshirish`, `Eskilari avval`).
  5. `DocRow` cards (10 at a time, footer `{a} / {b}` + `Yana 10 ta`): flow icon tile, title (2 lines), client avatar + name, age (amber with a clock after 24 h `kechikmoqda`), status pill (`Ochiq`, `Yangi`, `Jarayonda`, `To'lov kutilmoqda`, `Yuborilgan`, `Bekor qilingan`), file-count pill, quick action `Qabul qilish` (`can_claim`) or `Tahrirlash` (editor allowed).
- **Actions:** card → `DocSheet` (bottom sheet): head, `DocBlock` (`Muharrir yopiq` + `editor_block_reason`, `Bu hujjat uchun tizim ichidagi muharrir qo'llab-quvvatlanmaydi…`), `Jarayon` 3-step line (`Yangi` → `Jarayonda` → `Yuborildi`) with a hint per status (`Qabul qiling va ishni boshlang`, `Mijoz to'lovi kutilmoqda`, `Muharrirda tayyorlab, mijozga yuboring`, `Hujjat mijozga yuborilgan`, `So'rov to'xtatilgan`, `So'rov rad etilgan`), `Mijoz so'rovi` (need, answers; `Mijoz matn yozmagan`), `Asosiy ma'lumotlar` (`Hujjat turi`, `Xizmat`, `Advokat`, `Yaratilgan`, `Yangilangan`, `Til`, `Kutmoqda` (+ `kechikmoqda`), `Fayllar`), `Fayllar` (roles `Yuborilgan` / `Mijoz fayli` / `Shablon`, tap shares), `Mijoz bahosi` stars; sticky bar: contextual `Qabul qilish` / `Tahrirlash` / `Topshirish`, big `Yuklab olish` (disabled with `Hozircha yuklab olinadigan fayl yo'q`), round `Batafsil` → `/pro/docs/{id}`. Claim → `POST /call-center/document-requests/{id}/claim` then replace to the editor; `Topshirish` → `TransferSheet` `Boshqa advokatga topshirish` (candidate search `Advokat ismi bo'yicha qidirish`, `Call-center` tag, `Band: {n}`, `Sabab (ixtiyoriy)`, confirm `Ish {name} advokatga topshiriladi va sizdan olinadi. Davom etasizmi?`, `Topshirish` → `POST /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}/transfer`; toast `Ish topshirildi`). Filter sheet `Filtrlar`: `Ish turi` tiles with counts, `Saralash` `Yangilari birinchi` / `Eskilari birinchi`, `Tozalash` + `Ko'rsatish · {n}`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** 4 row skeletons; error; empty `Hozircha so'rov yo'q` + per tab (`Siz olgan va hozir ishlayotgan hujjatlar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`, `Sizga to'g'ridan-to'g'ri biriktirilgan yangi so'rovlar…`, `Mijoz to'lovini kutayotgan so'rovlar…`, `Mijozga yuborilgan hujjatlar…`, `Sizga tegishli barcha hujjat ishlari…`); filtered `Filtrga mos ish topilmadi` / `Filtrlarni o'zgartiring yoki tozalang.` + `Tozalash`; claim errors `Bu ishni boshqa advokat allaqachon oldi.`, `Ishni faqat call-center advokatlar olishi mumkin.`, `So'rov topilmadi yoki pooldan olib tashlangan.`. Realtime `useDocSync` (document_request events).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                           | Used for                    | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /lawyers/me/document-requests?status=`                                        | tabs                        | `id,document_request_id,status,title,need,requested_document_type,custom_type,flow,language,editor_source,client{name},service{title},template{title,file},answers[]{label,value},files[]{id,name,mime,size,path,note},final_file,assigned_lawyer{id,name},can_claim,can_open_editor,editor_block_reason,system_editor,legacy_upload,chat_room_id,created_at,updated_at` |
| `GET /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}`                                           | sheet (live)                | same                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `POST /call-center/document-requests/{id}/claim`                                   | take                        | → `id,status,chat_room_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `POST /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}/transfer`                                 | hand over                   | `{target_lawyer_user_id, reason}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /call-center/urgent-advokat/candidates` (fallback `GET /matching/candidates`) | transfer picker             | `id,name,kind,region,rating,workload,call_center`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /document-requests/{id}`                                                      | client rating (best effort) | `rating{submitted,available,stars,comment,deadline}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

- **UX note:** the three tiles + five tabs + search + filter is a lot of chrome before the first card; the tiles already are the three tabs that matter. The late marker (amber clock) is the key signal and deserves the first row.

---

### `/pro/docs/[id]` — `So'rov`, subtitle = client name

- **Who:** both; actions by backend flags.
- **Purpose:** the full record of one document request with the one next step pinned at the bottom.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `DocHead`: title (or client), pills status / flow (`Shablon bo'yicha` …) / requested type; facts `Mijoz`, `Xizmat`, `Shablon`, `Biriktirilgan advokat`, `Yaratilgan`, `Yangilangan`.
  2. `DocBlock` (editor closed) in the backend's words.
  3. `Mijoz ehtiyoji`: the need + answered fields.
  4. `Yuborilgan hujjat` (final file) and `Fayllar` (client files + clean template; `Mijoz fayl biriktirmagan`) — tap opens the in-app preview.
  5. `Mijoz bahosi`: `★ n/5 — comment` / `Baholash muddati: {at}` / `Mijoz hali baholamagan` (hidden if the endpoint is closed).
  6. Hint `Mijozning telefon raqami ko'rsatilmaydi — u bilan chat yoki uchrashuv orqali bog'laning.`
  7. Sticky footer: `Ishni olish` (claim) / `Muharrirni ochish` / `Yuborilgan hujjatni ko'rish` + `Yana` menu.
- **Actions:** claim → `POST …/claim` then the editor; `Muharrirni ochish` → `/pro/docs/{id}/editor`; menu: `Mijoz bilan chat` (`/chat/{room}`), `Uchrashuv boshlash` / `Uchrashuvga qo'shilish` (`POST /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}/meeting {call_type, title}` → call screen; toast `Uchrashuv ochilmoqda…`), `O'z faylimni yuborish (DOCX)` (`FulfillSheet` `Tayyor faylni yuborish` → `POST …/fulfill-file` multipart, `.docx` ≤ 25 MB), `Boshqa advokatga topshirish`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** QueryBoundary skeleton / error.
- **Data:** `GET /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}` (see list), `POST …/claim`, `POST …/transfer`, `POST …/meeting`, `POST …/fulfill-file`, `GET /document-requests/{id}` (rating), `GET /secure-chats/{room}/calls` (live call lookup for the menu).
- **UX note:** the footer already carries the decision; the body could lead with the need and the files instead of the fact rows.

---

### `/pro/docs/[id]/editor` — `Hujjat muharriri`

- **Who:** both; only when the backend allows the system editor (`can_open_editor`, `system_editor`, status in `open_pool/claimed/completed`). Edge-swipe back is disabled; screenshots are blocked in release builds; the screen stays awake up to 5 min after the last touch.
- **Purpose:** take the work, choose what the document starts from, edit it in OnlyOffice and send it to the client.
- **Layout, top → bottom (by state):**
  1. `ClaimGate` (editor answers 409 / `can_claim`): `Bu ish hali olinmagan` / `Muharrirni ochish uchun avval ishni o'zingizga oling.` + `Ishni olish`.
  2. `DoneGate` (completed): `Hujjat mijozga yuborilgan` / `Yuborilgan faylni ko'rib chiqishingiz mumkin.` + `Yuborilgan hujjatni ko'rish`.
  3. `ClosedGate` (`can_open_editor` false): `Muharrir yopiq` + reason (`editor_block_reason` or fallback), `Shablonni ko'rish`, `O'z faylimni yuborish (DOCX)`, retry.
  4. `SourceStage` `Qanday boshlaymiz?` (first open): `Toza shablon`, `Bo'sh hujjat`, `AI tayyorlagan draft`, `Mijoz yuborgan fayl` (unavailable ones marked `Bu variant bu ish uchun mavjud emas`), `O'z DOCX faylimni yuklayman`; footer `Muharrirni ochish`.
  5. `EditorSession`: `EditorBar` (back, title, status pill, save chip `Saqlanmoqda…` / `Saqlandi` / `Saqlashda xatolik`, gradient `Yuborish` (enabled once ready and not saving), more); OnlyOffice WebView with `Muharrir ochilmoqda…` overlay (after 30 s `Muharrir kutilganidan sekin ochilmoqda.` + retry); fallback `Onlayn muharrir ulanmagan` / `Muharrir ochilmadi` with `Loyiha faylini ochish` (.docx preview) and retry.
- **Actions:** menu: `So'rov ma'lumotlari` (InfoSheet with head, need, files), `Manbani tanlash` (SourceSheet; `Manbani almashtirasizmi? Joriy muharrir sessiyasi yopiladi…`), chat, meeting, `O'z faylimni yuborish (DOCX)`, `Boshqa advokatga topshirish`; `Yuborish` → `FinalizeSheet` `Mijozga yuborish` (`Tayyor hujjat mijozga yuboriladi va ish yakunlanadi.`, `Mijoz uchun izoh (ixtiyoriy)`, `Ha, yuborish`) → `POST …/editor/finalize {notes}` → toast `Mijozga yuborildi` → replace to `/pro/docs/{id}`; back while saving asks `Muharrirdan chiqasizmi?`.
- **States:** skeleton; `ErrorView`; `Muharrir fayli hali serverga saqlanmadi…` when finalising too early.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                               | Used for           | Key fields                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}`                                                                               | gates              | flags above                                                                       |
| `GET …/editor`                                                                                                         | OnlyOffice session | `session_id, provider, configured, server_url, origin, config (signed), file_url` |
| `GET …/editor/sources`, `POST …/editor/source {source_type, attachment_id}`, `POST …/editor/source-upload` (multipart) | source choice      | `current, options[]{key,type,title,note,available,attachment_id,kind}`            |
| `PUT …/editor/draft`                                                                                                   | save state         | `saved_at`                                                                        |
| `POST …/editor/finalize`                                                                                               | send               | `{notes}` → `status, file_name, file_path, rating_deadline`                       |
| `POST …/fulfill-file`                                                                                                  | own DOCX           | multipart + `notes`                                                               |
| `POST …/meeting`, `POST …/claim`, `POST …/transfer`                                                                    | menu               | —                                                                                 |

- **UX note:** on a phone the WebView must own the screen; the header is already minimal. The source stage is the right place to explain what the AI draft is.

### `/pro/tasks` — `Vazifalar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Vazifalar`); hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** the professional's personal to-do list by stage, with checklists and links to cases.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Ishlaringizni bosqichlar bo'yicha boshqaring`, header-right `+`.
  2. `StaleNote` (when showing cached data).
  3. Search `Vazifa, ish yoki tavsif bo'yicha qidirish`.
  4. Segmented `Kanban` / `Ro'yxat`; list mode adds chips `Barchasi`, `Kutilmoqda`, `Jarayonda`, `Tekshiruvda`, `Bajarildi` with counts.
  5. Board: a horizontal carousel, one column per stage (dot, name, count; 10 cards then `ShowMore`; empty `Bu bosqichda vazifa yo'q`). `TaskCard`: title (struck when done), priority pill (`Past` / `O'rta` / `Yuqori`), stage chip (tap → move menu), `Bloklangan` pill, pending-sync pill, `case title · Ro'yxat {done}/{total}` + progress bar, due date (red when overdue), arrows to the previous / next stage (`Kutilmoqdaga`, `Jarayonga`, `Tekshiruvga`, `Bajarildi`, `Qayta ochish`); swipe left/right does the same.
  6. `UndoBar` after a move: `"{stage}" bosqichiga o'tdi` + `Qaytarish` (5 s).
- **Actions:** `+` → `TaskFormSheet` `Vazifa qo'shish` (`Nima qilish kerakligini yozing`): `Vazifa` (title + description `Qo'shimcha tafsilot yoki eslatma`), `Muhimlik` segments, `Muddat` (`Bugun` / `Ertaga` / `1 hafta` / calendar), `Ish` select (`Ishga bog'lanmagan`), `Nazorat ro'yxati` editor; editing adds `Holat` (`Kutmoqda` / `Jarayonda` / `Tekshiruv` / `Tayyor`), `Bloklangan` switch (`Vazifa to'siqqa duch kelgan bo'lsa belgilang`), `Xavfli amal` delete (confirm `Vazifani o'chirish`); closing a dirty form asks `O'zgarishlar saqlanmasin?`. Moves → `PATCH /tasks/{id}/status` (optimistic); move menu `Vazifani ko'chirish: {title}` lists the four stages + `Bloklangan deb belgilash` / `Blokni olib tashlash`. Toasts `Vazifa qo'shildi`, `Vazifa saqlandi`, `Vazifa o'chirildi`, `Bloklangan deb belgilandi`, `Blok olib tashlandi`.
- **States:** skeleton cards; error; empty `Vazifa yo'q` / `Yangi vazifalar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; offline: creates/updates/status changes are queued (`nt3.net.pending` pill on the card).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                           | Used for      | Key fields                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /tasks/me`                    | list          | `id,title,status (todo·doing·review·blocked·done),priority,due_date,case_id,case_title,description,checklist[]{text,done},updated_at` |
| `POST /tasks`, `PATCH /tasks/{id}` | create / edit | `{title,priority,due_date,case_id,case_title,description,checklist[]}`                                                                |
| `PATCH /tasks/{id}/status`         | move          | `{status}`                                                                                                                            |
| `DELETE /tasks/{id}`               | delete        | —                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cases`            | case picker   | `id,title,number`                                                                                                                     |

- **UX note:** the board's first column (`Kutilmoqda`) with the overdue tasks on top is the right first screen; the list mode duplicates it and could be the only mode on narrow phones.

### `/pro/calendar` — `Kalendar va muddatlar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Kalendar`); hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** court dates, meetings and procedural deadlines of the professional, with a deadline calculator.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title; subtitle `Hozir ish vaqti · {start}–{end}` / `Ish vaqtidan tashqari · {start}–{end}` from business hours, else `Sud majlislari, uchrashuvlar va muddatlar`.
  2. `CalHero` dark gradient: month name + year with ‹ › arrows and a `Bugun` pill; numbers `Bu oyda`, `Bugun`, `Kelgusi`; buttons `Tadbir qo'shish` (white) and `Muddat hisoblash` (glass).
  3. Segmented `Oy` / `Hafta` / `Ro'yxat`.
  4. `Oy`: Monday-first `MonthGrid` (today ringed, selected filled, up to 3 dots), `Legend` chips (`Sud majlisi` red, `Tergov harakati` amber, `Uchrashuv` blue, `Hujjat topshirish muddati` violet, `Apellyatsiya muddati` pink), `DayAgenda` of the selected day (`Bu kunga voqea yo'q. Qo'shish uchun "+" ni bosing.`), `Upcoming` (next 5). `Hafta`: 7-day agenda with week stepping. `Ro'yxat`: chips `Yaqinlashayotgan` / `O'tgan` with counts, 10 at a time + ShowMore.
- **Actions:** event row → `EventFormSheet` `Voqeani tahrirlash`; `Tadbir qo'shish` → `Voqea qo'shish` (`Turi` select, `Nomi`, `Sana`, `Boshlanishi`, `Tugashi (ixtiyoriy)`, `Manzil` (`Sud, ofis…`), `Eslatma` (`Eslatmasiz`, `15 daqiqa oldin`, `30 daqiqa oldin`, `1 soat oldin`, `1 kun oldin`), `Ish (ixtiyoriy)`; editing adds `Bekor qilingan` switch, `Kalendarga yuklab olish (.ics)`, `Eslatma kaskadi (7 kun · 3 kun · 1 kun · 2 soat)` (`POST …/reminders/cascade`, toast `Eslatmalar rejalashtirildi`), delete (confirm `Voqeani o'chirish`); toasts `Voqea qo'shildi` / `Voqea saqlandi` / `Voqea o'chirildi`). `Muddat hisoblash` → `DeadlineSheet` `Protsessual muddat kalkulyatori` (`Asos sanani kiriting — tizim muddatni hisoblaydi va xohlasangiz kalendarga qo'shadi.`): kind tiles `Apellyatsiya` 1 oy / `Hujjat topshirish` 10 kun / `Shikoyat` 30 kun / `Umumiy` 7 kun, `Asos sana (qaror/hujjat sanasi)`, `Kalendar yozuvi nomi`, switch `Kalendarga qo'shish (1 kun oldin eslatma)`, `Hisoblash` → result card `OXIRGI MUDDAT` date + `{n} kun qoldi` / `bugun` / `{n} kun o'tdi` (red when past), `Asos sana: …`, `Kalendarga qo'shildi` / `Kalendarga qo'shilmadi.`, `Boshqa hisoblash`. Pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton grid; error; empty (list) `Hozircha voqea yo'q` / `Sud majlislari, uchrashuvlar va muddatlar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; `.ics` failure `.ics faylni yuklab bo'lmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                               | Used for         | Key fields                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /calendar-events`                                                                 | all views        | `id,type (hearing·investigative·meeting·filing_deadline·appeal_deadline),title,case_id,starts_at,ends_at,location,status,reminder_minutes_before,reminder_scheduled` |
| `POST /calendar-events`, `PATCH /calendar-events/{id}`, `DELETE /calendar-events/{id}` | form             | `{type,title,starts_at,ends_at,location,case_id,reminder_minutes_before,status}`                                                                                     |
| `POST /calendar-events/{id}/reminders/cascade`                                         | reminder cascade | reminders                                                                                                                                                            |
| `POST /calendar/deadline-calculator`                                                   | calculator       | `{kind, base_date, create_event, title}` → `deadline, base_date, added`                                                                                              |
| `GET /calendar/business-hours`                                                         | subtitle         | `start,end`                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cases`                                                                | case picker      | —                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `.ics` download path of an event                                                       | export           | —                                                                                                                                                                    |

- **UX note:** the hero (month + 3 numbers + 2 buttons) pushes the grid below the fold on small phones; today's agenda should come first with the grid collapsible.

### `/pro/services` — `Mening xizmatlarim`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Xizmatlarim`). Limited: a yurist keeps the row (LIMITED_OK), an advokat loses it. A yurist cannot pick catalogue services marked `Faqat advokat` (`advokat_required`). Unverified sellers see the warn notice and their services are not listed.
- **Purpose:** the services the seller sells on the marketplace: add from the Studio catalogue, set a price inside the server's corridor, switch on/off, accept the rules, promote.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, header-right refresh (`Yangilash`).
  2. Segmented `Xizmatlar` | `Paketlar` (the second tab embeds `/pro/packages`).
  3. `ServicesHero` dark band: `{n} ta xizmat · {active} ta faol`; tiles `Faol`, `Pauzada`, `Nofaol` (tap filters), `Targ'ibda` (+ `{n} ta kutilmoqda`); centred `Xizmat qo'shish`.
  4. `PromoteStrip` card `Profilingizni TOPga chiqaring` / `Ko'proq mijoz sizni topadi` → `/pro/promotions`; while a promotion runs it turns green: `Profilingiz TOPda` / `Xizmat reklamasi faol` / `Banner faol` / `Targ'ib faol` + `Yana {days} kun · Boshqarish`.
  5. `LocationCard` `Joylashuvni ulashish` toggle (`Yoqsangiz, yaqin atrofdagi mijozlar sizni taxminiy masofa bilan ko'radi…`, on: `Ulashilmoqda · yana {time}` / `Ulashilmoqda · o'chirmaguningizcha`, progress bar, last-sent line).
  6. `RulesBanner` (when rules are pending): `Xizmatlarni yoqish uchun qoidalarni qabul qiling` / `{n} ta qoida hali qabul qilinmagan…` + `Qoidalarni ochish` (rules sheet, `POST /marketplace/service-rules/{id}/accept`); error variant with `Qayta urinish`.
  7. (unverified) `Profil hali tasdiqlanmagan` / `Tasdiqlanmaguncha xizmatlaringiz marketplace'da ko'rinmaydi…` + `Profilga o'tish` → `/pro/onboarding`; (boosted) `Profilingiz Topda` / `Yana {days} kun — …`.
  8. Toolbar: search `Xizmatlarim ichidan qidirish…` + `Filtr` button; active chip `Holat: {name}`.
  9. `ServiceRow` cards (paged): name, `category · subcategory`, on/off `Toggle` (active ↔ paused; off when the catalogue entry is inactive), price (`Narx belgilanmagan`), status pill when not active, eye / eye-off (listed or not), lock (rules pending), warning (price capped), `Targ'ibda · {days} k` or clock (promotion waiting).
- **Actions:** row → `ServiceSheet` (title = service): Segmented `Faol` / `Pauzada` / `Nofaol` + hint (`Marketplace'da ko'rinadi va buyurtma qabul qiladi` / `Vaqtincha buyurtma qabul qilinmaydi` / `Marketplace ro'yxatidan yashiriladi`), lock notice `Avval xizmat qoidalarini qabul qiling` + `Qoidalar`, `PriceGauge`, `VisibilityCard` `Marketplace'da ko'rinish` (`Marketplace'da ko'rinadi` / `Hozir ro'yxatda ko'rinmaydi`; checks `Xizmat faol`, `Katalogda yoqilgan`, `Narx belgilangan`, `Profil tasdiqlangan`), `Tajriba izohi` (tap edits; `Tajriba izohini qo'shing — mijoz ishonchi oshadi`), promo pill, `Katalogda nofaol — tahrirlab bo'lmaydi`, `ConfigPanel` (rules card, price limits with LexGo's explanation, negotiation switch, growth card — `GET/PATCH /marketplace/services/{id}/runtime-configuration`), bar `Tahrirlash` + round promote (→ `/pro/promotions/new?service=`) + round remove (confirm `Xizmatni o'chirish` / `«{title}» ro'yxatdan olib tashlanadi…`, `Bu xizmat targ'ibda — qolgan targ'ib muddati qaytarilmaydi.`). `Xizmat qo'shish` / `Tahrirlash` → `ServiceFormSheet` `Xizmat qo'shish` / `Xizmatni tahrirlash`: `Xizmat` (catalogue picker: search `Xizmat nomini yozing…`, categories, `Qo'shilgan`, `Faqat advokat`), `Narx` (`Masalan: 250 000`; `Bo'sh qoldirsangiz katalog narxi qo'llanadi.`; `BandCard` `Ruxsat etilgan oraliq` bar + `Ruxsat etilgan oraliq: {min} – {max} so'm.`, `Tavsiya narx`, `Katalog narxi`, modifiers; `Bu xizmatga katalogda narx qo'yilmagan…`), `Tajriba izohi` (2000, `Mijozlar xizmat kartasida ko'radi. Telefon raqami va havolalar yozmang.`), `Holat` cards, checkbox `Narx siyosatiga roziman: …`, `Avval xizmatni tanlang`, bar `Live ko'rish` (preview sheet `Mijozlar shunday ko'radi` with marketplace list / profile views), `Bekor qilish` / `Saqlash`. Status toggles → `PATCH …/status` (a service switched on while rules are missing opens the rules sheet). Toasts `Xizmat qo'shildi`, `O'zgarishlar saqlandi`, `Xizmat olib tashlandi`, `Xizmat faollashtirildi` / `…pauzaga qo'yildi` / `…nofaol qilindi`, promotion `Targ'ib tasdiqlandi — …` / `Targ'ib so'rovi bekor qilindi`.
- **States:** skeleton tiles; error `Xizmatlarni yuklab bo'lmadi` + `Qayta urinish`; empty `Hali xizmat qo'shilmagan` / `Katalogdan xizmat tanlang, narx va tajribangizni yozing — mijozlar sizni marketplace'da topadi.` + `Xizmat qo'shish`; filtered `Bu bo'limda xizmat yo'q` / `Boshqa holatni tanlang yoki qidiruvni tozalang.` + `Tozalash`; form errors `Narx {min} – {max} so'm oralig'ida bo'lishi kerak.`, `Bu xizmatni faqat advokatlar qo'sha oladi.`, `Profil admin tasdig'idan o'tgach xizmatlarni boshqarish ochiladi.`, `Internet aloqasi yo'q…`. Realtime: rules and promotion events.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                 | Used for                    | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/services/manage?include_inactive=true`                                                  | list + seller header        | `seller{name,avatar_url,kind,verified,rating,reviews,…}`, `seller_id`, `region`, `verified`, `verification_status`, `seller_type`, `account_status`, `items[]{id,name,category_title,subcategory,catalog_price,catalog_active,advokat_required,status,selected_price,effective_price,price_capped,note,visible,promotion{id,package_title,days_left,ends_at},profile_boost,limits{recommended,min,max,base,currency,modifiers[]},negotiation_allowed,rules_pending}` |
| `POST /lawyers/me/services/manage`                                                                       | add                         | `{service_id, selected_price, note, status, negotiation_allowed, selected_rule_ids}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PATCH /lawyers/me/services/{id}`, `PATCH …/{id}/status`, `DELETE …/{id}`                                | edit / switch / remove      | `{status}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /pricing/quote?…`                                                                                   | price corridor while typing | `recommended,min,max,base,modifiers[]{code,label,percent,multiplier,amount}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET/PATCH /marketplace/services/{id}/runtime-configuration`                                             | config panel                | rules, `missing_rule_ids`, `required_rules`, price limits, negotiation, growth                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /marketplace/service-rules?service_id=`, `POST /marketplace/service-rules/{ruleId}/accept {device}` | rules gate                  | pending count                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /promotions/me`                                                                                     | promote strip               | `items[]{placement,days_left,…}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `POST /marketplace/me/location-sharing`                                                                  | location toggle             | `{enabled, …}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| catalogue search (`GET /services/search`, categories)                                                    | picker                      | `id,name,category,price,advokat_required`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** first screen = hero numbers + the first two service rows with their switches; the promote strip, location card, rules banner and notices all stack above the list and push it down — keep only the one that needs action.

### `/pro/packages` — `Xizmat paketlari`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Paketlarim`, label `ntd.sellerV2.menuPackages`); hidden while limited; a 403 shows the locked state. PREMIUM tariffs need LexGo's extended verification.
- **Purpose:** switch on the Studio-made packages (fixed price and content) the seller is willing to deliver.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title.
  2. `PackagesHero` dark band: icon, `Xizmat paketlari`, lead `Paketlarni LexGo Studio yaratadi. Siz qaysi paket va tarifni bajarishingizni tanlaysiz — shundan keyin mijozlar paket uchun sizni tanlay oladi.`, counters `Yoqilgan`, `Yoqish mumkin`, `Mos emas`.
  3. Lock row `Paket narxi va tarkibini LexGo belgilaydi — ularni o'zgartirib bo'lmaydi.`
  4. Chips `Hammasi` / `Yoqilgan` / `Yoqish mumkin` / `Mos emas` with counts; search `Paket nomi yoki toifa…`.
  5. One group per package code: title, `category · code`, then a `TierCard` per tariff: tariff `Basic` / `Standard` / `Premium` with icon, state pill (`Faol`, `To'xtatilgan`, `Yoqilmagan`, `Mos emas`), price + `so'm`, `Narx qat'iy — o'zgartirilmaydi`, duration, 3 included items + `+{n} ta`, PREMIUM note (`Kengaytirilgan tekshiruvdan o'tgansiz` / `Bu tarif uchun LexGo kengaytirilgan tekshiruvi kerak`), not-eligible box `Hozircha bu tarifni yoqib bo'lmaydi` + reasons (`LexGo kengaytirilgan tekshiruvidan o'tish kerak`, `Profilingiz hali tasdiqlanmagan`, `Paketga kiruvchi xizmatlar sizda yoqilmagan`, `Darajangiz bu tarifga mos emas`, `Marketplace profilingiz to'ldirilmagan`, `Paketdagi {n} ta xizmat sizda yoqilmagan — avval ularni «Xizmatlar» bo'limida yoqing.`), button `Yoqish` (gradient) / `To'xtatish` (line) / `Qayta yoqish`.
- **Actions:** each button confirms (`Paketni yoqish` / `«{name}» yoqilsinmi? Mijozlar shu paket uchun sizni tanlay oladi.`, `Paketni to'xtatish` / `«{name}» to'xtatiladi — yangi buyurtmalar kelmaydi.`, `Paketni qayta yoqish`), then `POST …/enable` or `PATCH …/status`; toasts `Paket yoqildi — mijozlar endi sizni tanlay oladi`, `Paket to'xtatildi — yangi buyurtmalar kelmaydi`, `Paket qayta yoqildi`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeletons; 403 `Paketlar yopiq` / `Paketlarni faqat tasdiqlangan advokat va yuristlar yoqa oladi.`; error `Paketlar yuklanmadi` / `Internetni tekshirib, qayta urinib ko'ring.` + `Qayta urinish`; empty `Hozircha faol paket yo'q` / `LexGo Studio yangi paket e'lon qilganda u shu yerda chiqadi.`; `Filtrga mos paket topilmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                 | Used for       | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/packages/manage`        | list           | `id, package{id,code,group_code,group_title,title,category,tariff,price,duration,included[]}, enabled, status (active·disabled), price_editable, eligibility{eligible,extended_verified,base_verified,reasons[],missing_service_ids[]}` |
| `POST /lawyers/me/packages/{id}/enable`  | enable         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PATCH /lawyers/me/packages/{id}/status` | pause / resume | `{status: active                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | disabled}` |

- **UX note:** the hero and the lock row say the same thing twice; a phone wants the first enabled/enable-able tier card high. Reasons are long sentences — a single line + "why" disclosure would fit better.
