### `/pro/templates` — `Hujjatlar`

- **Who:** yurist only — the drawer row `Hujjatlar` → `/pro/templates` exists only in the lawyer nav (an advokat reaches document work through `Hujjat so'rovlari`). Hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** the document-template catalogue as a PDF library to download samples.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title `Hujjatlar`, subtitle `{n} ta hujjat namunasi · PDF qilib yuklab oling` (else `Hujjat namunalarini PDF qilib yuklab oling`).
  2. Chips `Hammasi`, `Fuqarolik`, `Iqtisodiy`, `Ma'muriy`, `Jinoiy` with counts.
  3. Search pill `Namuna qidirish: ijara, ariza, shartnoma…` with a live line `{n} ta natija ({total} tadan)` / `Hech narsa topilmadi`.
  4. `TemplateCard` rows (10 at a time): red `PDF` tile, name, `branch · {n} ta maydon`, round download button.
  5. Footer `{n} / {total}`, `Yana {n} ta`, `Boshqa namunalar yuklanmoqda…`.
- **Actions:** card → `TemplateSheet` `Hujjat namunasi` (PDF tile, name, branch pill, description, facts `To'ldiriladigan maydon`, `Bo'limlar`, `Til`, `Yangilangan`, pinned `Yuklab olish`); download button → share the PDF (`GET /document-templates/{id}`); pull to refresh; end of list loads more.
- **States:** 6 skeletons; `ErrorView`; empty `Hozircha hujjat yo'q` / `Mavjud hujjat namunalari shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; search empty `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Boshqa so'z yozing yoki yo'nalishni almashtiring.` + `Tozalash`; fail toast `Namunani yuklab bo'lmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                   | Used for             | Key fields                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /document-template-categories`                        | branch chips         | categories                                                         |
| `GET /services?catalog_only=false&summary=false&limit=500` | templates per branch | `id,name,category,fields,sections,language,updated_at,description` |
| `GET /document-templates/{id}`                             | PDF download         | file                                                               |

- **UX note:** fine for a phone as is; the sheet adds little over the card — the download button alone would do.

---

### `/pro/files` — `Fayllar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Fayllar`); hidden while limited; a limited cabinet or a 403 shows the pending state.
- **Purpose:** the professional's workspace storage: folders, files, quota, uploads, and the documents requested from clients.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Ish fayllari va papkalar`.
  2. `QuotaCard` dark gradient (red above 90 %): `Xotira`, percent, `{used} / {total}`, bar split by type with legend `PDF · Hujjatlar · Jadvallar · Rasmlar · Boshqa`, `{size} bo'sh`, `{f} papka · {n} fayl`.
  3. `UploadBar` (`Yuklanmoqda {i}/{n}`, cancel).
  4. `FolderButtons`: `Yangi papka` (name sheet `Papka nomi`, toast `Papka yaratildi`), `Yuklash` (menu `Fayllar` / `Galereya` / `Kamera` + scan → `/pro/scan?folder=`).
  5. Search `Fayl yoki papka qidirish` + sliders (`Filtrlar`, badge) + grid/list toggle (`Katakcha ko'rinishi` / `Ro'yxat ko'rinishi`); removable chips (section, type, sort); breadcrumb `Bosh papka › …` (section `Barchasi`).
  6. Folder / file cards (3D folder cover; file tile with type badge) or rows; folder meta `{n} ta element`, file meta size. Section `So'rovlar` shows `RequestRows`: title, file name or message, `Kutilmoqda` / `Yuklangan` pill, tap previews an uploaded one; empty `Hujjat so'rovlari yo'q`.
- **Actions:** folder → enter; file → preview (`POST …/signed-url`); more → `ItemActions` (`Ochish`, `Ulashish / yuklab olish`, `Nomini o'zgartirish` (`Nom o'zgartirildi`), `Ko'chirish` (`Papkaga ko'chirish`, `Ko'chirildi`), `Yulduzcha qo'yish` / `Yulduzchani olib tashlash`, `Ma'lumot` (versions, comments), delete with confirm `"{name}" fayli o'chiriladi…` / `"{name}" papkasi va ichidagi {n} ta element o'chiriladi…`); filter sheet: `Bo'lim` tiles `Barchasi` / `So'nggi` / `Yulduzli` / `So'rovlar`, `Fayl turi` chips (`Papkalar`, `PDF`, `Hujjatlar`, `Jadvallar`, `Rasmlar`, `Boshqa`), `Saralash` (`Nomi bo'yicha`, `Yangilari avval`, `Hajmi bo'yicha`), reset + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · n` / `Tayyor`.
- **States:** pending `Fayl xotirasi hali ochilmagan` / `Xotira administrator profilingizni tasdiqlagandan so'ng ochiladi.` + `Tasdiqlash holati` → `/pro/onboarding`; skeleton rows; empty `Bu yerda hali hech narsa yo'q` / `Papka yarating yoki fayl yuklang.`, `Yulduzli elementlar yo'q`, `Hozircha fayl yo'q`; upload refusals `"{name}" juda katta (maks. {mb} MB)`, `"{name}" formati qabul qilinmaydi`, `Xotirada joy yetarli emas`, `{n} ta fayl yuklandi` / `{n} ta fayl yuklanmadi`; infected file `Xavfli fayl`; `Faylni ochib bo'lmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                              | Used for            | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /workspace/tree`                                                                                 | browser             | `folders[]{id,work_id,name,parent_id,case_id,starred,size,file_count,folder_count,status,created_at}`, `files[]{id,work_id,folder_id,case_id,name,size,mime,ext,starred,status,scan,created_at}`, `quota{quota_bytes,used_bytes,remaining_bytes,limit_gb}` |
| `POST /workspace/folders`, `PATCH /workspace/folders/{id}`, `DELETE`                                  | folders             | `{name,parent_id,case_id,starred}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `POST /workspace/files/upload` (multipart)                                                            | upload              | `folder_id` / `case_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PATCH /workspace/files/{id}`, `DELETE`, `POST …/signed-url`, `GET …/versions`, `GET/POST …/comments` | file actions        | `{file_name,folder_id,case_id,starred}`, `{text}`                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /workspace/document-requests?role=requester`                                                     | `So'rovlar` section | `id,title,message,status,file_name,file_url,mime,created_at`                                                                                                                                                                                               |

- **UX note:** the quota card is decorative on a phone — a one-line bar would leave the folders visible; the search/filter/grid row plus chips plus crumbs is three rows of chrome.

---

### `/pro/scan` — `Hujjat skaneri`

- **Who:** both; reached from the upload menu of `/pro/files` (and from a case's documents), with `?folder=` for the target folder. Needs a development build (native scanner / OCR modules).
- **Purpose:** photograph pages, clean them up, and save them as PDF / Word / JPG / PNG into the workspace or the phone.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Kamera bilan sahifalarni skanerlang` → `{n} ta sahifa`.
  2. Empty: dark camera tile, `Hujjatni skanerlang`, `Sahifani kamera bilan suratga oling yoki galereyadan tanlang. Keyin PDF, Word yoki rasm qilib saqlang.`, (denied: `Kameraga ruxsat berilmagan` + `Sozlamalarni ochish`), buttons `Skanerlash` / `Galereyadan`.
  3. With pages: `PageCanvas` (`Aylantirish`, `O'chirish`, `Chapga surish` / `O'ngga surish`), `FilterBar` `Ko'rinish` chips `Asl` / `Hujjat` / `Rangli` / `Kulrang` / `Oq-qora`, `PageStrip` thumbnails + `Sahifa qo'shish` (`Skanerlash` / `Galereyadan`).
  4. Footer `Davom etish`.
- **Actions:** `Davom etish` → `ExportSheet` `Saqlash` (`{n} ta sahifa · Papka: {name}` / `Bosh papka`): `Fayl nomi`, `Format` tiles PDF `Ko'p sahifali hujjat` / Word `Tahrirlanadigan fayl` / JPG `Rasm (ko'p sahifa .zip)` / PNG `Aniq rasm (ko'p sahifa .zip)`, `Matnni tanish` (PDF/Word only) chips `O'zbek (lotin)`, `Ўзбек (кирилл)`, `Русский`, `English` (`PDF ichidan qidirish va Word'da tahrirlash uchun (sekinroq)` / `Matnsiz (faqat rasm)`), `Fayllarga yuklash` (upload then back) / `Qurilmaga saqlash / ulashish`. Leaving with pages asks `Skanerni bekor qilasizmi?` / `Olingan sahifalar saqlanmaydi.` (`Qolish` / `Chiqish`).
- **States:** `Tayyorlanmoqda…`; errors `Skanerni tayyorlab bo'lmadi`, `Yuklab bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, `Matnni taniy olmadi, fayl rasm sifatida saqlandi.`, `Kamerani ochib bo'lmadi`; toast `Skaner yuklandi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                       | Used for                  | Key fields                    |
| ------------------------------ | ------------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| `GET /workspace/tree`          | folder name + quota check | `folders[]{id,name}`, `quota` |
| `POST /workspace/files/upload` | upload the export         | multipart, `folder_id`        |

- **UX note:** good single-purpose flow; the export sheet is long (name, 4 formats, 4 languages, 2 buttons) — defaulting to PDF + Uzbek and hiding the rest under "more" would make it one-thumb.

---

### `/pro/profile` — `Profil va verifikatsiya`

- **Who:** both. advokat-only fields: `Litsenziya raqami`, `Advokatlar palatasi`, `Advokatlik tuzilmasi`, `Advokat sifatida tajriba (yil)` (+ `Yurist sifatida tajriba (yil)`); a yurist sees `Tajriba (yil)` only and cannot pick advocate-only practice areas. Reachable from the drawer only through the onboarding/profile sections (no direct `lsb` row); `?section=` deep-links a sheet.
- **Purpose:** edit the professional profile (what the marketplace and the pricing corridor use) and see how clients see it.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `ProfileHeaderCard`: avatar with camera badge (tap → `PhotoSheet`: gallery / camera / remove), name, public id, pills kind (`Advokat` / `Yurist` / `Advokat tashkiloti`) and `Tasdiqlangan` / `Tasdiqlanmagan`; `Profil to'liqligi` bar + %.
  2. `VerificationCard` `Verifikatsiya`: `LexGo tasdiqlagan` / `Tekshiruv kutilmoqda` / `Tasdiqlanmadi` (+ badge label) with text (`Mijozlar sizni marketplace'da ko'ra oladi.` / `Profil tasdiqlanmaguncha mijozlarga ko'rinmaydi.` / `Ma'lumotlarni to'g'rilab, qayta tekshiruv so'rang.`); `Tekshiruv so'rash` when not verified (confirm `Tekshiruv so'raysizmi?`, toast `Tekshiruv so'rovi yuborildi`).
  3. `ProfileSectionRows`: `Rol va kasbiy tur` (read-only), `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` (name · email · `{n} ta til`), `Litsenziya va ta'lim` (licence · bar · organization · education), `Yo'nalishlar va tavsif` (`{n} ta yo'nalish` · bio), `Tajriba va statistika` (`{n} yil tajriba` · `{n} ta ish`), `Hudud` (region · district); `Hali to'ldirilmagan` when empty; icon amber when incomplete.
  4. `PublicPreviewCard` `Mijozlar sizni qanday ko'radi`: avatar, name, `{r} · {n} ta baho` or `Yangi`, online dot, badge, `{sum} dan`, `{n} ta xizmat`, bio, `Telefon raqamingiz mijozlarga ko'rsatilmaydi.`; unverified: `Profil hali marketplace'da ko'rinmaydi: tasdiqlanishini kuting.`
- **Actions:** rows open sheets (`SectionSheet` with sticky Save, `Saqlanmagan qoralama tiklandi` + `Qoralamani o'chirish`): `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` (`To'liq ism`, `E-pochta`, `Tillar` chips → `PUT /clients/me` + `PUT /lawyers/me`), `Litsenziya va ta'lim`, `Yo'nalishlar va tavsif` (`Yo'nalishlar` chips, `Men haqimda` 1000), `Tajriba va statistika` (`Jami ishlar`, `To'liq g'alaba`, `Qisman g'alaba`, `Soatlik narx (so'm)`; `G'alabalar soni jami ishlardan oshmasligi kerak`), `Hudud` (`Viloyat` select — fixed backend keys, decides the price corridor; `Tuman / shahar`). Pull to refresh.
- **States:** QueryBoundary; preview skeleton; validation errors (`Kamida 2 ta belgi kiriting`, `E-pochta noto'g'ri`).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                             | Used for             | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------ | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me`                    | profile              | `user_id, public_id, name, seller_type, region, district, license_number, bar_association, advocate_structure, organization_name, experience_years, lawyer_experience_years, specializations[], languages[], bio, education, total_cases, wins_count, partial_wins_count, success_rate, hourly_price, verification_status, is_verified, badge_label` |
| `PUT /lawyers/me` (full upsert)      | save a section       | the fields above                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /clients/me`, `PUT /clients/me` | name / email / photo | `name, email, avatar_url, phone`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /marketplace/lawyers/{userId}`  | public preview       | `rating, reviews_count, online_now, available, service_titles[], price_from, badge_label, bio`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /lawyers/me/verifications`     | ask for a check      | `{check_type: "manual"}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

- **UX note:** completeness + verification state belong at the top as they are; the six rows are clear. The "role" row cannot be changed in the app but looks like the others.

---

### `/pro/experience` — `Kategoriya bo'yicha tajriba`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Tajriba`); hidden while limited. Every submission is a revision reviewed by the call centre; only approved ones count for price and rating.
- **Purpose:** declare verified experience per category (workplace, positions, stages, results, evidence) and follow its review.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Tajriba`; lead `Har bir o'zgarish call-markaz tekshiruvidan keyin kuchga kiradi. Faqat tasdiqlangan tajriba narx va reytingga ta'sir qiladi.`
  2. Centred `Tajriba qo'shish`.
  3. `EntryCard`s (10 + ShowMore): `category · direction`, `StatePill` (`Tasdiqlangan · {n} yil`, `Tekshiruvda`, `Rad etilgan`, cancelled/superseded grey), workplace, positions (`title · period`, `hozirgacha`), description, `Bosqichlar: …`, `Natijalar: …`, evidence files (open), `Rad etish sababi: {text}`, a note box for a newer revision (pending / rejected / withdrawn: version, date, changed fields, `Tuzatish`), pricing line (counts / does not count), buttons `O'zgartirish` or `Tekshiruvni bekor qilish` (confirm) / fix / resend, and history.
- **Actions:** `Tajriba qo'shish` / edit → `ExperienceSheet` `Yangi tajriba` / `Tajribani o'zgartirish` (`Tahrir yangi tekshiruvga yuboriladi.` / `Tajribangiz LexGo tomonidan tekshiriladi.`): `Kategoriya` (searchable, `GET /service-categories/tree`), `Yo'nalish (ixtiyoriy)` (`Butun kategoriya`), `Ish joyi`, positions editor 1–30 (`Lavozim`, `Boshlangan sana`, `Tugagan sana` / `Hozir ham shu yerda ishlayman`), `Tajriba tavsifi`, `Bosqichlar` chips (`Tergovgacha`, `Tergov`, `Birinchi instansiya`, `Apellyatsiya`, `Kassatsiya`, `Nazorat tartibi`, `Ijro`, `Maslahat`, `Bitimlar`), `Natijalar` chips (`To'liq yutuq`, `Qisman yutuq`, `Kelishuv`, `Ish tugatilgan`, `Oqlov`, `Jazo yengillashgan`, `Da'vo qanoatlantirilgan`, `Da'vo qisman qanoatlantirilgan`, `Himoya muvaffaqiyatli`, `Boshqa`), `Tasdiqlovchi fayllar` (`PDF, JPG, PNG, WEBP, DOC, DOCX · 20 MB gacha`; `Fayl qo'shish` / `Rasm qo'shish`), `Tekshiruvga yuborish`; 422 field errors are placed on the inputs. History → `HistorySheet` (revisions sent, call-centre decisions with reason and checklist items). Pull to refresh; live refresh on the call centre's decision.
- **States:** QueryBoundary; empty `Hali tajriba qo'shilmagan.`; errors `Sanalar noto'g'ri`, `Sana kelajakda bo'lmasin`, `Bu davr boshqa tajriba bilan ustma-ust tushadi`, `Bu tajribaning tahriri allaqachon tekshiruvda`, …; toasts `Tekshiruvga yuborildi.`, `Tekshiruvdagi tahrir bekor qilindi.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                               | Used for                                             | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/me/experience?limit&offset`          | entries (grouped by root)                            | records `id, root_id, version, status (approved·pending_review·rejected·cancelled·superseded), category_id, subcategory_id, workplace, positions[]{title,start,end,current,description}, description, stages[], results[], evidence[]{id,file_name,url}, years, affects_pricing, rejection_reason, submitted_at` |
| `GET /marketplace/me/experience/{id}/history`          | history sheet                                        | `revisions[]`, reviews `{decision, reason, checklist[]{key,verdict,note}}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /marketplace/me/experience`                      | add / edit (new revision, names the verified source) | the form fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `POST /marketplace/me/experience/{id}/cancel`          | withdraw a pending revision                          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `POST /marketplace/me/experience/evidence` (multipart) | files                                                | `id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /service-categories/tree`                         | category picker                                      | `id,title,children[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** the card tries to show the verified data and the pending revision side by side — on a phone the pill + one "pending edit" line is enough, with the diff in the history sheet.

---

### `/pro/achievements` — `Yutuqlar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Yutuqlar`); hidden while limited. Add only; the call centre approves.
- **Purpose:** submit professional achievements that, once approved, show on the public profile and feed the rating.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Yutuqlar`; lead `Har bir yutuq call-markaz tekshiruvidan keyin profilingizda ko'rinadi. Reytingga faqat tasdiqlangani ta'sir qiladi.`
  2. Centred `Yutuq qo'shish`.
  3. `AchievementCard`s (10 per page + `ShowMore`): type label (`Sud nizosi natijasi`, `Sudgacha hal etish`, `Mediatsiya kelishuvi`, `Korporativ bitim`, `Soliq va bojxona`, `Intellektual mulk`, `Davlat organiga murojaat`, `Mehnat va kadrlar`, `Komplayens va audit`, `Nashr va tadqiqot`, `Malaka va sertifikat`, `Mukofot va e'tirof`, `Bepul yordam`, `Boshqa`), state pill (`Tasdiqlangan` / `Tekshiruvda` / `Rad etilgan` / `Boshqa holat`), title, `date · category`, description, evidence files, `Sabab: {text}`.
- **Actions:** `Yutuq qo'shish` → sheet `Yangi yutuq` (`Yutuq LexGo tomonidan tekshiriladi.`): `Turi` select, `Nomi` (2–255), `Kategoriya (ixtiyoriy)` (`Kategoriyasiz`), `Sana` (not in the future), `Tavsif` (5–5000), `Tasdiqlovchi fayllar` (`… · 20 MB gacha · 20 tagacha`), `Tekshiruvga yuborish`. Pull to refresh; live refresh on decisions.
- **States:** 3 skeletons; empty `Hali yutuq qo'shilmagan.`; issues `Turini tanlang`, `Nomi 2 dan 255 tagacha belgi bo'lsin`, `Sanani kiriting (kelajakdagi sana bo'lmasin)`, `Tavsif 5 dan 5000 tagacha belgi bo'lsin`, `Fayllar 20 tadan oshmasin`; toasts `Tekshiruvga yuborildi.` / `Yuborib bo'lmadi.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                 | Used for        | Key fields                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /marketplace/me/achievements?limit&offset`          | list            | `items[]{id,type,status (approved·pending_review·rejected),title,category_id,category_name,achieved_at,description,evidence[],rejection_reason}`, `total` |
| `POST /marketplace/me/achievements`                      | submit          | `{type,title,category_id,achieved_on,description,evidence_ids[]}`                                                                                         |
| `POST /marketplace/me/achievements/evidence` (multipart) | files           | `id,file_name`                                                                                                                                            |
| `GET /service-categories/tree`                           | category picker | —                                                                                                                                                         |

- **UX note:** simple and phone-friendly; the add button above the list is right.

---

### `/pro/availability` — `Ish vaqti`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Ish vaqti`); hidden while limited. The marketplace preview needs a public profile (unverified: `Profil hali ko'rinmaydi (tasdiqlanmagan)`).
- **Purpose:** set the weekly working hours and how fast the seller answers an order (regular / auto-assigned / SOS), and see how the marketplace shows them right now.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Standart jadval` / `Sizning jadvalingiz` (`source`).
  2. `StatusCard` `Hozir mijozlar sizni shunday ko'rishadi`: coloured dot + `Onlayn` / `Ish vaqtidan tashqari` / `Oflayn rejim yoqilgan` and a line (`{time} gacha ishlaysiz`, `Bugun {time} da ochiladi`, `Ertaga {time} da ochiladi`, `{day} kuni {time} da ochiladi`, `Ish kuni belgilanmagan`, `Profilingizda holat «oflayn» qilib qo'yilgan.`); quiet line of the app's socket (`Ulangan` / `Ulanmoqda…` / `Ulanmagan`). Re-renders every 30 s.
  3. `PresetBar` chips `Du–Ju 09–18`, `Har kuni`, `24/7`, `Tozalash` (the one matching the week is lit).
  4. `WeekCard` `Haftalik jadval`: 7 `DayRow`s (`Dushanba`…`Yakshanba`): switch, time chips start/end (open `TimeSheet` with ± steppers for hour / 5-min, quick minute chips, `Tugash vaqti boshlanishidan keyin bo'lishi kerak.`), length, 24 h bar, copy-all icon (`{day} soatlarini hammasiga nusxalash`); hint `Nusxa belgisi shu kun soatlarini barcha kunlarga qo'llaydi.` or error `Kamida bitta ish kunini yoqing.`
  5. `DeadlinesCard` `Javob tezligi`: per kind `Oddiy buyurtma` (`Mijoz sizni o'zi tanlab yozgan buyurtma.`), `Avtomatik taqsimot` (`Tizim sizga o'zi biriktirgan buyurtma.`), `SOS (shoshilinch)` (`Shoshilinch so'rov: tezroq javob kerak.`) — preset tiles (`{n} daq` / `{h} soat`) + `Boshqa` stepper (1–1440 min); note `Muddat faqat ish vaqtida hisoblanadi. Kechiksangiz buyurtma boshqa ijrochiga o'tishi mumkin.` Defaults 30 / 15 / 5 min.
  6. `MarketPreview` `Bozorda qanday ko'rinasiz`: the seller card (avatar, name, `specialization · region`, status line, pill `Onlayn` / `Ish vaqtidan tashqari`) + `Mijoz ro'yxatda shu holatni ko'radi.`
  7. Sticky `SaveBar` only while dirty: reason line (`Tugash vaqti boshlanishidan keyin bo'lishi kerak.`, `Kamida bitta ish kunini yoqing.`, `Javob muddati 1 dan 1440 daqiqagacha bo'lishi kerak.`), `Bekor` / `Saqlash` (disabled while invalid).
- **Actions:** `Saqlash` → `PUT /lawyers/me/availability`; toast `Ish vaqti saqlandi`; leaving with unsaved changes asks `Saqlanmagan o'zgarishlar` / `Chiqsangiz, jadvaldagi o'zgarishlar yo'qoladi.`; pull to refresh.
- **States:** screen-shaped skeleton; error; preview skeleton.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                            | Used for               | Key fields                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/availability`      | schedule               | `timezone, weekly[]{day (0=Mon),enabled,start,end}, exceptions[], deadlines{manual,auto,sos}, source (default·custom)` |
| `PUT /lawyers/me/availability`      | save                   | same body                                                                                                              |
| `GET /marketplace/lawyers/{userId}` | preview + offline flag | `name, region, specializations[], rating, reviews_count, available`                                                    |
| user socket state                   | connection line        | —                                                                                                                      |

- **UX note:** status card + presets answer the common need in one screen; the seven rows with bars are long — collapse the week to "Du–Ju 09–18 · edit" unless a custom schedule is set.

---

### `/pro/stats` — `Statistika`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Statistika`); hidden while limited; a 403 shows `Statistika tasdiqlangan mutaxassislar uchun`.
- **Purpose:** the professional's numbers: earnings, workload, performance, rating, case mix and the payout ledger.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Ish yuki, daromad va ko'rsatkichlar`.
  2. Segmented `Bugun` / `Oy` (`Davr`).
  3. `Hero` gradient: label `Bugungi daromad` / `Oylik daromad`, big counted-up amount + `so'm`, sparkline of the last 6 payouts with the change vs. the previous one (`+N%`, `o'sish` / `pasayish` / `o'zgarmagan`), caption `So'nggi to'lovlar · oldingisiga nisbatan` / `To'lovlar paydo bo'lgach grafik chiqadi`.
  4. New-seller card (no numbers yet): `Raqamlar tez orada paydo bo'ladi` / `Birinchi buyurtma qabul qilinib, ish boshlanganda ish yuki, daromad va reyting shu yerda ko'rinadi.`
  5. `Ish yuki` rows: `Faol ishlar`, `Bugungi majlislar`, `Bugungi muddatlar`, `Yangi xabarlar`, `Ko'riladigan hujjatlar`.
  6. `Moliyaviy holat` rows: `Kutilayotgan to'lov`, `LexGo orqali`, `To'lanadigan` (so'm).
  7. `Ko'rsatkichlarim`: rating number + stars + `{n} ta sharh` (or `Yangi` + `Reyting 5 ta sharhdan keyin ko'rinadi.`), meters `Javob berish`, `Qabul qilish`, `Javob tezligi` (`{n} daq`), tip (`O'rtacha javob vaqti {now} daqiqa — uni 15 daqiqagacha qisqartiring.` / `Javob berish darajasini 90% dan oshiring.` / `Ko'rsatkichlar yaxshi — shunday davom eting.`), `Super advokat bo'lishga yaqinlik` `{done} / 4 shart bajarildi` + progress + 4 criteria (`Reyting 4.5 dan yuqori`, `Javob berish darajasi 90% dan yuqori`, `Tajriba kamida 3 yil (sizda {n})`, `Kamida 10 ta ish (sizda {n})`).
  8. `Mening reytingim` (`MyRatingView`; hidden on 404; `Reytingni olib bo'lmadi · Qayta urinish`).
  9. `Ishlar holati bo'yicha`: stacked bar + legend of own cases by status (`{n} ta ish`; `Ishlar qo'shilgach holatlar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`).
  10. `To'lovlar (payout)`: `Jami`, `Kutilmoqda` / `To'langan`, 5 rows (`amount so'm`, `date · period`, status pill) + `Yana (n)`; empty `Hozircha to'lovlar yo'q`.
- **Actions:** period switch; `Yana`; pull to refresh (stats, payouts, cases, rating).
- **States:** skeletons per block; `ErrorView`; forbidden empty state.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                     | Used for               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /lawyers/me/stats`      | hero, tiles, meters    | `workload{active_cases,courts_today,deadlines_today,unread_messages,documents_to_review}`, `finance{earnings_today,earnings_month,pending_payout,earnings_via_lexgo,payable}`, `performance{response_rate,acceptance_rate,avg_response_minutes,rating}` |
| `GET /lawyers/me`            | super-advokat criteria | `experience_years, total_cases, reviews_count`                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /payouts/me`            | sparkline + ledger     | `items[]{id,amount,status,period,created_at}`                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cases`      | case mix               | `status`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GET /marketplace/me/rating` | rating block           | see `/workspace`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

- **UX note:** the hero number and the meters are the two things to keep above the fold; workload/finance rows repeat the home counters and could be one compact tile row.

### `/pro/onboarding` — `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari`, always visible — also while limited). Document kind defaults: advokat `Litsenziya`, yurist `Diplom`. State from cabinet + progress: `incomplete` / `ready` / `submitted` / `approved` / `rejected`.
- **Purpose:** complete the seller profile, upload proof documents, secure the account and send the application for review; follow the review.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `OnboardingStatusCard`: progress ring (check when approved), pill (`Jarayonda` / `Tayyor` / `Kutilmoqda` / `Faol` / `Rad etildi`), title (`Boshlaymiz` when nothing is done, else `Ariza to'ldirilmoqda` / `Yuborishga tayyor` / `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda` / `Tasdiqlangan` / `Rad etilgan`), hint (`Birinchi qadamdan boshlang: bir necha daqiqa yetadi.`, `Keyingi: {step}` / `Yaxshi ketyapsiz. Davom eting.`, `Hammasi tayyor: arizani yuborishingiz mumkin.`, `Endi buyurtma qabul qila olasiz.`, `Arizangizni moderator ko'rib chiqmoqda. Odatda bu taxminan 24 soat oladi.`, `Ariza rad etildi. Ma'lumotlarni to'g'rilab, qayta yuboring.`), `{done} / {total} bosqich bajarildi`, rejected `Sabab: {text}`, and (not approved) `Tasdiqlanmaguncha profilingiz marketplace'da ko'rinmaydi va xizmat sota olmaysiz.`
  2. (limited, not approved) notice `Kabinet cheklangan: tasdiqlanguncha buyurtma qabul qilish, chat va qo'ng'iroqlar yopiq.`
  3. `Bosqichlar` timeline — one card per server step (`Profil ma'lumotlari`, `Shaxsni tasdiqlash`, `Hujjatlar`, `Xizmatlar`, `Narxlar`): numbered node (pulsing on the current one), title, required dot, line `Bajarildi` / `Navbatdagi qadam` / `Majburiy` / `Ixtiyoriy` or a note (`{n} ta hujjat yuklangan`, `Profil {pct}% to'liq`), action `Boshlash` / `Yuklash` / `Tahrirlash` (→ profile / documents sheet / services / pricing routes).
  4. `Profil bo'limlari` card (`{pct}%` pill): rows `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar`, `Rol va kasbiy tur`, `Litsenziya va diplom`, `Tajriba va yo'nalishlar`, `Hudud`, `Tashkilot`, `Profil rasmi` with the missing items (`ism`, `e-pochta`, `tillar`, `kasbiy tur`, `litsenziya raqami`, `advokatlar palatasi`, `ma'lumoti`, `tajriba`, `yo'nalishlar`, `tavsif`, `hudud`, `tuzilma`, `rasm`) and `To'liq` / `{n} ta qoldi` → `/pro/profile?section=`.
  5. `SecurityCard` `Xavfsizlik tayyor` / `Xavfsizlikni kuchaytiring` + `Sozlash`, chips `Telegram`, `Ikki bosqichli kirish` → `/security`.
  6. Sticky `SubmitFooter`: `Yana {n} ta majburiy bosqich qoldi` / `Majburiy bosqichlar tayyor`, button `Yuborish` / `Qayta yuborish` (confirm `Arizani yuborasizmi?` / `Moderator ma'lumotlaringizni tekshiradi. Natija bildirishnoma orqali keladi.`); while submitted only `Ko'rib chiqilmoqda`; hidden when approved.
- **Actions:** `DocumentsSheet` `Tasdiqlovchi hujjatlar` (`Litsenziya, diplom yoki boshqa hujjat. PDF, JPG yoki PNG, 15 MB gacha.`): uploaded rows (kind icon, name, `kind · date`, pill `Yuklandi` / `Tekshirilmoqda` / `Tasdiqlandi` / `Rad etildi`, `Sabab: …`, `Almashtirish`), `Yangi hujjat`: `Hujjat turi` (`Litsenziya` / `Diplom` / `Boshqa malaka hujjati`), `Fayl tanlash` / `Suratga olish`, `Yuklash` → `POST /seller-onboarding/documents` (toast `Hujjat yuklandi`; `Fayl 15 MB dan katta`, `Faqat PDF, JPG yoki PNG`); submit → `POST /seller-onboarding/submit` (toast `Ariza yuborildi`; 422 toast when steps are missing). Pull to refresh.
- **States:** screen-shaped skeleton; error only when both progress and cabinet fail; empty docs `Hali hujjat yuklanmagan`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                        | Used for              | Key fields                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /seller-onboarding/progress`               | steps + state         | `status, completed_count, total_count, steps[]{key,title,required,completed}, reason` |
| `GET /seller-onboarding`                        | uploaded documents    | `items[]{id,work_id,name,kind,status,created_at,reason}`                              |
| `POST /seller-onboarding/documents` (multipart) | upload                | `file, kind`                                                                          |
| `POST /seller-onboarding/submit`                | send for review       | 422 on missing steps                                                                  |
| `GET /lawyers/me/cabinet`                       | state, limited notice | `limited_access, limited_reason, account_status, verification, seller_type`           |
| `GET /lawyers/me`, `GET /clients/me`            | profile sections      | —                                                                                     |
| session user                                    | security card         | `telegram_linked, two_factor_enabled`                                                 |

- **UX note:** the ring + "next step" line is exactly what a pending seller needs first; the timeline and the profile-sections card overlap (both list profile work) — one list with the next action highlighted would be shorter.

### `/pro/assistant` — `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi`); the home AI card sends only an advokat here (a yurist gets the AI chat tab). Hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** paste case material and get one of eight structured AI outputs or a free answer.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Ish bo'yicha tez yordam: xulosa, muddatlar, javob loyihasi`.
  2. Card: input `Ish materiallari yoki savol` (`Ish holatini, faktlarni yoki hujjat matnini shu yerga joylang…`, 20 000 chars), counter `{n} ta belgi` / `Ishdan kamida bir necha qator joylashtiring`; `Vositalar` grid `Xulosa`, `Xronologiya`, `Taqqoslash`, `Ziddiyatlar`, `Yetishmaydi`, `Muddatlar`, `Savollar`, `Loyiha` (full names on the result: `Ishni qisqacha bayon qil`, `Voqealar xronologiyasi`, `Hujjatlarni taqqosla`, `Ziddiyatlarni top`, `Yetishmayotgan hujjatlar`, `Muddatlarni ajrat`, `Savollar ro'yxati`, `Javob loyihasini tuz`); gradient `So'rash` (free question); `AI advokat o'rnini bosmaydi — yakuniy huquqiy qaror advokatda qoladi.`; `Maxfiylik: telefon raqamlari, email manzillar, JShShIR (PINFL) va pasport raqamlari AI'ga yuborilishidan oldin yashiriladi.`
  3. Busy card `{tool} · O'ylayapti…` with skeleton lines.
  4. Result cards newest first: tool name + `Nusxalash`, markdown answer; `Tozalash` at the end.
- **Actions:** tool tile / `So'rash` → `POST /ai/assistant`; copy (toast `Nusxalandi`); clear.
- **States:** tiles disabled until enough text or while a request runs; error toast `Hozircha javob berib bo'lmadi.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint             | Used for      | Key fields                                                                             |
| -------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ------ |
| `POST /ai/assistant` | every request | `{prompt, context:{text}}` (tool instruction as prompt) or `{prompt}` (free) → `answer | text | reply` |

- **UX note:** the eight tiles under a long textarea work on a phone; results could pin to the top so the user does not scroll past the form each time.

### `/pro/referrals` — `Takliflar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Referal`); hidden while limited.
- **Purpose:** share the referral code, see the commission ladder and who joined.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Hamkasblaringizni taklif qiling va komissiyani kamaytiring`.
  2. `InviteCard` gradient: `Taklif kodingiz`, big mono code, copy, `Ulashing` (native share with `LexGo — yuridik xizmatlar platformasi. Ro'yxatdan o'ting: {code} {link}`), QR round button → `QrSheet` (`QR kod`, PNG from the API, `Saqlash`).
  3. `LadderTrack` `Komissiya darajalari`: pill `Hozir {pct}%`, bar + `{pct}% gacha yana {n} ta faol taklif` / `Eng past komissiya ochilgan`, vertical steps (`{pct}%`, `Boshlang'ich` / `{n} ta faol taklifdan`, states `Ochilgan` / `Joriy` / `Keyingi` (`Yana {n} ta taklif`) / `Yopiq`).
  4. Stat tiles `Taklif qilinganlar`, `Faol`, `Daromad`.
  5. `Takliflar` list (`{a} / {b}`, 10 at a time + `Yana {n} ta`): monogram, name/phone, `status · date`, pill `Kutilmoqda` / `Ro'yxatdan o'tdi` / `Faol`, `+reward`; empty `Hali taklif yo'q` / `Kodingizni hamkasbingizga yuboring — birinchi taklif shu yerda ko'rinadi.` + `Ulashish`.
  6. `Qanday ishlaydi?` three steps `Yuboring` · `Ro'yxatdan o'tadi` · `Komissiya tushadi`.
- **Actions:** copy (`Kod nusxalandi`), share, QR save/share (`QR kod hali tayyor emas`, `QR kodni yuborib bo'lmadi`); pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton blocks; `ErrorView`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint            | Used for   | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /referrals/me` | everything | `code, link, qr (public PNG path), invited, joined, reward, discount_unlocked, discount_percent, eligible_after, remaining, applies_to[], items[]{name,phone,status,reward,joined_at}` |

- **UX note:** code + share above the fold is right; the ladder is tall — it could fold to the current and next level.

### `/pro/promotions` — `Reklama va boost`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Targ'ibot`); hidden while limited; an advokat also reaches it from the home `Obro' va reyting` card. Running spots cannot be bought again until they end.
- **Purpose:** see what advertising runs, buy a placement, and read reach numbers.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title.
  2. `Faol targ'ib` — one green card per running promotion: placement name, pill `yana {d} kun`, what (`Profil` or the service), package, progress bar, `{date} gacha`, `Muddat tugagach qayta yoqishingiz mumkin`, `To'langan: {sum}`. Hidden when nothing runs.
  3. `Intro`: kicker `Ko'rinuvchanlik vositasi`, `Profilingizni targ'ib qiling`, `Ko'rinuvchanligingizni oshiring va ko'proq potensial mijozlar sizni topsin.`
  4. `PendingList` `Tasdiq kutilmoqda` (`To'lov Telegram orqali admin tomonidan tasdiqlanadi. Tasdiqlangach reklama avtomatik yoqiladi — ilova to'lovni o'zi «to'langan» deb belgilamaydi.`): rows package · placement · amount, pill `Kutilmoqda` / `Telegramga yuborilmadi`.
  5. `Reklama turini tanlang`: three tall cards `Marketplace tepasi` (`Kompyuterda hero'dagi kartalar qatorida birinchi; telefon va planshetda sahifa tepasida keng karta.`), `Ro'yxatda TOP karta` (`Kartangiz oltin ramka va TOP belgisi bilan ro'yxatda yuqoriroq chiqadi.`), `Xizmat reklamasi` (`Xizmatingiz filtrlar ostidagi «Tavsiya etilgan xizmatlar» blokida chiqadi.`) with a preview art and pill `Faol emas` / `Tasdiq kutilmoqda` / `Faol · yana {days} kun` (green, not tappable); note `Targ'ibot faqat ko'rinuvchanlikni oshiradi. Yuridik natijaga ta'sir qilmaydi.`
  6. `Qamrov tahlili` card: `Ko'rsatishlar (14 kun)` sparkline (`Hozircha ko'rsatishlar yo'q`), 2×2 `Profil ko'rsatishlari`, `Qidiruvda ko'rinishlar`, `Profil bosishlari`, `Bog'lanish so'rovlari`, `Filtr` chip → sheet (`Davr`: `Targ'ib davri`, `Butun davr`, `7/14/30/90 kun`, `Maxsus` with `Boshlanish sanasi` / `Tugash sanasi`; `Targ'ib turi` tiles; `Tanlangan: {from} → {to} · {d} kun`; `Tozalash` / `Qo'llash`; rules `Boshlanish sanasi tugash sanasidan keyin bo'lmasin.`, `Sana oralig'i 90 kundan oshmasin.`); `Raqamlar targ'ib boshlangan {date} dan hisoblanadi.`
  7. `Xizmatlarni targ'ib qilish` (`Alohida xizmatni targ'ib qiling — …`): rows of active services (+ boosted catalogue services), `Targ'ibda · {d} kun` pill, tap → checkout; empty `Targ'ib qilish uchun faol xizmat yo'q.` + `Xizmat qo'shish`; link `Xizmatlarni boshqarish` → `/pro/services`.
- **Actions:** placement card → `/pro/promotions/new?placement=`; service row → `/pro/promotions/new?placement=service_boost&service=`; filter; pull to refresh; refetch on focus / foreground and on promotion socket events (toasts `Reklama tasdiqlandi va yoqildi` / `Reklama so'rovi rad etildi`).
- **States:** skeleton pills; analytics error + retry (`Tahlilni yuklab bo'lmadi.`); services error.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                      | Used for          | Key fields                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /promotions/me`                          | running           | `items[]{id,placement,package_title,service_id,service_title,starts_at,ends_at,days_left,price}`       |
| `GET /promotions/analytics?placement&from&to` | reach             | `impressions, search_appearances, profile_clicks, contact_requests, series[]{date,impressions,clicks}` |
| `GET /lawyers/me/services/manage`             | service rows      | `items[]{id,name,category_title,status,promotion}`                                                     |
| `GET /notifications?limit=40` + local store   | pending checkouts | `request_id, target, package_title, amount, telegram_sent`                                             |

- **UX note:** the three placement cards are the action and should come right after the active card; the intro block is dead weight on a phone.

### `/pro/promotions/new` — `Reklama berish` (with `?service=`: `Xizmatni targ'ib qilish`)

- **Who:** both; hidden while limited; `promotion_seller_not_verified` for unverified sellers.
- **Purpose:** a 3–4 step checkout: where → package → (banner) → confirm; payment is approved by an admin in Telegram, some packages go live at once.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `StepHeader` segments `Joy` · `Paket` · `Banner` (only if the package accepts one) · `Tasdiq` (`{n}-qadam, jami {m}`).
  2. Step `1. Reklama joyi`: lead `Reklama joyini va paketni tanlang. To'lov tasdiqlangach reklama avtomatik yoqiladi.`, radio rows `Marketplace tepasi` / `Ro'yxatda TOP karta` / `Xizmat reklamasi` with hints, then `Mutaxassis` row (name) or `Qaysi xizmat reklama qilinadi` picker (`Reklama qilish mumkin bo'lgan faol xizmat yo'q`).
  3. Step `2. Paket`: tiles with price as hero, `{d} kun · {x}x gacha qamrov`, ribbon `Eng foydali` (pre-selected); empty `Faol targ'ib paketlari yo'q` / `Admin paket qo'shgach ular shu yerda chiqadi.`; error + retry.
  4. Step `Banner rasmi (majburiy)` / `(ixtiyoriy)`: three reference portraits `Rasm shunday bo'lishi kerak:` (`Yaqindan, aniq yuz`, `Belgacha, muvozanatli`, `Ish muhitida`), rules `Kamida 720×720, ko'pi bilan 12 MB…`, `Rasm tanlash` (server check — verdict shown in the server's words), `yoki` `Banner rasmi havolasi` (https), `Sarlavha`, `Qisqa matn`, `Tugma yozuvi`, `Tugma havolasi`.
  5. Step `Tasdiq`: `Tekshiring va yuboring`, `PlacementPreview` (how it will look), `Chek` receipt (placement, service, package, days, `Jami`).
  6. Sticky `TotalBar`: `Jami` amount, `Orqaga` / `Davom etish` / `Yuborish`, a reason line when disabled (`Davom etish uchun xizmatni tanlang.`, `Bu paket uchun banner rasmi kerak…`, banner under review).
- **Actions:** `Yuborish` → `POST /promotions/checkout` (or `POST /lawyers/me/services/{id}/promotion-checkout` for a service boost) with `provider: telegram_manual` and an idempotency key → `ResultSheet`: `Reklama yoqildi` / `To'lov talab qilinmadi — reklama hozirdan ko'rinmoqda.`; `Telegram orqali tasdiqlash kutilmoqda` / `Admin tasdiqlagach targ'ib avtomatik yoqiladi…` + `Telegram'ga yuborildi`; or `Telegram'ga yuborilmadi` + `Qayta yuborish`; `Yopish` → `/pro/promotions`.
- **States:** package skeletons; error codes `promotion_already_exists`, `promotion_package_placement_mismatch`, `service_id_required`, `service_id_not_allowed`, `promotion_days_invalid`, `promotion_seller_not_verified`, `promotion_placement_invalid` (Uzbek texts in `promoSeller.ck.errors`); banner `Fayl {mb} MB dan katta bo'lmasligi kerak.`, `Havola https:// bilan boshlanishi kerak.`
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                          | Used for                     | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /promotions/packages?status=active&placement=`                               | packages + placement options | `items[]{id,title,price,currency,days,placement,boost_score,reach,requires_service,requires_banner,accepts_banner,surface,surface_label}`                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /lawyers/me/services/manage`                                                 | service picker               | active services                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /promotions/banner-assets` (multipart)                                      | banner check                 | `file_url, status (active·manual_review·rejected), message, reasons[], suggestions[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /promotions/checkout` / `POST /lawyers/me/services/{id}/promotion-checkout` | buy                          | `{package_id, days, provider:"telegram_manual", placement, service_id, banner_image_url, banner_file_url, banner_title, banner_subtitle, banner_cta_label, banner_cta_url, preview_context, idempotency_key, language}` → `request_id, work_id, amount, currency, telegram_sent, activated` |
| `GET /lawyers/me`, `GET /clients/me`                                              | preview name / avatar        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

- **UX note:** one step per screen with the total pinned is right for a phone; the banner step is the only long one and could default to "no picture".

### `/meetings` — `Uchrashuvlar`

- **Who:** both (drawer `Uchrashuvlar`); hidden while limited. The `+` / create sheet appears only when the account may start calls (`canCalls` permission and cabinet `available_actions.calls`); everybody can join invitations.
- **Purpose:** the professional's agenda of calls / meetings (hosted or invited), join what runs, start a new one.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title.
  2. `MeetingsSummary` `Kutilayotgan: {n} · O'tgan: {n}` + green `{n} ta jonli`.
  3. `LiveCard` per running meeting: `Hozir jonli`, time range, title, host (`Siz`), big `Join` / `Rejoin`.
  4. Segmented `Kutilayotgan · n` / `O'tgan · n`.
  5. `WeekStrip` day picker (`Oldingi hafta` / `Keyingi hafta`), search `Uchrashuv qidirish` (only above 8 items), `Barcha kunlar` link when a day is chosen.
  6. Sections per day (`Bugun`, `Kecha`, dates) with `MeetingRow`: time + dot on a rail, card with title, pill `Hozir jonli` / `Tez orada` / status, audio/video mark, host, `{time} gacha`, short `Join` while live or soon.
  7. FAB `Yangi uchrashuv` (when allowed).
- **Actions:** row → `/meetings/{callId}?room=`; join → `/call/{roomId}?call=`; FAB → `CreateMeetingSheet` (title (`admMeet.titleLabel`, 120), `Format` tiles Video / Audio, `Davomiyligi` `15 daq` / `30 daq` / `60 daq`, `Ishtirokchilar` avatar stack + picker page (`Ism, telefon yoki ID`, `Kamida bitta ishtirokchi tanlang`), `Bekor` / `Boshlash`) → creates a room then a call and opens the call screen. Pull to refresh.
- **States:** agenda skeleton; error; empty `Kutilayotgan uchrashuv yo'q` + `Yangi uchrashuv yarating yoki taklifni kuting` (or `Sizni taklif qilishsa, uchrashuv shu yerda paydo bo'ladi`), `O'tgan uchrashuvlar yo'q` / `Yakunlangan uchrashuvlar shu yerda ko'rinadi`, `Bu kunda uchrashuv yo'q` + `Barcha kunlar`, `Hech narsa topilmadi`. Live refresh on `call.*`, `document_request.meeting_created`, `urgent_advokat.meeting_created`, `urgent_advokat.transferred`; polling only while the socket is down.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                          | Used for               | Key fields                                             |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /calls/invited`              | agenda                 | `call_id                                               | id, room_id, title, caller_id, caller_name, call_type | type, status (mine: joined·removed·declined…), call_status, started_at, auto_end_at` |
| `POST /secure-chats`              | room for a new meeting | `{client_user_id, seller_user_id}` → `id`              |
| `POST /secure-chats/{room}/calls` | start                  | `{type, title, participant_ids[], max_minutes}` → `id` |
| `GET /users/search`               | people picker          | `id,name,phone,lexgo_id`                               |

- **UX note:** live card → upcoming list is the right order; the week strip is only useful with many meetings and could appear with the search (above 8 items).

---

### `/meetings/[id]` — title = meeting title (`Uchrashuv`), subtitle = work id

- **Who:** both; `End for all` only for the host (`perms.can_end`).
- **Purpose:** one meeting's status, roster and the allowed actions.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Title + status pill.
  2. Facts: type (`Video` / `Audio`), creator, started, `Tugash vaqti` (live) / ended, duration (`{n} daqiqa`), `Yozib olish` state.
  3. `Participants (n)` rows: avatar, name, `host · state`.
  4. Buttons: gradient `Join` / `Rejoin` (live and not removed/declined), soft `Chatga o'tish`, line `End for all` (host, live; confirm `Uchrashuvni tugatasizmi?` / `Uchrashuv barcha ishtirokchilar uchun yakunlanadi.`; toast `Uchrashuv tugatildi`).
- **Actions:** join → `/call/{room}?call=`; chat → `/chat/{room}`; end → `POST /secure-chats/{room}/calls/{id}/end` (fallback `PATCH /calls/{id} {status:"ended"}`).
- **States:** skeleton; error; `Uchrashuv topilmadi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                  | Used for                        | Key fields                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /calls/invited`                      | the row (host, my status, room) | as above                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /secure-chats/{room}/calls/{callId}` | live snapshot                   | `title, status, type, started_at, ended_at, auto_end_at, max_minutes, participants[]{user_id,name,role,status}, recording{state}, perms{can_end}` |
| `GET …/join-token`                        | join                            | LiveKit token                                                                                                                                     |
| `POST …/end` / `PATCH /calls/{id}`        | end                             | —                                                                                                                                                 |
| socket `call.*`                           | refetch                         | `call_id`                                                                                                                                         |

- **UX note:** fine as is; the join button should sit above the roster on a phone.

---

### `/team-tasks` — `Jamoa vazifalari`

- **Who:** anyone whose session holds the `tasks.view` permission, whatever the role (the team's own staff sign in as clients or yurists); it is then the first drawer row. Without the permission: `Sizda vazifalarni ko'rish huquqi yo'q.` What each person may do comes from the server's `actions` on every task and `meta.capabilities`.
- **Purpose:** the internal team task board (new → in progress → testing → ready) with assignees, deadlines and history.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. AdminPage title, subtitle `Jamoadagi barcha vazifalar: holati, muddati va kimga biriktirilgani.`, header `+` `Yangi vazifa` (when `can_create`).
  2. `TaskKpis` card: `Bugun berilgan`, `Bugun bajarilgan` (green), `Bajarilmagan` (red when late) — the first two open exactly those tasks; `Bajarilish darajasi` bar + `Jami {total} ta vazifadan {done} tasi tayyor`, `{n} ta vazifaning muddati o'tgan`, `{n} ta vazifa biriktirilmagan`.
  3. Search `Kod, nom yoki tavsif bo'yicha qidirish` + `Filtrlar` (badge).
  4. `StatusBar` chips `Barchasi · n`, `Yangi`, `Bajarilmoqda`, `Testlanmoqda`, `Tayyor` with server counts.
  5. Active filter chips (assignee, `Muddat: date – date`) + `Filtrlarni tozalash`.
  6. `TaskRow` cards (paged): code, status pill, title, assignees (`Biriktirilmagan` in amber), deadline (`Muddati o'tgan ·`, `Bugun 18:00`, `Ertaga 10:00`, `Kecha …`), updated ago, coloured edge, one-tap forward button `Boshlash` / `Testga yuborish` / `Tayyor deb tasdiqlash` when the server allows.
- **Actions:** row → `TaskDetailSheet` (code; `O'chirilgan`): title, pills (status, `Muddati o'tgan`), red box `Qayta ishlashga qaytarilgan` + comment, facts `Muddat`, `Yaratgan`, `Oxirgi o'zgarish`, `Tasdiqlagan`, `O'chirish sababi`, `Ijrochilar`, description (`Tavsif yozilmagan.`), actions = next statuses (`Boshlash`, `Testga yuborish`, `Tayyor deb tasdiqlash`, `Qayta ishlashga qaytarish` → `ReasonSheet` `{code} — qayta ishlashga qaytarish` with a required comment), `Tahrirlash`, `Ijrochilar` (`AssigneesSheet` multi-select), `O'chirish` (`ReasonSheet` with `O'chirish sababi`); `Tarix` list (`Yaratildi`, `Tahrirlandi`, `Ijrochilar o'zgardi`, `Holat o'zgardi`, `Qaytarildi`, `O'chirildi`). `+` → `TaskFormSheet` `Yangi vazifa` (`Nomi`, `Tavsif`, `Muddat` date + time + chips `Bugun 18:00` / `Ertaga 10:00` / `3 kundan keyin` / `1 haftadan keyin`, `Ijrochilar` when `can_assign`; `Ijrochisiz vazifa yaratilsa, biriktirish uchun mas'ul xodimga Telegram orqali so'rov yuboriladi.`; `Yaratish`). Filter sheet `Filtrlar`: `Ijrochi` (`Hammasi`, `Menga biriktirilgan`, `Biriktirilmagan`, people), `Davr` field `Muddat` / `Yaratilgan` / `Bajarilgan` + range calendar (`Davr 367 kundan oshmasin.`), `Tiklash` / `Qo'llash`. Deep link `/team-tasks?task=<id>` opens the detail.
- **States:** skeleton; error texts (`Vazifalarni yuklab bo'lmadi`, `Internet aloqasi yo'q…`, `Vazifa boshqa joyda o'zgartirilgan — yangi holati yuklandi.`); empty `Hali vazifa yo'q` / `Bu filtrlar bo'yicha vazifa topilmadi`; toasts `{code}: «{status}» holatiga o'tkazildi`, `{code} qayta ishlashga qaytarildi`, `{code} o'chirildi`, `{code} yaratildi`, `{code} saqlandi`; live via socket task events.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                     | Used for                                | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /task-management?status&assignee_user_id&q&date_from&date_to&period_field&limit&offset` | list                                    | `items[]{id,code,title,description,status,deadline_at,overdue,creator,assignees[],ready_by,ready_at,lock_version,created_at,updated_at,deleted_at,delete_reason,actions{can_edit,can_assign,can_delete,can_change_status,next_statuses[],is_assigned}}`, `total`, `status_counts` |
| `GET /task-management/{id}`, `GET …/history`                                                 | detail                                  | `history[]{id,event,from,to,comment,actor,created_at}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GET /task-management/meta`                                                                  | `capabilities{can_create,can_assign,…}` | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GET /task-management/stats?date_from&date_to`                                               | KPIs                                    | `created, ready_in_period, total, active, ready, overdue, unassigned, by_status`                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /task-management/users?q&limit&offset`                                                  | assignee picker                         | `items[]{id,name,lexgo_id,role}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `POST /task-management`                                                                      | create                                  | `{title, description, deadline_at, assignee_user_ids[]}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PATCH /task-management/{id}`                                                                | edit                                    | `{title, description, deadline_at, expected_lock_version}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PUT /task-management/{id}/assignees`                                                        | assign                                  | `{user_ids[], expected_lock_version}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /task-management/{id}/status`                                                          | move                                    | `{status, comment, expected_lock_version}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DELETE /task-management/{id}`                                                               | delete                                  | `{reason, expected_lock_version}`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

- **UX note:** KPIs + status chips + first card fit a phone; the one-tap forward button on the row is the best part. The filter chips and the search both sit above the status bar — grouping them in one row would save height.
