## Cross-cutting for professionals

**Seller tab bar** (`widgets/tab-bar/tabConfig.ts`, role `seller` for both kinds): `Ish maydoni` (`workspace`, icon layers) · `So'rovlar` (`requests`, inbox, badge = items needing action) · centre AI button `AI` (`ai`) · `Mijozlar` (`clients`, users) · `Xabarlar` (`messages`, badge). Rules: while `limited_access` only `Ish maydoni` and the AI button stay; the `Xabarlar` tab disappears when `available_actions.secure_chat` is false; an unknown cabinet (still loading) hides nothing. `Profil` has no tab — it is behind the avatar in every header. Tabs are lazy (`lazyTab`), the home route is `workspace`.

**Drawer rows** (`widgets/client-sidebar/navItems.ts` `sellerNav`, labels `lsb.*` unless noted; a `tasks.view` holder gets `Jamoa vazifalari` (`ttm.title`) as the very first row):

| #   | yurist (`lawyer`)                                         | advokat (`advocate`)                                   |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1   | `Boshqaruv paneli` → `/(tabs)/workspace`                  | `Boshqaruv paneli` → `/(tabs)/workspace`               |
| 2   | `Ishlar bozori` → `/pro/market` (needs `accept_orders`)   | `Imkoniyatlar` → `/pro/market` (needs `accept_orders`) |
| 3   | `Xizmat buyurtmalari` → `/pro/orders`                     | `Xizmat buyurtmalari` → `/pro/orders`                  |
| 4   | `Mening ishlarim` → `/pro/cases`                          | `Mening ishlarim` → `/pro/cases`                       |
| 5   | `Tezkor ishlar` → `/pro/urgent`                           | `Tezkor ishlar` → `/pro/urgent`                        |
| 6   | `Vazifalar` → `/pro/tasks`                                | `Hujjat so'rovlari` → `/pro/docs`                      |
| 7   | `Kalendar` → `/pro/calendar`                              | `Kalendar` → `/pro/calendar`                           |
| 8   | `Mijozlar` → `/(tabs)/clients`                            | `Mijozlar` → `/(tabs)/clients`                         |
| 9   | `Xizmatlarim` → `/pro/services`                           | `Xizmatlarim` → `/pro/services`                        |
| 10  | `Hujjatlar` → `/pro/templates`                            | `Vazifalar` → `/pro/tasks`                             |
| 11  | `Hujjat so'rovlari` → `/pro/docs`                         | `Xabarlar` → `/(tabs)/messages` (needs `secure_chat`)  |
| 12  | `Fayllar` → `/pro/files`                                  | `Uchrashuvlar` → `/meetings`                           |
| 13  | `Xavfsiz chat` → `/(tabs)/messages` (needs `secure_chat`) | `Bildirishnomalar` → `/notifications` (badge)          |
| 14  | `Uchrashuvlar` → `/meetings`                              | `Fayllar` → `/pro/files`                               |
| 15  | `Bildirishnomalar` → `/notifications` (badge)             | `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi` → `/pro/assistant`        |
| 16  | `Sun'iy Intellekt chat` → `/(tabs)/ai`                    | `Referal` → `/pro/referrals`                           |
| 17  | `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi` → `/pro/assistant`           | `Targ'ibot` → `/pro/promotions`                        |
| 18  | `Referal` → `/pro/referrals`                              | `Tariflar` → `/subscription`                           |
| 19  | `Targ'ibot` → `/pro/promotions`                           | `Yordam markazi` → `/support` (badge)                  |
| 20  | `Tariflar` → `/subscription`                              | —                                                      |
| 21  | `Yordam markazi` → `/support` (badge)                     | —                                                      |

Then, for both, the mobile-only tool rows from `PRO_SECTIONS` (labels `nav1.menu.*` unless noted): `Statistika` → `/pro/stats`, `Ish vaqti` → `/pro/availability`, `Tajriba` (`mf.exp.menu`) → `/pro/experience`, `Yutuqlar` (`ach.menu`) → `/pro/achievements`, `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari` → `/pro/onboarding`, `Paketlarim` (`ntd.sellerV2.menuPackages`) → `/pro/packages`; then the account rows (`py9.menu.*`): `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` → `/profile/edit`, `To'lovlar va cheklar` → `/payments`, `Tarifim imkoniyatlari` → `/entitlements`, `Oila a'zolari` → `/profile/family`, `To'lov usullari` → `/profile/methods`, `Roziliklar` → `/profile/consents`, `Sovg'alar` → `/profile/gifts`, `Shikoyat va baholar` → `/complaints`, `LexGo kafolati` → `/warranty`; then the app settings (`nav1.menu.*`): `Bildirishnoma sozlamalari` → `/settings/notifications`, `Xavfsizlik` → `/security`, `Ilova qulfi (Face ID)` → `/settings/security-lock`, `Xotira va kesh` → `/settings/storage`; and the sign-out button at the foot. The drawer header shows the brand, the user's name and the role word (`profile.roleAdvocate` / `profile.roleLawyer`). `/pro/profile` and `/pro/scan` have no drawer row (reached from the home ring / onboarding sections, and from the files upload menu).

**Limited-access state** (cabinet `limited_access: true`, or `account_status === "pending"` until the cabinet answers): the drawer keeps only — yurist: `Boshqaruv paneli`, `Xizmatlarim`, `Tariflar`, `Bildirishnomalar`, `Yordam markazi`, `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari`; advokat: the same without `Xizmatlarim` — plus the account and settings rows, which are always there. The tab bar keeps `Ish maydoni` + AI. The home shows the `Profilingiz hali tasdiqlanmagan` banner with the locked actions and the onboarding button; `/pro/market` and `/pro/orders` show their lock notes; `/pro/files` and `/pro/packages` (403) show their pending states; `/pro/services` shows the unverified notice. Rows that need an action: `Ishlar bozori` / `Imkoniyatlar` need `accept_orders`; `Xavfsiz chat` / `Xabarlar` need `secure_chat`; meetings creation needs `calls`.

**Onboarding gate**: state = rejected (`account_status` / `verification` / progress `status` matches rejected) → approved (`!limited_access` and verified and account active/approved) → submitted (progress or verification in a review status) → ready (every required step completed) → incomplete. Only an approved seller is public and can sell; the home's `StatusBanner` and the services page's notice both send to `/pro/onboarding`; the submit button appears only when not approved and not under review.

**Header buttons**: tab pages and the home hero share one row — left `MenuButton` (opens the drawer; absent on a docked tablet), then `HeaderActions`: `DemoPill` (demo data toggle, `Demo` / `Demo yoqilgan`), `SupportMenu` headset (sheet `cpd.support.*`: operator chat → `/support`, phone call with business-hours badge, hub link; the client-only complaint item is not shown to sellers), bell with unread badge → `/notifications`, avatar → `/(tabs)/profile`. Every AdminPage screen has a back arrow, the title/subtitle, and the registered `HeaderRight` (support headset) unless the page passes its own `right` (e.g. `+` on cases / tasks / team tasks, refresh on services). `HeaderExtra` is a second slot under the header for an app-registered banner.

---

# PART C — SHARED screens (auth, profile, payments, security, notifications, support, chat, calls) and app-level rules

## LexGo mobile — pages shared by every signed-in role, auth and app-level behaviour

Read from the code at `/Users/projects/lexgo-mobile` (expo-router routes in `app/`, pages in `src/pages`, widgets in `src/widgets`, features in `src/features`, entities in `src/entities`, Uzbek copy in `src/shared/i18n/locales/uz`). Uzbek UI strings are quoted verbatim in backticks. Roles: `client`, `advokat` (UI role `advocate`), `yurist` (UI role `lawyer`); `guest` = signed out. "Seller" / "professional" = advokat or yurist (`useTabRole()` → `seller`; `useAccess().seller`).

Conventions used below: "device headers" = `deviceHeaders()` sent on login / 2FA / register-verify (a device label for the sessions list). `AdminPage` = the shared stack-page frame (back button, title, optional subtitle / right button). `PagedList` = list with skeleton, error view, empty state, pull-to-refresh and "show N more". All list screens reveal 10 rows at a time unless noted.

---

## Auth

### `/auth/login` — `LexGo ga kirish`

- **Who:** guests (any role signs in here). Presented as a native iOS modal (slide from bottom); toasts and the confirm dialog draw inside it (`OverlayHost`). No per-role difference on the screen itself; the _account kind_ decides what happens after a successful login (see "Roles and sign-in rules").
- **Purpose:** phone + password sign-in, with a hand-off to the OTP screen when the account has 2FA.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AuthHero` — dark brand gradient panel: glass back button (left; `router.back()` or, with nothing to go back to, `/(tabs)/profile`), `BrandLogo` 48 px centred, title `LexGo ga kirish`, subtitle `Telefon raqamingiz bilan tizimga kiring`.
  2. Notice line (conditional: the session store holds a `notice`): `Sessiya muddati tugadi. Qayta kiring.` (server ended the session) or `Bu hisob turi mobil ilovada ishlamaydi. Ilova mijoz, advokat va yurist uchun — veb-portal orqali kiring.` (`webOnly`). Cleared when the screen unmounts.
  3. `LoginForm`: phone field `Telefon` (placeholder `+998 __ ___ __ __`, autofocus), password field `Parol` (placeholder `Kamida 8 ta belgi`, error text under it), right-aligned link `Parolni unutdingizmi?`, gradient button `Kirish` (busy → `Iltimos, kuting…`; during a pause → `Qayta urinish: 0:49`, disabled), caption `Telefon va parolingiz faqat xavfsiz kirish uchun ishlatiladi.`, row `Hisobingiz yo'qmi?` + bold `Hisob yaratish`, then the `SpecialistInvite` card (briefcase badge on the brand gradient, `Mutaxassis sifatida qo'shiling`, `Yurist yoki advokat bo'lib mijozlar toping`, chevron).
     Max width 520 on tablets (`TabletColumn`).
- **Actions:** `Parolni unutdingizmi?` → push `/auth/forgot`; `Kirish` → POST `/auth/login`; `Hisob yaratish` → replace `/auth/register`; specialist card → push `/auth/register?role=advokat`; back → previous screen / Profil tab.
  On success: session stored (`signIn`), success haptic, toast `Xush kelibsiz, {name}!` (or `Tizimga kirdingiz.`), consents ticked at sign-up are posted, every auth modal is dismissed and the app lands on `/` (Home). A 428 / `two_factor_required` answer starts the OTP flow (`purpose: login2fa`, `totp` when the method is an authenticator) and pushes `/auth/otp`.
- **States:** loading = button spinner. Wrong password → `Telefon yoki parol noto'g'ri.`; from the 3rd miss a local pause of 5 s, then 10, 20, 40, 60 s on top of the server's own limit. 429 → server `Retry-After` (or 60 s) countdown on the button; text `Juda ko'p urinish. Biroz kuting.` (Uzbek users see the server's own sentence). Blocked → `Urinishlar chegarasi tugadi. Keyinroq qayta urinib ko'ring.`. 503 → `Kod yuborish kanali (Telegram/SMS) hozircha ulanmagan.`. Offline → `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.`. 5xx → `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`. An account the app does not serve (organisation / staff / call-centre / sales) signs in on the server, is logged out again at once and the form shows the `webOnly` text. A 2FA challenge without `verification_id` shows the server message instead.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                     | Used for                                               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| POST `/auth/login` (no auth, device headers) | sign in                                                | req `phone`, `password`; 200 `access_token`/`token`, `refresh_token`, `user{ id, lexgo_id, name, first_name, last_name, middle_name, region, phone, role, account_status/status, roles[], permissions[], two_factor_enabled, two_factor_method, phone_verified, telegram_linked, created_at }`; 428 (or `two_factor_required: true`) `detail{ method/two_factor_method, verification_id, expires_at, message }`, `telegram_bot_link` |
| POST `/auth/logout`                          | close the server session of a web-only account         | `refresh_token`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| POST `/legal/consents/{id}/accept`           | post the documents ticked at sign-up (`flushConsents`) | `version`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** one screen for every role; the professional's way in is the invite card, not a separate login. The pause after wrong passwords is visible on the button itself, so the user is never told "try again" without a time.

### `/auth/register` — `Hisob yaratish`

- **Who:** guests. Native iOS modal. `?ref=CODE` pre-fills the referral field, `?role=advokat|yurist|client` pre-selects the account kind. The three kinds the app signs up are `client`, `yurist`, `advokat` (an organisation registers on the web). Sellers are told they wait for an admin.
- **Purpose:** step 1 of sign-up — collect the details and ask the server for a Telegram code.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AuthHero`: title `Hisob yaratish`, subtitle `Hisob turini tanlang va ma'lumotlaringizni kiriting.`.
  2. `RoleTiles` — label `Hisob turi`, three equal tiles `Mijoz` (user icon) / `Yurist` (briefcase) / `Advokat` (shield); under them the chosen kind's description (`Yuridik xizmat, hujjat va maslahat olish uchun.` / `Yurist sifatida mijozlarga xizmat ko'rsatish uchun.` / `Advokat sifatida ish yuritish va mijozlar bilan ishlash uchun.`) and, for a seller, the warn caption `Mutaxassis hisobi administrator tasdig'idan keyin to'liq faollashadi. Kirgach profilingizni to'ldirasiz.`.
  3. `NameFields`: `Ism`, `Familiya`, `Otasining ismi` (2–80 letters; middle name optional).
  4. Region select `Hudud` (placeholder `Hududni tanlang`): `Toshkent`, `Samarqand`, `Farg'ona`, `Buxoro`, `Namangan`, `Andijon`, `Qashqadaryo`, `Xorazm`, `Jizzax`, `Navoiy` (keys tashkent … navoi).
  5. Phone `Telefon`.
  6. Password `Parol` with hint `Kamida 8 ta belgidan foydalaning.` (8–128 chars, new-password keyboard hint).
  7. Referral `Taklif kodi · Ixtiyoriy`, placeholder `LX…`, gift icon, upper-cased, formatted by `lxCodeFrom`.
  8. `ConsentRow`: checkbox + `Foydalanuvchi ofertasi, maxfiylik siyosati va shaxsiy ma'lumotlarni qayta ishlashga roziman.`, link `Hujjatlarni o'qish` (opens `ConsentSheet` with the sign-up documents of the chosen kind), red error under it when not ticked.
  9. Server error line (red, centred) — conditional.
  10. Soft button `Tizimga kirish` — conditional: the phone already has an account (409).
  11. Gradient button `Kod yuborish` (busy `Kod yuborilmoqda…`, pause `Qayta urinish: {time}`).
  12. `Hisobingiz bormi?` + `Kirish`.
- **Actions:** `Hujjatlarni o'qish` → consent sheet (GET `/legal/consents`, the newest version per slug that the kind must accept, in the order user_offer, privacy, personal_data_consent, platform_rules, age_18; sellers also lawyer_partnership_offer / advocate_partnership; `client_advocate_contract_template`, `client_provider_contract`, `cookie`, `organization_agreement` never). `Kod yuborish` → POST `/auth/register/start`, remembers the ticked documents locally (`lexgo_pending_consents`), starts the OTP flow (`purpose: register`) and pushes `/auth/otp`. `Tizimga kirish` / `Kirish` → replace `/auth/login`.
- **States:** validation messages per field (names, region `au1.regionReq`, phone, password hint, `agreed`). 409 → `Bu telefon raqami bilan hisob allaqachon mavjud. Tizimga kiring.` + the login button. 429 → countdown. Offline / server as on login. Loading the consent list never blocks sign-up.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                              | Used for               | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/legal/consents` (public)        | sign-up documents      | `items[]{ id, slug, version, title / title_json{uz,ru}, body_json, is_active }`                                                                                                                                                 |
| GET `/legal/consents/{id}` (public)   | full text in the sheet | `body` / `body_json`                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| POST `/auth/register/start` (no auth) | step 1                 | req `role`, `name`, `phone`, `password`, `first_name`, `last_name`, `middle_name`, `region`, `referral_code`; res `verification_id`, `expires_at` / `expires_in`, `telegram_bot_link`, `message`; 409 phone taken, 429 cooldown |

- **UX note:** the kind picker is first because it changes the consent set and the note about admin review. The code only ever arrives through the Telegram bot; the screen says so on the next step.

### `/auth/otp` — `Tasdiqlash kodini kiriting` / `Ikki bosqichli tasdiqlash` / `Kod va yangi parol`

- **Who:** anyone in a pending OTP flow (register, 2FA login, password reset). Without a flow the screen redirects: to `/auth/login` when signed out, to `/` when already signed in (the flow is dropped only after the auth modals are dismissed). Sellers whose registration waits for an admin are sent to `/auth/pending`.
- **Purpose:** one screen for the three OTP purposes; the reset purpose also takes the new password.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AuthHero`: title by purpose — register `Tasdiqlash kodini kiriting`, login2fa `Ikki bosqichli tasdiqlash`, reset `Kod va yangi parol`; subtitle `6 xonali kod {phone} raqamiga Telegram bot orqali yuborildi` (phone masked), authenticator `Authenticator ilovangizdagi 6 xonali kodni kiriting`, reset `Agar {phone} raqami ro'yxatdan o'tgan bo'lsa, 6 xonali kod Telegram bot orqali yuborildi.`.
  2. `CodeSlots` — six code boxes (auto-verify on the 6th digit except for reset; paste-friendly overlay input).
  3. `OtpStatus` — server error (red) / note `Yangi kod yuborildi.` (green) / countdown `Kod yana {time} amal qiladi` / `Kod muddati tugadi. Yangisini so'rang.` (red).
  4. Reset only: `PasswordField` `Yangi parol` with hint `Kamida 8 ta belgidan foydalaning.`.
  5. Gradient button `Tasdiqlash` (reset: `Parolni yangilash`; busy `Tekshirilmoqda…`), disabled until 6 digits, while expired or blocked.
  6. `OtpResendRow`: soft button `Telegram botni ochish` (when the challenge carries a bot link and the code is not expired), link `Kodni qayta yuborish` / `Qayta yuborish: {time}` (60 s cooldown; never for an authenticator code).
- **Actions:** verify → POST `/auth/login/2fa` | POST `/auth/register/verify` | POST `/auth/password/reset`; resend → the same request as the previous screen (`/auth/register/start` with the same details, `/auth/password/forgot`, or `/auth/login` again — a plain session may come back when 2FA was switched off meanwhile). Register verify: tokens → signed in (same finish as login); `pending` → replace `/auth/pending?role=…&message=…`; 428 / `loginRequired` → toast `Hisob yaratildi. Xavfsizlik uchun endi tizimga kiring.` and replace `/auth/login`. Reset → toast `Parol yangilandi. Endi tizimga kirishingiz mumkin.` and replace `/auth/login`. Expired authenticator → `Tasdiqlash muddati tugadi. Tizimga qayta kiring.` + button `Qayta kirish` (back).
- **States:** wrong code → `Kod noto'g'ri. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, slots drained. Rate (429 short) → countdown on the button. Blocked (three wrong codes, 15 min): the inputs are replaced by the `OtpLocked` card — lock icon, `Kod bloklandi`, `Qayta urinish uchun kuting: {time}` / after it `Eski kod endi ishlamaydi. Yangi kod so'rang.`, gradient `Yangi kod so'rash` (after the block), ghost `Raqamni o'zgartirish` (back). Expired → `Kod muddati tugadi. Yangisini so'rang.`. Reset with a short password → `Kamida 8 ta belgidan foydalaning.`. Channel down (503) → `Kod yuborish kanali (Telegram/SMS) hozircha ulanmagan.`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                      | Used for       | Key fields                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| POST `/auth/login/2fa` (no auth, device headers)                              | 2FA login      | req `verification_id`, `code`; res tokens + `user`                                                                            |
| POST `/auth/register/verify` (no auth, device headers)                        | finish sign-up | req `verification_id`, `code`; res tokens + `user`, or `{ message, status: "pending", role }`, or 428 / `two_factor_required` |
| POST `/auth/password/reset` (no auth)                                         | new password   | req `verification_id`, `code`, `password`                                                                                     |
| POST `/auth/register/start`, POST `/auth/password/forgot`, POST `/auth/login` | resend         | as above                                                                                                                      |

- **UX note:** the clock (expiry, resend cooldown, block) lives in one hook so the three texts never contradict each other; the lock card replaces the inputs instead of greying them.

### `/auth/forgot` — `Parolni tiklash`

- **Who:** guests.
- **Purpose:** ask for a reset code (sent to the Telegram bot); the code and the new password are typed on `/auth/otp`.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AuthHero`: `Parolni tiklash` / `Telefon raqamingizni kiriting — tasdiqlash kodini yuboramiz.`.
  2. Phone field `Telefon` (autofocus).
  3. Error line (red) — conditional.
  4. Gradient button `Kod yuborish` (`Kod yuborilmoqda…`, pause `Qayta urinish: {time}`).
  5. Caption `Xavfsizlik uchun raqam ro'yxatdan o'tgan yoki o'tmaganligi ko'rsatilmaydi.`.
- **Actions:** `Kod yuborish` → POST `/auth/password/forgot` → OTP flow `purpose: reset` → push `/auth/otp`. A 404 (unknown phone) is treated like success with an empty `verification_id` and a 2-minute clock, so known and unknown phones look the same.
- **States:** 429 → countdown; offline / server text as on login.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                               | Used for   | Key fields                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| POST `/auth/password/forgot` (no auth) | reset code | req `phone`; res `verification_id`, `expires_at`, `telegram_bot_link`, `message` |

- **UX note:** no account enumeration: the only difference between a known and unknown phone is that the latter's code can never verify.

### `/auth/pending` — `So'rov qabul qilindi`

- **Who:** a seller (advokat / yurist) whose sign-up was accepted but issued no tokens (waits for an admin). Params `role` (`yurist` → subtitle `Yurist`, otherwise `Advokat`) and `message` (the server's own text).
- **Purpose:** say what happens next and send the person back to sign-in.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `AuthHero` without a back button: `So'rov qabul qilindi` / role name.
  2. Body text: the server `message` if sent, else `Hisobingiz administrator tasdig'idan keyin faollashadi.`.
  3. `PendingSteps` — three numbered glass rows: `Administrator so'rovingizni ko'rib chiqadi.`, `Natija haqida xabarnoma olasiz.`, `Tizimga kirib, mutaxassis profilingizni to'ldirasiz.`.
  4. Gradient button `Tizimga kirish`.
- **Actions:** `Tizimga kirish` → replace `/auth/login`.
- **States:** none (static).
- **Data:** none. (The consents ticked at sign-up are kept locally and posted at the first sign-in of the same phone.)
- **UX note:** the same three steps are shown again inside the app by the soft account gate once the seller signs in while still `pending`.

---

## Profile and account

### `/profile` — `Profil` (tab page; title shown by the header avatar, no tab slot)

- **Who:** everyone. Three bodies: **guest** → `GuestWelcome`; **client** → `ProfileHeader` (cover + identity) + `ClientSections` (five tabs) + `AppSettings`; **seller** (advokat / yurist) → `ProfileHeader` + `ProfileSummary` + `IdentityCard` + `AppSettings` — a professional's account links live in the drawer, not here (`ProfileMenu` / `ProfileExtrasMenu` render only for roles the app no longer serves, i.e. never). Remote assistance page id `client.profile` for a client.
  Reached from the header avatar on every tab page (`/(tabs)/profile`; on iOS as the stack screen `/m/profile`, since Profil has no tab slot).
- **Purpose:** the account: who I am, my data, security, payments, complaints and activity (client), or a summary and identity check (seller); app settings and sign-out for both.
- **Layout, top → bottom (signed in):**
  1. `ProfileCover` — dark brand gradient band (`insets.top + 118` high, two glows, two thin rings), then `ProfileIdentity`: 92 px avatar overlapping the cover's edge (client photo from GET `/clients/me` `avatar_url`), name (or masked phone), phone row, pills: role `Mijoz` / `Advokat` / `Yurist` and `LexGo ID {lexgo_id}` when known.
  2. **Client:** `ProfileTabs` — one glass bar with a gliding brand-gradient thumb and five tabs: `Profil` (user), `Xavfsizlik` (shield), `To'lovlar` (credit-card), `Shikoyatlar` (message-square), `Faollik` (clock). The tab content slides in from the side of the tab (FadeInRight / FadeInLeft 320 ms). Remote assistance may switch only to `Profil` and `Shikoyatlar` (control `profile.tabs`, label `Profil bo'limlari`).
     - `Profil`: **`PersonalPanel`** (`Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar`, edit action `Profilni tahrirlash`): gradient `IdentityHero` (60 px avatar with pencil badge → edit sheet, name, `Tasdiqlangan profil` glass pill when identity is verified, copyable `Mening ID` row with the LexGo ID), then `FactTiles` `Telefon`, `Bank kartasi` (`•••• last4` of the first saved card), `Email` (wide), unset values shown dim as `Kiritilmagan`; the `ProfileEditSheet` (photo picker `Rasm tanlash` / `Olib tashlash`, hint `JPG yoki PNG, 512×512 gacha`, fields `Ism` / `Email`, submit `Saqlash`). Then **`IdentityCard`** (`Shaxsni tasdiqlash`, lead `MyID (asosiy) yoki OneID orqali shaxsingizni tasdiqlang — … Advokat va yuristlar uchun majburiy, mijoz uchun ixtiyoriy.`): verified box `Shaxs tasdiqlangan` + name · PINFL; or pending `Tasdiqlash jarayonda. …` + `Holatni yangilash`; or the provider tiles (MyID `Biometrik ID`, OneID `Davlat xizmatlari ID`) and, after a demo-mode start, the code step. Then **`FamilyPanel`** (`Oila a'zolari`, action `Oila a'zosini qo'shish`, note `Faol tarifingiz bo'lsa, ruxsat berilgan oila a'zolari undan foydalanishi mumkin.`): member rows (avatar, name, phone, relation pill, toggle `Ruxsat berilgan` / `Ruxsatsiz`, remove ×), `AddRow`, empty `Oila a'zolari qo'shilmagan`.
     - `Xavfsizlik`: `TwoFactorCard`, `TelegramCard`, `NotifPrefsCard`, `SessionsPanel` (`Aktiv sessiyalar`, this device first, revoke per row; empty `Faol sessiya yo'q`). These four cards are the same as on `/security` (see there).
     - `To'lovlar`: `CardsPanel` (`To'lov usullari`, mini bank cards, `Karta qo'shish`, empty `Saqlangan kartalar yo'q`), `SubscriptionPanel` (`Obuna`: plan name large or `Standard · faol`, status pill, `Boshqarish` → `/subscription`, row `Boshqarish` / `Tarifni o'zgartirish, to'lov muddati va avto-to'lov`), `PaymentsPanel` (`To'lovlar tarixi` with the count badge: `PaymentSummary` KPI tiles `Jami to'langan` / `Kutilayotgan`, `PaymentFilters`, rows → `/payments/{id}`, 10 at a time; empty `Hozircha to'lov yo'q` / `To'lovlar va cheklar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`, no match `Mos to'lov topilmadi`).
     - `Shikoyatlar`: `ComplaintsHero` (navy gradient, three-step stepper, white `Yangi shikoyat` button), `ComplaintExtras` (two quiet lines: low rating opens a quality complaint by itself; operator chat), `ComplaintNotes`, section `Shikoyatlar` with filters, complaint tiles 10 at a time, detail sheet, new-complaint sheet (POST `/complaints`); empty `Hozircha shikoyat yo'q` / `Bu filtrga mos shikoyat yo'q.`.
     - `Faollik`: `EventsPanel` (`Xavfsizlik hodisalari`, lead `Yangi qurilma yoki IP'dan kirish va kod urinishlari shu yerda ko'rinadi.`, timeline, empty `Shubhali hodisalar yo'q`), `ActivityPanel` (`Harakatlar jurnali`, caption `{n} yozuv`, group chips `Hammasi` / `Kirish va xavfsizlik` / `Buyurtma va to'lov` / `Hujjatlar` / `Profil va sozlamalar` / `Boshqa` with counts, search `Jurnaldan qidirish`, first 100 rows, 10 at a time; empty `Hali harakatlar yo'q`, no match `Mos yozuv topilmadi`).
       2'. **Seller:** `ProfileSummary` card `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` (+ `Tasdiqlangan profil` pill, soft button `Profilni tahrirlash` → `/profile/edit`): facts `Mening ID`, `Ism`, `Telefon`, `Email`, `Bank kartasi`, divider, `Obuna` (`{plan} · status` or `Standard · faol`); then `IdentityCard`.
  3. `AppSettings` — kicker `ILOVA`: card with `Mavzu` + `ThemeSegment` (`Yorug'` / `Qorong'i` / `Qurilma`), `Til` + `LocaleSegment` (`O'zbek` / `Русский` / `English`); `AppUpdateCard` (`Ilova yangilanishi`, `Versiya {v} · {updateId}`, `Yangilanishni tekshirish` → `Tekshirilmoqda…` / `Yuklab olinmoqda…`, ready → `Qayta ishga tushirish`; builds without the update module say `Bu o'rnatilgan nusxada avtomatik yangilanish yo'q. …`); red full-width `LogoutButton` `Chiqish`; caption `Versiya {v}`.
- **Layout (guest):** `GuestHero` (dark gradient, living `AiOrb` 190 px, `LexGo ga xush kelibsiz`, `Yuridik yordam hamma uchun — tez, ishonchli, shaffof.`), `Benefits` carousel with dots (`Sun'iy intellekt` / `Dastlabki 5 ta savolga bepul javob`, `Tasdiqlangan advokatlar` / `Reyting va narxlarni solishtiring`, `Buyurtmalarni kuzating` / `Har bir xizmat holati bir joyda`), `GuestActions` (`Kirish` gradient, `Ro'yxatdan o'tish` soft, `SpecialistInvite`), `GuestSettings` (theme, language, version; no sign-out).
- **Actions:** avatar / pencil → edit sheet (client) or `/profile/edit` (seller); `Boshqarish` → `/subscription`; payment row → `/payments/{id}`; `Oila a'zosini qo'shish` → `FamilySheet`; `Karta qo'shish` → `MethodSheet`; `Yangi shikoyat` → `ComplaintSheet`; 2FA / Telegram / notification switches as on `/security`; session revoke → confirm; `Chiqish` → confirm dialog `Chiqish` / `Hisobdan chiqmoqchimisiz?` → POST `/auth/logout` (best effort) + local wipe; guest buttons → `/auth/login`, `/auth/register`, `/auth/register?role=advokat`.
- **States:** per panel: skeletons while loading, compact `EmptyState`s (texts above), `ErrorView` with retry in the notification card; sharing the plan with a family member without an active tariff → toast `Buning uchun avval faol tarif sotib olishingiz kerak.` (402); other 402s open the "plan required" dialog (`To'lov yoki tarif kerak` / `Tariflarni ko'rish`).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                         | Used for                                                                        | Key fields                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/clients/me`                                                                                | name, phone, email, photo, plan (client header, personal panel, seller summary) | `name`, `phone`, `email`, `avatar_url`, `subscription{ plan_name, status, renews_at, auto_renew }`                                                       |
| PUT `/clients/me`                                                                                | edit sheet                                                                      | `name`, `email`, `avatar_url` (512 px JPEG data URL, only when changed)                                                                                  |
| GET `/identity/me`                                                                               | identity status                                                                 | list or object: `verified`/`identity_verified`, `provider`, `status`, `full_name`, `pinfl`                                                               |
| POST `/identity/start`, POST `/identity/verify-demo`                                             | start / demo-code verification                                                  | req `provider` (oneid/myid), `redirect_uri`, `purpose: profile_verification`; res `auth_url`, `state`, `demo_code`; demo req `provider`, `state`, `code` |
| GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE `/clients/me/family-members[/{id}]`                                        | family panel                                                                    | `id`, `name`, `phone`, `relation`, `shared_access`/`can_use_plan`, `created_at`                                                                          |
| GET/POST/DELETE `/clients/me/payment-methods[/{id}]`                                             | cards panel + "card" tile                                                       | `id`, `brand`, `last4`, `expires`, `is_default`, `created_at`                                                                                            |
| GET `/payments`                                                                                  | payments panel                                                                  | see `/payments`                                                                                                                                          |
| GET `/auth/me`                                                                                   | 2FA / Telegram state (security tab)                                             | `two_factor_enabled`, `two_factor_method`, `telegram_linked` / `telegram{linked}` / `telegram_chat_id`                                                   |
| POST `/auth/2fa/start`, POST `/auth/2fa/verify`, DELETE `/auth/2fa`, POST `/telegram/link/start` | security tab cards                                                              | see `/security`                                                                                                                                          |
| GET/PUT `/notifications/preferences`                                                             | notification card                                                               | see `/security`                                                                                                                                          |
| GET `/auth/sessions`, DELETE `/auth/sessions/{id}`                                               | sessions panel                                                                  | `id`, `device_label`/`device`, `ip`, `last_used_at`, `created_at`, `expires_at`, `current`/`is_current`                                                  |
| GET `/auth/security-events` (optional)                                                           | events panel                                                                    | `id`, `type`, `title`, `status`, `payload{ previous_ip, current_ip, user_agent }`, `created_at`                                                          |
| GET `/users/me/activity`                                                                         | activity panel                                                                  | `id`, `action`/`type`, `detail`, `title_uz`, `description_uz`, `ip`, `outcome`, `created_at`                                                             |
| GET `/complaints`, POST `/complaints` (+ quality complaints)                                     | complaints tab                                                                  | complaint items (stage, title, text, work id)                                                                                                            |
| POST `/auth/logout`                                                                              | sign out                                                                        | `refresh_token`                                                                                                                                          |

- **UX note:** the client profile is the web client profile folded into five tabs so nothing is more than one tap away; the professional gets a short summary because their tools and account links sit in the drawer.

### `/profile/edit` — `Profilni tahrirlash`

- **Who:** signed-in users (redirects guests to `/auth/login`). Reached from the seller summary's edit button and the drawer's `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` row; a client edits in the sheet on `/profile`. Remote assistance page `client.profile` — the name and e-mail are personal fields on the map (`profile.full_name`, `profile.email`), `Saqlash` is a final control shown to the client only; the photo is never on the map.
- **Purpose:** change name, e-mail and photo.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Profilni tahrirlash`; `QueryBoundary` (skeleton / error / retry) around `ProfileEditForm`: 92 px avatar, masked phone under it, buttons `Rasm tanlash` (camera icon) and, when a photo is set, `Olib tashlash`; `Ism` (placeholder `Ismingiz`); `Email` (placeholder `you@email.com`); gradient `Saqlash` disabled until something changed.
- **Actions:** `Rasm tanlash` → image picker (≤ 15 MB, jpg/png/webp/heic → 512 px JPEG data URL); `Saqlash` → PUT `/clients/me`, toast `Profil saqlandi`, the stored session name follows, `router.back()`.
- **States:** photo errors `Rasm juda katta (15 MB gacha)` / `Faqat JPG, PNG yoki WEBP rasm` / `Rasmni qayta ishlab bo'lmadi`; save errors via the payment-error handler (402 → plan dialog, else toast with the server's text).
- **Data:** GET `/clients/me`, PUT `/clients/me` (`name`, `email`, `avatar_url`) — as above.
- **UX note:** the phone is the login and stays read-only; a full page exists only for the professional and the drawer link.

### `/profile/family` — `Oila a'zolari`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Reached from the drawer (seller `Oila a'zolari` row); the client has the same panel inline on `/profile`.
- **Purpose:** family members who may use the owner's active plan.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Oila a'zolari`, right `+` icon button; tinted note card `Faol tarifingiz bo'lsa, ruxsat berilgan oila a'zolari undan foydalanishi mumkin.`; `PagedList` of `FamilyRow`s (avatar, name, phone, relation pill, `Ruxsat berilgan` / `Ruxsatsiz` state); empty `Oila a'zolari qo'shilmagan` / `Yaqinlaringizni qo'shing — ular tarifingizdan foydalana oladi.`.
- **Actions:** row tap → `ActionList` titled with the name: `Tahrirlash`, `Tarifdan foydalanishga ruxsat berish` / `Ruxsatni olib tashlash`, `O'chirish` (danger, confirm `O'chirishni tasdiqlang` / `{name} oila a'zolaridan o'chiriladi.`). `+` / edit → `FamilySheet` (rose accent): `F.I.Sh.` (placeholder `Ism familiya`), `Telefon` (+998), `Aloqadorlik` (placeholder `Turmush o'rtoq, farzand…`) with chips `Turmush o'rtog'i` / `Farzand` / `Ota-ona` / `Aka-uka / opa-singil` / `Boshqa`, switch `Tarifdan foydalanishga ruxsat` (`Faol tarifingiz bo'lsa, ruxsat berilgan oila a'zosi undan foydalanadi.`), `Saqlash`. Validation: real name, complete Uzbek number, no duplicate (`Bu raqam allaqachon qo'shilgan`).
- **States:** toasts `Oila a'zosi qo'shildi`, `Saqlandi`, `O'chirildi`; share toggle 402 → `Buning uchun avval faol tarif sotib olishingiz kerak.`; other errors via the payment-error handler. Entitlements are invalidated after every write.
- **Data:** GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE `/clients/me/family-members[/{id}]` (`name`, `phone`, `relation`, `shared_access`; res `id`, `created_at`).
- **UX note:** the backend validates nothing here, so the form does; the share toggle is the only write that can be refused for money.

### `/profile/methods` — `To'lov usullari`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Drawer row for sellers; inline panel for clients.
- **Purpose:** saved cards (masked) — add and remove. Only brand, last 4 digits and expiry ever leave the phone.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `To'lov usullari`, right `+`; note `Ilovada faqat karta turi, oxirgi 4 raqam va muddati saqlanadi.`; `PagedList` of `MethodRow` = `CardFace` mini bank card (brand gradient, chip, `•••• •••• •••• 1234`, expiry, `Asosiy` glass pill when default); empty `Saqlangan kartalar yo'q` / `Karta qo'shsangiz, avtomatik uzaytirishda ishlatiladi.`.
- **Actions:** row tap → `ActionList` `{BRAND} •••• 1234` with `O'chirish` (confirm `Kartani o'chirish` / `{card} saqlangan kartalardan o'chiriladi.`). `+` → `MethodSheet` (screen capture blocked while open): live `CardFace` preview, `Karta turi` chips UZCARD / HUMO / VISA / MASTERCARD, `Oxirgi 4 raqam` (hint `Faqat oxirgi 4 raqam. To'liq karta raqamini kiritmang.`), `Amal qilish muddati` MM/YY (error `Muddat noto'g'ri yoki o'tib ketgan`), switch `Asosiy karta`, caption `Karta ma'lumotlari ekranni suratga olishdan himoyalangan.`, `Saqlash`.
- **States:** toasts `Karta qo'shildi`, `O'chirildi`.
- **Data:** GET/POST/DELETE `/clients/me/payment-methods[/{id}]` (`brand`, `last4`, `expires`, `is_default`).
- **UX note:** the card is drawn as a card so the person recognises it; a full number is never asked for.

### `/profile/consents` — `Roziliklar`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Drawer row `Roziliklar` for everyone (client `Qo'shimcha` section, seller account rows).
- **Purpose:** legal documents and the user's answers; read and accept (re-accept a new version). Withdrawal is not possible in the app.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Roziliklar`; tinted header card `Qabul qilish kerak: {n} ta hujjat` (warn) or `Barcha hujjatlar qabul qilingan.`; `PagedList` of `ConsentRowCard`s (file icon, title — Russian title when the locale is ru, subtitle `Versiya {v}` · accepted date, pill `Qabul qilinmagan` / `Qayta qabul qilish kerak` (warn) or `Qabul qilingan` (ok, check)); documents needing action sort first; seller-only slugs (`lawyer_partnership_offer`, `advocate_partnership`, `organization_agreement`) show for a client only if already accepted. Empty `Hujjatlar yo'q`.
- **Actions:** row → `ConsentSheet` (snaps 0.7 / 0.95): title, `Versiya {v} · qabul qilingan: {d}`, the full text (selectable), submit `Qabul qilaman` only when action is needed (confirm `Rozilikni tasdiqlaysizmi?` / `«{title}», versiya {v}.`); otherwise caption `Rozilikni qaytarib olish ilovada mavjud emas — buning uchun qo'llab-quvvatlash xizmatiga murojaat qiling.` + line button `Qo'llab-quvvatlashga yozish` → `/support?new=1`.
- **States:** sheet skeleton / `ErrorView` / `Hujjat matni yuklanmadi.`; toast `Rozilik qabul qilindi`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                           | Used for          | Key fields                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/legal/consents/me`           | list with answers | rows `{ consent{ id, slug, version, title, title_json{ru} }, consent_id, accepted, requires_reaccept, accepted_at }` |
| GET `/legal/consents/{id}`         | full text         | `body` / `content`, `title`, `version`                                                                               |
| POST `/legal/consents/{id}/accept` | accept            | `version`                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** the header card is the to-do count; the sheet never offers a withdraw button because the backend has no such call.

### `/profile/gifts` — `Sovg'alar`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login); `?code=…` (a gift link) opens the claim sheet with the code filled. Client drawer row `Sovg'alar`, seller account row; the client upsell banner's gift variant also lands here.
- **Purpose:** gifts sent and received: give a plan or a service, claim one by code, open one to share its link / QR.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Sovg'alar`; header: `GiftActions` two big tiles — primary gradient `Sovg'a qilish` / `Obuna yoki xizmat`, glass `Kodni kiritish` / `Sovg'a olish`; when there are gifts, `ChipBar` `Hammasi` / `Yuborilgan` / `Olingan` with counts; flat `PagedList` of `GiftRowCard`s (tile blue = sent, green = received; title or `Obuna sovg'asi`; `Tarif`/`Xizmat` · recipient phone or `Sizga sovg'a` · `{n} oy`; work id · date; status pill `Kutilmoqda` / `Faollashtirilmagan` / `Faollashtirilgan` / `Yuborilgan` / `To'langan` / `To'lanmagan` / `Muddati o'tgan` / `Bekor qilingan`); empty `Hozircha sovg'a yo'q` / `Yuborgan yoki olgan sovg'alaringiz shu yerda ko'rinadi.`.
- **Actions:** `Sovg'a qilish` → `GiftSheet` (`Obuna sovg'a qilish` / `Qabul qiluvchiga havola va QR kod beriladi.`): segmented `Tarif` / `Xizmat`; plan: select `Tarif` (giftable plans), `Muddat` (`{n} oy` as the plan allows), `Taxminiy narx: {sum}. Yakuniy summani server hisoblaydi.`, or `Hozircha sovg'a qilish mumkin bo'lgan tarif yo'q.`; service: `Xizmat turi` search (`Xizmat nomini yozing (kamida 2 harf)`, 6 results); `Qabul qiluvchi` (`Ism yoki eslatma (ixtiyoriy)`), `Telefon (ixtiyoriy)`, `Xabar` (`Qabul qiluvchiga ixtiyoriy xabar`, 300); submit `Sovg'a yuborish` → POST `/gifts` trying the provider ladder (a 503 / "provider" 400 skips to the next provider), then the share sheet opens with the payment on top. `Kodni kiritish` → `ClaimSheet` (`Sovg'ani olish` / `Sovg'a kodini kiriting — obuna muddati boshlanadi.`, field `Sovg'a kodi` placeholder `LX-12345678`, `Faollashtirish`) → POST `/gifts/{code}/claim` → `Tabriklaymiz!` / `Obunangiz faollashtirildi. Tugash sanasi: {to}.` + `Tayyor`. Row tap → `GiftShareSheet`: fresh gift title `Sovg'angiz tayyor!` + hint `Havola yoki QR kodni qabul qiluvchiga yuboring — ro'yxatdan o'tganda muddat boshlanadi.`, `PaymentPendingCard` (fresh) or status pill, QR on a white tile, dashed code ticket `Sovg'a kodi` (tap = copy, `Nusxalash uchun bosing`), `Ulashish` (system share) and `Nusxalash` (link), `Tayyor`.
- **States:** claim error → server detail or `Sovg'ani olib bo'lmadi. Kod noto'g'ri yoki muddati o'tgan bo'lishi mumkin.`; create errors via the payment-error handler (`To'lov tizimi hozircha ulanmagan. Keyinroq urinib ko'ring.` when every provider is down). Payments list and entitlements are refreshed after a gift / claim.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                        | Used for                                    | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| GET `/gifts`                                                    | list                                        | `id`, `work_id`, `direction` (sent/received), `recipient_phone`, `plan_name` / `service_name`, `term_months`/`duration_months`, `share_url`, `qr_url`, `gift_code`/`code` (or parsed from `share_url`), `status`, `created_at` |
| POST `/gifts`                                                   | create + its payment                        | req `provider`, `term_months`, `duration_months`, `recipient_hint`, `recipient_phone`, `message`, `plan_slug` or `service_id`; res `id`, `work_id`, `status`, `share_url`, `qr_url`, `payment{ id, payment_url, status }`      |
| POST `/gifts/{code}/claim`                                      | claim                                       | res `status`, `starts_at`, `ends_at`                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| GET `/gifts/{code}/qr`                                          | QR image (public PNG)                       | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET `/subscription-plans` (giftable), GET `/services/search?q=` | plan / service pickers                      | plan `slug`, `name`, `is_giftable`, allowed months; service `id`, `name`                                                                                                                                                       |
| GET `/payments`                                                 | the pending-payment card in the share sheet | see `/payments`                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

- **UX note:** the gift is a ticket: QR, code and two short actions; the price shown is an estimate and the backend's payment status is the only truth.

### `/profile/referral` — `Referral`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Client drawer row `Do'stni taklif qilish`; the seller has `/pro/referrals` instead (its own page).
- **Purpose:** invite code + share + QR, progress to the discount, how it works, who joined.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Referral`; pull-to-refresh `PayScroll`: `ReferralHero` (dark gradient, gift badge, `Do'stni taklif qiling`, `Har bir do'stingiz uchun bonus. 5 ta do'st obuna bo'lsa, obunangizga chegirma.`, dashed `ReferralCode` ticket with a round copy button, `ReferralQr` white tile + hint `Skanerlang yoki do'stingizga ko'rsating`, buttons `Ulashish` (gradient) and `Nusxalash` (glass)); `ReferralStats` card with three tiles `Taklif qilingan` / `Qo'shilgan` / `Bonus` (UZS); `ReferralJourney` card `Referral chegirmasi` — a dot per friend needed (`eligible_after`, default 5), gift at the end, text `{pct}% chegirma faol — obunangizga avtomatik qo'llanadi.` or `Yana {n} ta do'st obuna bo'lsa, {pct}% chegirma ochiladi.`, caption `Qo'llanadi: obuna, buyurtma komissiyasi`; `ReferralSteps` `Qanday ishlaydi` (1 `Havolangizni do'stlaringizga yuboring.`, 2 `Ular ro'yxatdan o'tib, birinchi xizmatdan foydalanadi.`, 3 `Ikkalangiz ham bonus olasiz.`); heading `Takliflaringiz` with `RowCard`s (name/phone, status `Taklif qilingan` / `Qo'shilgan` / `Bonus berilgan`, date, `+{reward}`) or `Hali hech kim taklif qilinmagan.`.
- **Actions:** copy code / link → clipboard + `Nusxalandi`; `Ulashish` → system share with `LexGo — ishonchli yuridik yordam. Mening havolam orqali qo'shiling: {link}`.
- **States:** `QueryBoundary` skeleton / error; pull to refresh.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                   | Used for      | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/referrals/me`        | everything    | `code`, `link`/`register_url`/`share_url`, `qr_url`, `invited_count`, `joined_count`, `reward_balance`/`balance`, `discount_unlocked`, `discount_percent`, `eligible_after`, `remaining_to_unlock`, `applies_to[]`, `items[]`/`referrals[]{ name, phone, status, reward, joined_at }` |
| GET `/referrals/{code}/qr` | public QR PNG | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

- **UX note:** the QR sits on a white tile so it scans on the dark hero; progress is drawn as steps, not a percentage.

---

## Payments

### `/payments` — `To'lovlar`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Clients: history only. Sellers: a segmented switch `To'lovlar` / `Daromadim` — the second tab is the read-only payout ledger.
- **Purpose:** payment history with receipts; a seller's payouts beside it.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `To'lovlar`; seller only: `Segmented` `To'lovlar` / `Daromadim`.
  **`PaymentHistory`** (flat `PagedList`): header `PaymentWallet` (blue gradient card: `Jami to'langan` big amount, three tappable counters `To'lovlar` / `Kutilmoqda` (warm tint when > 0) / `Qaytarilgan` that set the status filter) + `PaymentFilters` (search `Qidirish (xizmat, holat, summa)…` + sliders button with a dot when a filter is on; applied filters as removable chips); rows grouped under `MonthHead` (`Oktabr 2026`), each `PaymentRowCard`: status-tinted tile, title (`py1.kind.*`, e.g. `Xizmat buyurtmasi`, `Obuna`, `Hujjat namunasi`, `Qo'ng'iroqni uzaytirish`), `date · work id`, amount, `PaymentStatusPill` (`Kutilmoqda` / `Tasdiq kutilmoqda` / `To'langan` / `Qaytarilgan` / `Bekor qilingan` / `Muvaffaqiyatsiz` …). Filter sheet `Filtrlar` (snaps 0.8/0.94): `Holat` options `Hammasi` / `Kutilmoqda` / `To'langan` / `Bekor / xato` / `Qaytarilgan` with colour dots and live counts, `Davr` period picker (custom reveals two dates), pinned bar `Filtrlarni tozalash` + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N`.
  **`PayoutsList`** (seller `Daromadim`): note `Faqat ko'rish: to'lovlarni platforma hisoblaydi.`, `RowCard`s amount / period / date / status pill; empty `Hozircha to'lov yo'q` / `Mutaxassis sifatidagi to'lovlaringiz shu yerda ko'rinadi.`.
- **Actions:** row → `/payments/{id}`; wallet counters and sheet → filters; chips `×` → remove.
- **States:** skeleton; empty `Hozircha to'lov yo'q` / `To'lovlar va cheklar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; filtered empty `Mos to'lov topilmadi` / `Filtrni o'zgartiring.`; error view with retry; list refetches on user-socket payment / subscription / gift / order events (`usePaymentSync`).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                            | Used for                                                                                                          | Key fields                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/payments`                     | history (whole list, newest first; one payment is read out of it)                                                 | `id`, `work_id`, `amount`, `currency`, `status`, `method`/`provider`, `kind`/`target_type`, `description`, `order_id`, `created_at`, `receipt_url` |
| GET `/payouts/me` (seller tab only) | payouts                                                                                                           | `id`, `amount`/`seller_share`, `currency`, `status`, `period`/`month`, `created_at`                                                                |
| user socket `/ws/users/me`          | refresh on `payment.*`, `subscription.*`, `gift.*`, `order.*`, `document_request.payment`, `notification.created` | —                                                                                                                                                  |

- **UX note:** status and period live in one sheet; the wallet card doubles as the quickest filter.

### `/payments/[id]` — payment title (e.g. `Xizmat buyurtmasi`) / fallback `To'lov`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login); any role, its own payments.
- **Purpose:** one payment: state, facts, receipt, where the money went, refund request.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` titled by the payment kind; pull-to-refresh: `PaymentHero` tinted card (kind, big amount, status pill, date-time); `PaymentPendingCard` while the phase is `awaiting` or `pending_confirmation` (titles `To'lov kutilmoqda` / `Tasdiq kutilmoqda`, texts `To'lovni provayder sahifasida yakunlang…` / `So'rov operatorga Telegram orqali yuborildi…`, buttons `To'lov sahifasini ochish` (awaiting with a URL), `Holatni tekshirish`; polls every 15 s only while the user socket is down; paid → `To'lov qabul qilindi` + `Chekni ko'rish`); `SectionCard` `Ma'lumotlar` with `Nima uchun`, `To'lov usuli` (`Payme`, `Click`, `ATMOS`, `Telegram orqali tasdiqlash`, …), `Raqami`; `ReceiptSection` `Chek`: verification pill `Chek haqiqiy` / `Chek tasdiqlanmadi`, `To'langan summa`, button `Chekni ochish (PDF)` (in-app `FilePreview` of the authed PDF); `SplitSection` `Pul qanday taqsimlandi` (paid only; hidden when the provider has no split): `Umumiy summa`, `Platforma ulushi`, `Provayder komissiyasi`, `Mutaxassis ulushi`; paid only: line button `Qaytarishni so'rash` → `RefundSheet` (`Qaytarish so'rovi`, amount, `Sabab` textarea placeholder `Nima uchun pulni qaytarishni so'rayapsiz?`, hint `Kamida 10 ta belgi. So'rovni call-center ko'rib chiqadi.`, `So'rov yuborish`).
- **Actions:** open provider page (in-app browser, https only; the list is re-read when the user returns); open receipt; request refund → POST `/refund-requests` → toast `Qaytarish so'rovi yuborildi ({id})`.
- **States:** skeleton; `ErrorView`; not in the list → `To'lov topilmadi. Ro'yxatni yangilab ko'ring.`; phases `failed` (`To'lov amalga oshmadi`), `cancelled` (`To'lov bekor qilindi`), `refunded` (`To'lov qaytarildi`).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                               | Used for                         | Key fields                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/payments`                                        | the payment (no detail endpoint) | as above; phase from `status` + `method` (`telegram_manual` pending = `pending_confirmation`)                    |
| GET `/payments/{id}/receipt/verify`                    | receipt check                    | `valid`, `status`, `amount`, `paid_amount`, `currency`, `provider`                                               |
| GET `/payments/{id}/receipt`                           | PDF (authed, previewer)          | —                                                                                                                |
| GET `/payments/{id}/split` (paid only; 400/503 = none) | split                            | `gross_amount`, `platform_fee`, `provider_fee`, `seller_share`, `currency`                                       |
| POST `/refund-requests`                                | refund request                   | req `record_type: "refund"`, `title`, `price`, `currency: "UZS"`, `payload{ payment_id, reason }`; res `work_id` |

- **UX note:** the app never marks a payment paid; the pending card only watches the backend (socket, then a slow poll) and offers the provider page.
