## Security and settings

### `/security` — `Xavfsizlik`

- **Who:** every signed-in role (guest → login). Drawer `Xavfsizlik` row (settings section); client also has the same cards in the `Xavfsizlik` profile tab. Never opened by remote assistance.
- **Purpose:** 2FA, Telegram link, app lock, notification preferences, signed-in devices and the audit journal.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Xavfsizlik`; `Segmented` `Himoya` / `Sessiyalar` / `Jurnal`.
  - **`Himoya` (`ProtectionTab`, pull to refresh):**
    1. `TwoFactorCard` `Ikki bosqichli himoya (2FA)` — `Har kirishda Telegram botga yoki Authenticator ilovaga tasdiq kodi keladi.`; `TwoFactorStatus` box: on → `Telegram kodi` / `SMS kodi` / `Authenticator ilova` with text (`Har kirishda 6 xonali kod LexGo Telegram botiga yuboriladi.` / `Kod Authenticator ilovasida har 30 soniyada yangilanadi.`) and badge `Yoqilgan`; off → `O'chirilgan` / `Hisobingiz hozircha faqat parol bilan himoyalangan.`. Mandatory roles (every non-client; a client holding a staff role) see `Sizning rolingiz uchun ikki bosqichli himoya majburiy — uni o'chirib bo'lmaydi.` (or `… uni hozir yoqing.`). Buttons: off → gradient `Authenticator ilova` (→ `/security/totp`) + line `Telegram / SMS kodi`; on + mandatory + not totp → soft `Authenticator ilovaga o'tish`; on + optional → line `O'chirish`.
       `CodeSheet` (violet): `Tasdiq kodi` / `6 xonali kod Telegram botga (ulanmagan bo'lsa — SMS orqali) yuborildi`, code slots, expiry / `Kodni qayta yuborish`, submit `Tasdiqlash`.
    2. `TelegramCard` `Telegram hisobi` — `Hisobingizni LexGo Telegram botiga ulang — bildirishnomalar Telegramga ham keladi.`; linked → ok box `Telegram ulangan` / `Bildirishnomalar Telegram orqali ham yuboriladi.`; unknown → `Ulanish holati shu yerda ko'rinmasligi mumkin — botda Start bosing.`; button `Telegramni ulash` (gradient) or `Boshqa Telegram hisobini ulash` (line). `TelegramSheet` (`Telegramni ulash`): one-time link field `Bir martalik havola` with a share icon (share sheet, never the clipboard), `Telegram boshqa qurilmada bo'lsa, havoladan nusxa olib, o'sha yerda oching.`, `Havola amal qiladi: {time}` (10 min), `Telegramdan tasdiq kutilmoqda…`; submit `Telegramda ochish` or, expired, `Yangi havola olish`; secondary `Bekor qilish` / `Tayyor`.
    3. `AppLockCard` `Ilovani qulflash` — row `Biometrik qulf` with `Yoqilgan` / `O'chirilgan` → `/settings/security-lock`.
    4. `NotifPrefsCard` `Xabarnoma sozlamalari` — kicker `Kanallar`: `Push`, `SMS`, `Telegram` (hint `Telegram ulanmagan — yuqorida ulang` when not linked), `Email`; kicker `Mavzular`: `Buyurtmalar`, `To'lovlar`, `Xabarlar`, `Marketing`; caption `SMS faqat muhim hodisalar uchun zaxira kanal sifatida ishlatiladi.`. Each switch saves on its own (optimistic, reverts with the server's message).
  - **`Sessiyalar` (`SessionsTab`):** lead `Hisobingizga kirgan qurilmalar. Notanish qurilmani chiqarib yuboring. Faol sessiyalar: {n}`; `SessionRow`s 20 at a time, this device first (found once via `identifyDevice`), each with `Chiqarish` (confirm `Sessiya tugatilsinmi?` / `Bu qurilma hisobdan chiqariladi va qayta kirishi kerak bo'ladi.` or, for this device, `Bu sizning joriy qurilmangiz — hisobdan chiqasiz.`); empty `Faol sessiya yo'q` / `Hozircha hech qaysi qurilma kirmagan.`.
  - **`Jurnal` (`HistoryTab`):** `JournalControls` — search `Jurnaldan qidirish`, `Filtr` button (dot + `Filtr, tanlangan: {n}`), `{n} ta yozuv`, selected filters as removable chips; day-grouped timeline (`Bugun`, `Kecha`, dates) of `JournalRow`s (round kind tile, bold title, subtitle device/detail · IP, time, red chip `Xato` / `Shubhali`), 10 at a time with `Yana {n} ta` + `{shown} / {total}`; pull to refresh. `JournalFilterSheet` (`Filtr`): `Turi` 2-column tiles `Hammasi`, `Kirish`, `Himoya`, `Buyurtma`, `Hujjat`, `Profil`, `Ogohlantirish`, `Boshqa` with counts; `Davr` segmented `Hammasi` / `Bugun` / `7 kun` / `30 kun`; bar `Tozalash` + `Ko'rsatish · {n}`. `JournalEntrySheet`: kind tile + result pill (`Muvaffaqiyatli` / `Muvaffaqiyatsiz` / `Shubhali`), facts `Vaqt`, `Turi`, `Qurilma`, `IP manzil` or `Oldingi IP → IP`, `Natija`, `Izoh`; `Yopish`.
- **Actions:** as above; 2FA disable → confirm `2FA o'chirilsinmi?` / `Hisobingiz faqat parol bilan himoyalanadi.` → DELETE `/auth/2fa` (403 → `Sizning rolingiz uchun … o'chirib bo'lmaydi.`); revoke this device → the app signs out; revoke another → GET `/auth/me` probe.
- **States:** toasts `2FA yoqildi`, `2FA o'chirildi`, `Telegram hisobingiz ulandi`, `Sessiya tugatildi`; errors `Amalni bajarib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`, `Kod noto'g'ri yoki muddati o'tgan. Qayta urining.`, `Telegram bot hozircha ulanmagan. Keyinroq urinib ko'ring.` (503), `Havolani olib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; journal empty `Jurnal bo'sh` / `Hisobdagi harakatlar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`, no match `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Boshqa filtr yoki so'z sinab ko'ring.` + `Filtrni tozalash`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                           | Used for                                                                   | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/auth/me`                                                     | 2FA state, Telegram link; polled every 5 s while the Telegram link is open | `two_factor_enabled`/`twofa_enabled`/`two_factor{enabled,method}`, `two_factor_method`, `telegram_linked`/`is_telegram_linked`/`telegram_connected`/`telegram{linked}`/`telegram_chat_id` |
| POST `/auth/2fa/start`                                             | code to the bot / SMS                                                      | res `verification_id`, `expires_at`                                                                                                                                                       |
| POST `/auth/2fa/verify`                                            | enable                                                                     | req `verification_id`, `code`                                                                                                                                                             |
| DELETE `/auth/2fa`                                                 | disable (403 for mandatory roles)                                          | —                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| POST `/telegram/link/start`                                        | one-time t.me link                                                         | res `share_url` (or `deep_link`/`url`/…; or `bot_username` + `token`), `expires_at`, `linked`/`already_linked`                                                                            |
| GET `/notifications/preferences`, PUT `/notifications/preferences` | channel / topic switches                                                   | flags `push`, `sms`, `telegram`, `email`, `orders`, `payments`, `messages`, `marketing` (flat or under `channels` / `events`); PUT sends the one key toggled                              |
| GET `/auth/sessions`, DELETE `/auth/sessions/{id}`                 | devices                                                                    | `id`, `device_label`/`device`, `ip`/`ip_address`, `last_used_at`/`last_active`, `created_at`, `expires_at`, `current`/`is_current`/`this_device`                                          |
| GET `/users/me/activity`                                           | audit log (whole, sorted newest first)                                     | `id`, `action`/`type`, `detail`/`description`/`message`, `title_uz`, `description_uz`, `ip`, `outcome`, `created_at`/`timestamp`                                                          |
| GET `/auth/security-events` (optional; failure = none)             | events merged into the journal                                             | `id`, `type`/`action`, `title`/`detail`, `status`, `payload{ previous_ip, current_ip, user_agent }`, `created_at`                                                                         |

- **UX note:** the authenticator setup is a page of its own so its QR and key can be protected from screen capture; the journal merges the audit log and security events into one timeline.

### `/security/totp` — `Authenticator ulash`

- **Who:** every signed-in role (guest → login). From the 2FA card (`Authenticator ilova` / `Authenticator ilovaga o'tish`).
- **Purpose:** connect an authenticator app; 2FA counts as enabled only after the server accepted the first code.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Authenticator ulash`; `SecretGuard` (screen capture blocked, app-switcher preview blank); text `QR kodni Google Authenticator, Authy yoki shunga o'xshash ilovada skanerlang, so'ng ilovadagi 6 xonali kodni kiriting.`; QR on a white tile (or `QR kodni ko'rsatib bo'lmadi — kalitni qo'lda kiriting.`); `TotpSecret`: soft `Authenticator ilovada ochish` (otpauth:// link; if no app `Authenticator ilova topilmadi — kalitni qo'lda kiriting.`), key box `Yoki kalitni qo'lda kiriting` masked `•••• •••• •••• ••••` with an eye toggle (`Kalitni ko'rsatish` / `Kalitni yashirish`; never copied to the clipboard); label `Ilovadagi 6 xonali kod` + code slots; error line; caption `Bu ekran skrinshot va yozib olishdan himoyalangan.`; gradient `Tasdiqlash`.
- **Actions:** the secret is requested when the page opens; verify → POST `/auth/2fa/totp/enable` → profile re-read, toast `2FA yoqildi`, back to `/security`.
- **States:** skeleton while the secret loads; `ErrorView` with retry when the setup is empty; wrong code `Kod noto'g'ri yoki muddati o'tgan. Qayta urining.`.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                     | Used for               | Key fields                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| POST `/auth/2fa/totp/setup`  | secret + QR            | res `setup_id`/`id`/`verification_id`, `secret`, `otpauth_url`/`provisioning_uri`, `qr_code` (data:image PNG / base64 / https) |
| POST `/auth/2fa/totp/enable` | first code             | req `setup_id`, `code`                                                                                                         |
| GET `/auth/me`               | re-read after enabling | —                                                                                                                              |

- **UX note:** a phone cannot scan its own screen, so the otpauth link and the typed key are first-class, not fallbacks hidden in a footnote.

### `/settings/notifications` — `Bildirishnoma sozlamalari`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Drawer settings row `Bildirishnoma sozlamalari` (every role).
- **Purpose:** OS permission, server-side channel / topic switches, on-device alert behaviour, remote-push status.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Bildirishnoma sozlamalari`, subtitle `Kanallar, mavzular va shu qurilma`.
  1. `PushPermissionCard` (always shown here): granted → green row `Bildirishnomalar yoqilgan` / `Ilova orqa fonda bo'lsa ham yangi voqealar haqida ogohlantiramiz.`; not granted → sky gradient card with a swinging bell, `Bildirishnomalarni yoqing` / `Yangi xabar, qo'ng'iroq va to'lov holatidan darhol xabardor bo'lasiz.`, white button `Yoqish`; blocked by the OS → amber card `Bildirishnomalar o'chirilgan` / `Ularni telefon sozlamalarida yoqing.`, button `Sozlamalarni ochish`. (On other screens the card also has `Keyinroq`, hiding it 3 days.)
  2. `ServerPrefs` `Kanal va mavzular`: `Kanallar` switches `Push`, `SMS` (hint `SMS faqat muhim voqealar uchun zaxira kanal.`), `Telegram` (hint `Telegram ulanmagan: profil xavfsizligida ulang.` when not linked), `Email`; `Mavzular`: `Buyurtmalar`, `To'lovlar`, `Xabarlar va qo'ng'iroqlar`, `Takliflar va yangiliklar`; caption `Ilova ichidagi bildirishnomalar doim yoqilgan.`. Each switch sends the whole set at once; offline it is queued (`Oflayn: internet qaytganda saqlanadi`).
  3. `DevicePrefs` `Shu qurilmada` (local, not on the server): `Ilova ichida banner` (`Ilova ochiq paytda yangi voqea tepada ko'rinadi.`), `Orqa fonda ogohlantirish` (`Ilova yopilganda ham vaqti-vaqti bilan yangi voqealarni tekshiradi.`), `Ovoz`, `Tebranish`; polling status caption `Orqa fon tekshiruvi faol` / `… o'chiq` / `… telefon cheklagan (quvvat tejash).`; `TestAlertButton` `Sinov bildirishnomasi` (schedules a local alert in 5 s: `LexGo sinov bildirishnomasi` / `Bildirishnomalar shu qurilmada ishlayapti.`; toast `Sinov bildirishnomasi 5 soniyadan so'ng keladi. Ilovani orqa fonga o'tkazib ko'ring.` or `Bildirishnomalarga ruxsat yo'q`).
  4. `RemotePushInfo` `Masofaviy push`: pill `Yoqilgan` / `Kutilmoqda` / `Hozircha yo'q` with the matching text (`Bildirishnomalar yoqilgan: qo'ng'iroq va vazifalar ilova yopiq bo'lsa ham shu telefonga yetib keladi.` / `Qurilma hali serverda ro'yxatdan o'tmadi (internet yoki shu yig'ilmada push xizmati yo'q). …` / `Bildirishnomaga ruxsat berilmagan, …`).
- **Actions:** as above; `Yoqish` asks the OS; `Sozlamalarni ochish` opens the app's system settings.
- **States:** server prefs skeleton / `ErrorView`; save failure `Saqlab bo'lmadi` (reverts the switch).
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                    | Used for                          | Key fields                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/notifications/preferences`            | switches                          | `push`, `sms`, `telegram`, `email`, `orders`, `payments`, `messages`, `marketing` (defaults push/sms/orders/payments/messages on) |
| PUT `/notifications/preferences`            | save (whole set; queued offline)  | same keys                                                                                                                         |
| POST `/devices` (elsewhere, `usePushToken`) | the "registered" state shown here | `platform`, `token_type` (`fcm` / `apns`), `token`, `installation_id`, `app_version`, `locale`, `environment`                     |

- **UX note:** the page is honest about remote push: "on" only once the backend holds this phone's token; otherwise it says that alerts still come over the live connection and the periodic check.

### `/settings/security-lock` — `Ilovani qulflash`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). From the `Himoya` tab's app-lock row and the drawer settings row `Ilova qulfi (Face ID)`.
- **Purpose:** lock the app with Face ID / fingerprint (device passcode as fallback) and choose the grace period.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Ilovani qulflash`; pull to refresh re-reads the biometric info. `LockToggleCard` `Ilovani qulflash`: description `Yoqilsa, ilova ochilganda va fonda biroz turgandan keyin Face ID yoki barmoq izi talab qilinadi. Kirish ma'lumotlaringiz qurilmada saqlanadi, tizimga kirish uchun baribir parol/2FA kerak.`; switch `Biometrik qulf` with hint = what the device offers (`Face ID` / `Barmoq izi` / `Ko'z qorachig'i` joined, `Faqat qurilma parolidan foydalaniladi`, or `Bu qurilmada biometriya yoki ekran qulfi sozlanmagan`); button `Qurilma sozlamalarini ochish` when nothing can lock; `Qulfni sinab ko'rish` when enabled. `LockTimeoutCard` (enabled only) `Qachon qulflansin`: select `Darhol` / `1 daqiqa` / `5 daqiqa` / `15 daqiqa`; caption `Fonda shuncha vaqt turgandan keyin ilova qayta ochilganda qulf so'raladi.`.
- **Actions:** switching on or off both require a passed system prompt (`Qulfni yoqish uchun tasdiqlang` / `Qulfni o'chirish uchun tasdiqlang`) → toasts `Ilova qulfi yoqildi.` / `Ilova qulfi o'chirildi.`; test → `Tasdiqlandi.`. The lock itself (`AppLock` modal, mounted app-wide): dark `LockScreen` with the logo, `LexGo qulflangan`, `Davom etish uchun o'zingizni tasdiqlang.`, result line (`Juda ko'p urinish. Qurilma biroz kutishni so'radi.` / `Biometriya mavjud emas.` / `Tasdiqlanmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`), gradient `Qulfni ochish`, glass `Chiqish va parol bilan kirish` (signs out). It prompts by itself 450 ms after it comes up, raises on cold start and after `timeoutMin` in the background (0 = every time), blurs the iOS app-switcher snapshot while enabled, and is suppressed while another account's lock would apply (sign-in of a different user disables the old lock).
- **States:** skeleton while reading biometrics; `Avval qurilmada ekran qulfi yoki biometriyani sozlang.` when switching on without hardware; `Tasdiqlanmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` on a failed prompt.
- **Data:** none (local: `useLockStore` enabled / owner / timeout, SecureStore tokens).
- **UX note:** biometrics only reopen the saved session; "sign out" is the deliberate way back to the password, and a borrowed unlocked phone cannot turn the lock off without the prompt.

### `/settings/storage` — `Xotira va kesh`

- **Who:** anyone (no auth guard). Drawer settings row `Xotira va kesh`.
- **Purpose:** what the app keeps on the phone, free it, unsent offline writes, link status, anonymous diagnostics.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Xotira va kesh`, subtitle `Telefondagi nusxalar va yuborilmagan yozuvlar`.
  1. `SizeCard` `Band joy`: `Saqlangan yozuvlar`, `Ma'lumotlar hajmi`, `Baza fayli`, `Yuklangan fayllar`, `Jami` (skeleton until read; never persisted).
  2. `ClearCard` `Joy bo'shatish`: `Bular faqat nusxalar: serverdagi ma'lumotlar o'zgarmaydi va kerak bo'lganda qayta yuklanadi.`; buttons `Saqlangan ma'lumotlarni tozalash` (confirm `Saqlangan ma'lumotlar tozalansinmi?` / `Ro'yxatlar va qoralamalar qurilmadan o'chadi.`) and `Yuklangan fayllarni tozalash` (`Yuklangan fayllar tozalansinmi?` / `Ochilgan hujjatlar qurilmadan o'chadi va kerak bo'lsa qayta yuklanadi.`), confirm label `Tozalash`, done toasts `Saqlangan ma'lumotlar tozalandi` / `Yuklangan fayllar tozalandi`.
  3. `OutboxCard` `Yuborilmagan yozuvlar`: `Yuborilmagan yozuv yo'q.` or `{n} ta yozuv internet qaytishini kutmoqda.` + `Hozir yuborish` (disabled offline; toast `{n} ta yozuv yuborildi`) and `Bekor qilish` (confirm `Yuborilmagan yozuvlar bekor qilinsinmi?` / `Ular serverga yuborilmaydi va qaytarib bo'lmaydi.`).
  4. `ConnectionCard` `Ulanish holati`: facts `Tarmoq` (`Wi-Fi` / `Mobil internet` / `Ulangan` / `Internet yo'q` / `LexGo uchun internet o'chiq`), `Sabab` (`Telefonda tarmoq yo'q` / `Telefon LexGo'ga internetni bermayapti`), `Server` (`Ishlayapti` / `Sekin` / `Javob bermayapti`), `Jonli ulanish` (`Ulangan` / `Ulanmoqda…` / `Uzilgan`), `Tafsilot: …` (the OS's own words); buttons `Qayta tekshirish`, `Jurnalni ulashish` (shares the connection journal text).
  5. `DiagnosticsCard` `Anonim statistika`: switch `Statistika yig'ish` (`Faqat ekran nomlari, vaqt oralig'i va xato turi. Telefon, ism, matn, kod va havolalar hech qachon yig'ilmaydi.`), `Qurilmada {n} ta hodisa`, `Tozalash` → `Statistika tozalandi`. There is no sink yet: events only fill a local buffer.
- **Actions:** as above.
- **States:** none beyond the texts; the page works offline.
- **Data:** none (SQLite cache stats, file dir size, outbox count, net store, socket state).
- **UX note:** the connection card is the support tool: it names the three links separately and tells the person when it is the phone that denies the app its network.

---

## Notifications

### `/notifications` — `Bildirishnomalar`

- **Who:** signed-in users (guest → login). Reached from the header bell (every tab page), the drawer `Bildirishnomalar` row (client, seller), a notification tap that resolves to nothing. Same body is reused as a staff tab (not served by this app).
- **Purpose:** the inbox of events, folded one per event, with category filters, search and a detail drawer that deep-links into the app.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** stack `Header` title `Bildirishnomalar`, right: check-circle icon (mark all / shown read) when something unread is shown. `FlashList`:
  1. `PushPermissionCard` (not granted and not snoozed).
  2. `NotificationFilters`: summary line `{n} ta o'qilmagan` / `Hammasi o'qilgan`; `SearchBox` `Sarlavha yoki matn bo'yicha qidirish`; `ChipBar` `Barchasi` (count) + categories `Buyurtmalar`, `To'lovlar`, `Chat va qo'ng'iroqlar`, `Hujjatlar`, `Tizim`, `Takliflar` with their unread counts; `Segmented` `Hammasi` / `O'qilmaganlar` (eye-off) / `O'qilganlar` (check).
  3. Day headings `BUGUN` / `KECHA` / `OLDINROQ` (uppercased) over `NotificationRow`s (category icon/colour: orders blue shopping-bag, payments green credit-card, chat cyan message-circle, documents violet file-text, system slate bell, marketing amber gift; title/body from `nt5`/`nt6` templates or the backend text with ids replaced by public numbers; unread marker; an `Ochish` action when the event has a screen).
  4. Footer `Yana yuklash` while more pages exist (also auto-loads on scroll; stops at 800 rows); filters that leave fewer than 10 rows fetch older pages by themselves.
- **Actions:** row → `NotificationSheet` (marks read): category badge, pills (`Buyurtma` / `To'lov` / `Xabar` / `Hujjat` / `Tizim` / `Taklif`, `Yangi`), date · time, title, body, `NotifFacts` rows (`Holat` as an order-status pill, `Raqam`, `Narx` / `Summa` in `so'm`, `Sahifalar`, `Fayl`, `Advokat`; never raw ids), `Yetkazilgan kanallar` chips, and — when the viewer's role has a screen for it — a gradient button `… ochish` (`Buyurtmani ochish`, `To'lovni ochish`, `Suhbatni ochish`, `Hujjatni ochish`, `Qo'ng'iroqqa qo'shilish`, `Murojaatni ochish`, `Tezkor so'rovni ochish`, `Obunani ochish`, `Sovg'alarni ochish`, `Takliflarni ochish`, `Xavfsizlikni ochish`, …) that closes the sheet and opens the target (role-aware `openLink`). Header check → POST `/notifications/read-all` (unfiltered) or per-row reads for the shown ones (`Ko'rsatilganlarni o'qilgan`). Pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton rows (7); offline without data `Internet yo'q` / `Ulanish tiklangach bildirishnomalar yuklanadi.`; error view; filtered empty `Mos bildirishnoma topilmadi` / `Boshqa bo'limni tanlang yoki qidiruvni tozalang.`; empty `Bildirishnomalar yo'q` / `Ishlaringiz va to'lovlaringiz haqidagi yangiliklar shu yerda ko'rinadi.`; mark-read failure `O'qilgan deb belgilab bo'lmadi` (cache refetched). Live: `notification.created` / `notification.read` / `notifications.read_all` / `notifications.unread_count` on the user socket update the list and the unread index; without the socket the list polls every 30 s.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                               | Used for                                        | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/notifications?limit=100&offset=`                                                 | pages of per-channel rows, folded on the device | `id`, `kind`/`type` (channel or event), `channel`, `title`, `body`/`message`, `category`, `data`/`meta`/`metadata{ event, order_id, work_id, status, price, amount, page_count, main_file_name, lawyer_name, … }`, `entity_type`, `entity_id`, `deep_link`, `read`/`is_read`/`read_at`, `created_at` |
| GET `/notifications?unread_only=true…` (up to 3 pages)                                 | unread index (badge = folded events)            | same                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| GET `/notifications/unread-count`                                                      | cheap gate / drift check                        | `count` (per-channel rows)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| POST `/notifications/{id}/read` (per row of the event), POST `/notifications/read-all` | mark read                                       | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GET `/marketplace/me/orders` / `/orders`                                               | public work number for an order id in the sheet | `id`, `work_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| user socket                                                                            | live updates (events above)                     | `notification{…}`, `notification_id`, `unread_count`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

- **UX note:** the backend stores one row per delivery channel and category "system" for everything, so the app folds rows into events and derives the category itself; the list is the truth for the badge, not the server count.
