## Support

### `/support` — `Yordam markazi`

- **Who:** clients and sellers; a guest sees the `SignInGate` (`Kirish kerak` / `Yordam markazi hisobingiz bilan ishlaydi. Davom etish uchun tizimga kiring.` / `Kirish`). Entry points: header headset → `/support` (operator card) or `/support?new=1` (the headset sheet's `Operatorga ulanish`), drawer `Yordam markazi` / `Support` row (badge = unread operator messages), consent sheet, closed-chat banner (`?new=1&force=1`), `/support/new`. Remote assistance page `client.support`: control `support.new_chat` (`Yangi murojaat`) opens the window; its fields are on the map too. Query `?new=1&topic=…&q=…&force=1` opens the new-chat window once per distinct set (closing clears the params).
- **Purpose:** the help hub in the web order: hero + hours, ongoing chats, AI first, operator, other ways, then my chats.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title `Yordam markazi`; `PagedList` header `SupportHome`:
  1. `SupportHero` navy gradient: life-buoy badge, `Qanday yordam kerak?`, `AI instruktor darhol javob beradi, operator esa ish vaqtida chatda yordam beradi.`, `BusinessHoursBadge`.
  2. `OngoingSection` (when there are active chats): `Davom etayotgan suhbatlar ({n}+)` + `Barchasi`, up to three `TicketRow`s.
  3. `AiAskCard` violet→blue: tag `Onlayn — kun-u tun`, `AI instruktor`, `Savolingizga darhol javob beradi va kerakli joyni ko'rsatadi.`, glass field `Masalan: tarifni qanday almashtiraman?` + round send, `Tez savollar:` pills `Tarif` / `To'lov` / `Hujjat` / `Advokat topish` (each sends a canned question), `Oldingi suhbatni ochish ({n})` when turns exist. Opens `AskAiSheet` (`AI yordamchi` / `Avval AI javob beradi. Hal bo'lmasa — operatorga yozing.`): `AiTurnView`s (question bubble; answer card with kicker `AI yordamchi`, markdown, numbered steps, `Ochish: {screen}` button when the answer points at a screen, `Qayta urinish` on failure, `Javob topilmadi — operatorga yozing.`), follow-up field `Yana savol bering…`, buttons `Hal bo'ldi` (clears, toast `Ajoyib! Yana savol bo'lsa, bemalol yozing.`) and `Operatorga yozish` (→ new-chat window with the AI turns attached).
  4. `OperatorCard`: headset tile with a green live dot, `Operatorga ulanish`, `Ish vaqtida jonli operator bilan yozishasiz.`, note `Javob shu yerda va bildirishnomada keladi`, blue button `Chatni boshlash`.
  5. `Boshqa yo'llar` + `OtherWays` two tiles: green `Qo'ng'iroq qilish` (the support phone, bold, + schedule) → dialer; amber `Shikoyat yuborish` / `Xizmat yoki mutaxassis ustidan rasmiy shikoyat` → `ComplaintSheet`.
  6. `ListControls`: `Mening suhbatlarim` / `Operator bilan yozishmalaringiz shu yerda saqlanadi` + soft `Yangi suhbat`; when any chat exists: `Segmented` `Faol (n)` / `Yakunlangan (n)` and search `Mavzu yoki xabar bo'yicha qidirish`.
     Then the `TicketRow`s of the chosen tab (icon headphones / check-circle, title, last message or `Operator: {name}` or `✓ resolution`, meta `Shoshilinch · topic · SPT-id · age`, status pill `Operator kutilmoqda` / `Operator ulandi` / `AI javob bermoqda` / `Tasdiq kutilmoqda` / `Qayta ochilgan` / `O'tkazilgan` / `Hal qilingan` / `Yakunlangan` / `Bekor qilingan`, unread badge).
- **Actions:** row → `/support/{id}`; `Chatni boshlash` / `Yangi suhbat` → `NewChatSheet`: if an active chat exists and not forced, `Sizda faol suhbat bor` / `Yangi suhbat o'rniga shu suhbatni davom ettiring — …` with that ticket's row, submit `Davom ettirish`, secondary `Baribir yangi suhbat`; else `Operator bilan yangi suhbat` / `Muammoni yozing — operator shu chatda javob beradi.` with `NewTicketFields`: textarea `Muammoni qisqacha yozing` (`Masalan: tarif sotib olishda to'lov o'tmayapti`, 3–4000, counter), `Nima haqida? (ixtiyoriy)` topic chips (`Tarif / obuna`, `To'lov`, `Hujjat`, `Advokat topish`, `Tezkor advokat`, `Hisob`, `Texnik muammo` + any extra from `/support/meta`; hint `Mavzuni tanlamasangiz, uni matndan aniqlaymiz.` / `Mavzu matndan aniqlandi: {topic}`), switch `Shoshilinch` (`Masala kechiktirib bo'lmaydigan bo'lsa yoqing`), note `Operator AI bilan suhbatingizni ko'radi` when AI turns exist; submit `Chatni boshlash` → POST `/support/tickets` → toast `Chat ochildi: {SPT-id}` → `/support/{id}`.
- **States:** list skeleton (78 px rows); empties: first time `Operator bilan hali yozishmagansiz` / `Avval AI instruktordan so'rab ko'ring — …`, active `Faol suhbat yo'q` / `Savol tug'ilsa — operatorga yozing, javob shu yerga keladi.`, closed `Yakunlangan suhbatlar hali yo'q.`, search `Hech narsa topilmadi` + `Qidiruvni tozalash`; feature off `Bu imkoniyat tez orada ishga tushadi.`; create failed `Chatni ochib bo'lmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.` or the 422 `platform_contact_blocked` message; AI failure `AI javob bera olmadi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`. Live: `support.*` user-socket events refresh lists, unread and the open ticket; without the socket the lists poll (20 s ticket, 10 s chat) while focused. App-wide toasts (`useSupportAlerts`): `{name}: {text}`, `Operator ulandi: {name}`, `Suhbat yakunlandi` when the chat is not on screen.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                               | Used for                                     | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/support/tickets/me?status=active | closed&limit&offset`                         | my chats (two tabs), ongoing, "resume" head, unread total                                                                                                                                                        | `items[]{ id, work_id, subject/title, description, status, category, priority, source, last_message, last_message_at, client_user_id, assigned_operator_user_id, assigned_operator_name / assigned_operator{name}, resolution, reopen_reason, unread_count, created_at, updated_at, closed_at }`, `total`, `has_more` |
| POST `/support/tickets`                | new chat                                     | req `subject`, `message`, `description`, `category`, `priority` (`high` when urgent), `source: "mobile"`, `context{ current_path, role, topic_picked, ai_history[{question,answer,created_at}], ai_session_id }` |
| GET `/support/meta`                    | extra categories / priorities                | `categories[]`, `priorities[]` (`key`, `label`)                                                                                                                                                                  |
| GET `/calendar/business-hours`         | hours badge / schedule                       | `days[]`, `start`, `end`, open state                                                                                                                                                                             |
| POST `/ai/instructor/chat`             | the AI step (route `/mobile/client/support`) | req `locale`, `message`, `history[]`, `runtime_state`, `ui_manifest`, `session_id`; res `answer`/`reply`, `steps[]`, `session_id`, `actions[]{type:"navigate", href}`, `support_fallback{available}`             |
| POST `/complaints` (complaint sheet)   | complaint                                    | complaint body                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| user socket                            | `support.*`, `notification.created`          | ticket / message payloads                                                                                                                                                                                        |

- **UX note:** AI first, operator second, phone and complaint last — the web hub's order; the new-chat window offers to resume an ongoing chat before opening another.

### `/support/new` — (redirect)

- **Who:** anyone with an old link `/support/new?topic=…&q=…&force=1`.
- **Purpose:** compatibility: redirects to `/support` with `new=1` and the same params, so the hub opens with its new-chat window.
- **Layout / Actions / States / Data:** none of its own.
- **UX note:** keeps old deep links working.

### `/support/[id]` — chat title (ticket subject or first words) / fallback `Suhbat`

- **Who:** the ticket's own client (any role); guest → `SignInGate`; 403/404 → `Bu chat topilmadi yoki sizga tegishli emas.` + `Orqaga`. Remote assistance `client.support`: the operator may point at the conversation (`support.ticket.chat`, `Yozishmalar`) and open the facts (`support.ticket.details`, `Murojaat tafsilotlari`); writing stays the client's.
- **Purpose:** one chat with the operator: status, live messages, composer, the ticket's facts.
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `AdminPage` title = ticket title, subtitle = `SPT-…` work id, right info button `Batafsil` → `TicketDetailsSheet` (facts `Ariza ID`, `Holat` pill, `Mavzu`, `Muhimlik` (`Past` / `Oddiy` / `Yuqori` / `Tezkor`), `Operator`, `Ochilgan`, `Yakunlangan`, description, `Yakuniy xulosa`, `Qayta ochish sababi`).
  1. `TicketBanner`: status pill + who (`Operator: {name}` / `Operator ulanmoqda…` / `AI javob bermoqda` / `LexGo yordam`); line `Odatda tez javob beramiz` or `Ish vaqti: {hours}` or, closed, `topic · SPT-id`; box `Xabaringiz qabul qilindi` / `Operator tez orada qo'shiladi. Sahifadan chiqsangiz ham xabar beramiz.` while waiting without an operator; warn box `Sizning tasdig'ingiz kutilmoqda` / `Operator siz uchun amal tayyorladi. To'lov Telegram orqali tasdiqlanadi.` + `Ko'rish` when status is `waiting_client_confirm`.
  2. `ChatView`: inverted `ChatList` — day labels (`Bugun` / `Kecha` / date), `MessageBubble`s (mine by sender id; system/AI styled by role; attachments open in `FilePreview`; failed → retry), `AssistNote` cards (`Operator yordami` kicker, `Operator siz uchun tarif tanladi` / `… tarif to'lovini tayyorladi` / `… hujjat so'rovini yaratdi` / `… xizmat buyurtmasini yaratdi` / `… tezkor advokat so'rovini yaratdi`, title · amount · work id → the global `AssistSheet`), `CallRow` when the operator starts a call (`Operator sizga qo'ng'iroq qilmoqda`, `Audio qo'ng'iroq` / `Video qo'ng'iroq`, `Qo'shilish` → `/call/{room}?callId=…`, `Keyinroq`); footer `Oldingi xabarlar` loads older pages; meta `topic · SPT-id` at the very top.
  3. Bottom: `Composer` (`Xabaringizni yozing…`, multiline ≤ 4000, round send; a refused message comes back with `Telefon raqam, havola va boshqa kontaktlarni yozish mumkin emas.`) — or, for a closed ticket, `ClosedBanner`: `Suhbat yakunlandi`, `Yakunlangan vaqti: {date}`, `Yakuniy xulosa: …` / `Operator yakuniy xulosa qoldirmagan.`, buttons `Qayta ochish` (→ `ReopenSheet`: `Nima hal bo'lmadi? (ixtiyoriy)`, `Suhbatni qayta ochish`, toast `Suhbat qayta ochildi — operatorga yozishingiz mumkin`) and `Yangi suhbat` (→ `/support?new=1&force=1`).
- **Actions:** send (optimistic "sending" bubble, retry on failure), open attachment, join the operator's call, open an assist card (its sheet offers `Telegramni ulash` / `Telegramda ochish`, `Tariflarni ko'rish` / `To'lovni ochish` / `Hujjatni ochish` / `Buyurtmani ochish` / `So'rovni ochish`, `Chatni ochish`, `Keyinroq`, and `Rad etish` for an unpaid marketplace order), reopen, new chat.
- **States:** chat skeleton; `ErrorView`; `Hali xabar yo'q.`; `QueryBoundary` for the ticket.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                               | Used for                                                                  | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/support/tickets/{id}`                                            | ticket (polls 20 s only while the socket is down)                         | as the list row                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| GET `/support/tickets/{id}/messages?order=desc&limit&offset`           | history (fetching marks them read)                                        | `items[]{ id, ticket_id, sender_user_id / sender{id,name,role}, sender_name, sender_role, message_type, content / payload.message, attachments[{ name, url, size, mime }], created_at }`, `total`, `has_more`, `ticket` |
| POST `/support/tickets/{id}/messages`                                  | send                                                                      | req `message`, `content`, `message_type: "text"`, `attachments: []`; 422 `platform_contact_blocked`                                                                                                                     |
| POST `/support/tickets/{id}/reopen`                                    | reopen (client only)                                                      | `reason`                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| POST `/telegram/link/start`, POST `/marketplace/me/orders/{id}/cancel` | assist-card writes                                                        | `share_url`; `reason`                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| GET `/calendar/business-hours`, GET `/support/meta`                    | banner / labels                                                           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| user socket                                                            | `support.*` (messages, claimed, closed, `support.call_created`), `call.*` | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

- **UX note:** the operator's prepared actions appear as cards inside the timeline and the payment is confirmed in Telegram — the app never marks anything paid.

---

## Messages and calls

### `/messages` — `Xabarlar` (tab)

- **Who:** every role's inbox. Guest: tab press asks to sign in; the page itself shows `Xabarlarni ko'rish uchun kiring` / `Himoyalangan suhbatlar hisobingizga bog'langan.` + `Kirish`. Seller whose cabinet denies `secure_chat` (or still under review): the tab is hidden and the page shows `Xabarlar tasdiqlangach ochiladi` / `Profilingiz tekshiruvdan o'tgach, mijozlar bilan himoyalangan chat ishlay boshlaydi.`. Clients and approved sellers: the inbox.
- **Purpose:** my secure conversations, newest first, with unread counters (also the tab badge), search, filter sheet and "new chat".
- **Layout, top → bottom:** `TabPage` header (menu button, greeting, name, support headset, bell, avatar; upsell banner for clients), title `Xabarlar`, subtitle `{n} ta o'qilmagan` or `{n} ta suhbat`. `RoomsList` (FlashList):
  1. `PushPermissionCard` (when applicable).
  2. Lock line `Mutaxassislar bilan himoyalangan suhbatlaringiz.`.
  3. When rooms exist: search `Suhbatlarni qidirish` + sliders button (dot while a kind is chosen); the chosen kind as a removable chip.
  4. `RoomRow`s split by hairlines: avatar (people icon for a group), title = the other members' names (`Name`, `Name, Name`, `Name +2`; never a phone), role chip (`Mijoz` / `Advokat` / `Yurist` / `Mutaxassis` / `Navbatchi advokat` / `Advokat (guruh)` / `Operator`), last message preview, time, unread badge, optional tag (`service · work id` for a Tezkor Advokat room).
  5. Floating blue round button (pencil) bottom-right above the tab bar.
     `InboxFilterSheet` (`Filtrlar`): two big cards `Hammasi` / `O'qilmagan` with counts, list `Turi`: `Tezkor advokat` (red) / `Hujjat` (violet) / `Buyurtma` (blue) / `Ish` (cyan) / `Boshqa` (grey) — only kinds that exist; bar `Filtrlarni tozalash` + `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N`.
- **Actions:** row → `/chat/{roomId}`; FAB / empty-state action `Yangi chat` → `NewChatSheet` (`Yangi chat` / `Mutaxassis yoki shaxsni tanlang va himoyalangan suhbat boshlang.`, `PeopleSheet` search of the directory): a seller creates the room (POST `/secure-chats`); a client opens the private chat (GET `/lawyers/{id}/private-chat`) — allowed only with a specialist they have a running paid order with or already chat with (`Chat faqat shu mutaxassisning xizmatini sotib olganingizdan keyin, xizmat davom etayotganda ochiladi.`); a 402 shows the server's sentence and `Tariflarni ko'rish` → `/subscription`. Pull to refresh.
- **States:** skeleton rows; `ErrorView`; empty `Hozircha suhbat yo'q` / `Xizmat buyurtma qilsangiz yoki mutaxassis tanlasangiz, suhbat shu yerda paydo bo'ladi.`; filtered `Hech narsa topilmadi` / `Boshqa ism yoki so'z bilan qidirib ko'ring.`. Live: `secure_chat.*` user-socket events fold into the list (new message → room to the top; `secure_chat.read_only` → re-read); polling every 15 s only while the socket is down; the last list is restored from the device snapshot at start.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                        | Used for                                           | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/secure-chats`                             | rooms (no paging)                                  | `rooms[]/items[]{ id, status (active/archived/completed/read_only), order_id, case_id, urgent_advokat_request_ids[], document_lawyer_request_ids[], active_record_id, client_user_id, seller_user_id, participants[{ user_id, name, role, phone }], participant_count, last_message{ id, sender_user_id, sender_name, message_type, filtered_content/content, created_at, is_mine }, unread_count, last_read_at, auto_delete_hours, archived_until, last_message_at, updated_at, created_at }` |
| POST `/secure-chats` (seller)                   | new room                                           | req `client_user_id`, `seller_user_id`; res room                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| GET `/lawyers/{user_id}/private-chat` (client)  | open / create the paid private chat                | res room; 402 until paid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| GET `/urgent-advokat/requests/me`               | row tags for Tezkor rooms                          | `secure_chat_room_id`/`room_id`, `service_title`, `service_kind`, `work_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GET `/lawyers/me/cabinet` (seller)              | `available_actions.secure_chat`, `limited_access`  | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET `/marketplace/me/orders` (client, new chat) | who may be chatted with                            | `seller` ids, status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| user socket                                     | `secure_chat.*`, `marketplace.cancellation_chat_*` | `room_id`, message                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

- **UX note:** Telegram-style flat rows; the filter sheet puts "All" and "Unread" first because that is what people reach for.

### `/chat/[roomId]` — the other member's name (fallback `Chat`)

- **Who:** every role, the rooms they belong to. Params `?join=<callId>` opens that running call at once; `?doc=<documentRequestId>` turns on the client's "call the advocate" button. Screenshots and recording are blocked while open (TZ 40). The header's call buttons depend on role and order: audio / video only for accounts with `meetings.manage` (professionals) when the seller cabinet allows calls and the order's gate allows that type; a client in a document-request room gets `Advokatga qo'ng'iroq` (audio, 3 calls max).
- **Purpose:** one secure chat: history + live socket, sending text / files / voice, calls, members, auto-delete, completion (Tezkor), delete.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `ChatHeader`: back (or `/`), avatar / group icon, name, line with a dot: `Onlayn` / `Qayta ulanmoqda…` (+ `· {n} ishtirokchi` for a group); right: phone / video icon buttons (conditional), `Advokatga qo'ng'iroq` (client + `doc`), `⋮` menu.
  2. `ActiveCallBar` (green) when a call is live in the room: `{title}` or `Qo'ng'iroq davom etmoqda` + `Qo'shilish`.
  3. `SecureNote` pill `Xabarlar filtrlanadi va uchdan-uchgacha himoyalangan.`.
  4. `ChatThread` — inverted list: day pills (`Bugun` / `Kecha` / date), `{n} ta yangi xabar` divider above the first unread, `MessageBubble`s (text, file card, voice player, system / result / meeting / call notes with a join action, reply quote → jump-to-quote with a flash, double checks, blocked content `[metadata_only]` for staff reveals, upload progress, failed / queued markers); older messages revealed on scroll (40 at a time).
  5. `Composer`: reply strip (×), safety line `Telefon raqam, Telegram yoki tashqi havolalar bloklanadi. Hammasini LexGo ichida saqlang.`, `TextBar` (text, emoji sheet, attach: `Galereyadan rasm` / `Kamera` / `Fayl`, mic → `VoiceBar` recording); a closed room shows `Suhbat yakunlangan — yangi xabar yuborib bo'lmaydi.` instead.
     Sheets (`ChatSheets`): `MessageMenu` long-press — `Javob berish`, `Nusxalash`, `Faylni ulashish`, or for a failed send `Qayta yuborish` / `O'chirib tashlash` (no retry for a file the server refused), pending `Yuborishni bekor qilish`; `⋮` `Chat sozlamalari` → `{n} ishtirokchi`, `Avto-o'chirish: o'chiq|24 soat|7 kun|30 kun`, `Chatni yakunlash` (advocate / operator side of a Tezkor room), `Chatni o'chirish` (danger; confirm `Chat o'chiriladi va 1 oy arxivda saqlanadi.`); `MembersSheet` `Chat ishtirokchilari` / `{n} ishtirokchi` (names + role, `(siz)`; phones hidden; `Ishtirokchilar ro'yxati hozircha yo'q.`); TTL `OptionSheet`; `CompleteSheet` (summary → POST complete, toast `Chat yakunlandi`); `FilePreview`.
- **Actions:** send text → POST messages; file / voice → multipart upload one at a time in order (progress, cancel); call buttons → join the live call or POST a new call with the other members invited, then `/call/{room}?callId=…`; `Advokatga qo'ng'iroq` → POST `/document-requests/{doc}/calls/audio`; menu actions above.
- **States:** skeleton bubbles; `ErrorView` + retry; empty `Suhbat boshlanishi` / `Bu maxfiy va himoyalangan suhbat. Xabarlar filtrlanadi.`; send failure `Xabar yuborilmadi.` or the server's sentence; a file refused by type / size / quota is explained in the user's language; 409 "consultation completed" closes the composer; call failure `Qo'ng'iroqni boshlab bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`; mic denied → dialog `Mikrofonga ruxsat yo'q` → settings; `Qayta ulanmoqda…` while the room socket is down (history polled every 10 s then). Order-gated rooms: no writing while the order made the room read-only (finished, refund, reassignment) and only the call types the order allows.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                         | Used for                                                                                                           | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/secure-chats/{id}`                                                         | the room                                                                                                           | as the list row                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| GET `/secure-chats/{id}/messages`                                                | whole history (also marks the room read)                                                                           | `messages[]{ id, sender_user_id, sender{id,name,role}, message_type (text/file/voice/system/result/meeting/call), content / filtered_content, is_blocked, block_reason, created_at, meta{ file_name, download_url, mime_type, size, reply_to_message_id, urgent_advokat_request_id }, reply_to{…}, reply_to_message_id }` |
| POST `/secure-chats/{id}/messages`                                               | text                                                                                                               | req `message_type: "text"`, `content`, `meta{ reply_to_message_id }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| POST `/secure-chats/{id}/messages/upload` (multipart, `expo-file-system` upload) | file / voice                                                                                                       | file + type / reply                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| PATCH `/secure-chats/{id}/settings`                                              | auto-delete                                                                                                        | `auto_delete_hours` (0/24/168/720)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| DELETE `/secure-chats/{id}`                                                      | archive the chat                                                                                                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| POST `/urgent-advokat/requests/{id}/chat/complete`                               | finish a Tezkor chat (advocate / operator)                                                                         | `summary`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| GET `/secure-chats/{id}/calls`, POST `/secure-chats/{id}/calls`                  | live-call bar, start a call                                                                                        | req `call_type`, `title`, `participant_user_ids[]`, `max_duration_minutes`; call fields see `/call/[roomId]`                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| POST `/document-requests/{id}/calls/audio`                                       | client rings the advocate (3 max, 429 after)                                                                       | res call                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| GET `/marketplace/me/orders/{id}` (client) / seller order (`/lawyers/me/…`)      | order gate (write / call types)                                                                                    | `room_id`, chat / call flags, status, `payment_status`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| socket `/ws/secure-chats/{room}?token=`                                          | `secure_message.created` frames, `call.*`; reconnects on foreground / network, closed after 30 s in the background | `message{…}`, `event`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| GET `/auth/me`                                                                   | token refresh when the socket answers 401                                                                          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

- **UX note:** optimistic bubbles with real upload progress; the room is the same screen for every role, only the header's call buttons change.

### `/call/[roomId]` — call title / `Uchrashuv`

- **Who:** any signed-in user invited to the call (host or participant). `?callId=` names the call; without it the room's live call is joined. Reached from a chat's call buttons / `Qo'shilish` bar, the incoming-call ring, a notification (`Qo'ng'iroqqa qo'shilish`), support `CallRow`, the meetings list. Lazy-loaded (native LiveKit / WebRTC); gestures disabled, slides from the bottom; screenshots / recording blocked; the screen stays awake.
- **Purpose:** the in-app LiveKit call (audio / video / meeting).
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. `CallHeader`: title (or `Uchrashuv`), `● REC` when recording, status dot + `Ulanmoqda…` / `Kutilmoqda…` / `Ulandi` / `Qayta ulanmoqda…` / `Tugadi` / `Ulanish muammosi` · elapsed mm:ss, time left `Qoldi mm:ss` (red under 2 min) or `Pauza`; layout toggle (video calls) `Ko'rinish`; people button with a count badge.
  2. `CallBanners` (stacked): `Ulanish muammosi` + `Qayta urinish`, `Qayta ulanmoqda…`, `Mikrofonga ruxsat berilmadi. Sozlamalardan yoqing.` / `Kameraga ruxsat berilmadi. …` + `Sozlamalar`, `Uchrashuv to'xtatilgan — vaqt hisoblanmayapti.`, alone: `Hamma chiqib ketdi — {s} soniyadan keyin chiqasiz` + `Qolish` (10 s grace).
  3. `ExtensionBar` (blue) while a paid extension waits: host `Mijoz Telegram'da tasdiqlashi kutilmoqda: {minutes} daqiqa · {amount}.` / client `Advokat {minutes} daqiqa qo'shishni so'ramoqda ({amount}). To'lovni Telegram'da tasdiqlang.`, `Javob muddati: mm:ss` / `So'rov muddati tugadi — uchrashuv davom etadi.`.
  4. `RecordingBar`: `{name} yozib olmoqda` (red); host sees `{name} uchrashuvni yozib olishni so'ramoqda.` + `Ruxsat berish` / `Rad etish`; requester `Yozib olish so'rovi tasdiq kutmoqda…`; allowed `Yozib olishga ruxsat berildi.` + `Boshlash`; denied `Yozib olishga ruxsat berilmadi.`.
  5. `CallStage`: grid (1–2 stacked, more in two columns, scrolls past six) or speaker mode (screen share / pinned / talking person large, the rest in a strip); `ParticipantTile` = video or avatar + `Kamera o'chiq`, name `(siz)`, mic-off icon, green border while speaking; waiting text `Boshqa ishtirokchilar kutilmoqda…` while alone.
  6. `CallControls`: `Mikrofon`, `Kamera` (or `Videoga o'tish` on an audio call), `Almashtirish` (lens), `Karnay`, `Yana` (badge = unread chat), red `Chiqish` (hang-up).
     Sheets: `Yana` → `Chat`, `Ishtirokchilar`, `So'zlovchi ko'rinishi` / `Panjara ko'rinishi`, host: `Pauza` / `Davom ettirish`, `+{n} daqiqa bepul` (while offered), `Uchrashuvni uzaytirish`, client: `Yozib olishni so'rash`, host: `Yozib olishni boshlash`, host with a document request: `Boshqa advokatga uzatish`. Hang-up `Uchrashuvdan chiqish` → `Chiqish (uchrashuv davom etadi)` and host `Hamma uchun tugatish`. `ParticipantsSheet` (roster with live mic / speaking, `Tashkilotchi`, `Qo'shildi` / `Taklif qilingan` / `Rad etdi` / `Chiqdi`; host can mute, `Chiqarish` (confirm `Ishtirokchini chiqarasizmi?`) and `Odam taklif qilish` → `InviteSheet`). `CallChatSheet` `Chat` / `Faqat shu uchrashuv ishtirokchilari ko'radi` (LiveKit data channel, not stored). `ExtendSheet` `Uchrashuvni uzaytirish` / `Daqiqasiga {price} so'm`: stepper + `Daqiqa` field, presets 5/10/15/30, `Jami: {amount} so'm`, note `To'lovni mijoz Telegram botda tasdiqlaydi; shu vaqtgacha uchrashuv to'xtatiladi.`, submit `To'lovni so'rash`. `TransferSheet` `Boshqa advokatga uzatish` / `Uchrashuv pauza qilinadi, ish yangi advokatga o'tadi.`: `Advokatni tanlang`, `Sabab`, `Uzatish`.
     Before / after the live room `CallStatusView` (dark): spinner `Uchrashuvga ulanmoqda…` + `Bekor qilish`; `Ulanib bo'lmadi` + message (`Bu uchrashuv allaqachon tugagan.` 409, `Uchrashuv topilmadi.` 404, `Bu uchrashuvga qo'shilishga ruxsatingiz yo'q.` 403, `Uchrashuvga ulanib bo'lmadi. Qayta urining.`) + `Qayta urinish` / `Orqaga`; `Uchrashuv tugadi`; `Qo'ng'iroq rad etildi`; `Javob bermadi`; dropped `Aloqa uzildi, lekin uchrashuv davom etmoqda. Qayta ulaning.`.
- **Actions:** media toggles (muted by host → `Sizni tashkilotchi ovozsiz qildi`; screen being recorded → `Ekran yozib olinayotganda mikrofonni yoqib bo'lmaydi`), leave / end, pause / resume, free / paid extension, recording request / allow / start, invite, mute / kick, transfer. Leaving goes back or to `/meetings`. The caller hears a quiet ringback while the call rings; after everybody left or the call ended the screen closes itself after ~2 s.
- **States:** as in the status view and banners; a hardware back opens the hang-up sheet.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                                                                | Used for                                                                                                                                              | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/secure-chats/{room}/calls`                                                                                                        | resolve the live call without `callId`                                                                                                                | `calls[]{ id, status }` (`active`/`scheduled` = live)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| GET `/secure-chats/{room}/calls/{call}/join-token`                                                                                      | LiveKit url + token and the first snapshot (409 ended, 404 gone, 403 forbidden)                                                                       | `livekit_url`, `livekit_token`, `id`, `work_id`, `room_id`, `caller_user_id`, `call_type`, `title`, `status`, `started_at`, `ended_at`, `auto_end_at`, `remaining_seconds`, `max_duration_minutes`, `participants[{ user_id, name, role, status, mic_enabled, camera_enabled, screen_enabled }]`, `permissions{ can_invite, can_mute, can_kick, can_end }`, `paused`, `paused_remaining_seconds`, `pause_expires_at`, `free_extension_available`, `free_extension_used`, `free_extension_max_minutes`, `paid_extension_price_per_minute`, `pending_extension_request{ id, minutes, amount, status, expires_at }`, `recording_status`, `recording_requested_by_user_id`, `recording_allowed_by_user_id`, `recording_started_by_user_id`, `document_request_id`, `document_lawyer_record_id`, `client_call_usage{ used, limit, remaining }`, `participant_count`, `can_start_now`, `quality_policy{…}` |
| GET `/secure-chats/{room}/calls/{call}`                                                                                                 | roster / limits re-read                                                                                                                               | same                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| POST `…/join`, POST `…/leave`, POST `…/end` (fallback PATCH `/calls/{id}` `{status:"ended"}`)                                           | join / leave / end                                                                                                                                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| PATCH `…/participants/{user}`                                                                                                           | my mic / camera / status, host mute / kick, decline with `answered_device_id`                                                                         | `mic_enabled`, `camera_enabled`, `status`, `role`, `answered_device_id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| POST `…/participants`                                                                                                                   | invite                                                                                                                                                | `user_id`, `role: "participant"`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| POST `…/pause` (`minutes`, `reason`), POST `…/resume`, POST `…/free-extend` (`minutes`), POST `…/extension-payment-request` (`minutes`) | clock controls (host)                                                                                                                                 | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| POST `…/recording-request`, PATCH `…/recording-permission` (`allowed`, `reason`), POST `…/recording/start`                              | recording consent                                                                                                                                     | `recording_status`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| POST `/lawyers/me/document-requests/{id}/transfer`                                                                                      | transfer (host, advocate)                                                                                                                             | `target_lawyer_user_id`, `reason`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| GET `/users/search` (PeopleSheet)                                                                                                       | invite / transfer pickers                                                                                                                             | people                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| socket `/ws/secure-chats/{room}/calls/{call}?token=`                                                                                    | signalling `call.join` / `call.leave` / `call.end`; frames `participant.*`, `media.*`, `call.*` refresh the roster, `auto_ended` / `call.ended` close | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| LiveKit (`livekit_url`)                                                                                                                 | media; chat over the data channel                                                                                                                     | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

- **UX note:** every money or consent step (paid minutes, recording) is a request the backend settles; the app only shows the request's state and never confirms a payment.
