## Assistant

### `/assistant` — `AI yordamchi`

- **Who:** signed-in users (the floating robot is hidden for guests; a guest can still open the route, the welcome shows the client suggestions). Opened by the floating LexGo robot, `/instructor`, "Ask the assistant" buttons; the role shapes the suggested questions and the cross-screen target catalogue (client vs seller: sellers get plans and support targets only).
- **Purpose:** the platform instructor: it explains where things are, opens screens, highlights controls and — only after a native confirmation — runs actions (support hand-off, purchases) through the backend. It is not legal advice.
- **Layout, top → bottom:**
  1. Dark gradient header: `AiOrb` (compass), `AI yordamchi`, `Ilova bo'yicha yo'l ko'rsatadi · yuridik maslahat emas`, glass `×` (back or `/`).
  2. `ChatView` list: no messages → `Welcome` card (compass badge, `Qayerga borishni ko'rsataman`, `Nima qilmoqchisiz? Kerakli ekranni ochib, kerakli tugmani ko'rsataman. Muhim amallar faqat sizning tasdig'ingiz bilan bajariladi.`, suggestion chips — client `Pro tarifni qayerdan sotib olaman?`, `Hujjat yaratmoqchiman`, `Advokat tanlamoqchiman`, `Operator bilan bog'lanmoqchiman`, `Tezkor advokat kerak`, `Hujjatlarim qayerda?`; seller `So'rovlarni qayerda ko'raman?`, `Profilimni qanday to'ldiraman?`, `Buyurtmalarim qayerda?`, `Operator bilan bog'lanmoqchiman`; a tinted box `Yuridik savol bo'lsa, AI yuridik chatdan foydalaning.` + gradient `AI yuridik chatga o'tish` → `/ai`); with messages → `Yangi suhbat` ghost button on top, user bubbles (blue; a failed one shows `Qayta yuborish`), assistant cards (text, `CommandChips` — `Sahifa` / `Ajratish` / `Izoh` / `Surish` / `Oyna` / `Maydon` / `To'ldirish` / `Amal` / `Tasdiq` / `Operator` · `Kutilmoqda` / `Bajarilmoqda` / `Bajarildi` / `Topilmadi` / `Bajarilmadi` / `To'xtatildi` / `O'tkazildi`, `ReplyExtras`: `Qadamlar` list, `Kerakli ma'lumotlar`, `Yana ko'rsat`, follow-up chips, support card `Javob topilmadimi?` / `Operator yordam beradi. Nima yuborilishini ko'rsatib, tasdig'ingizni so'raymiz.` + `Operatorga ulanish`); footer `Qidiryapman…` while sending.
  3. `InstructorComposer`: field `Nima qilmoqchisiz?` (≤ 1000) + round arrow-up send.
     When an answer starts pointing at something on another screen (a highlight step) this screen pops itself so the `HighlightOverlay` is visible: dimmed screen with a live hole, pulsing ring, caption card with `{i} / {n}`, `To'xtatish`, `Keyingi` / `Tushunarli`, and an icon back to the assistant.
- **Actions:** send → POST `/ai/instructor/chat` with the current path, visible registered targets and the cross-screen catalogue; commands run (navigate within the contract's allowed routes, highlight, tooltip, scroll, open modal, fill form after `Maydonlarni to'ldirish` confirmation, preview → native confirm `Amalni tasdiqlang` → confirm); `Operatorga ulanish` → the `support_handoff` action path (preview → confirm → ticket); `Yangi suhbat` → forget the backend session; a restart restores the last answer from the stored session.
- **States:** failed send → the bubble is marked and a toast gives the error text; unknown session → a fresh one is started silently; contract 403/404 → the robot disappears for this account.
- **Data:**

| Endpoint                                                                                           | Used for                                                                           | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET `/ai/instructor/contract`                                                                      | command types, mutating actions, allowed routes, aliases                           | `contract_version`, `command_types[]`, `mutating_actions[]`, `allowed_routes[]`, `route_aliases{}`, `target_aliases{}`, `role`, `accepted_targets_limit`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| POST `/ai/instructor/chat`                                                                         | a question                                                                         | req `message`, `locale`, `current_path`, `visible_targets[]`, `targets[{ ai_id, type, label, route }]`, `history[]`, `session_id`; res `session_id`, `response_id`, `answer`, `intent`, `commands[]`/`actions[]{ id, type, href, target, modal, text, style, duration_ms, fields, action, payload, steps }`, `steps[]`, `suggestions[]`, `missing_requirements[]`, `support_fallback{ available, reason, confirm_action }`, `event_url`, `runtime_url`, `actions_preview_url`, `actions_confirm_url` |
| GET `/ai/instructor/session/{id}`                                                                  | restore after restart                                                              | `session{ last_response }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| POST `/ai/instructor/runtime`                                                                      | screen + visible targets while the assistant is in use (debounced, no form values) | `session_id`, `locale`, `runtime_state{ current_route, visible_ai_ids, viewport }`, `ui_manifest{ route, role, page_title, elements[] }`, `visible_targets[]`, `targets[]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| POST `/ai/instructor/events`                                                                       | telemetry (ids, routes, reasons only)                                              | `session_id`, `event_type`, `command_id`, `status`, `current_route`, `details`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| POST `/ai/instructor/actions/preview`, POST `/ai/instructor/actions/confirm` (+ `Idempotency-Key`) | an action: preview, then confirm after the native dialog                           | req `session_id`, `command_id`, `action`, `target`, `payload`; preview `title`, `summary`, `amount`, `details[]`, `warnings[]`, `confirm_token`, `requires_confirmation`; confirm adds `confirm_token`, `idempotency_key`; res `status`, `message`, `payment_url`, `next{href}`, `ticket{id, work_id}`, `commands[]`                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| user socket `ai_instructor.response`                                                               | a pushed answer for the open session (delivered once by response id)               | same as the chat response                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

- **UX note:** the assistant never acts without a native confirmation and never lists its own button unless asked "where is the assistant"; "show again" replays only the guiding commands, never a mutating one.

### `/instructor` — (redirect)

- **Who:** anyone following the deep link or an "Ask the assistant" button.
- **Purpose:** raise the assistant (`openInstructor()`) over the screen the person came from, then leave (`back` or `/`); the open flag is turned into a push of `/assistant` by `InstructorSheet`, so there is exactly one assistant UI.
- **Layout / States / Data:** none (renders nothing).
- **UX note:** keeps every entry point (robot, link, buttons) on one screen.

---

## Technical routes

### `/m/[name]` — the tab pages as stack screens

- **Who:** iOS. The system tab bar can only reach the tabs it shows; links to a tab page outside the current role's bar (`profile`, `lawyers`, `orders`, a client's `works`, `services`, `messages`, `workspace`, `requests`, `clients`, `ai`, `index`) are rewritten by `installTabReach()` (patches `router.navigate/push/replace`) to `/m/<name>` and open the very same page component as an ordinary pushed screen. An unknown name shows `NotFoundPage`.
- **Purpose / Layout:** `PAGES[name]` lazily imports `app/(tabs)/<name>.tsx`; nothing of its own. Remote assistance canonicalises `/m/<name>` back to `/<name>`.
- **UX note:** on Android the flat bar shows every role route, so this route is not used there.

### `+not-found` — `Sahifa topilmadi`

- **Who:** anyone hitting an unknown route.
- **Layout:** shared `Header` (back), `EmptyState` compass icon, `Sahifa topilmadi`, `Siz izlayotgan sahifa mavjud emas yoki ko'chirilgan.`, action `Bosh sahifaga` → replace `/`.
- **Data:** none.

### `/gap` — the empty tab slot

- **Who:** iOS only. `GapTab` renders `null`.
- **Purpose:** with an even number of visible tabs the `NativeTabsBar` inserts a disabled, caption-less slot (`gap`, a clear 8 pt image) next to the AI slot so the AI tab stays in the exact middle of the system bar and the raised AI button sits at the centre.
- **Data:** none.

---

## App-level behaviour

### 1. Roles and sign-in rules (`src/entities/session`, `src/features/auth`)

- **Types:** `Role = "client" | "lawyer" | "advocate"` (UI); `backendRole` is the API word: `client`, `yurist` → `lawyer`, `advokat` → `advocate` (`fromBackendRole`). `User` also carries `roles[]` (assigned roles), `permissions[]` (`tasks.view` opens the team-tasks row for anyone; `meetings.manage` = may start calls), `accountStatus`, `twoFactorEnabled/Method`, `telegramLinked`, `lexgoId`, `region`.
- **Served accounts:** `MOBILE_ROLES = ["client", "yurist", "advokat"]`. Organisation (`advokat_tashkiloti`), `admin`, `superadmin`, `manager`, `call_center*`, `sales*` are refused: after a successful login their server session is closed (`POST /auth/logout`) and the form shows the `webOnly` text; a stored session of such an account from an older build starts signed out with the same notice. Registration offers only the three kinds.
- **Tab role:** `useTabRole()` → `guest` (no session), `client`, `seller` (lawyer / advocate). `useAccess()` → `seller`, `canCalls` (`meetings.manage`), `can(perm)`.
- **OTP purposes (`otpFlowStore`, memory only, dropped on sign-out):** `register` (POST `/auth/register/start` → `/auth/register/verify`), `login2fa` (428 on login → `/auth/login/2fa`; `totp` flag = authenticator, no resend), `reset` (`/auth/password/forgot` → `/auth/password/reset` with the new password). Code TTL default 2 min (server `expires_at` honoured up to 10 min), resend cooldown 60 s, three wrong codes = 15 min block (`Retry-After ≥ 180 s` or "attempts" in the message counts as a block).
- **Session issue (`useAuthFinish`):** store tokens (SecureStore), disable another account's app lock, toast `Xush kelibsiz, {name}!`, post pending consents, dismiss all auth modals, replace `/`, then clear the OTP flow.
- **Refresh / expiry:** `http()` single-flights one `POST /auth/refresh` (`refresh_token`) on a 401; 400/401/403 from refresh (or a refresh token shorter than 20 chars) = revoked → `signOut(expired=true)` keeps the app-lock setting and the account's unsent outbox, sets `notice: "sessionExpired"`; network / 429 / 5xx keep the session. `SessionExpiryWatcher` pushes `/auth/login` once the splash is over and the login screen shows `Sessiya muddati tugadi. Qayta kiring.`. `SessionBoundary` clears the whole query cache and runs every session cleaner when `user.id:backendRole` changes; `useProfileRefresh` re-reads `GET /auth/me` (fields merged, undefined never erases).
- **Sign-out (`useLogout`):** confirm `Chiqish` / `Hisobdan chiqmoqchimisiz?`; best-effort `POST /auth/logout` with the captured tokens (one refresh-and-retry on 401); wipe tokens, stored profile, lock, badges, caches, push-token "sent" marker (`DELETE /devices` on a deliberate sign-out).
- **Account gate (`AccountGate`, TZ 3.2):** `accountGate(account_status)`: `active|approved|verified|enabled` (or empty) → ok; `pending*`, `awaiting_approval`, `under_review` → **pending**, a soft full-screen modal `Hisob tasdig'ini kutmoqda` / `Administrator hisobingizni ko'rib chiqmoqda. Profilingizni to'ldirishingiz mumkin; ish va mijozlar bilan amallar tasdiqdan keyin ochiladi.` with `Profilni to'ldirish` (sellers → `/pro/onboarding`), `Ilovaga o'tish` (dismiss until the status changes), `Holatni yangilash` (re-read `/auth/me`; `Hisob holati hali o'zgarmadi.`), `Chiqish`; `block|ban|suspend` → **blocked** (`Hisob bloklangan`), `delet|remov|closed` → **deleted** (`Hisob o'chirilgan`), `reject|declin|denied` → **rejected** (`So'rov rad etildi`), anything else → **restricted** (`Hisobdan foydalanib bo'lmaydi`) — these cannot be closed, only re-checked or signed out. The gate is suppressed while the biometric lock is up. A pending seller keeps only the workspace + AI tab and the drawer rows `dashboard`, `services` (yurist only), `subscription`, `notifications`, `support`, `onboarding` plus the account / settings rows.
- **2FA mandatory roles:** every non-client, and a client holding a staff role (`admin`, `manager`, `call_center*`, `sales*`, `advokat_tashkiloti`) — `DELETE /auth/2fa` answers 403.

### 2. Tab bars (`src/widgets/tab-bar/tabConfig.ts`)

- **guest:** `Bosh sahifa` (index, home) · `Hujjatlar` (services) · **AI** (centre) · `Ishlar` (works, `auth`) · `Xabarlar` (messages, `auth`, badge `messages`). The two `auth` tabs are disabled for a visitor; a tap sends them to `/auth/login` (iOS `tabPress` listener, Android `onPress`).
- **client:** `Bosh sahifa` · `Hujjatlar` · **AI** · `Ishlar` · `Xabarlar` (badge).
- **seller:** `Ish maydoni` (workspace, layers) · `So'rovlar` (requests, inbox, badge `requests`) · **AI** · `Mijozlar` (clients, users) · `Xabarlar` (badge). A pending / limited seller keeps only `Ish maydoni` and AI; `Xabarlar` needs the cabinet's `secure_chat` action (unknown cabinet hides nothing).
- `homeRoute(role)` = the first tab (`index` / `index` / `workspace`); `allowedRoutes` adds `profile`, `services`, `lawyers`, `orders`, `messages` as reachable-without-a-slot routes; a route outside the role (after sign-out / role switch) is replaced by the role's home.
- **AI centre button:** `AiButton` (Android flat bar) — brand-gradient pill with a zap icon, label `AI`; iOS `NativeTabsBar` (UITabBar, Liquid Glass on iOS 26) leaves the AI slot empty and draws `AiRaisedButton` rising above the bar at the exact centre (the `gap` slot keeps the count odd). The AI tab hides the bar (`/ai`). On a wide tablet the bar is hidden (docked sidebar) and only the AI button stays. Feather → SF Symbols: house.fill, doc.text.fill, sparkles, briefcase.fill, message.fill, square.stack.3d.up.fill, tray.fill, person.2.fill.
- **Badges (`badgeStore`, `99+` cap):** `messages` = unread of my rooms (`useUnreadBadge`, MsgMounts, from `GET /secure-chats` kept live by `secure_chat.*`); `requests` = seller request counts (`ReqMounts`, `useRequestCounts`); `notifications` = unread folded events (`useBadgeSync`, also the app-icon badge); `support` = unread operator messages (`useSupportBadge`, drawer rows). Captions are measured and shortened (`Xab…` avoided by using the first word or hiding captions) so the raised button stays centred.

### 3. Drawer (`src/widgets/client-sidebar`)

- **Opening:** `MenuButton` (round `menu` icon, label `Menyuni ochish`) at the left of every page header row (`TopBar`) and the Home heroes; hidden on routes without a sidebar (`auth`, `chat`, `call`, `lawyer`, `assistant`, `instructor`, `specialist`, the `/ai` tab) and on a wide tablet, where the sidebar is docked beside the page (`TabletFrame`, 288 px open / 76 px icon rail, edge tab toggles, remembered per device). Opening dismisses the keyboard; the page dims, loses touch and screen-reader focus (`UnderSidebar`); close by tapping the dim, dragging left, or the Android back button. Width = min(84 % of the screen, 340); glass backdrop; rows cascade in.
- **Panel (`SidebarPanel`):** brand row (`BrandLogo`, `LexGo`, `{name} · Mijoz|Advokat|Yurist` or `Mehmon`); the rows; footer `Chiqish` (confirm) or, for a guest, `Kirish`.
- **Client / guest rows (`NAV`, titles `cpd.menu.*`, web CLIENT_NAV order):** `Boshqaruv paneli` (/), `SOS — Shoshilinch` (/sos), `Tezkor Advokat` (/urgent), `Mening so'rovlarim` (/urgent/requests), `Huquqiy hujjatlar` (/services), `Mening hujjatlarim` (/documents), `Advokat va yuristlar` (/lawyers), `Mening ishlarim` (/works), `Xabarlar` (/messages), `Sun'iy Intellekt` (/ai), `Muammo tahlili` (/intake), `Akademiya` (/academy), `Paketlar` (/packages), `Xizmat buyurtmalarim` (/orders), `Mos advokatlar` (/matches), `Tariflar` (/subscription), `To'lovlar` (/payments), `Sovg'alar` (/profile/gifts), `Do'stni taklif qilish` (/profile/referral), `Baholash va sharhlar` (/ratings), `Kafolat` (/warranty), `Shikoyatlar` (/complaints), `Yordam markazi` (/support, badge support); then kicker `QO'SHIMCHA` with **EXTRA**: `Tarif huquqlari` (/entitlements), `Roziliklar` (/profile/consents). `GUEST_OPEN = ["/", "/services", "/lawyers", "/ai", "/academy", "/packages", "/subscription"]` — every other row sends a visitor to `/auth/login`.
- **Seller rows (`sellerNav`, titles `lsb.*`, web LAWYER_NAV / ADVOCATE_NAV order):** yurist: `Boshqaruv paneli` (workspace), `Ishlar bozori` (/pro/market), `Xizmat buyurtmalari` (/pro/orders), `Mening ishlarim` (/pro/cases), `Tezkor ishlar` (/pro/urgent), `Vazifalar` (/pro/tasks), `Kalendar` (/pro/calendar), `Mijozlar` (clients tab), `Xizmatlarim` (/pro/services), `Hujjatlar` (/pro/templates), `Hujjat so'rovlari` (/pro/docs), `Fayllar` (/pro/files), `Xavfsiz chat` (messages tab), `Uchrashuvlar` (/meetings), `Bildirishnomalar` (/notifications, badge), `Sun'iy Intellekt chat` (/ai), `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi` (/pro/assistant), `Referal` (/pro/referrals), `Targ'ibot` (/pro/promotions), `Tariflar` (/subscription), `Yordam markazi` (/support, badge); advokat: `Boshqaruv paneli`, `Imkoniyatlar` (/pro/market), `Xizmat buyurtmalari`, `Mening ishlarim`, `Tezkor ishlar`, `Hujjat so'rovlari`, `Kalendar`, `Mijozlar`, `Xizmatlarim`, `Vazifalar`, `Xabarlar` (messages tab), `Uchrashuvlar`, `Bildirishnomalar`, `Fayllar`, `Sun'iy intellekt yordamchi`, `Referal`, `Targ'ibot`, `Tariflar`, `Yordam markazi`. Then the mobile-only pro tools from **PRO_SECTIONS** (`stats` `Statistika`, `availability` `Ish vaqti`, `experience`, `achievements`, `onboarding` `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari`, `proPackages`); then the **ACCOUNT** rows (titles `py9.menu.*`): `Shaxsiy ma'lumotlar` (/profile/edit), `To'lovlar va cheklar` (/payments), `Tarifim imkoniyatlari` (/entitlements), `Oila a'zolari` (/profile/family), `To'lov usullari` (/profile/methods), `Roziliklar` (/profile/consents), `Sovg'alar` (/profile/gifts), `Shikoyat va baholar` (/complaints), `LexGo kafolati` (/warranty); then **COMMON_SECTION** settings (minus `notifications`): `Xavfsizlik` (/security), `Ilova qulfi (Face ID)` (/settings/security-lock), `Xotira va kesh` (/settings/storage) — `Bildirishnoma sozlamalari` (/settings/notifications) is the first item of COMMON_SECTION and is reachable for clients through `ProfileMenu`'s definition only (clients reach it from the security page's cards). Permission rules: under review only `LIMITED_OK` rows + onboarding; `marketplace` / `opportunities` need `accept_orders`, `chat` / `messages` need `secure_chat`. No role has a profile row (the header avatar is the profile button).
- **TEAM_TASKS:** `Jamoa vazifalari` (/team-tasks) is prepended for anyone holding `tasks.view` (not guests).
- **Full PRO_SECTIONS (`widgets/profile/menu/proItems.ts`, titles `nav1.menu.*`):** pro: `Ro'yxatdan o'tish bosqichlari`, `Xizmatlarim va narxlar`, `Xizmat paketlari` (label `ntd.sellerV2.menuPackages`), `Ish vaqti`, experience (`mf.exp.menu`), achievements (`ach.menu`), `Reklama va boost`; proWork: `Hujjat so'rovlari`, `Marketplace buyurtmalari`, `Tezkor so'rovlar`, `Mening ishlarim` (/pro/cases), `Kalendar`, `Vazifalar`, `Fayllar (workspace)`, `Statistika`; billing: `Tarif va obuna`, `Referral`, `Support`. (Only the six keys listed above are pulled into the drawer.)

### 4. Header (`src/widgets/tab-page/HeaderActions.tsx`, `src/core/nudge/NudgeSlot.tsx`)

- Every tab page (`TabPage` → `TopBar`): `MenuButton` + greeting `Assalomu alaykum` + name (or `Mehmon`) on the left; on the right, page tools then `HeaderActions`: `DemoPill` (round `layers` button `Demo` / `Demo yoqilgan` with a green dot while sample data is on; flipping it refetches every query — plain GETs with demo data are answered locally), `SupportMenu` headset (client and seller; two soft rings + a wiggle play once every 7 s; sheet `Yordam kerakmi?` / `Biz shu yerdamiz — qulay yo'lni tanlang.`: gradient `Operatorga ulanish` / `Jonli chat — operator shu yerda javob beradi` → `/support`, client-only amber `Shikoyat yuborish` → `ComplaintSheet`, green `Qo'ng'iroq qilish` with the support phone and the `BusinessHoursBadge`, outline `Yordam markazini ochish`), the bell (`badge` dot while unread events exist; → `/notifications`, guest → `/auth/login`), the 40 px avatar → `/(tabs)/profile` (guest: a `user` icon → `/auth/login`).
- Under the title: `HeaderExtra` = `NudgeSlot` → `GiftNudge`, clients only and not on conversation / order screens (`/support/{id}`, `/chat/*`, `/sos`, `/urgent`, `/urgent/new`, `/services`, `/services/direction`). One variant is picked at random per app session from gift / pro / lite / lawyers — a plan the client already has is skipped (`pro` hidden for lexgo-ai-pro, `lite` for lite/pro; from GET `/subscription-plans` + entitlements `plan_ids`) — and shown until dismissed (`Yopish`): `Yaqinlaringizga g'amxo'rlik qiling` / `Sovg'a qilish` (→ /profile/gifts), `LexGo Pro bilan cheksiz imkoniyat` / `Pro'ga o'tish` (→ /subscription), `LexGo Lite — arzonroq, qulayroq` / `Lite'ni ko'rish`, `Eng yaxshi advokatlarni toping` / `Advokatlarni ko'rish` (→ /lawyers). Warm gradient card (dark variant in dark mode) with a shine.

### 5. Notifications and push (`src/entities/notification`, `src/core/push`, `src/shared/realtime`)

- **List:** `GET /notifications` 100 rows per page, infinite; rows folded into events by `event + payload` within one minute (`foldNotifications`), the `in_app` row represents the group; category derived on the device (exact names → prefix rules → delivery channel → title words → `system`). Text from `nt5`/`nt6` templates per event (`support_ticket_created` …) with `work`, `status`, `amount`, `name`, `service`, `minutes` variables; otherwise the backend body with UUIDs replaced by public numbers. Persisted on the device (`["notifications"]` 3 days, `["notification-prefs"]` 7 days).
- **Unread index:** `GET /notifications/unread-count` as a cheap gate, then up to 3 pages of `unread_only=true`; the badge and the app-icon badge count folded events; a server count mismatch (≤ 300) re-reads the index after a 700 ms settle.
- **Preferences:** `GET/PUT /notifications/preferences` — `push`, `sms`, `telegram`, `email`, `orders`, `payments`, `messages`, `marketing` (the security card sends one key; the settings page sends the whole set, queued offline). Local device prefs (`lexgo_notif_local`): banner, background, sound, vibrate, askedAt, snoozeUntil.
- **User socket `/ws/users/me?token=`** (`SessionBoundary` connects it with the access token, reconnects with backoff, pings every `PING_MS`, two silent pings = reconnect, kicked on foreground / network return; a reconnect runs `resync` handlers and one debounced active refetch of non-public queries). Channels consumed app-wide: `notification.created|read`, `notifications.read_all|unread_count` (inbox + unread); `payment.*`, `payments.*`, `subscription.*`, `gift.*`, `order.*`, `document_request.payment` (payments / entitlements / features); `secure_chat.*`, `marketplace.cancellation_chat_*` (rooms + badge); `call.incoming`, `call.*` (ring / call screen); `support.*` (tickets, assist cards, ringing support call, alerts); `ai_instructor.response`; `remote_assist.*`; `marketplace.*`, `document_request.*` (assist settle); `tasks.*`. The room and call sockets are separate (`/ws/secure-chats/{room}` and `…/calls/{call}`), closed while the app is away; the user socket stays open.
- **Foreground alerts (`useRealtimeAlerts`):** one alert per event: in-app `NotificationBanner` while active (unless the screen is about that event, or it is a remote-assist event), an OS local notification while backgrounded but connected (if `background` pref and the topic / `push` pref allow; chat bodies are hidden on the lock screen: `O'qish uchun ilovani oching`). `handler.ts` keeps the OS quiet in the foreground.
- **Permission:** asked in context (`PushPermissionCard`) and, for calls, 5 s after a signed-in launch at most once in 3 days (`useAskCallAlerts`); never at launch otherwise.
- **Background poll (`lexgo-notification-poll`, expo-background-task):** restores the session + locale, `unread-count` gate, newest 50 unread rows after a watermark → at most 4 OS alerts (+ `Yana {n} ta yangi voqea`), then the offline sync. First run only sets the watermark.
- **Taps (`useResponseRouter`):** a tapped notification (cold start included) opens the screen the event is about via `openLink` (role-aware, resource state checked), marks its rows read; `tasks.*` → `/team-tasks?task=`; remote-assist → consent sheet; nothing resolvable → `/notifications`. Call notifications: `Decline` answers without opening (`PATCH …/participants/{me}` `declined` + `answered_device_id`), Join / tap opens the call.
- **Remote push (README "LexGo Mobile Integration: Remote Push for Calls and Tasks", 2026-10-20):** WebSocket stays the foreground transport; remote push is the background / closed-app transport. `POST /devices` registers `{ platform, token_type (fcm | apns | apns_voip), token, installation_id, app_version, locale, environment }` (idempotent; `DELETE /devices {token}` on sign-out); `useRemoteRegistered` drives the settings page's `Masofaviy push` state. Payloads: `call.incoming { type, room_id, call_id, caller_name, caller_user_id, call_type }` (TTL 45 s; dedupe by `type + call_id`), `call.ended`, task alerts (`type + task_id`). The app defines `lexgo-remote-push` (expo-notifications background task) that rings on `call.incoming`, dismisses on `call.ended` and handles the Decline action. Per the README and the memory note, iOS incoming calls require APNs VoIP / PushKit + CallKit, Android calls an FCM high-priority data message; this build registers only the APNs alert token / FCM token (`token_type: apns|fcm`, no PushKit / CallKit / Firebase), so a **closed** app receives no call ring; a backgrounded-but-connected app rings over the socket and the periodic poll catches the rest.

### 6. Secure chat and calls (`src/features/secure-chat`, `src/widgets/secure-chat`, `src/widgets/call`, `src/features/call`)

- **Rooms:** `GET /secure-chats` — every room the account can see; `myRooms` keeps those where I am client, seller or a participant; kinds `urgent` (Tezkor request ids), `doc` (document lawyer requests), `order`, `case`, `other`; `isClosed` = archived / completed / read_only / closed; `unreadTotal` skips archived rooms. Room list persisted (`["secure-chat","rooms"]`, 3 days); messages, files and calls are never persisted (deny list). Names only — phone numbers are never shown (TZ 40).
- **Unread:** per room `unread_count`, cleared locally when the room opens and on the server by re-reading `GET …/messages` (also 1.2 s after a frame from somebody else); `secure_chat.*` events keep the list current; the tab badge follows.
- **Messages:** whole history over REST (newest 40 shown, more on scroll), live frames from `/ws/secure-chats/{room}` merged into the cache, optimistic outbox; text, file, voice (one upload at a time, in order, progress, cancel, refusal reasons), replies, blocked content (`[metadata_only]`), system / result / meeting / call notes. Long-press menu, auto-delete 0/24/168/720 h (`PATCH …/settings`), delete = archive (`DELETE`, recoverable ~1 month), `Chatni yakunlash` for the advocate / operator of a Tezkor room.
- **Screenshot blocking:** `useScreenGuard()` (expo-screen-capture `preventScreenCaptureAsync`) on the chat room and call screens; `SecretGuard` on the TOTP page; `MethodSheet` while open; the biometric lock blurs the iOS app switcher; a recording detected during a call (`ScreenGuard` native module: iOS `UIScreen.isCaptured`, Android 15+) mutes the mic and warns (`Ekran yozib olinmoqda` / `Skrinshot olib bo'lmaydi`).
- **Calls:** two `CallType`s `audio` | `video`; a "meeting" is a video call started from the meetings area (`POST …/calls` with `participant_user_ids` and `max_duration_minutes`, 60 min max for meetings); call `status` `active|scheduled` = live, `ended|cancelled|expired`. Who may start: accounts with `meetings.manage` (professionals, `useAccess().canCalls`) and the seller cabinet's `calls` action, limited by the order's gate; a client only through a document request (`POST /document-requests/{id}/calls/audio`, 3 calls, 4th = 429) or by joining. Reaching a call: chat header buttons / `ActiveCallBar` → `callPath(room, callId)` = `/call/{room}?callId=`; `chatPath(room, { join })` opens the chat and joins at once; the incoming ring (`IncomingCallWatcher`, mounted once: full-screen `IncomingCard` with a pulsing halo, caller, `Kiruvchi audio qo'ng'iroq` / `Kiruvchi video qo'ng'iroq`, title, `Rad etish` / `Qo'shilish`; ringtone + vibration, stops after 45 s; sources: `call.incoming` on the user socket (bare or inside a notification), a push handed over while the app is open, and `GET /calls/invited?limit=5` on start / foreground / reconnect / every 8 s while the socket is down (30 s safety net while up); quiet while a call screen is open); notifications (`Qo'ng'iroqqa qo'shilish`); support `CallRow`; the meetings list. Inside the call: LiveKit room from `join-token`, `POST …/join` once connected, call socket for signalling, roster / limits re-read on every `call.*` / `participant.*` / `media.*` frame; host controls (pause, free / paid extension, mute, kick, invite, end for all, transfer); recording consent (client asks, host allows, host starts — nothing records before yes); chat over the data channel; auto-leave after everybody left (10 s) and ~2 s after the call ends.

### 7. Theme and locale (`src/features/theme-switch`, `src/features/locale-switch`, `src/shared/theme`, `src/shared/i18n`)

- **Theme:** `useThemeStore` (`lexgo_theme` in AsyncStorage) `mode: light | dark | system` (default `system`); `ThemeProvider` picks `lightColors` / `darkColors`; `ThemeSegment` = `Yorug'` (sun) / `Qorong'i` (moon) / `Qurilma` (smartphone) on `/profile` (and the guest page). `SystemChrome` hands the choice to iOS (`Appearance.setColorScheme`, so the Liquid Glass tab bar matches), paints the page colour behind the status / navigation bars, resyncs the status-bar icon colour after Face ID / background / native modals; `NavigationTheme` feeds the palette to React Navigation; the root view uses the theme's `bg` so no black flash in light mode.
- **iOS glass flags (`shared/theme/flags.ts`):** `AURORA = false` (aurora backdrop, transparent pages, frosted cards — kept in code, off) and `FLOATING_GLASS = false` (glass icon buttons / pills / toasts / sheets / dialogs — off). The drawer and the system tab bar stay glass regardless (they ask for it explicitly).
- **Locale:** `useLocaleStore` (`lexgo_locale`) `uz | ru | en`, default `uz`; `LocaleSegment` labels `O'zbek` / `Русский` / `English`. Copy lives per namespace file under `locales/<lang>/`; Uzbek is the source of truth (auth errors show the server's own Uzbek sentence for uz users, translated kinds for ru/en). Consents and some backend titles arrive as `{uz, ru}` maps and follow the UI locale.

### 8. Remote assistance (`src/shared/remote-assist`, `src/features/remote-assist`, `src/widgets/remote-assist`)

- **What it is (TZ.md "Remote Assistance Frontend Integration Contract"):** an operator, with the client's consent, sees a _map_ of the screen the client has in front (page id + registered controls with their label, allowed actions and coarse state — never a field's value) and may navigate, highlight, tooltip, scroll, focus a field, set a value (personal fields only with the separate `personal_form_fill` consent), pick an option, toggle a switch, open a sheet, step a wizard or present a final button to the client. A final submit, a payment or a signature is never executed by the operator (`RA_FINAL` = shown to the client, pressed only by them). Secret controls (ids / labels naming passwords, OTP, PIN, cards, bank, signature, biometrics, passport / PINFL, identity, tokens, 2FA, TOTP, security …) are never put on the map; the backend can also block an id for the app run. Mounted only for a signed-in **client** (`RaMounts`: `RaHost` + `RaLayer` in the top-most overlay — banner, consent sheet, confirmations, outline); events arrive on the user socket (`remote_assist.*`) and as notifications whose tap opens the consent sheet.
- **Routes the operator may open (`routes.ts`):** `/` client.dashboard, `/ai`, `/services[/id]`, `/works[/id]`, `/messages`, `/lawyers` & `/marketplace`, `/specialist/{id}`, `/orders[/id]`, `/matches`, `/packages`, `/ratings`, `/warranty`, `/profile`, `/profile/edit`, `/profile/referral`, `/profile/gifts`, `/subscription(s)`, `/support[/id]`, `/documents[/id]`, `/notifications`, `/academy`, `/doc-analysis`, `/cases`, `/intake`, `/urgent[/id]`, `/sos`, `/team-tasks`, `/complaints` (web portal paths are translated). Sign-in, payments, security, identity, settings, the call screen, the professional's area and external links are never opened; anywhere else is published as `client.outside`.
- **Pages / controls registered among the shared routes:** `/profile` (`client.profile`): `profile.tabs` (`Profil bo'limlari`, pick — only `Profil` / `Shikoyatlar`), `profile.edit` (`Shaxsiy ma'lumotlarni tahrirlash`, opener), in the edit sheet / `/profile/edit` `profile.full_name` (`To'liq ism`, personal), `profile.email` (`Elektron pochta`, personal), `profile.save` (`Saqlash`, final). `/support` (`client.support`): `support.new_chat` (`Yangi murojaat`, opener); new-chat fields `support.message` (`Murojaat matni`), `support.topic` (`Mavzu`, pick), `support.urgent` (`Shoshilinch`, toggle). `/support/{id}`: `support.ticket.details` (`Murojaat tafsilotlari`, opener), `support.ticket.chat` (`Yozishmalar`, view). Other registered pages (documents, subscription, marketplace …) belong to the role-specific briefs.

### 9. Offline / errors (`src/shared/api`, `src/core/network`, `src/shared/cache`, `src/core/providers`)

- **`http()`:** `fetch(API_BASE + path)` with base headers + `Authorization: Bearer`; JSON body; one transparent refresh on 401; identical GETs in flight are shared (`shareGet`); a network failure throws `ApiError(0, "network_error")`; `httpRaw` for 428 login; `httpText` for huge JSON; `refusalOf` re-reads a refused download's error. `ApiError { status, detail, code, retryAfter, data, fieldErrors }` parsed from FastAPI `detail` (string / `{message, code}` / 422 list → `fieldErrors`). Helpers `isOfflineError` (status 0), `isPaymentRequired` (402 → the plan dialog), `isRateLimited`, `isLimitError`. Demo mode answers plain GETs locally. `GET /platform/policies` is shared for 10 min.
- **Query client:** staleTime 2 min, gcTime 30 min, `networkMode: offlineFirst`, retries twice except 4xx, `refetchOnReconnect`, focus = only a real return from the background; persisted with a buster, 1 day max age, public catalogue queries kept in AsyncStorage for everybody; signed-in snapshots in SQLite per user scope under the allow / deny rules (`persistRules.ts`: notifications, prefs, entitlements, chat room list, pro tasks / calendar / cases / clients / services / availability / workspace / requests / files / docs, client cases, my docs, orders, market orders, business hours, works, client stats — never auth, payments, security, identity, calls, AI, SOS, chat contents, editors, payouts). The cache is wiped on sign-out / account switch, except that a server-expired session keeps the account's unsent outbox.
- **Mutations** never wait for a connection (`networkMode: "always"` → fail at once); only the explicit **outbox** (idempotent PUT drafts such as notification preferences, offline-written drafts and messages) is queued and flushed in order on reconnect / foreground / every 30 s (`Yuborilmagan yozuvlar` card; failures toast `{n} ta o'zgarish serverga o'tmadi`).
- **Network monitor (`NetworkMount`):** connectivity → react-query `onlineManager`, socket kick, outbox flush, `GET /health` probe after three network failures (and every 8 s while "server down"); **`OfflineBanner`** pill under the status bar: `Internet yo'q` (+ `{n} ta yozuv yuborilmagan`) after 3 s offline, `LexGo uchun internet o'chiq` when the phone denies the app its network (plus a once-per-launch dialog `LexGo internetga ulana olmayapti` with OS-specific instructions and a `Sozlamalar` button), `Server javob bermayapti`, `Sekin ulanish` after 6 s, `{n} ta o'zgarish yuborilmoqda`, and a 2.4 s `Ulanish tiklandi`. **Soft offline:** a refetch that fails for lack of a connection keeps the cached data on screen (status back to success, `Oflayn: saqlangan nusxa` note where shown) instead of an error view; a first load stalled offline becomes a "no internet" `ErrorView` with retry once the disk restore had its chance, and continues by itself when the connection returns.
- **Per-screen error texts:** `common.offline` = `Server bilan bog'lanib bo'lmadi. Internet aloqasini tekshirib, qayta urining.`, `common.serverError` = `Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.`, `common.rateLimited` = `Juda ko'p urinish. Bir ozdan so'ng qayta urining.`; payment-related writes use `usePaymentError` (402 → `To'lov yoki tarif kerak` dialog → `/subscription`; provider down → `To'lov tizimi hozircha ulanmagan. Keyinroq urinib ko'ring.`; else the server's `detail`).
- **Updates:** `UpdateMount` checks for a new JS bundle 4 s after start and on foreground (≤ every 10 min); a downloaded one is offered once (`Yangilanish tayyor` / `Qayta ishga tushirish` / `Keyinroq`); the profile's `AppUpdateCard` is the manual way.

---

### Not determined from the code

- The exact Uzbek strings of the `nt5` / `nt6` per-event notification templates and of `cpd.menu`-less rows that fall back to the server's text (they are data-driven).
- Whether the backend sends `telegram_linked` on `/auth/me` on the current staging (the UI handles "unknown").
- The `GET /users/search` response shape used by `PeopleSheet` (invite / transfer / new chat) is outside the shared files read here.

# PART D — Design system and UX rules for the whole app

## D1. Mobile UX rules (every screen, every role)

- Canvas 393 × 852 (iPhone 15/16 Pro) with safe areas; the layout must also survive 360 × 800 (Android). Android follows Material 3 Expressive in the LexGo colours; iOS uses the system tab bar (Liquid Glass on iOS 26) with a solid fallback before iOS 26 and with Reduce Transparency.
- **First viewport decides.** On every screen the primary action and the one fact the person came for are visible without scrolling: client Home = ask field + status of current request + urgent entry; documents = search + category chips + 3 rows; workspace = next deadline/order/request; a list page = its first 3 rows; a detail page = status + next action.
- One-hand use: primary and urgent actions in the lower 60 % of the first viewport; nothing important only behind a horizontal scroll.
- Tap targets ≥ 44 × 44 pt; 16 pt screen gutter; 4 pt grid; 8–12 pt inside a group, 20–28 pt between groups; lists show 10 rows then `Yana ko'rsatish`.
- Copy is short: labels ≤ 3 words, no sentence longer than one line in the first viewport, no second explanation of the same thing. Full sentences only in sheets and empty states (one line + one action).
- Loading = skeletons in place; errors = one line + `Qayta urinish`; offline = a thin banner, the page keeps the last data (React Query cache is persisted).
- Contrast ≥ 4.5:1 for text in Light, Dark and Glass, including text on gradients and frosted panes. Reduce Motion: no idle animations; Reduce Transparency: solid surfaces.
- Status is always a coloured badge with an Uzbek label (never a raw key) and a dot of the same colour on the row.
- Money: `45 000 so'm`; dates: `28-okt, 14:30`; countdowns in minutes; `Bepul` for zero price.
- Every list has search or filters in a bottom sheet with a pinned `Natijalarni ko'rsatish · N` button and removable chips; filters never live only behind an icon when the category count is ≤ 5 (show chips).
- Destructive or paid actions confirm in a sheet, never inline.

## D2. Navigation to design

- **Tab bar**, 5 slots, AI raised in the centre as a flat spark glyph on the brand gradient (no 3D gem, orb, particle ring or idle animation). Labels one word, 11–12 pt; icons 24 pt; the `Xabarlar` badge shows `1`, `12`, `99+`; the `So'rovlar` badge for professionals the same. The AI screen hides the bar.
- **Header** on every tab page: ☰ drawer button, greeting + name (or the page title), then four 40 pt round buttons: demo pill (demo accounts only), support headset, bell with unread dot, avatar (opens Profil; there is no Profil tab). Stack pages: back arrow + title, header turns into a blurred pane as the page scrolls under it (iOS).
- **Drawer**: the role's full menu (section 3 per role); clients get the web portal's rows, professionals get workspace + tools + account + app settings. Active row highlighted; badges on notifications and support.
- **Sheets** for filters, pickers, order forms, confirmations, "who should answer", support menu, notification detail. Snap points ~0.6–0.95; pinned action bar at the bottom.

## D3. Visual system (to specify per direction)

- **Colour:** brand navy `#0B1F45` → blue `#123A86`/`#0E7FD0` → cyan `#00CFE8`. Tokens per mode (Light / Dark / Glass): `bg`, `canvas`, `surface`, `surface2`, `glassFill`, `glassLine`, `line`, `ink`, `gray`, `gray2`, `tint`, `tint2`, `accent`, `accentStrong`, status pairs `ok/okBg`, `warn/warnBg`, `err/errBg`, `cyBg/cyText`. Gradient only for one hero band or one primary CTA per screen. Role glow: advokat gold, yurist magenta (optional accent, not a theme).
- **Glass mode:** dim navy-tinted backdrop, frosted cards (translucent fill + 1 px bright top hairline, no shadow), real blur only on the floating layer (tab bar, header buttons, sheets, toasts). Give fill alpha, blur radius and hairline colour; a solid fallback must exist.
- **Radius scale:** ≤ 4 radii per direction, e.g. 10 (chips, icon tiles) · 14 (fields, inner tiles) · 20 (cards) · 28 (hero, sheets) + pill.
- **Type:** Onest for display (`hx` 28–32, `h2` 22, `h3` 20), Inter for UI (`h4` 17, `body` 15, `lead` 16, `small` 13, `caption` 11–12, `kicker` 11 caps). One alternative free pair may be proposed per direction; it must render Uzbek apostrophes (`o'`, `g'`) cleanly.
- **Elevation:** resting card, floating (sheet, tab bar, toast), primary CTA. Three levels.
- **Icons:** one stroke set (Feather/Lucide style, 1.5–2 px), 24 pt in bars, 20 pt in rows, 16 pt in chips.
- **Motion:** 150–250 ms ease-out, spring on press (scale 0.96–0.98 + light haptic), no loops.

## D4. The 5 directions (same for every screen)

1. **Calm Desk** — editorial, ink-first, hairline cards, minimal brand colour.
2. **Status First** — the active item (request / order / deadline) is the hero of every home; dense dashboard rhythm.
3. **Ask Bar** — one big composer/search as the anchor of each home; 2-column action grids.
4. **Soft Tiles** — tonal surfaces, 20–28 radii, large touch tiles (Material 3 Expressive feel).
5. **Disciplined Gradient** — a short navy→cyan band (≤ 30 % of the first viewport) that holds only the greeting + primary field; everything else neutral.

Each direction: Light, Dark, Glass of the same layout; identical content and section order across the three.

## D5. Deliverables

1. Overview frame: the 5 directions side by side (client Home, Light), one sentence each.
2. Per direction: client Home, `Hujjatlar`, `Ishlar`, one detail page (`/works/[id]`), the AI screen, professional `Ish maydoni`, `So'rovlar`, one order page (`/pro/orders/[id]`), `Mijozlar`, the drawer, the tab bar component (rest / pressed / active / badge), the filter sheet, the order/confirm sheet — in Light, Dark, Glass; plus one 360-wide Android frame per screen family (Light).
3. Token sheet per direction (hex, radii, type scale, elevation, glass parameters).
4. Component library: header, tab bar, card, list row with status badge, chip, search field, segmented control, button set, sheet, empty state, skeleton, toast, countdown, progress bar, avatar, paper glyph.
5. Rationale per direction (≤ 120 words) and a final recommendation with two A/B tests.

## D6. Final checklist

- [ ] Every screen in Parts A–C has a frame or a documented component state; nothing from the layouts is dropped.
- [ ] Section order and tab/drawer order per role match this document exactly.
- [ ] All Uzbek labels used verbatim; no lorem ipsum; sample data from the "Data" tables.
- [ ] AI = ask field (client Home) + centre tab button only; the AI button is flat.
- [ ] First-viewport rule met on every home and list page.
- [ ] Contrast, tap targets, gutters, safe areas, scroll-under-bar verified in all three modes.
- [ ] Glass has a solid fallback; Reduce Motion has no idle animation.
- [ ] Marketing (nudge, promo, referral) is last, small and dismissible on every page where it appears.
